6
Empirical Study

In this section we consider two empirical applications: the heterogeneous investment equation and the heterogeneous
quantile effect of foreign direct investment on unemployment.

6.1
Investment Equation

In this subsection, we revisit the investment equation.
Fazzari et al., 1988
point out that investment may show sensitivity to
movements in cash flow when firms face constraints for external finance.
Since
Fazzari et al., 1988
, there has been a large literature on the effect of cash flow on
the corporate investment; see
Devereux and Schiantarelli, 1990
,
Gilchrist and Himmelberg, 1995
,
Kaplan and Zingales, 1995
,
Cleary, 1999
,
Rauh, 2006
, and
Almeida and Campello, 2007
, among others. Using the panel dataset, we consider
the scaled version of the investment equation as follows:

\frac{I_{it}}{K_{i,t-1}}=\Theta_{0,it}+\Theta_{1,it}\frac{CF_{it}}{K_{i,t-1}}+\Theta_{2,it}q_{i,t-1}+u_{it},

where
I
is the corporate investment,
CF
is the cash flow,
q
is the
Tobin’s q,
K
is the capital stock and
u
is the innovation.
\Theta_{0,it}
refers to the fixed effects (FEs). Rather than the mean estimation,

Galvao and Wang, 2015
estimate the effects of the firm’s cash flow and
Tobin’s q on investment at different quantiles. By using the panel quantile
regression with individual FEs, they show that the slope estimates change
across
\tau
. However, they do not allow the slope
coefficients,
\Theta_{1}
and
\Theta_{2},
to change either over
i
or
t.
Inspired by
Galvao and Wang, 2015
, we estimate the following model

\mathscr{Q}_{\tau}\left(IK_{it}\big|\left\{CFK_{it},q_{i,t-1}\right\}_{t\in[T]},\left\{\Theta_{j,it}\right\}_{t\in[T],j\in\{0,1,2\}}\right)=\Theta_{0,it}(\tau)+\Theta_{1,it}(\tau)CFK_{it}+\Theta_{2,it}(\tau)q_{i,t-1},

(6.1)

where
IK_{it}=\frac{I_{it}}{K_{i,t-1}}
, and
CFK_{it}=\frac{CF_{it}}{K_{i,t-1}}
. Here we don’t restrict the specific structure on the FEs and
they can be either additive or interactive.

The data are taken from the China Stock Market & Accounting Research
(CSMAR) Database. We use quarterly data for 195 manufacturing firms in China
from 2003 to 2020. Based on the model (
6.1
), we define corporate
investment as
I_{it}=LI_{it}-LI_{i,t-1}
, where
LI_{it}
is the total
value of long-term corporate investment as the sum of long-term equity
investment, long-term bound investment, fixed assets and immaterial assets.
The investment measures the change of firm’s total investment compared to
the last period. All these four variables can be easily obtained from the
balance sheet. We directly use Tobin’s q from the CSMAR database, where by
definition
q=\frac{MV}{K}
and
MV
is the market value of the firm. We obtain a balanced panel dataset with 195 firms and 72
time periods. The units of corporate investment, capital and cash flow are measured by billions of
Chinese RMB.

By using the SVT approach, we obtain the estimates of the ranks of
\Theta_{1}
and
\Theta_{2}:

\hat{r}_{1}=\hat{r}_{2}=1
for each
\tau=\{0.25,0.5,0.75\}
. Consequently, we can consider the test that whether
\Theta_{j,it}
is constant over
i
or constant over
t
for both
j=1,2
.
Specifically, we want to test whether the effect of cash flow and Tobin’s q
on the firm’s investment is homogeneous over
i
or across
t
with market
imperfection. That is, for
j\in\{1,2\},
we shall test

•

H_{0}^{a}
:
\Theta_{j,it}
is a constant over
i
,

•

H_{0}^{b}
:
\Theta_{j,it}
is a constant over
t
.

Figure
1
shows the estimation results for the factor and
factor loadings of two slope coefficient matrices under different quantiles.
In each sub-figure, the first and second rows report the results for
\Theta_{1}
and
\Theta_{2},
respectively. Specifically, the first row of Figure
1
(a) gives the plot of
\left\{\hat{u}_{i,1}\right\}_{i\in[N]}
as a catenation of
\{\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(1,2)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(2,3)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)}\}
at the left and as a cantenation of
\{\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(1,3)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(2,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,2)}\}
at
the right in the first row, and similarly the plot of
\left\{\hat{u}_{i,2}\right\}_{i\in[N]}\
in the second row. Similarly, the first
row of Figure
1
(d) shows
\{\hat{v}_{t,1}^{(a,b)}\}_{t\in[T]}
for
a\in[3],

b\in[3]\setminus\{a\}
in the
first row and
\{\hat{v}_{t,3}^{(a,b)}\}_{t\in[T]}
for
a\in[3],

b\in[3]\setminus\{a\}
in the second row.

Table
7
reports the test statistics, critical values, and
p
-values. Tobin’s q can measure a firm’s investment demand. After controlling
the Tobin’s q and the intercept FEs, the coefficient of cash flow captures a
firm’s potential for external investment with the variation of internal
finance. It is clear that we can reject the homogeneous hypotheses for both
i
and
t
at the 1% significance level for each
\tau\in\{0.25,0.5,0.75\}
. This indicates that with high probability, the slope
coefficient of both
CFK
and Tobin’s q follow the factor structure with one
factor.

The above study shows strong evidence that under imperfect market, the
sensitivity of corporate investment to cash flow exhibits both individual
heterogeneity and time heterogeneity across quantiles. It implies that
neither the usual homogenous panel QR model nor the panel QR model with
either cross-section or time heterogeneity alone in the slope coefficients
fails to fully capture the unobserved heterogeneity in the investment
equation.

6.2
Foreign Direct Investment and Unemployment

Investment is one of the major driving forces for economic growth and
employment. Among the investment, foreign direct investment (FDI) is an
important contributor to the employment. See
Craigwell, 2006
,

Aktar et al., 2009
,
Karlsson et al., 2009
,
Mucuk and Demirsel, 2013
, and

Strat et al., 2015
, among others. Controversially,
Mucuk and Demirsel, 2013

argue that FDI may have both positive and negative effects on employment. On
the one hand, FDI adds to the net capital and creates jobs through forward and backward linkages and
multiplier effects in local economy. On the other hand, acquisitions may
rely on imports or displacement of existing firms which may result in job
loss.

To study the relationship of FDI, economic growth rate and unemployment at
the country level, we consider the following panel quantile regression
model,

\mathscr{Q}_{\tau}\left(U_{it}\big|\left\{G_{i,t-1},FDI_{it}\right\}_{t\in[T]},\left\{\Theta_{j,it}\right\}_{t\in[T],j\in\{0,1,2\}}\right)=\Theta_{0,it}(\tau)+\Theta_{1,it}(\tau)G_{i,t-1}+\Theta_{2,it}(\tau)FDI_{it},

where
U_{it}
is the unemployment rate of country
i
at year
t
,
G_{i,t-1}
is the economic growth measured by the growth of real GDP.
\Theta_{0,it}
is the FEs of country
i
and year
t
,
\Theta_{1,it}
is
the elasticity of the economic growth in the previous year to the
unemployment this year, and
\Theta_{2,it}
is the elasticity of FDI to the
unemployment.

We draw the data for 126 countries from 1992-2019. The data for the
unemployment rate are taken from International Labor Organization (ILO) and
GDP growth and FDI are from the World Bank Development Indicators (WDI)
historical database. The rank estimation procedure shows that
\hat{r}_{1}=2

and
\hat{r}_{2}=1
. Consequently, we can test whether the elasticity of FDI
to the unemployment rate is homogeneous across individual countries and over
years 1992-2009, and whether the elasticity of growth rate to unemployment
follows the additive structure, i.e.,

•

H_{0}^{c}
:
\Theta_{1,it}=\Theta_{1,i}+\Theta_{1,t}
,

•

H_{0}^{d}
:
\Theta_{2,it}
is a constant over
i
,

•

H_{0}^{e}
:
\Theta_{2,it}
is a constant over
t
.

Table
8
reports the test results under quantiles 0.25, 0.5 and
0.75 for the above three null hypotheses. Figure
2
gives the
estimation results for the factor and factor loading estimates of the slope
coefficient
\Theta_{2}
. As Table
8
suggests, we can reject
all the above three null hypotheses safely at the conventional 5%
significance level. This means that the effect of FDI on the unemployment
rate is different across both countries and time even though the estimated
rank of
\Theta_{2}
is one, and the effect of economic growth rate on the
unemployment is heterogeneous across both countries and time and it does not
exhibit an additive structure.

7
Conclusion

This paper considers panel QR model with heterogeneous slopes over both
i
and
t
. Compared to
Chernozhukov et al., 2019
, to remove the bias from the nuclear norm regularization, we split the full sample into three subsamples. We then use the first subsample to compute initial estimators via NNR, the second sample to refine the convergence rate of the initial estimator, and the last subsample to debias the refined estimator. Our asymptotic theory shows that the factor estimates, factor loading estimates and the slope estimates all follow the normal distributions asymptotically. By constructing the consistent estimator for the asymptotic variance, we also conduct two specification tests: (1) the slope coefficient is constant over time or individuals under the case that true rank of slope matrix equals one and (2) the slope coefficient exhibits the additive structure under the case that true rank of the slope coefficient matrix equals two. Our test statistics are shown to follow the Gumbel distribution asymptotically under the null, consistent under the global alternative and have non-trivial power against local alternatives. Monte Carlo simulation and empirical studies illustrate the finite sample performance of our algorithm and test statistics.

Online Supplement for

“Low-rank Panel Quantile Regression: Estimation and
Inference”

Yiren Wang
a
, Liangjun Su
b
and Yichong Zhang
a

a
School of Economics, Singapore Management University, Singapore

b
School of Economics and Management, Tsinghua University, China

This supplement contains three sections. Section A contains the
proofs of the main results by calling upon some technical lemmas in Section
B. Section B states and proves the technical lemmas used in Section A.
Section C provides detail algorithm for the nuclear norm regularized panel
quantile regression.

Appendix A
Proofs of the Main Results

A.1
Proof of Theorem
3.1

We focus on the full sample estimators
\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j}}
,
\tilde{\sigma}_{k,j}
, and
\tilde{V}_{j}
in the proof. The results for their
subsample counterparts can be established in the same manner, and we omit
the detail for brevity.

A.1.1
Proof of Statement (i)

Recall that

\mathcal{R}(C_{1},C_{2}):=\left\{(\{\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\}_{j=0}^{p}):\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\mathcal{P}_{j}^{\bot}(\Delta_{\Theta_{j}})\right\|_{\ast}\leq C_{1}\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\mathcal{P}_{j}(\Delta_{\Theta_{j}})\right\|_{\ast},\,\,\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{F}^{2}\geq C_{2}\sqrt{NT}\right\}.

Define
\mathcal{R}(C_{1}):=\left\{\{\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\}_{j=0}^{p}:\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\mathcal{P}_{j}^{\bot}(\Delta_{\Theta_{j}})\right\|_{\ast}\leq C_{1}\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\mathcal{P}_{j}(\Delta_{\Theta_{j}})\right\|_{\ast}\right\}
. By Lemma
B.4
,
\mathbb{P\{}\{\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j}}(\tau)\}_{j=0}^{p}

\in\mathcal{R}(3)\}\rightarrow 1.
When
\{\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j}}\}_{j=0}^{p}\in\mathcal{R}(C_{1})
and
\{\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j}}\}_{j=0}^{p}\notin\mathcal{R}(3,C_{2})
, we have

\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{F}^{2}<C_{2}\sqrt{NT},
which implies
\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\left\|\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{F}=O_{p}\left((N\wedge T)^{-1/2}\right),\hskip 9.24994pt\forall j\in[p]\cup\{0\}.
It suffices to consider
the case that
\{\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j}}\}_{j=0}^{p}\in\mathcal{R}(3,C_{2})
.

Define

\displaystyle\mathbb{Q}_{\tau}\left(\left\{\Theta_{j}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\rho_{\tau}\left(Y_{it}-\Theta_{0,it}-\sum_{j=1}^{p}X_{j,it}\Theta_{j,it}\right),\text{ and}

\displaystyle Q_{\tau}(\left\{\Theta_{j}\right\}_{j=0}^{p})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\rho_{\tau}\left(Y_{it}-\Theta_{0,it}-\sum_{j=1}^{p}X_{j,it}\Theta_{j,it}\right)\bigg|\mathscr{G}_{i,t-1}\right],

where
\mathscr{G}_{i,t-1}
is defined in Assumption
1
. Then we
have

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\geq\mathbb{Q}_{\tau}\left(\left\{\Theta_{j}^{0}+\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j}}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\right)-\mathbb{Q}_{\tau}\left(\left\{\Theta_{j}^{0}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\right)+\sum_{j=0}^{p}\nu_{j}\left(\left\|\Theta_{j}^{0}+\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{\ast}-\left\|\Theta_{j}^{0}\right\|_{\ast}\right)

\displaystyle=\left\{\mathbb{Q}_{\tau}\left(\left\{\Theta_{j}^{0}+\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j}}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\right)-\mathbb{Q}_{\tau}\left(\left\{\Theta_{j}^{0}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\right)-\left[Q_{\tau}\left(\left\{\Theta_{j}^{0}+\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j}}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\right)-Q_{\tau}\left(\left\{\Theta_{j}^{0}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\right)\right]\right\}

\displaystyle+\left[Q_{\tau}\left(\left\{\Theta_{j}^{0}+\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j}}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\right)-Q_{\tau}\left(\left\{\Theta_{j}^{0}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\right)\right]+\sum_{j=0}^{p}\nu_{j}\left(\left\|\Theta_{j}^{0}+\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{\ast}-\left\|\Theta_{j}^{0}\right\|_{\ast}\right),

(A.1)

where the first inequality holds by the definition of the estimator. Noted
that

\nu_{j}\left|\left\|\Theta_{j}^{0}+\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{\ast}-\left\|\Theta_{j}^{0}\right\|_{\ast}\right|\leq\nu_{j}\left\|\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{\ast}\leq c_{8}\nu_{j}\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{F}

(A.2)

where the first inequality is due to triangle inequality and the second
inequality holds by Lemma
B.7
with positive constant
c_{8}
defined in the lemma.

Define

\displaystyle\rho_{it}\left(\left\{\Delta_{\Theta_{j},it},X_{j,it}\right\}_{j=0}^{p},\epsilon_{it}\right)=\rho_{\tau}\left(\epsilon_{it}-\Delta_{0,it}-\sum_{j=1}^{p}X_{j,it}\Delta_{\Theta_{j},it}\right)-\rho_{\tau}\left(\epsilon_{it}\right),

(A.3)

\displaystyle\bar{\rho}_{it}\left(\left\{\Delta_{\Theta_{j},it},X_{j,it}\right\}_{j=0}^{p},\epsilon_{it}\right)=\mathbb{E}\left[\rho_{\tau}\left(\epsilon_{it}-\Delta_{0,it}-\sum_{j=1}^{p}X_{j,it}\Delta_{\Theta_{j},it}\right)-\rho_{\tau}\left(\epsilon_{it}\right)\bigg|\mathscr{G}_{i,t-1}\right],

\displaystyle\tilde{\rho}_{it}\left(\left\{\Delta_{\Theta_{j},it},X_{j,it}\right\}_{j=0}^{p},\epsilon_{it}\right)=\rho_{it}\left(\left\{\Delta_{\Theta_{j},it},X_{j,it}\right\}_{j=0}^{p},\epsilon_{it}\right)-\bar{\rho}_{it}\left(\left\{\Delta_{\Theta_{j},it},X_{j,it}\right\}_{j=0}^{p},\epsilon_{it}\right),

(A.4)

\displaystyle\mathscr{A}_{1}=\left\{\sup_{\left\{\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\in\mathcal{R}(3,C_{2})}\frac{\left|\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\tilde{\rho}_{it}\left(\left\{\Delta_{\Theta_{j},it},X_{j,it}\right\}_{j=0}^{p},\epsilon_{it}\right)\right|}{\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{F}}\leq C_{5}a_{NT}\right\},

with
a_{NT}=\frac{\sqrt{\left(N\vee T\right)\log\left(N\vee T\right)}}{NT}
for some positive constant
C_{5}
, and
\mathscr{A}_{1}^{c}
as the
complement of
\mathscr{A}_{1}
.

On
\mathscr{A}_{1}
, following (
A.1.1
), we have w.p.a.1

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\geq\left[Q_{\tau}\left(\left\{\Theta_{j}^{0}+\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j}}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\right)-Q_{\tau}\left(\left\{\Theta_{j}^{0}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\right)\right]

\displaystyle-\left|\mathbb{Q}_{\tau}\left(\left\{\Theta_{j}^{0}+\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j}}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\right)-\mathbb{Q}_{\tau}\left(\left\{\Theta_{j}^{0}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\right)-\left[Q_{\tau}\left(\left\{\Theta_{j}^{0}+\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j}}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\right)-Q_{\tau}\left(\left\{\Theta_{j}^{0}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=0}^{p}\nu_{j}\left|\left\|\Theta_{j}^{0}+\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{\ast}-\left\|\Theta_{j}^{0}\right\|_{\ast}\right|

\displaystyle\geq\frac{c_{7}C_{3}}{NT\xi_{N}^{2}}\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{F}^{2}-\frac{c_{7}C_{4}}{NT\xi_{N}^{2}}\left(N+T\right)-\frac{C_{5}\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{F}\sqrt{\left(N\vee T\right)\log\left(N\vee T\right)}}{NT}

\displaystyle-c_{8}\sum_{j=0}^{p}\nu_{j}\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{F},

where the first inequality is by triangle inequality, the second inequality
holds by (
A.2
) and Lemmas
B.6
and
B.11
. It follows that

\displaystyle\frac{c_{7}C_{3}}{NT\xi_{N}^{2}}\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{F}^{2}-\frac{c_{8}\left(p+1\right)c_{0}\sqrt{N\vee\left(T\log T\right)}+C_{5}\sqrt{\left(N\vee T\right)\log\left(N\vee T\right)}}{NT}\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle-\frac{c_{7}C_{4}}{NT\xi_{N}^{2}}\left(N+T\right)\leq 0,

which implies

\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\left\|\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{F}=O\left(\frac{\sqrt{\log(N\vee T)}\xi_{N}^{2}}{\sqrt{N\wedge T}}\right)

under the event
\mathscr{A}_{1}
.

By Lemma
B.11
, for any
\delta>0
, we can choose a
sufficiently large
C_{5}
such that
\mathbb{P}\left\{\mathscr{A}_{1}^{c}\right\}\leq\delta
. This implies

\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\left\|\tilde{\Theta}_{j}-\Theta_{j}^{0}\right\|_{F}=O_{p}\left(\frac{\sqrt{\log(N\vee T)}\xi_{N}^{2}}{\sqrt{N\wedge T}}\right),\hskip 9.24994pt\forall j\in\left\{0,\cdots,p\right\}.\hskip 9.24994pt\blacksquare

A.1.2
Proof of Statement (ii)

With the statement (i), the second statement holds by the Weyl’s inequality.

\blacksquare

A.1.3
Proof of Statement (iii)

For
\forall j\in\left\{0,\cdots,p\right\}
, let
\tilde{D}_{j}=\frac{1}{NT}\tilde{\Theta}_{j}^{\prime}\tilde{\Theta}_{j}=\hat{\tilde{\mathcal{V}}}_{j}\hat{\tilde{\Sigma}}_{j}\hat{\tilde{\mathcal{V}}}_{j}^{\prime}
, and
recall that
D_{j}^{0}=\frac{1}{NT}\Theta_{j}^{0\prime}\Theta_{j}^{0}=\mathcal{V}_{j}^{0}\Sigma_{j}^{0}\mathcal{V}_{j}^{0\prime}
. Define the
event
\mathscr{A}_{2}(M)=\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\left\|\tilde{\Theta}_{j}-\Theta_{j}^{0}\right\|_{F}\leq M\eta_{N},\forall j\in\left\{0,\cdots,p\right\}\right\}
with
\eta_{N}=\frac{\sqrt{\log(N\vee T)}\xi_{N}^{2}}{\sqrt{N\wedge T}}
. On event
\mathscr{A}_{2}(M)
, for some
positive constant
C_{6}
,

\left\|\tilde{D}_{j}-D_{j}^{0}\right\|_{F}^{2}\leq C_{6}\eta_{N}.

By Lemma C.1 of
Su et al., 2020
and Davis-Kahan sin
\Theta
theorem in

Yu et al., 2015
, there exists an orthogonal rotation matrix
O_{j}
such
that

\left\|\mathcal{V}_{j}^{0}-\hat{\tilde{\mathcal{V}}}_{j}O_{j}\right\|_{F}\leq\sqrt{K_{j}}\left\|\mathcal{V}_{j}^{0}-\hat{\tilde{\mathcal{V}}}_{j}O_{j}\right\|_{op}\leq\sqrt{K_{j}}\frac{\sqrt{2}C_{6}\eta_{N}}{\Sigma_{K_{j},1}^{2}-C_{6}\eta_{N}}\leq\sqrt{K_{j}}\frac{\sqrt{2}C_{6}\eta_{N}}{c_{\sigma}^{2}-C_{6}\eta_{N}}\leq\sqrt{K_{j}}\frac{\sqrt{2}C_{6}\eta_{N}}{C_{7}c_{\sigma}^{2}}\leq C_{8}\eta_{N},

for
C_{8}=\frac{\sqrt{2}C_{6}\sqrt{\bar{K}}}{C_{7}c_{\sigma}^{2}}
. The
second last inequality holds with some positive constant
C_{7}
and the
fact that
\eta_{N}
is sufficiently small.

Then
\left\|V_{j}^{0}-\tilde{V}_{j}O_{j}\right\|_{F}\leq C_{8}\sqrt{T}\eta_{N}
by the definition of
\tilde{V}_{j}
and
V_{j}
. Let
\mathscr{A}_{2}^{c}(M)
be the complement of event
\mathscr{A}_{2}(M)
. Combining the
fact that
\mathbb{P}\left\{\mathscr{A}_{2}^{c}(M)\right\}\rightarrow 0
,
it implies
\left\|V_{j}^{0}-\tilde{V}_{j}O_{j}\right\|_{F}=O_{p}\left(\sqrt{T}\eta_{N}\right).\hskip 9.24994pt\blacksquare

A.2
Proof of Theorem
3.2

A.2.1
Proof of Statement (i)

We prove that
\max_{i\in I_{2}}\left\|O_{j}^{(1)\prime}\dot{u}_{i,j}^{(1)}-u_{i,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}(\eta_{N})
and the
\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|O_{j}^{(1)\prime}\dot{u}_{i,j}^{(1)}-u_{i,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}(\eta_{N})
can be derived in the same manner once statement (ii) is satisfied. Define

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathbb{Q}}_{\tau i}\left(\left\{u_{i,j}\right\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\right)=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\rho_{\tau}\left(Y_{it}-u_{i,0}^{\prime}\tilde{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}-\sum_{j=1}^{p}u_{i,j}^{\prime}\tilde{v}_{t,j}^{(1)}X_{j,it}\right),

\displaystyle u_{i}^{0}=\left[u_{i,0}^{0\prime},\cdots,u_{i,p}^{0\prime}\right]^{\prime},\hskip 9.24994pt\dot{\Delta}_{i,j}=O_{j}^{(1)\prime}\dot{u}_{i,j}^{(1)}-u_{i,j}^{0},\hskip 9.24994pt\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}=\left(\dot{\Delta}_{i,0}^{\prime},\cdots,\dot{\Delta}_{i,p}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime},

\displaystyle\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}=\left[\left(O_{0}^{(1)\prime}\tilde{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime},\left(O_{1}^{(1)\prime}\tilde{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}X_{1,it}\right)^{\prime},\cdots,\left(O_{p}^{(1)\prime}\tilde{v}_{t,p}^{(1)}X_{p,it}\right)^{\prime}\right]^{\prime},\hskip 9.24994pt\tilde{\Phi}_{i}^{(1)}=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)\prime},

\displaystyle w_{1,it}=Y_{it}-\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i0}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\tilde{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}-\sum_{j=1}^{p}\left(O_{j}^{(1)}u_{i,j}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\tilde{v}_{t,j}^{(1)}X_{j,it}=Y_{it}-u_{i}^{0\prime}\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}=\epsilon_{it}-u_{i}^{0\prime}(\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}-\Phi_{it}^{0}),

and for
i\in I_{2}
, recall that
\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}}
is the
\sigma
-field generated by

\left\{\epsilon_{i^{*}t},e_{i^{*}t}\right\}_{i^{*}\in I_{1},t\in[T]}\bigcup\left\{e_{it}\right\}_{t\in[T]}\bigcup\left\{V_{j}^{0}\right\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\bigcup\left\{W_{j}^{0}\right\}_{j\in[p]}.

By construction, we have

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\geq\dot{Q}_{\tau i,u}\left(\left\{\dot{u}_{i,j}^{(1)}\right\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\right)-\dot{Q}_{\tau i,u}\left(\left\{O_{j}^{(1)}u_{i,j}^{0}\right\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\rho_{\tau}\left(Y_{it}-\dot{u}_{i,0}^{(1)\prime}O_{0}^{(1)}O_{0}^{(1)\prime}\tilde{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}-\sum_{j=1}^{p}\dot{u}_{i,j}^{(1)\prime}O_{j}^{(1)}O_{j}^{(1)\prime}\tilde{v}_{t,j}^{(1)}X_{j,it}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\rho_{\tau}\left(Y_{it}-u_{i,0}^{0\prime}O_{0}^{(1)\prime}\tilde{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}-\sum_{j=1}^{p}u_{i,j}^{0\prime}O_{j}^{(1)\prime}\tilde{v}_{t,j}^{(1)}X_{j,it}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left[\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{w_{1,it}\leq 0\right\}\right)\right]+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\int_{0}^{\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{w_{1,it}\leq s\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{w_{1,it}\leq 0\right\}\right)ds

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{w_{1,it}\leq 0\right\}\right)\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}}\right]\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}

\displaystyle+\left\{\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left[\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{w_{1,it}\leq 0\right\}\right)-\mathbb{E}\left(\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{w_{1,it}\leq 0\right\}\right)\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}}\right)\right]\right\}\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\int_{0}^{\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq s\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)-\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq s\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}}\right)ds

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\int_{0}^{\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}}\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq s\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}}\right)ds

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\int_{0}^{\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{w_{1,it}\leq s\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq s\right\}\right)-\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{w_{1,it}\leq s\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq s\right\}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}}\right)ds

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\int_{0}^{\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}}\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{w_{1,it}\leq s\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq s\right\}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}}\right)ds

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left[\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{w_{1,it}\leq 0\right\}\right)\right]

\displaystyle:=\sum_{m=1}^{7}A_{m,i},

(A.5)

where the first inequality holds by the definition of the estimator and the
second equality holds by Knight’s identity in
Knight, 1998

which states that

\rho_{\tau}(u-v)-\rho_{\tau}(u)=v(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{u\leq 0\right\})+\int_{0}^{v}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{u\leq s\right\}-\mathbf{1}\{u\leq 0\}\right)ds.

After simple manipulation, we have

\displaystyle\left|A_{4,i}\right|

\displaystyle=A_{4,i}\leq-A_{1,i}-A_{2,i}-A_{3,i}-A_{5,i}-A_{6,i}-A_{7,i}

\displaystyle\leq\left|A_{1,i}\right|+\left|A_{2,i}\right|+\left|A_{3,i}\right|+\left|A_{5,i}\right|+\left|A_{6,i}\right|+\left|A_{7,i}\right|.

Define, for some constant
M
, an event set

\mathscr{A}_{3}(M)=\left\{\max_{i\in I_{2}}\left(|A_{m,i}|/\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}\right\|_{2}\right)\leq M\eta_{N},\hskip 9.24994ptm=1,2,3,5,6,7\right\}

and

q_{i}^{I}=\inf_{\Delta}\frac{\left[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left(\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\Delta\right)^{2}\right]^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left|\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\Delta\right|^{3}}.

(A.6)

Then, under
\mathscr{A}_{3}(M)
, we have

\displaystyle M\eta_{N}\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\geq|A_{4,i}|

\displaystyle\geq\min\left(\frac{\left(3c_{11}^{2}\underline{\mathfrak{f}}-c_{11}^{3}\bar{\mathfrak{f}}^{\prime}\right)c_{\phi}\max_{i\in I_{2}}\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}\right\|_{2}^{2}}{12},\frac{\left(3c_{11}^{2}\underline{\mathfrak{f}}-c_{11}^{3}\bar{\mathfrak{f}}^{\prime}\right)\sqrt{c_{\phi}}q_{i}^{I}\max_{i\in I_{2}}\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}\right\|_{2}}{6\sqrt{2}}\right),

(A.7)

where
c_{11}<\min(\frac{3\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}{\bar{\mathfrak{f}}^{\prime}},1)
and the second inequality holds by Lemma
B.15
.
In addition, note that

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{2}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}^{3}=\max_{i\in I_{2}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left(\left\|\tilde{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\sum_{j\in[p]}\left\|\tilde{v}_{t,j}^{(1)}X_{j,it}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{3/2}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i\in I_{2}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left[\left(\frac{2M}{c_{\sigma}}\right)^{2}\left(1+\sum_{j\in[p]}X_{j,it}^{2}\right)\right]^{3/2}\leq C_{9},\hskip 9.24994pt\text{a.s.}

with
C_{9}
being a positive constant, where the first inequality holds by
Lemma
B.13
(ii) and the second inequality is by
Assumption
1
(iv). Then we have by Lemma
B.14

q_{i}^{I}\geq\inf_{\Delta}\frac{\left\|\Delta\right\|_{2}^{3}\left[\lambda_{\min}\left(\tilde{\Phi}_{i}^{(1)}\right)\right]^{2/3}}{\left\|\Delta\right\|_{2}^{3}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}^{3}}\geq\frac{\min_{i\in I_{2}}\left[\lambda_{\min}\left(\tilde{\Phi}_{i}^{(1)}\right)\right]^{2/3}}{\max_{i\in I_{2}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}^{3}}>\frac{\left(c_{\phi}/2\right)^{2/3}}{C_{9}},

which implies

\frac{\left(3c_{11}^{2}\underline{\mathfrak{f}}-c_{11}^{3}\bar{\mathfrak{f}}^{\prime}\right)\sqrt{c_{\phi}}q_{i}^{I}\max_{i\in I_{2}}\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}\right\|_{2}}{6\sqrt{2}}>C_{10}\max_{i\in I_{2}}\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}\right\|_{2}\eta_{N},

as
C_{10}
is defined to be the positive constant and
\eta_{N}=o(1)
.
Combining this with (
A.7
), we have

\max_{i\in I_{2}}\left\|O_{j}^{(1)\prime}\dot{u}_{i,j}^{(1)}-u_{i,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}\leq\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}\right\|_{2}\leq M^{\prime}\eta_{N}

for some constant
M^{\prime}
which may depends on
M
. In addition, for
an arbitrary constant
e>0
, we can find a sufficiently large constant
M

such that
\mathbb{P}(\mathscr{A}_{2}^{c}(M))\leq e
, which implies
\max_{i\in I_{2}}\left\|O_{j}^{(1)\prime}\dot{u}_{i,j}^{(1)}-u_{i,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}(\eta_{N}).\hskip 9.24994pt\blacksquare

A.2.2
Proof of Statement (ii)

Differently from the proof in the previous subsection, owing to the
dependence of
\dot{u}_{i,j}^{(1)}
and
\epsilon_{it}
, we can not directly
use conditional exponential inequality. In this subsection, we will show how
to handle this dependence in detail. Recall that
\mathscr{D}_{e_{it}}
is
the
\sigma
-field generated by
\{e_{j,it}\}_{j\in[p]}\cup\left\{V_{j}^{0}\right\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\cup\left\{W_{j}^{0}\right\}_{j\in[p]}
and define

\displaystyle\dot{Q}_{\tau t,v}\left(\left\{v_{t,j}\right\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\right)=\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\rho_{\tau}\left(Y_{it}-v_{t,0}^{\prime}\dot{u}_{i,0}^{(1)}-\sum_{j=1}^{p}v_{t,j}^{\prime}\dot{u}_{i,j}^{(1)}X_{j,it}\right),

\displaystyle v_{t}^{0}=\left(v_{t,0}^{0\prime},\cdots,v_{t,p}^{0\prime}\right)^{\prime},\hskip 9.24994pt\dot{\Delta}_{t,j}=O_{j}^{(1)\prime}\dot{v}_{t,j}^{(1)}-v_{t,j}^{0},\hskip 9.24994pt\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}=\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,0}^{\prime},\cdots,\dot{\Delta}_{t,p}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime},

\displaystyle\dot{\Psi}_{it}^{(1)}=\left[\left(O_{0}^{(1)\prime}\dot{u}_{i,0}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime},\left(O_{1}^{(1)\prime}\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}X_{1,it}\right)^{\prime},...,\left(O_{p}^{(1)\prime}\dot{u}_{i,p}^{(1)}X_{p,it}\right)^{\prime}\right]^{\prime},

\displaystyle\dot{\Psi}_{t}^{(1)}=\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\dot{\Psi}_{it}^{(1)}\dot{\Psi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}.

As in (
A.2.1
), we have

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\geq\dot{Q}_{\tau t,v}\left(\left\{\dot{v}_{t,j}^{(1)}\right\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\right)-\dot{Q}_{\tau t,v}\left(\left\{O_{j}^{(1)}v_{t,j}^{0}\right\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\rho_{\tau}\left(Y_{it}-\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)\prime}O_{0}^{(1)}O_{0}^{(1)\prime}\dot{u}_{i,0}^{(1)}-\sum_{j=1}^{p}\dot{v}_{i,j}^{(1)\prime}O_{j}^{(1)}O_{j}^{(1)\prime}\dot{u}_{t,j}^{(1)}X_{j,it}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\rho_{\tau}\left(Y_{it}-v_{t,0}^{0\prime}O_{0}^{(1)\prime}\dot{u}_{i,0}^{(1)}-\sum_{j=1}^{p}v_{t,j}^{0\prime}O_{j}^{(1)\prime}\dot{u}_{t,j}^{(1)}X_{j,it}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\left[\dot{\Psi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{w_{3,it}\leq 0\right\}\right)\right]+\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\int_{0}^{\dot{\Psi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{w_{3,it}\leq s\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{w_{3,it}\leq 0\right\}\right)ds

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\left[\Psi_{it}^{0\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)\right]+\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\left[\left(\dot{\Psi}_{it}^{(1)}-\Psi_{it}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\left[\dot{\Psi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{w_{3,it}\leq 0\right\}\right)\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\int_{0}^{\dot{\Psi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq s\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)ds\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{it}}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\left\{\int_{0}^{\dot{\Psi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}}\left[\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq s\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)-\mathbb{E}\left(\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq s\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)ds\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{it}}\right)\right]\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\int_{0}^{\dot{\Psi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{w_{3,it}\leq s\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq s\right\}\right)ds

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\int_{0}^{\dot{\Psi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{w_{3,it}\leq 0\right\}\right)ds

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\left[\Psi_{it}^{0\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)\right]+\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\left[\left(\dot{\Psi}_{it}^{(1)}-\Psi_{it}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\left[\dot{\Psi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{w_{3,it}\leq 0\right\}\right)\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\int_{0}^{\dot{\Psi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq s\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{it}}\right]ds

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\left\{\int_{0}^{\dot{\Psi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}}\left[\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq s\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)-\mathbb{E}\left(\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq s\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{it}}\right)\right]ds\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\int_{0}^{\dot{\Psi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{w_{3,it}\leq s\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq s\right\}\right)ds

\displaystyle:=\sum_{m=1}^{6}B_{m,t}

(A.8)

where
w_{3,it}=Y_{it}-v_{t,0}^{0\prime}O_{0}^{(1)\prime}\dot{u}_{i,0}^{(1)}-\sum_{j=1}^{p}v_{t,j}^{0\prime}O_{j}^{(1)\prime}\dot{u}_{i,j}^{(1)}X_{j,it}=Y_{it}-v_{t}^{0\prime}\dot{\Psi}_{it}^{(1)}=\epsilon_{it}-v_{t}^{0\prime}\left(\dot{\Psi}_{it}^{(1)}-\Psi_{it}^{0}\right)
, the last equality is by the fact that the third and the last
terms after the second equality are identical. Then we obtain
\left|B_{4,t}\right|\leq\sum_{m\neq 4}\left|B_{m,t}\right|.
By
Lemma
B.16
and similar arguments for Theorem
3.2
(i), we obtain
that

\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|O_{j}^{(1)\prime}\dot{v}_{t,j}^{(1)}-v_{t,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}\leq\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}(\eta_{N}).

\blacksquare

A.2.3
Proof of Statement (iii)

In this proof we derive the linear expansion of
\{\dot{u}_{i,j}^{(1)}\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}
for each
i\in I_{3}
. Recall that
\forall i\in I_{3}
,

\left\{\dot{u}_{i,j}^{(1)}\right\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}=\argmin\limits_{\left\{u_{i,j}\right\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\rho_{\tau}\left(Y_{it}-u_{i,0}^{\prime}\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}-\sum_{j\in[p]}u_{i,j}^{\prime}\dot{v}_{t,j}^{(1)}X_{j,it}\right).

Define
\dot{\varpi}_{it}=\left(\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)\prime},X_{1,it}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)\prime},\cdots,X_{p,it}\dot{v}_{t,p}^{(1)\prime}\right)^{\prime}
and

\displaystyle\dot{\mathbb{H}}_{i}\left(\{u_{i,j}\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left[\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq g_{it}(\{u_{i,j}\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}})\right\}\right]\dot{\varpi}_{it},

\displaystyle\dot{\mathcal{H}}_{i}\left(\{u_{i,j}\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left\{\left[\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq g_{it}(\{u_{i,j}\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}})\right\}\right]\dot{\varpi}_{it}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right\},

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{\left[\tau-\mathfrak{F}_{it}\left(g_{it}(\{u_{i,j}\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}})\right)\right]\dot{\varpi}_{it}\right\},

\displaystyle\dot{\mathbb{W}}_{i}\left(\{\dot{u}_{i,j}^{(1)}\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\right)

\displaystyle=\dot{\mathbb{H}}_{i}\left(\{\dot{u}_{i,j}^{(1)}\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\right)-\dot{\mathbb{H}}_{i}\left(\{O_{j}^{(1)}u_{i,j}^{0}\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\right)

\displaystyle-\left\{\dot{\mathcal{H}}_{i}\left(\{\dot{u}_{i,j}^{(1)}\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\right)-\dot{\mathcal{H}}_{i}\left(\{O_{j}^{(1)}u_{i,j}^{0}\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\right)\right\},

where
\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}
being the
\sigma
-field
generated by
\left\{\epsilon_{i^{*}t},e_{i^{*}t}\right\}_{i^{*}\in I_{1}\cup I_{2},t\in[T]}\bigcup\left\{e_{it}\right\}_{t\in[T]}\bigcup\left\{V_{j}^{0}\right\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\bigcup\left\{W_{j}^{0}\right\}_{j\in[p]}
, and

g_{it}(\{u_{i,j}\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}})=u_{i,0}^{\prime}\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}+\sum_{j\in[p]}u_{i,j}^{\prime}\dot{v}_{t,j}^{(1)}X_{j,it}-u_{i,0}^{0\prime}v_{t,0}^{0}-\sum_{j\in[p]}u_{i,j}^{0\prime}v_{t,j}^{0}X_{j,it}.

Then we have

\displaystyle\dot{\mathbb{H}}_{i}\left(\{\dot{u}_{i,j}^{(1)}\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\right)

\displaystyle=\dot{\mathbb{H}}_{i}\left(\{O_{j}^{(1)}u_{i,j}^{0}\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\right)+\dot{\mathcal{H}}_{i}\left(\{\dot{u}_{i,j}^{(1)}\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\right)-\dot{\mathcal{H}}_{i}\left(\{O_{j}^{(1)}u_{i,j}^{0}\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\right)

\displaystyle+\dot{\mathbb{W}}_{i}\left(\{\dot{u}_{i,j}^{(1)}\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\right).

(A.9)

By Assumptions
1
(v) and
2
and Theorem
3.2
(ii), we
have
\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left\|\dot{\varpi}_{it}\right\|_{2}\leq C_{11}\xi_{N}
a.s.. By the first order condition of the quantile
regression, we have

\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\dot{\mathbb{H}}_{i}\left(\{\dot{u}_{i,j}^{(1)}\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\right)\right\|_{2}=O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{T}\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left\|\dot{\varpi}_{it}\right\|_{2}\right)=O_{p}\left(\frac{\xi_{N}}{T}\right).

(A.10)

Next, we show that
\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\dot{\mathbb{W}}_{i}\left(\{\dot{u}_{i,j}^{(1)}\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\right)\right\|_{2}=o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-1/2}\right)
. Notice that

\displaystyle\dot{\mathbb{W}}_{i}\left(\{\dot{u}_{i,j}^{(1)}\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\dot{\varpi}_{it}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq g_{it}(\{O_{j}^{(1)}u_{i,j}^{0}\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}})\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq g_{it}(\{\dot{u}_{i,j}^{(1)}\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}})\right\}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\dot{\varpi}_{it}\left[\mathfrak{F}_{it}\left(g(\{O_{j}^{(1)}u_{i,j}^{0}\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}})\right)-\mathfrak{F}_{it}\left(g_{it}(\{\dot{u}_{i,j}^{(1)}\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}})\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\dot{\mathbb{W}}_{i}^{I}-\dot{\mathbb{W}}_{i}^{II}\left(\{\dot{u}_{i,j}^{(1)}\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\right),

(A.11)

where
\dot{\mathbb{W}}_{i}^{I}=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\dot{\mathbb{W}}_{it}^{I}
with

\displaystyle\dot{\mathbb{W}}_{it}^{I}=\dot{\varpi}_{it}\left\{\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq g_{it}(\{O_{j}^{(1)}u_{i,j}^{0}\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}})\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)-\left[\mathfrak{F}_{it}\left(g_{it}(\{O_{j}^{(1)}u_{i,j}^{0}\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}})\right)-\mathfrak{F}_{it}(0)\right]\right\}

and

\displaystyle\dot{\mathbb{W}}_{i}^{II}\left(\{\dot{u}_{i,j}^{(1)}\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\dot{\varpi}_{it}\biggl\{\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq g_{it}(\{\dot{u}_{i,j}^{(1)}\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}})\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)

\displaystyle-\left[\mathfrak{F}_{it}\left(g_{it}(\{\dot{u}_{i,j}^{(1)}\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}})\right)-\mathfrak{F}_{it}(0)\right]\biggr\}.

Noting that

g_{it}(\{O_{j}^{(1)}u_{i,j}^{0}\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}})=\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}-O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}\right)+\sum_{j\in[p]}\left(O_{j}^{(1)}u_{i,j}\right)^{\prime}\left(\dot{v}_{t,j}^{(1)}-O_{j}^{(1)}v_{t,j}^{0}\right)X_{j,it},

and
\max_{i\in I_{2},t\in[T]}\left|g_{it}(\{O_{j}^{(1)}u_{i,j}^{0}\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}})\right|=O_{p}(\xi_{N}\eta_{N})
, we have,
\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left\|\dot{\mathbb{W}}_{it}^{I}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}(\xi_{N})
,
\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbb{W}_{it}^{I}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)=0
, and for some positive constants
C_{11}
,

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left\|Var\left(\dot{\mathbb{W}}_{it}^{I}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left\|\mathbb{E}\left\{\dot{\varpi}_{it}\dot{\varpi}_{it}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq g(\{O_{j}^{(1)}u_{i,j}^{0}\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}})\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)^{2}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right\}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left\|\dot{\varpi}_{it}\right\|_{2}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{0\leq\left|\epsilon_{it}\right|\leq\left|g(\{O_{j}^{(1)}u_{i,j}^{0}\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}})\right|\right\}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)=O_{p}(\xi_{N}^{3}\eta_{N}),

and

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\sum_{s=t+1}^{T}\left\|Cov\left(\dot{\mathbb{W}}_{it}^{I},\dot{\mathbb{W}}_{is}^{I}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\sum_{s=t+1}^{T}\left[\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|\dot{\mathbb{W}}_{it}^{I}\right\|_{F}^{2+\vartheta}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)\right]^{\frac{1}{2+\vartheta}}\left[\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|\dot{\mathbb{W}}_{is}^{I}\right\|_{F}^{2+\vartheta}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)\right]^{\frac{1}{2+\vartheta}}\left[\alpha(t-s)\right]^{1-\frac{2}{2+\vartheta}}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left[\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|\dot{\mathbb{W}}_{it}^{I}\right\|_{F}^{2+\vartheta}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)\right]^{\frac{2}{2+\vartheta}}\max_{t\in[T]}\sum_{s=t+1}^{T}\left[\alpha(t-s)\right]^{1-\frac{2}{2+\vartheta}}\text{a.s.}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\xi_{N}^{\frac{6+2\vartheta}{2+\vartheta}}\eta_{N}^{\frac{2}{2+\vartheta}}\right),

for any
\vartheta>0
, where the first inequality holds by Davydov’s
inequality for conditional strong mixing processes, and the last equality
holds by the fact that
\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|\dot{\mathbb{W}}_{it}^{I}\right\|^{2+\vartheta}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)=O_{p}\left(\xi_{N}^{3+\vartheta}\eta_{N}\right)
following
the similar argument as in (
A.10
). Combining (
A.10
) and (
A.11
) yields

\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left\{\left\|Var\left(\dot{\mathbb{W}}_{it}^{I}\right)\right\|_{F}+2\sum_{s>t}\left\|Cov\left(\dot{\mathbb{W}}_{it}^{I},\dot{\mathbb{W}}_{is}^{I}\right)\right\|_{F}\right\}=O_{p}\left(\xi_{N}^{\frac{6+2\vartheta}{2+\vartheta}}\eta_{N}^{\frac{2}{2+\vartheta}}\right).

For any constant
C_{12}>0
, define

\displaystyle\mathscr{A}_{5,i}(C_{12})

\displaystyle=\left\{\max_{t\in[T]}\left\{\left\|Var\left(\dot{\mathbb{W}}_{it}^{I}\right)\right\|_{F}+2\sum_{s>t}\left\|Cov\left(\dot{\mathbb{W}}_{it}^{I},\dot{\mathbb{W}}_{is}^{I}\right)\right\|_{F}\right\}\leq C_{12}\xi_{N}^{\frac{6+2\vartheta}{2+\vartheta}}\eta_{N}^{\frac{2}{2+\vartheta}}\right\},

\displaystyle\mathscr{A}_{5}(C_{12})

\displaystyle=\left\{\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left\{\left\|Var\left(\dot{\mathbb{W}}_{it}^{I}\right)\right\|_{F}+2\sum_{s>t}\left\|Cov\left(\dot{\mathbb{W}}_{it}^{I},\dot{\mathbb{W}}_{is}^{I}\right)\right\|_{F}\right\}\leq C_{12}\xi_{N}^{\frac{6+2\vartheta}{2+\vartheta}}\eta_{N}^{\frac{2}{2+\vartheta}}\right\}.

For any
e>0
, we can find a sufficiently large constants
C_{12}
such that

\mathbb{P}(\mathscr{A}_{5}^{c}(C_{12}))\leq e
. Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left\{\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\dot{\mathbb{W}}_{it}^{I}\right\|_{2}>C_{13}\xi_{N}^{\frac{5+\vartheta}{2+\vartheta}}\left(\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N\wedge T}\right)^{\frac{1}{4+2\vartheta}}\sqrt{\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{T}}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left\{\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\dot{\mathbb{W}}_{it}^{I}\right\|_{2}>C_{13}\xi_{N}^{\frac{5+\vartheta}{2+\vartheta}}\left(\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N\wedge T}\right)^{\frac{1}{4+2\vartheta}}\sqrt{\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{T}},\mathscr{A}_{5}(C_{12})\right\}+e

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\in I_{3}}\mathbb{P}\left\{\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\dot{\mathbb{W}}_{it}^{I}\right\|_{2}>C_{13}\xi_{N}^{\frac{5+\vartheta}{2+\vartheta}}\left(\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N\wedge T}\right)^{\frac{1}{4+2\vartheta}}\sqrt{\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{T}},\mathscr{A}_{5}(C_{12})\right\}+e

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\in I_{3}}\mathbb{P}\left\{\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\dot{\mathbb{W}}_{it}^{I}\right\|_{2}>C_{13}\xi_{N}^{\frac{5+\vartheta}{2+\vartheta}}\left(\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N\wedge T}\right)^{\frac{1}{4+2\vartheta}}\sqrt{\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{T}},\mathscr{A}_{5,i}(C_{12})\right\}+e

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\in I_{3}}\mathbb{E}\mathbb{P}\left\{\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\dot{\mathbb{W}}_{it}^{I}\right\|_{2}>C_{13}\xi_{N}^{\frac{5+\vartheta}{2+\vartheta}}\left(\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N\wedge T}\right)^{\frac{1}{4+2\vartheta}}\sqrt{\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{T}}\Bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right\}1\{\mathscr{A}_{5,i}(C_{12})\}+e,

(A.12)

where the second inequality is by the union bound, the third inequality is
by
\mathscr{A}_{5}(C_{12})\subset\mathscr{A}_{5,i}(C_{12})
, and the last
equality is owing to the fact that
\mathscr{A}_{5,i}(C_{12})
is
\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}
measurable. Given
\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}
, the randomness in
\dot{\mathbb{W}}_{i}^{I}

only comes from
\{\epsilon_{it}\}_{t\in[T]}
, which are strong mixing given

\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}
. Therefore, on
\mathscr{A}_{5,i}(C_{12})
, Lemma
B.12
(ii) implies

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left\{\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\dot{\mathbb{W}}_{it}^{I}\right\|_{2}>C_{13}\xi_{N}^{\frac{5+\vartheta}{2+\vartheta}}\left(\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N\wedge T}\right)^{\frac{1}{4+2\vartheta}}\sqrt{\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{T}}\Bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right\}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\left\{\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\dot{\mathbb{W}}_{it}^{I}\right\|_{2}>C_{13}\xi_{N}^{\frac{5+\vartheta}{2+\vartheta}}\left(\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N\wedge T}\right)^{\frac{1}{4+2\vartheta}}\sqrt{T\log(N\vee T)}\Bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left\{-\frac{c_{12}C_{13}^{2}T\left(\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N\wedge T}\right)^{\frac{1}{2+\vartheta}}\xi_{N}^{\frac{10+2\vartheta}{2+\vartheta}}\log(N\vee T)}{C_{12}T\left(\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N\wedge T}\right)^{\frac{1}{2+\vartheta}}\xi_{N}^{\frac{10+2\vartheta}{2+\vartheta}}+C_{11}^{2}\xi_{N}^{2}+C_{11}C_{13}\sqrt{T\log(N\vee T)}\xi_{N}^{\frac{7+2\vartheta}{2+\vartheta}}\left(\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N\wedge T}\right)^{\frac{1}{4+2\vartheta}}\left(\log T\right)^{2}}\right\},

(A.13)

which further implies

\mathbb{P}\left\{\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\dot{\mathbb{W}}_{it}^{I}\right\|_{2}>C_{13}\xi_{N}^{\frac{5+\vartheta}{2+\vartheta}}\left(\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N\wedge T}\right)^{\frac{1}{4+2\vartheta}}\sqrt{\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{T}}\right\}=o(1)+e.

As
e
is arbitrary, by Assumption
1
(ix), we obtain that

\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\dot{\mathbb{W}}_{it}^{I}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}\left(C_{13}\xi_{N}^{\frac{5+\vartheta}{2+\vartheta}}\left(\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N\wedge T}\right)^{\frac{1}{4+2\vartheta}}\sqrt{\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{T}}\right)=o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-1/2}\right).

For
\dot{\mathbb{W}}_{i}^{II}\left(\{\dot{u}_{i,j}^{(1)}\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\right)
, we observe that

\displaystyle g_{it}(\{\dot{u}_{i,j}^{(1)}\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}})

\displaystyle=\dot{u}_{i,0}^{(1)\prime}\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}+\sum_{j\in[p]}\dot{u}_{i,j}^{(1)\prime}\dot{v}_{t,j}^{(1)}X_{j,it}-u_{i,0}^{0\prime}v_{t,0}^{0}-\sum_{j\in[p]}u_{i,j}^{0\prime}v_{t,j}^{0}X_{j,it}

\displaystyle=\left(\dot{u}_{i,0}^{(1)}-O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}+\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}-O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{j\in[p]}\left(\dot{u}_{i,j}^{(1)}-O_{j}^{(1)}u_{i,j}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\dot{v}_{t,j}^{(1)}X_{j,it}+\sum_{j\in[p]}\left(O_{j}^{(1)}u_{i,j}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\dot{v}_{t,j}^{(1)}-O_{j}^{(1)}v_{t,j}^{0}\right)X_{j,it}

\displaystyle=\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}^{\prime}\dot{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}+\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}^{\prime}\Psi_{it}^{0},

where
\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}=\left(\dot{\Delta}_{i,0}^{\prime},\cdots,\dot{\Delta}_{i,p}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}
with
\dot{\Delta}_{i,j}=O_{j}^{(1)\prime}\dot{u}_{i,j}^{(1)}-u_{i,j}^{0}
,
\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}=\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,0}^{\prime},\cdots,\dot{\Delta}_{t,p}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}
with
\dot{\Delta}_{t,j}=O_{j}^{(1)\prime}\dot{v}_{t,j}^{(1)}-v_{t,j}^{0}
,

\displaystyle\dot{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}=\left[\left(O_{0}^{(1)\prime}\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime},\left(O_{1}^{(1)\prime}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}X_{1,it}\right)^{\prime},\cdots,\left(O_{p}^{(1)\prime}\dot{v}_{t,p}^{(1)}X_{p,it}\right)^{\prime}\right]^{\prime},\hskip 9.24994pt\text{and}

\displaystyle\Psi_{it}^{0}=(u_{i,0}^{0\prime},u_{i,1}^{0\prime}X_{1,it},...,u_{i,p}^{0\prime}X_{p,it})^{\prime}.

Unlike the analysis for
\dot{\mathbb{W}}_{it}^{I}
, to handle the
dependence between
\epsilon_{it}
and
\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}
, for any
constant
C_{14}>0
, we first define an event set
\mathscr{A}_{6}(C_{14})=\left\{\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}\right\|_{2}\leq C_{14}\eta_{N}\right\}
with
\mathbb{P}(\mathscr{A}_{6}^{c}(C_{14}))\leq e
for any
e>0
by Theorem
3.2
(i),
then we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\dot{\mathbb{W}}_{i}^{II}\left(\{\dot{u}_{i,j}^{(1)}\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\right)\right\|_{2}>C_{13}\xi_{N}^{\frac{5+\vartheta}{2+\vartheta}}\left(\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N\wedge T}\right)^{\frac{1}{4+2\vartheta}}\sqrt{\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{T}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\dot{\mathbb{W}}_{i}^{II}\left(\{\dot{u}_{i,j}^{(1)}\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\right)\right\|_{2}>C_{13}\xi_{N}^{\frac{5+\vartheta}{2+\vartheta}}\left(\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N\wedge T}\right)^{\frac{1}{4+2\vartheta}}\sqrt{\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{T}},\mathscr{A}_{6}(C_{14})\right)+e

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\sup_{s\in\mathbb{S}}\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\overline{\mathbb{W}}_{i}^{II}\left(s\right)\right\|_{2}>C_{13}\xi_{N}^{\frac{5+\vartheta}{2+\vartheta}}\left(\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N\wedge T}\right)^{\frac{1}{4+2\vartheta}}\sqrt{\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{T}}\right)+e

(A.14)

with
\mathbb{S}=\left\{s\in\mathbb{R}^{\left(\sum_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}K_{j}\right)\times 1}:\left\|s\right\|_{2}\leq C_{14}\eta_{N}\right\}
and

\overline{\mathbb{W}}_{i}^{II}\left(s\right)=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\dot{\varpi}_{it}\left\{\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq s^{\prime}\dot{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}+\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}^{\prime}\Psi_{it}^{0}\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)-\left[\mathfrak{F}_{it}\left(s^{\prime}\dot{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}+\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}^{\prime}\Psi_{it}^{0}\right)-\mathfrak{F}_{it}(0)\right]\right\}.

Similarly as in (
B.37
), we sketch the proof. Divide
\mathbb{S}
into
\mathbb{S}_{m}
with center
s_{m}
for
m=1,\cdots,n_{\mathbb{S}}
if
s\in\mathbb{S}_{m}
, then
\left\|s-s_{m}\right\|_{2}<\frac{\varepsilon}{T}
and
n_{\mathbb{S}}\lesssim T^{\sum_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}K_{j}}
. Then,
\forall s\in\mathbb{S}_{m}
, we have

\left\|\overline{\mathbb{W}}_{i}^{II}\left(s\right)\right\|_{2}\leq\left\|\overline{\mathbb{W}}_{i}^{II}\left(s_{m}\right)\right\|_{2}+\left\|\overline{\mathbb{W}}_{i}^{II}\left(s\right)-\overline{\mathbb{W}}_{i}^{II}\left(s_{m}\right)\right\|_{2},

(A.15)

with

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{3},m\in[n_{\mathbb{S}}]}\sup_{s\in\mathbb{S}_{m}}\left\|\overline{\mathbb{W}}_{i}^{II}\left(s\right)-\overline{\mathbb{W}}_{i}^{II}\left(s_{m}\right)\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i\in I_{3},m\in[n_{\mathbb{S}}]}\sup_{s\in\mathbb{S}_{m}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\dot{\varpi}_{it}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq s^{\prime}\dot{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}+\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}^{\prime}\Psi_{it}^{0}\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq s_{m}^{\prime}\dot{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}+\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}^{\prime}\Psi_{it}^{0}\right\}\right)\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle+\max_{i\in I_{3},m\in[n_{\mathbb{S}}]}\sup_{s\in\mathbb{S}_{m}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\dot{\varpi}_{it}\left[\mathfrak{F}_{it}\left(s^{\prime}\dot{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}+\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}^{\prime}\Psi_{it}^{0}\right)-\mathfrak{F}_{it}\left(s_{m}^{\prime}\dot{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}+\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}^{\prime}\Psi_{it}^{0}\right)\right]\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i\in I_{3},m\in[n_{\mathbb{S}}]}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|\dot{\varpi}_{it}\right\|_{2}\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\left|\epsilon_{it}-\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}^{\prime}\Psi_{it}^{0}-s_{m}^{\prime}\dot{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}\right|\leq\frac{\varepsilon||\dot{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}||_{2}}{T}\right\}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)+\max_{i\in I_{2},m\in[n_{\mathbb{S}}]}\left|\overline{\mathbb{W}}_{i}^{III}(m)\right|

\displaystyle+\max_{i\in I_{3},m\in[n_{\mathbb{S}}]}\sup_{s\in\mathbb{S}_{m}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|\dot{\varpi}_{it}\right\|_{2}||\dot{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}||_{2}\left\|s-s_{m}\right\|_{2}

(A.16)

such that
\overline{\mathbb{W}}_{i}^{III}(m):=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\overline{\mathbb{W}}_{it}^{III}(m)
and

\displaystyle\overline{\mathbb{W}}_{it}^{III}(m)

\displaystyle:=\left\|\dot{\varpi}_{it}\right\|_{2}\biggl[\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\left|\epsilon_{it}-\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}^{\prime}\Psi_{it}^{0}-s_{m}^{\prime}\dot{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}\right|\leq\frac{\varepsilon||\dot{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}||_{2}}{T}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle-\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\left|\epsilon_{it}-\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}^{\prime}\Psi_{it}^{0}-s_{m}^{\prime}\dot{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}\right|\leq\frac{\varepsilon||\dot{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}||_{2}}{T}\right\}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)\biggr].

Like (
B.2
), we can show that
\max_{i\in I_{3},m\in[n_{\mathbb{S}}]}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|\dot{\varpi}_{it}\right\|_{2}\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\left|\epsilon_{it}-\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}^{\prime}\Psi_{it}^{0}-s_{m}^{\prime}\dot{\Phi}_{it}\right|\leq\frac{\varepsilon||\dot{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}||_{2}}{T}\right\}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)

=O_{p}(\frac{\varepsilon}{T})
because
\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}^{\prime}\Psi_{it}^{0}+s_{m}^{\prime}\dot{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}
and
||\dot{\Phi}_{it}||

are measurable in
\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}
and the
conditional density of
\epsilon_{it}
given
\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}
is bounded. Also we have

\max_{i\in I_{3},m\in[n_{\mathbb{S}}]}\sup_{s\in\mathbb{S}_{m}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|\dot{\varpi}_{it}\right\|_{2}||\dot{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}||_{2}\left\|s-s_{m}\right\|_{2}=\frac{\varepsilon}{T}\max_{i\in I_{3}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|\dot{\varpi}_{it}\right\|_{2}||\dot{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}||_{2}=O_{p}\left(\frac{\varepsilon}{T}\right).

In addition, we note that

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{3},m\in[n_{\mathbb{S}}],t\in[T]}\left\|\dot{\varpi}_{it}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}(\xi_{N}),\hskip 9.24994pt\max_{i\in I_{3},m\in[n_{\mathbb{S}}],t\in[T]}Var\left(\overline{\mathbb{W}}_{it}^{III}(m)\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)=O_{p}\left(\frac{\xi_{N}^{3}\varepsilon}{T}\right),

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{3},m\in[n_{\mathbb{S}}],t\in[T]}\sum_{s=t+1}^{T}\left|Cov\left(\overline{\mathbb{W}}_{it}^{III}(m),\overline{\mathbb{W}}_{is}^{III}(m)\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)\right|=O_{p}\left(\xi_{N}^{8/3}\left(\frac{\varepsilon}{T}\right)^{2/3}\right),

and for any positive constant
C_{15}
and
C_{16}
, define event set

\displaystyle\mathscr{A}_{8,N}

\displaystyle=\begin{pmatrix}&\operatornamewithlimits{\max}\limits_{i\in I_{3},m\in[n_{\mathbb{S}}],t\in[T]}\left\{Var\left(\overline{\mathbb{W}}_{it}^{III}(m)\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)+2\sum_{s=t+1}^{T}\left|Cov\left(\overline{\mathbb{W}}_{it}^{III}(m),\overline{\mathbb{W}}_{is}^{III}(m)\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)\right|\right\}\leq C_{15}\xi_{N}^{8/3}\left(\frac{\varepsilon}{T}\right)^{2/3}\\
&\text{and}\hskip 9.24994pt\operatornamewithlimits{\max}\limits_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left\|\dot{\varpi}_{it}\right\|_{2}\leq C_{16}\xi_{N}\end{pmatrix},

\displaystyle\mathscr{A}_{8,N,i}

\displaystyle=\begin{pmatrix}&\operatornamewithlimits{\max}\limits_{m\in[n_{\mathbb{S}}],t\in[T]}\left\{Var\left(\overline{\mathbb{W}}_{it}^{III}(m)\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)+2\sum_{s=t+1}^{T}\left|Cov\left(\overline{\mathbb{W}}_{it}^{III}(m),\overline{\mathbb{W}}_{is}^{III}(m)\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)\right|\right\}\leq C_{15}\xi_{N}^{8/3}\left(\frac{\varepsilon}{T}\right)^{2/3}\\
&\text{and}\hskip 9.24994pt\operatornamewithlimits{\max}\limits_{t\in[T]}\left\|\dot{\varpi}_{it}\right\|_{2}\leq C_{16}\xi_{N}\end{pmatrix},

with
\mathbb{P}\left(\mathscr{A}_{8,N}^{c}\right)\leq e
for any positive
e
. Then we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{i\in I_{3},m\in[n_{\mathbb{S}}]}\left|\overline{\mathbb{W}}_{i}^{III}(m)\right|>C_{13}\xi_{N}^{\frac{5+\vartheta}{2+\vartheta}}\left(\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N\wedge T}\right)^{\frac{1}{4+2\vartheta}}\sqrt{\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{T}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{i\in I_{3},m\in[n_{\mathbb{S}}]}\left|\overline{\mathbb{W}}_{i}^{III}(m)\right|>C_{13}\xi_{N}^{\frac{5+\vartheta}{2+\vartheta}}\left(\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N\wedge T}\right)^{\frac{1}{4+2\vartheta}}\sqrt{\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{T}},\mathscr{A}_{8,N}\right)+e

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\in[I_{3}],m\in[n_{\mathbb{S}}]}\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\overline{\mathbb{W}}_{i}^{III}(m)\right|>C_{13}\xi_{N}^{\frac{5+\vartheta}{2+\vartheta}}\left(\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N\wedge T}\right)^{\frac{1}{4+2\vartheta}}\sqrt{\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{T}},\mathscr{A}_{8,N,i}\right)+e

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\in[I_{3}],m\in[n_{\mathbb{S}}]}\mathbb{E}\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\overline{\mathbb{W}}_{i}^{III}(m)\right|>C_{13}\xi_{N}^{\frac{5+\vartheta}{2+\vartheta}}\left(\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N\wedge T}\right)^{\frac{1}{4+2\vartheta}}\sqrt{\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{T}}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)\mathbf{1}\{\mathscr{A}_{8,N,i}\}+e

\displaystyle=o(1)+e,

(A.17)

where the last line is by Bernstein’s inequality similar to (
A.2.3
). As
e
is arbitrary, we have

\max_{i\in I_{3},m\in[n_{\mathbb{S}}]}\left\|\overline{\mathbb{W}}_{i}^{III}(m)\right\|_{2}=O_{p}\left(\xi_{N}^{\frac{5+\vartheta}{2+\vartheta}}\left(\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N\wedge T}\right)^{\frac{1}{4+2\vartheta}}\sqrt{\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{T}}\right),

which implies that
\max_{i\in I_{3},m\in[n_{\mathbb{S}}]}\sup_{s\in\mathbb{S}_{m}}\left\|\overline{\mathbb{W}}_{i}^{II}\left(s\right)-\overline{\mathbb{W}}_{i}^{II}\left(s_{m}\right)\right\|_{2}=O_{p}\left(\xi_{N}^{\frac{5+\vartheta}{2+\vartheta}}\left(\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N\wedge T}\right)^{\frac{1}{4+2\vartheta}}\sqrt{\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{T}}\right)
. Following the same argument in (
A.2.3
), we can show that

\max_{i\in I_{3},m\in[n_{\mathbb{S}}]}\left\|\overline{\mathbb{W}}_{i}^{II}\left(s_{m}\right)\right\|_{2}=O_{p}\left(\xi_{N}^{\frac{5+\vartheta}{2+\vartheta}}\left(\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N\wedge T}\right)^{\frac{1}{4+2\vartheta}}\sqrt{\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{T}}\right),

which, combined with (
A.14
) and (
A.15
), implies that

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\dot{\mathbb{W}}_{i}^{II}\left(\{\dot{u}_{i,j}^{(1)}\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\right)\right\|_{2}=O_{p}\left(\xi_{N}^{\frac{5+\vartheta}{2+\vartheta}}\left(\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N\wedge T}\right)^{\frac{1}{4+2\vartheta}}\sqrt{\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{T}}\right)=o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-1/2}\right),\hskip 9.24994pt\text{and thus,}

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\dot{\mathbb{W}}_{i}\left(\{\dot{u}_{i,j}^{(1)}\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\right)\right\|_{2}=o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-1/2}\right).

(A.18)

Next, we observe that

\displaystyle\dot{\mathcal{H}}_{i}\left(\{O_{j}^{(1)}u_{i,j}^{0}\}_{j=0}^{p}\right)-\dot{\mathcal{H}}_{i}\left(\{\dot{u}_{i,j}^{(1)}\}_{j=0}^{p}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathfrak{f}_{it}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}^{\prime}\Psi_{it}^{0}\right)\dot{\varpi}_{it}\dot{\varpi}_{it}^{\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}+O_{p}\left(\max_{i\in i_{3}}\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathfrak{f}_{it}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}^{\prime}\Psi_{it}^{0}\right)\dot{\varpi}_{it}\dot{\varpi}_{it}^{\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}+o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-1/2}\right)\hskip 9.24994pt\text{uniformly over $i\in I_{3}$},

(A.19)

where the first equality holds by Taylor expansion and Lemma
B.17
and
the second equality is by Assumption
1
(ix). Combining (
A.2.3
), (
A.10
), (
A.2.3
) and (
A.2.3
), we have shown that

\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}=\left[\dot{D}_{i}^{I}\right]^{-1}\dot{D}_{i}^{II}+o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-1/2}\right)\hskip 9.24994pt\text{uniformly over $i\in I_{3}$},

(A.20)

where
\dot{D}_{i}^{I}:=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathfrak{f}_{it}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}^{\prime}\Psi_{it}^{0}\right)\dot{\varpi}_{it}\dot{\varpi}_{it}^{\prime}\hskip 9.24994pt
and
\hskip 9.24994pt\dot{D}_{i}^{II}:=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left[\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}^{\prime}\Psi_{it}^{0}\right\}\right]\dot{\varpi}_{it}.
Recall that

\varpi_{it}^{0}=\left(\left(O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}\right)^{\prime},\left(O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}X_{1,it},\cdots,\left(O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,p}^{0}\right)^{\prime}X_{p,it}\right)^{\prime}
,
D_{i}^{I}=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathfrak{f}_{it}(0)\varpi_{it}^{0}\varpi_{it}^{0\prime},\hskip 9.24994pt
and
\hskip 9.24994ptD_{i}^{II}=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left[\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right]\varpi_{it}^{0}.
Noting that

\displaystyle\left[\dot{D}_{i}^{I}\right]^{-1}\dot{D}_{i}^{II}

\displaystyle=\left[D_{i}^{I}\right]^{-1}D_{i}^{II}+\left[D_{i}^{I}\right]^{-1}\left(\dot{D}_{i}^{II}-D_{i}^{II}\right)+\left[\left(\dot{D}_{i}^{I}\right)^{-1}-\left(D_{i}^{I}\right)^{-1}\right]D_{i}^{II}

\displaystyle+\left[\left(\dot{D}_{i}^{I}\right)^{-1}-\left(D_{i}^{I}\right)^{-1}\right]\left(\dot{D}_{i}^{II}-D_{i}^{II}\right),

we have by (
A.20
) and Lemma
B.18
, uniformly

\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}=\left[D_{i}^{I}\right]^{-1}D_{i}^{II}+\left[D_{i}^{I}\right]^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}^{\prime}\Psi_{it}^{0}\right\}\right]\varpi_{it}^{0}+o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-1/2}\right)

uniformly over
i\in I_{3},
where we use the fact that
\sqrt{\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{T}}\xi_{N}\eta_{N}=o\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-1/2}\right)
by
Assumption
1
(ix).
\blacksquare

A.3
Proof of Theorem
3.3

In this section, we extend the distribution theory of the least squares
framework in
Chernozhukov et al., 2019
to the quantile regression
framework and obtain the uniform error bound. We assume the model has only
one regressor in this section for notation simplicity.

A.3.1
Proof of Statement (i)

For
\forall i\in I_{3}
, recall from (
2.7
) that

\left\{\hat{u}_{i,j}^{(3,1)}\right\}_{j\in[p]}=\argmin\limits_{\left\{u_{i,j}\right\}_{j\in[p]}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\rho_{\tau}\left(\tilde{Y}_{it}-u_{i,0}^{\prime}\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}-u_{i,1}^{\prime}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\hat{e}_{1,it}\right).

(A.21)

where
\tilde{Y}_{it}=Y_{it}-\hat{\mu}_{1,it}\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)\prime}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}
. Let
\hat{\Delta}_{i,u}=\begin{bmatrix}\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)}-O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0}\\
\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\end{bmatrix}
and
\dot{\omega}_{it}=\begin{bmatrix}\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\\
\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\hat{e}_{1,it}\end{bmatrix}.
With generic
(u_{i,0},u_{i,1},\mathrm{u}_{i,1})
, define

\displaystyle\hat{\mathbb{H}}_{i}\left(u_{i,0},u_{i,1},\mathrm{u}_{i,1}\right)=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left[\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\iota_{it}\left(u_{i,0},u_{i,1},\mathrm{u}_{i,1}\right),\right\}\right]\dot{\omega}_{it}\text{ and}

\displaystyle\iota_{it}\left(u_{i,0},u_{i,1},\mathrm{u}_{i,1}\right)=u_{i,0}^{\prime}\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}-u_{i,0}^{0\prime}v_{t,0}^{0}+\hat{\mu}_{1,it}\mathrm{u}_{i,1}^{\prime}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-\mu_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}+\hat{e}_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{\prime}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}.

(A.22)

We can see that

\hat{\mathbb{H}}_{i}\left(u_{i,0},u_{i,1},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left[\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\tilde{Y}_{it}-u_{i,0}^{\prime}\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}-u_{i,1}^{\prime}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\hat{e}_{1,it}\leq 0\right\}\right]\dot{\omega}_{it}

is the first order subgradient of (
A.21
). In addition, we define

\displaystyle\hat{\mathcal{H}}_{i}\left(u_{i,0},u_{i,1},\mathrm{u}_{i,1}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left\{\left[\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\left(\iota_{it}\left(u_{i,0},u_{i,1},\mathrm{u}_{i,1}\right)\right)\right\}\right]\dot{\omega}_{it}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right\}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left[\tau-F_{it}\left(\iota_{it}\left(u_{i,0},u_{i,1},\mathrm{u}_{i,1}\right)\right)\right]\dot{\omega}_{it},

and

\displaystyle\hat{\mathbb{W}}_{i}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)

\displaystyle=\hat{\mathbb{H}}_{i}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)-\hat{\mathbb{H}}_{i}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)

\displaystyle-\left\{\hat{\mathcal{H}}_{i}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)-\hat{\mathcal{H}}_{i}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right\},

where
\mathscr{D}_{e}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}
is the
\sigma
-field generated by

\left\{\epsilon_{it}\right\}_{i\in I_{1}\cup I_{2},t\in[T]}\bigcup\left\{e_{it}\right\}_{i\in[N],t\in[T]}\bigcup\left\{V_{j}^{0}\right\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\bigcup\left\{W_{j}^{0}\right\}_{j\in[p]}
. Then it is clear that

\displaystyle\hat{\mathbb{H}}_{i}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)

\displaystyle=\hat{\mathbb{H}}_{i}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right))+\hat{\mathcal{H}}_{i}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)-\hat{\mathcal{H}}_{i}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)

\displaystyle+\hat{\mathbb{W}}_{i}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right).

(A.23)

For specific
u_{i,0}
and
u_{i,1}
, let
u_{i}=\left(u_{i,0}^{\prime},u_{i,1}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}
. Following similar arguments as
used in the proof of Lemma
B.17
, the second order partial derivative
of the function
\hat{\mathcal{H}}_{i}(\cdot)
with respect to
u_{i}
at
the true value can be shown to be bounded in probability. By Taylor
expansion, it yields

\hat{\mathcal{H}}_{i}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)-\hat{\mathcal{H}}_{i}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)=\frac{\partial\hat{\mathcal{H}}_{i}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)}{\partial u_{i}^{\prime}}\hat{\Delta}_{i,u}+R_{i},

(A.24)

where
\max_{i\in I_{3}}|R_{i}|\lesssim\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\hat{\Delta}_{i,u}\right\|_{2}^{2}
and

\frac{\partial\hat{\mathcal{H}}_{i}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)}{\partial u_{i}^{\prime}}=-\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}\left[\iota_{it}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right]\begin{bmatrix}\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)\prime}&\hat{e}_{1,it}\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)\prime}\\
\hat{e}_{1,it}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)\prime}&\hat{e}_{1,it}^{2}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)\prime}\end{bmatrix}=-\dot{D}_{i}^{F}.

Combing (
A.3.1
) and (
A.24
), we have

\displaystyle\hat{\Delta}_{i,u}

\displaystyle=\left(\dot{D}_{i}^{F}\right)^{-1}\Bigg\{\hat{\mathbb{H}}_{i}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)+\hat{\mathbb{W}}_{i}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)-\hat{\mathbb{H}}_{i}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)+R_{i}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle=\left(\dot{D}_{i}^{F}\right)^{-1}\left\{\hat{\mathbb{H}}_{i}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)+\hat{\mathbb{W}}_{i}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)+o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-1/2}\right)\right\},

(A.25)

uniformly over
i\in I_{3}
, where the second line is due to the fact that

\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\hat{\mathbb{H}}_{i}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right\|_{\max}=O_{p}\left(\frac{\xi_{N}}{T}\right)\hskip 9.24994pt\text{and}\hskip 9.24994pt\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\hat{\Delta}_{i,u}\right\|_{2}^{2}=O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right)=o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-1/2}\right),

following similar arguments as in (
A.2.3
) and the proof of Theorem
3.2
.

Next, we analyze the term
\hat{\mathbb{H}}_{i}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)
in (
A.24
), which can be written as

\displaystyle\hat{\mathbb{H}}_{i}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\dot{\omega}_{it}\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\iota_{it}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right\}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\dot{\omega}_{it}\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\dot{\omega}_{it}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\iota_{it}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right\}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\dot{\omega}_{it}\left[\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\dot{\omega}_{it}\left\{F_{it}(0)-F_{it}\left[\iota_{it}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right]\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\dot{\omega}_{it}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-F_{it}(0)\right]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\dot{\omega}_{it}\left\{\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\iota_{it}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right\}-F_{it}\left[\iota_{it}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right]\right\}.

(A.26)

For the second term after the last equality, we notice that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\dot{\omega}_{it}\left\{F_{it}(0)-F_{it}\left[\iota_{it}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right]\right\}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\dot{\omega}_{it}f_{it}\left(\tilde{\iota}_{it}\right)\begin{bmatrix}(\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}-O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0})^{\prime}&(\hat{e}_{1,it}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-e_{1,it}O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0})^{\prime}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0}\\
O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\dot{\omega}_{it}f_{it}\left(\tilde{\iota}_{it}\right)\left(\hat{\mu}_{1,it}\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)\prime}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-\mu_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)

\displaystyle:=\dot{D}_{i}^{J}\begin{bmatrix}O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0}\\
O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\end{bmatrix}+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\dot{\omega}_{it}f_{it}\left(\tilde{\iota}_{it}\right)\left(\mu_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}-\hat{\mu}_{1,it}\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)\prime}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\right),

(A.27)

where
\left|\tilde{\iota}_{it}\right|
lies between
0
and
\left|\iota_{it}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right|
and

\dot{D}_{i}^{J}=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(\tilde{\iota}_{it})\begin{bmatrix}\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}-\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime}&\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\left(e_{1,it}O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}-\hat{e}_{it}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime}\\
\hat{e}_{1,it}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}-\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime}&\hat{e}_{1,it}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\left(e_{1,it}O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}-\hat{e}_{1,it}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime}\end{bmatrix}.

The first equality above is due to the mean-value theorem and the definition
for
\iota_{it}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)
in (
A.22
). Inserting (
A.3.1
) into (
A.3.1
), we obtain that

\displaystyle\hat{\mathbb{H}}_{i}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)

\displaystyle=\dot{D}_{i}^{J}\begin{bmatrix}O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0}\\
O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\end{bmatrix}+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\dot{\omega}_{it}\left[\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\dot{\omega}_{it}f_{it}\left(\tilde{\iota}_{it}\right)\left(\mu_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}-\hat{\mu}_{1,it}\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)\prime}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\dot{\omega}_{it}\Bigg\{\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\iota_{it}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right\}\right]

\displaystyle-\left(F_{it}(0)-F_{it}\left[\iota_{it}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right]\right)\Bigg\}

\displaystyle:=\dot{D}_{i}^{J}\begin{bmatrix}O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0}\\
O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\end{bmatrix}+\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{1,i}+\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{2,i}+\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{3,i}.

(A.28)

Combining (
A.3.1
) and (
A.3.1
), we obtain that

\hat{\Delta}_{i,u}=\left(\dot{D}_{i}^{F}\right)^{-1}\left\{\dot{D}_{i}^{J}\begin{bmatrix}O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0}\\
O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\end{bmatrix}+\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{1,i}+\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{2,i}+\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{3,i}+\hat{\mathbb{W}}_{i}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)+o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-1/2}\right)\right\},

(A.29)

where the
o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-1/2}\right)
term holds
uniformly over
i\in I_{3}
. To prove Theorem
3.3
(i), we analyze each
term in (
A.29
) step by step.

Step 1: Uniform Convergence for
\dot{D}_{i}^{F}
and
\dot{D}_{i}^{J}
.

Define

\displaystyle D_{i}^{F}=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)\begin{bmatrix}O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}v_{t,0}^{0\prime}O_{0}^{(1)\prime}&0\\
0&e_{1,it}^{2}O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}O_{1}^{(1)\prime}\end{bmatrix},

\displaystyle D_{i}^{J}=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)\begin{bmatrix}\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}-\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime}&0\\
0&e_{1,it}^{2}O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}\left(O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}-\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime}\end{bmatrix}.

Lemmas
B.22
and
B.23
show that

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{3}}||\dot{D}_{i}^{F}-D_{i}^{F}||_{F}=O_{p}(\eta_{N})\hskip 9.24994pt\text{and}\hskip 9.24994pt\max_{i\in I_{3}}||\dot{D}_{i}^{J}-D_{i}^{J}||_{F}=\left\|\begin{bmatrix}O_{p}(\eta_{N}^{2})&O_{p}(\eta_{N})\\
O_{p}(\eta_{N}^{2})&O_{p}(\eta_{N}^{2})\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F},

with
\eta_{N}=\frac{\sqrt{\log\left(N\vee T\right)}\xi_{N}^{2}}{\sqrt{N\wedge T}}
.

Step 2: Uniform Convergence for
\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{1,i}
.

Let
\omega_{it}^{0}=\begin{bmatrix}O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}\\
O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}e_{1,it}\end{bmatrix}
. Then we can see that

\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{1,i}=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\omega_{it}^{0}\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\dot{\omega}_{it}-\omega_{it}^{0}\right)\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right).

Noting that

\displaystyle\dot{\omega}_{it}-\omega_{it}^{0}

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}-O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}\\
\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\hat{e}_{1,it}-O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}e_{1,it}^{0}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}-O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}\\
\left(\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)\left(\hat{e}_{1,it}-e_{1,it}\right)+e_{1,it}\left(\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)+O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{(1)}\left(\hat{e}_{1,it}-e_{1,it}\right)\end{bmatrix},

(A.30)

we have

\max_{i\in I_{3}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|\dot{\omega}_{it}-\omega_{it}^{0}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}(\eta_{N}).

(A.31)

In addition,
\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\dot{\omega}_{it}-\omega_{it}^{0}\right)\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)\right\|_{2}=O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right)
by Lemma
B.24
. It
follows that

\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{1,i}=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\omega_{it}^{0}\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)+O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right),

(A.32)

uniformly over
i\in I_{3}
.

Step 3: Uniform Convergence for
\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{2,i}
.

Note that

\displaystyle\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{2,i}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\dot{\omega}_{it}f_{it}\left(\tilde{\iota}_{it}\right)\left(\mu_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}-\hat{\mu}_{1,it}\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)\prime}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\omega_{it}^{0}f_{it}(0)\left(\mu_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}-\hat{\mu}_{1,it}\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)\prime}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\dot{\omega}_{it}-\omega_{it}^{0}\right)\left[f_{it}\left(\tilde{\iota}_{it}\right)-f_{it}(0)\right]\left(\mu_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}-\hat{\mu}_{1,it}\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)\prime}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\dot{\omega}_{it}-\omega_{it}^{0}\right)f_{it}(0)\left(\mu_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}-\hat{\mu}_{1,it}\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)\prime}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\omega_{it}^{0}\left[f_{it}\left(\tilde{\iota}_{it}\right)-f_{it}(0)\right]\left(\mu_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}-\hat{\mu}_{1,it}\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)\prime}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\right)

(A.33)

where

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\dot{\omega}_{it}-\omega_{it}^{0}\right)\left[f_{it}\left(\tilde{\iota}_{it}\right)-f_{it}(0)\right]\left(\mu_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}-\hat{\mu}_{1,it}\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)\prime}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\right)\right\|_{2}=O_{p}(\eta_{N}^{3}),

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\dot{\omega}_{it}-\omega_{it}^{0}\right)f_{it}(0)\left(\mu_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}-\hat{\mu}_{1,it}\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)\prime}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\right)\right\|_{2}=O_{p}(\eta_{N}^{2}),

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\omega_{it}^{0}\left[f_{it}\left(\tilde{\iota}_{it}\right)-f_{it}(0)\right]\left(\mu_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}-\hat{\mu}_{1,it}\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)\prime}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\right)\right\|_{2}=O_{p}(\eta_{N}^{2}).

To see why these three equalities hold, we focus on the third one. By
Cauchy’s inequality, Theorem
3.2
, and Lemma
B.21
, we have

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\omega_{it}^{0}\left[f_{it}\left(\tilde{\iota}_{it}\right)-f_{it}(0)\right]\left(\mu_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}-\hat{\mu}_{1,it}\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)\prime}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\right)\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\max_{i\in I_{3}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|\omega_{it}^{0}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left|\tilde{\iota}_{it}\right|^{2}}\sqrt{\max_{i\in I_{3}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left|\mu_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}-\hat{\mu}_{1,it}\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)\prime}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\right|^{2}}=O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right).

For the first term on the right hand side (RHS) of the second equality of (
A.3.1
), we have by Lemma
B.25

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}f_{it}(0)\left(\mu_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}-\hat{\mu}_{1,it}\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)\prime}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\right)\right\|_{2}=O_{p}(\eta_{N}),\text{ }

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}e_{1,it}O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}f_{it}(0)\left(\mu_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}-\hat{\mu}_{1,it}\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)\prime}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\right)\right\|_{2}=O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right),

and thus
\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{2,i}\right\|_{2}=\left\|\begin{bmatrix}O_{p}(\eta_{N})\\
O_{p}(\eta_{N}^{2})\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{2}
.

Step 4: Uniform Convergence for
\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{3,i}
.
