Note that

\displaystyle\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{3,i}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\dot{\omega}_{it}\Bigg\{\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\iota_{it}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right\}\right]

\displaystyle-\left(F_{it}(0)-F_{it}\left[\iota_{it}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right]\right)\Bigg\}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\omega_{it}^{0}\Bigg\{\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\iota_{it}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right\}\right]

\displaystyle-\left(F_{it}(0)-F_{it}\left[\iota_{it}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right]\right)\Bigg\}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\dot{\omega}_{it}-\omega_{it}^{0}\right)\Bigg\{\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\iota_{it}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right\}\right]

\displaystyle-\left(F_{it}(0)-F_{it}\left[\iota_{it}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right]\right)\Bigg\}.

(A.34)

By (
B.3
), we can show that

\displaystyle\iota_{it}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\leq R_{\iota,it}^{1}\left(\left|\mu_{1,it}\right|+\left|e_{1,it}\right|\right)+R_{\iota,it}^{2}\hskip 9.24994pt\text{with}

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left|R_{\iota,it}^{1}\right|=O_{p}(\eta_{N}),\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left|R_{\iota,it}^{2}\right|=O_{p}(\eta_{N}).

(A.35)

For the second term on the RHS of the second equality in (
A.3.1
), we
notice that

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{3}}\bigg\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\dot{\omega}_{it}-\omega_{it}^{0}\right)\Bigg\{\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\iota_{it}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right\}\right]

\displaystyle-\left(F_{it}(0)-F_{it}\left[\iota_{it}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right]\right)\Bigg\}\Bigg\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i\in I_{3}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\dot{\omega}_{it}-\omega_{it}^{0}\right\|_{2}\mathbf{1}\left\{0\leq\left|\epsilon_{it}\right|\leq\left|\iota_{it}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right|\right\}

\displaystyle+\max_{i\in I_{3}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\dot{\omega}_{it}-\omega_{it}^{0}\right\|_{2}\left|F_{it}\left[\iota_{it}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right]-F_{it}(0)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i\in I_{3}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\dot{\omega}_{it}-\omega_{it}^{0}\right\|_{2}\mathbf{1}\left\{0\leq\left|\epsilon_{it}\right|\leq\left|\iota_{it}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right|\right\}+O_{p}(\eta_{N}^{2}),

(A.36)

where the last line is by (
A.3.1
), (
A.3.1
) and Assumption
1
(iv).

Define the event
\mathscr{A}_{9}(M):=\left\{\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left|\iota_{it}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right|\leq M\eta_{N}\left(\left|\mu_{1,it}\right|+\left|e_{1,it}\right|+1\right)\right\}

with
\mathbb{P}\left\{\mathscr{A}_{9}^{c}(M)\right\}\leq e
for any
e>0
.
Then for a large enough constant
C_{17}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{i\in I_{3}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\dot{\omega}_{it}-\omega_{it}^{0}\right\|_{2}\mathbf{1}\left\{0\leq\left|\epsilon_{it}\right|\leq\left|\iota_{it}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right|\right\}>C_{17}\eta_{N}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{i\in I_{3}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\dot{\omega}_{it}-\omega_{it}^{0}\right\|_{2}\mathbf{1}\left\{0\leq\left|\epsilon_{it}\right|\leq\left|\iota_{it}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right|\right\}>C_{17}\eta_{N}^{2}\bigg|\mathscr{A}_{9}(M)\right)+e

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{i\in I_{3}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\dot{\omega}_{it}-\omega_{it}^{0}\right\|_{2}\mathbf{1}\left\{0\leq\left|\epsilon_{it}\right|\leq M\eta_{N}\left(\left|\mu_{1,it}\right|+\left|e_{1,it}\right|+1\right)\right\}>C_{17}\eta_{N}^{2}\right)+e

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\Bigg\{\mathbb{P}\bigg(\max_{i\in I_{3}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\dot{\omega}_{it}-\omega_{it}^{0}\right\|_{2}\bigg[\mathbf{1}_{it}-\bar{\mathbf{1}}_{it}\bigg]>\frac{C_{17}\eta_{N}^{2}}{2}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\bigg)\Bigg\}

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left\{\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{i\in I_{3}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left\{\left\|\dot{\omega}_{it}-\omega_{it}^{0}\right\|_{2}\mathbf{1}\left\{0\leq\left|\epsilon_{it}\right|\leq M\eta_{N}\left(\left|\mu_{1,it}\right|+\left|e_{1,it}\right|+1\right)\right\}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right\}>\frac{C_{17}\eta_{N}^{2}}{2}\right)\right\}+e

\displaystyle=o(1)+e

(A.37)

where
\mathbf{1}_{it}-\bar{\mathbf{1}}_{it}:=\mathbf{1}\left\{0\leq\left|\epsilon_{it}\right|\leq M\eta_{N}\left(\left|\mu_{1,it}\right|+\left|e_{1,it}\right|+1\right)\right\}-\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{0\leq\left|\epsilon_{it}\right|\leq M\eta_{N}\left(\left|\mu_{1,it}\right|+\left|e_{1,it}\right|+1\right)\right\}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)
, the last line holds by the fact that
\max_{i\in I_{3}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left\{\left\|\dot{\omega}_{it}-\omega_{it}^{0}\right\|_{2}\mathbf{1}\left\{0\leq\left|\epsilon_{it}\right|\leq M\eta_{N}\left(\left|\mu_{1,it}\right|+\left|e_{1,it}\right|+1\right)\right\}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right\}

=O_{p}(\eta_{N}^{2})
and

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{i\in I_{3}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\dot{\omega}_{it}-\omega_{it}^{0}\right\|_{2}\left[\mathbf{1}_{it}-\bar{\mathbf{1}}_{it}\right]>C_{17}\eta_{N}^{2}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\exp\left(-\frac{T^{2}\eta_{N}^{4}}{T\xi_{N}^{2}\eta_{N}^{2}+T\eta_{N}^{3}\xi_{N}\log T\log\log T}\right)=o(1)

by Bernstein’s inequality in Lemma
B.12
(i). Combining (
A.3.1
) and (
A.3.1
), we have shown the second term on the RHS of
the second equality in (
A.3.1
) is
O_{p}(\eta_{N}^{2})
uniformly
over
i\in I_{3}
. This result, in conjunction with Lemma
B.25
, (
A.3.1
) and Assumption
1
(ix), implies that
\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{3,i}\right\|=o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right).

Step 5: Uniform Convergence for
\hat{\mathbb{W}}_{i}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)
.

Note that

\displaystyle\hat{\mathbb{W}}_{i}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\dot{\omega}_{it}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\iota_{it}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\iota_{it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right\}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\dot{\omega}_{it}\left(F_{it}\left[\iota_{it}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right]-F_{it}\left[\iota_{it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right]\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\dot{\omega}_{it}-\omega_{it}^{0}\right)\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\iota_{it}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\iota_{it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right\}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\dot{\omega}_{it}-\omega_{it}^{0}\right)\left(F_{it}\left[\iota_{it}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right]-F_{it}\left[\iota_{it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right]\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\omega_{it}^{0}\Bigg\{\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\iota_{it}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\iota_{it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right\}\right)

\displaystyle-\left(F_{it}\left[\iota_{it}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right]-F_{it}\left[\iota_{it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right]\right)\Bigg\}

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\dot{\omega}_{it}-\omega_{it}^{0}\right)\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\iota_{it}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\iota_{it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right\}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\dot{\omega}_{it}-\omega_{it}^{0}\right)\left(F_{it}\left[\iota_{it}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right]-F_{it}\left[\iota_{it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right]\right)

\displaystyle+\hat{\mathbb{W}}_{i}^{I}-\hat{\mathbb{W}}_{i}^{II},

where we define

\displaystyle\hat{\mathbb{W}}_{i}^{I}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\omega_{it}^{0}\Bigg\{\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\iota_{it}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}

\displaystyle-\bigg\{F_{it}\left[\iota_{it}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right]-F_{it}(0)\bigg\}\Bigg\}\hskip 9.24994pt\text{and}

\displaystyle\hat{\mathbb{W}}_{i}^{II}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\omega_{it}^{0}\Bigg\{\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\iota_{it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle-\bigg\{F_{it}(0)-F_{it}\left[\iota_{it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)},\right)\right]\bigg\}\Bigg\}.

We first observe that

\displaystyle\iota_{it}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)-\iota_{it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)}-O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}+\left(\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\hat{e}_{1,it}

\displaystyle=\left(O_{0}^{(1)\prime}\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)}-u_{i,0}^{0}\right)^{\prime}v_{t,0}^{0}+\left(O_{1}^{(1)\prime}\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)}-u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}v_{t,1}^{(1)}e_{1,it}+O_{p}(\eta_{N}^{2})

\displaystyle=R_{\iota,it}^{3}e_{1,it}+R_{\iota,it}^{4}

(A.38)

such that
\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left|R_{\iota,it}^{3}\right|=O_{p}(\eta_{N})
and
\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left|R_{\iota,it}^{4}\right|=O_{p}(\eta_{N})
. As in Step 4, we can show that

\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\dot{\omega}_{it}-\omega_{it}^{0}\right)\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\iota_{it}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\iota_{it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right\}\right)\right\|=O_{p}(\eta_{N}^{2})

and

\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\dot{\omega}_{it}-\omega_{it}^{0}\right)\left(F_{it}\left[\iota_{it}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right]-F_{it}\left[\iota_{it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right]\right)\right\|=O_{p}(\eta_{N}^{2}).

Then by Lemma
B.26
, Lemma
B.27
and Assumption
1
(ix),
we obtain that

\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\hat{\mathbb{W}}_{i}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right\|_{2}=o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right).

Step 6: Distribution Theory for
\hat{\Delta}_{i,u}

Combining the above results, we have that uniformly over
i\in I_{3}
,

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)}\\
\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0}\\
O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\end{bmatrix}+\left(\dot{D}_{i}^{F}\right)^{-1}\left\{\dot{D}_{i}^{J}\begin{bmatrix}O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0}\\
O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\end{bmatrix}+\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{1,i}+\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{2,i}+\mathbb{I}_{3,i}+\hat{\mathbb{W}}_{i}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)+o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle=\left[I_{K_{0}+K_{1}}+\left(D_{i}^{F}\right)^{-1}D_{i}^{J}\right]\begin{bmatrix}O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0}\\
O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\end{bmatrix}+\left[\left(\dot{D}_{i}^{F}\right)^{-1}\dot{D}_{i}^{J}-\left(D_{i}^{F}\right)^{-1}D_{i}^{J}\right]\begin{bmatrix}O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0}\\
O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle+\left(D_{i}^{F}\right)^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\omega_{it}^{0}\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)+\left[\left(\dot{D}_{i}^{F}\right)^{-1}-\left(D_{i}^{F}\right)^{-1}\right]\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\omega_{it}^{0}\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)

\displaystyle+\left(D_{i}^{F}\right)^{-1}\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{2,i}+o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right).

(A.39)

Owing to the fact that

\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\omega_{it}^{0}\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)\right\|_{2}=O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log\left(N\vee T\right)}{T}}\right)

(A.40)

by similar arguments as (
B.3
) using Bernstein’s inequality in Lemma
B.12
(ii) and Lemma
B.22
, we notice that

\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\left[\left(\dot{D}_{i}^{F}\right)^{-1}-\left(D_{i}^{F}\right)^{-1}\right]\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\omega_{it}^{0}\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)\right\|_{2}=o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right).

Next, we define

\displaystyle D^{F}=O^{(1)}\begin{bmatrix}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[f_{it}(0)\bigg|\mathscr{D}\right]v_{t,0}^{0}v_{t,0}^{0\prime}&0\\
0&\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[e_{1,it}^{2}f_{it}(0)\bigg|\mathscr{D}\right]v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}\end{bmatrix}O^{(1)\prime},

\displaystyle D^{J}=\begin{bmatrix}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[f_{it}(0)\bigg|\mathscr{D}\right]\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}-\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime}&0\\
0&O_{1}^{(1)}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[e_{1,it}^{2}f_{it}(0)\bigg|\mathscr{D}\right]v_{t,1}^{0}\left(O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}-\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime}\end{bmatrix},

where
O^{(1)}=diag\left(O_{0}^{(1)},O_{1}^{(1)}\right).
Here
D^{F}
and
D^{J}
do not depend on
i
owing to stationary assumption of sequence
\left\{f_{it},f_{it}(0)e_{j,it}\right\}_{j\in[p]}
conditional on all
factors in Assumption
9
(iii). By Bernstein’s inequality
conditional on all factors similarly as in (
B.3
), we can show that
\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|D_{i}^{F}-D^{F}\right\|_{F}=O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{T}}\xi_{N}\right)
. Analogously, by Bernstein’s
inequality conditional on
\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}
, we can show that
\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|D_{i}^{J}-D_{j}\right\|_{F}=O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{T}}\eta_{N}\xi_{N}\right)
. Then it follows that

\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\left(D_{i}^{F}\right)^{-1}D_{i}^{J}-\left(D^{F}\right)^{-1}D^{J}\right\|_{F}=O_{p}(\eta_{N}^{2}).

In addition, uniformly over
i\in I_{3}
,

\displaystyle\left(\dot{D}_{i}^{F}\right)^{-1}\dot{D}_{i}^{J}-\left(D_{i}^{F}\right)^{-1}D_{i}^{J}

\displaystyle=\left[\left(\dot{D}_{i}^{F}\right)^{-1}-\left(D_{i}^{F}\right)^{-1}\right]\left[\dot{D}_{i}^{J}-D_{i}^{J}\right]+D_{i}^{J}\left[\left(\dot{D}_{i}^{F}\right)^{-1}-\left(D_{i}^{F}\right)^{-1}\right]+\left(D_{i}^{F}\right)^{-1}\left[\dot{D}_{i}^{J}-D_{i}^{J}\right]

\displaystyle=\left(D_{i}^{F}\right)^{-1}\left[\dot{D}_{i}^{J}-D_{i}^{J}\right]+O_{p}(\eta_{N}^{2})

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right)&O_{p}(\eta_{N})\\
O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right)&O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right)\end{bmatrix},

where the upper right block is dominated by
\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}O_{1}^{(1)\prime}\left(e_{1,it}-\hat{e}_{1,it}\right)
in the analysis of
J_{1,i}^{2}
in (
B.3
).

Let
I_{K_{0}+K_{1}}+\left(D^{F}\right)^{-1}D^{J}=O_{u,1}^{(1)}:=\begin{pmatrix}\bar{O}_{u,0}&0\\
0&\bar{O}_{u,1}\end{pmatrix}
,
O_{u,0}^{(1)}=\bar{O}_{u,0}O_{0}^{(1)}
, and
O_{u,1}^{(1)}=\bar{O}_{u,1}O_{1}^{(1)}
. Combining the above arguments, we obtain that

\displaystyle\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)}-O_{u,1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}

\displaystyle=O_{1}^{(1)}\hat{V}_{u_{1}}^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}e_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0}\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)+\mathcal{R}_{i,u}^{1},

(A.41)

\displaystyle\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)}-O_{u,0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0}

\displaystyle=O_{0}^{(1)}\hat{V}_{u_{0},i}^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}v_{t,0}^{0}\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)

\displaystyle+O_{0}^{(1)}\hat{V}_{u_{0},i}^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)v_{t,0}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}u_{i,1}^{0}\left(e_{1,it}-\hat{e}_{1,it}\right)

\displaystyle+O_{0}^{(1)}\hat{V}_{u_{0},i}^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)v_{t,0}^{0}\left(\mu_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}-\hat{\mu}_{1,it}\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)\prime}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\right)+\mathcal{R}_{i,u}^{0}

(A.42)

such that
\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left|\mathcal{R}_{i,u}^{0}\right|=o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)
,
\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left|\mathcal{R}_{i,u}^{1}\right|=o_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right)
,
\hat{V}_{u_{0}}=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[f_{it}(0)\big|\mathscr{D}\right]v_{t,0}^{0}v_{t,0}^{0\prime}
and
\hat{V}_{u_{1}}=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[f_{it}(0)e_{1,it}^{2}\big|\mathscr{D}\right]v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}
. From (
A.41
),
owing to the fact that
\hat{V}_{u_{1}}
is bounded a.s. and
\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}e_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0}\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)\right\|_{2}=O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{T}}\right)
by
Bernstein’s inequality in Lemma
B.12
(ii), we obtain that

\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)}-O_{u,1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{T}}\right)\hskip 9.24994pt\text{and }\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)}-O_{u,0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}(\eta_{N}).

(A.43)

Last, noting that
O_{1}^{(1)}
is a rotation matrix and the normal
distribution is invariant to rotation, for each
i\in I_{3},
we have

\sqrt{T}\left(\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)}-O_{u,1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)\rightsquigarrow\mathcal{N}(0,\Sigma_{u_{1},i}),

where
\Sigma_{u_{1},i}=O_{1}^{(1)}V_{u_{1}}^{-1}\Omega_{u_{1}}V_{u_{1}}^{-1}O_{1}^{(1)\prime}
,
V_{u_{1},i}=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left(f_{it}(0)e_{1,it}^{2}v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}\right),
and

\Omega_{u_{1},i}=Var\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}e_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0}\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)\right].\hskip 9.24994pt\blacksquare

A.3.2
Proof of Statement (ii)

Steps for the proof for statement (ii) are the same as those in the proof of
statement (i). Hence, we only sketch the proof. Recall from (
2.8
)
that
\forall t\in[T]
,

\{\hat{v}_{t,j}^{(3,1)}\}_{j\in[p]}=\argmin\limits_{\{v_{t,j}\}_{j\in[p]}}\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}\rho_{\tau}\left(\hat{Y}_{it}-v_{t,0}^{\prime}\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)}-v_{t,1}^{\prime}\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)}\hat{e}_{1,it}\right),

where
\hat{Y}_{it}=Y_{it}-\hat{\mu}_{1,it}\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)\prime}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}
. Let

\hat{\Delta}_{t,v}=\begin{bmatrix}\hat{v}_{t,0}^{(3,1)}-\left(O_{u,0}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,0}^{0}\\
\hat{v}_{t,1}^{(3,1)}-\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,1}^{0}\end{bmatrix}\text{and }\hat{\omega}_{it}=\begin{bmatrix}\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)}\\
\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)}\hat{e}_{1,it}\end{bmatrix}.

For generic
(u_{i,0},u_{i,1},v_{i,0},v_{i,1})
, define

\hat{\mathbb{S}}_{t}\left(u_{i,0},u_{i,1},v_{i,0},v_{i,1}\right)=\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}\left[\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\varrho_{it}\left(u_{i,0},u_{i,1},v_{i,0},v_{i,1}\right)\right\}\right]\hat{\omega}_{it},

with

\varrho_{it}\left(u_{i,0},u_{i,1},v_{i,0},v_{i,1}\right)=u_{i,0}^{\prime}v_{t,0}-u_{i,0}^{0\prime}v_{t,0}^{0}+\hat{\mu}_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{\prime}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-\mu_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}+\hat{e}_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{\prime}v_{t,1}-e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}.

We also define

\displaystyle\hat{\mathcal{S}}_{t}\left(u_{i,0},u_{i,1},v_{i,0},v_{i,1}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}\mathbb{E}\left\{\left[\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\left(\varrho_{it}\left(u_{i,0},u_{i,1},v_{i,0},v_{i,1}\right)\right)\right\}\right]\dot{\omega}_{it}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right\}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}\left[\tau-F_{it}\left(\varrho_{it}\left(u_{i,0},u_{i,1},v_{i,0},v_{i,1}\right)\right)\right]\dot{\omega}_{it}

and

\displaystyle\hat{\mathbb{M}}_{t}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\hat{v}_{t,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{v}_{t,1}^{(3,1)}\right)

\displaystyle=\hat{\mathbb{S}}_{t}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\hat{v}_{t,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{v}_{t,1}^{(3,1)}\right)-\hat{\mathbb{S}}_{t}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\left(O_{u,0}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,0}^{0},\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)

\displaystyle-\left\{\hat{\mathcal{S}}_{t}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\hat{v}_{t,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{v}_{t,1}^{(3,1)}\right)-\hat{\mathcal{S}}_{t}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\left(O_{u,0}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,0}^{0},\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)\right\}.

Then a similar result to that in (
A.29
) holds:

\hat{\Delta}_{t,v}=\left(\hat{D}_{t}^{F}\right)^{-1}\left\{\hat{D}_{t}^{J}\begin{bmatrix}\left(O_{u,0}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,0}^{0}\\
\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,1}^{0}\end{bmatrix}+\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{4,t}+\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{5,t}+\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{6,t}+\hat{\mathbb{M}}_{t}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\hat{v}_{t,0}^{(1)},\hat{v}_{t,1}^{(3,1)}\right)+o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)\right\},

(A.44)

where

\displaystyle\hat{D}_{t}^{F}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}f_{it}\left[\varrho_{it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\left(O_{u,0}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,0}^{0},\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)\right]\begin{bmatrix}\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)}\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)\prime}&\hat{e}_{1,it}\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)}\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)\prime}\\
\hat{e}_{1,it}\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)}\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)\prime}&\hat{e}_{1,it}^{2}\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)}\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)\prime}\end{bmatrix},

\displaystyle\hat{D}_{t}^{J}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}f_{it}(\tilde{\varrho}_{it})\begin{bmatrix}\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)}\left(O_{u,0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0}-\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)}\right)^{\prime}&\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)}\left(e_{1,it}O_{u,1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}-\hat{e}_{it}\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)}\right)^{\prime}\\
\hat{e}_{1,it}\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)}\left(O_{u,0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0}-\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)}\right)^{\prime}&\hat{e}_{1,it}\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)}\left(e_{1,it}O_{u,1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}-\hat{e}_{1,it}\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)}\right)^{\prime}\end{bmatrix},

\displaystyle\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{4,t}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}\hat{\omega}_{it}\left[\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right),

\displaystyle\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{5,t}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}\hat{\omega}_{it}f_{it}\left(\tilde{\varrho}_{it}\right)\left(\mu_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}-\hat{\mu}_{1,it}\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)\prime}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\right),

\displaystyle\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{6,t}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}\hat{\omega}_{it}\Bigg\{\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\varrho_{it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\left(O_{u,0}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,0}^{0},\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)\right\}\right]

\displaystyle-\left(f_{it}(0)-f_{it}\left[\varrho_{it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\left(O_{u,0}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,0}^{0},\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)\right]\right)\Bigg\},

and
\left|\tilde{\varrho}_{it}\right|
lies between
0
and
\left|\varrho_{it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\left(O_{u,0}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,0}^{0},\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)\right|
.

Then, we derive the linear expansion for
\hat{\Delta}_{t,v}
by analyzing
each term in (
A.44
). Define

\displaystyle D_{t}^{F}=\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}f_{it}(0)\begin{bmatrix}O_{u,0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0}u_{i,0}^{0\prime}O_{u,0}^{(1)\prime}&0\\
0&e_{1,it}^{2}O_{u,1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}O_{u,1}^{(1)\prime}\end{bmatrix},

\displaystyle D_{t}^{J}=\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}f_{it}(0)\begin{bmatrix}\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)}\left(O_{u,0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0}-\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)}\right)^{\prime}&0\\
0&e_{1,it}^{2}O_{u,1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}-\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)}\right)^{\prime}\end{bmatrix},

such that

\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\hat{D}_{t}^{F}-D_{t}^{F}\right\|_{F}=O_{p}(\eta_{N})\text{ and }\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\hat{D}_{t}^{J}-D_{t}^{J}\right\|_{F}=\left\|\begin{bmatrix}O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right)&O_{p}(\eta_{N})\\
O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right)&O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right)\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F}

by Lemma
B.28
. Let
\omega_{it}^{\ast}=\begin{bmatrix}O_{u,0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0}\\
e_{1,it}O_{u,1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\end{bmatrix}
. We can show that

\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{4,t}=\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}\omega_{it}^{\ast}\left[\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right]+O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right)

uniformly over
t\in[T]
by analogous analysis as in Step 2 in the previous
subsection. With Lemma
B.29
and by similar arguments as in Step 3 in
the previous subsection, we obtain

\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{5,t}\right\|_{2}=\left\|\begin{bmatrix}O_{p}(\eta_{N})\\
o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{2}

uniformly over
t\in[T]
.

Next, for
\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{6,t}
, From (
B.3
), we first note that

\displaystyle\varrho_{it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\left(O_{u,0}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,0}^{0},\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)\leq R_{\varrho,it}^{1}\left(\left|\mu_{1,it}\right|+\left|e_{1,it}\right|\right)+R_{\varrho,it}^{2}\hskip 9.24994pt\text{with}

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left|R_{\varrho,it}^{1}\right|=O_{p}(\eta_{N}),\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left|R_{\varrho,it}^{2}\right|=O_{p}(\eta_{N}).

(A.45)

We then observe that, uniformly over
t\in[T]
,

\displaystyle\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{6,t}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}\hat{\omega}_{it}\Bigg\{\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\varrho_{it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\left(O_{u,0}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,0}^{0},\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)\right\}\right]

\displaystyle-\left(F_{it}(0)-F_{it}\left[\varrho_{it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\left(O_{u,0}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,0}^{0},\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)\right]\right)\Bigg\}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}\omega_{it}^{\ast}\Bigg\{\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\varrho_{it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\left(O_{u,0}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,0}^{0},\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)\right\}\right]

\displaystyle-\left(F_{it}(0)-F_{it}\left[\varrho_{it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\left(O_{u,0}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,0}^{0},\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)\right]\right)\Bigg\}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}\left(\hat{\omega}_{it}-\omega_{it}^{\ast}\right)\Bigg\{\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\varrho_{it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\left(O_{u,0}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,0}^{0},\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)\right\}\right]

\displaystyle-\left(F_{it}(0)-F_{it}\left[\varrho_{it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\left(O_{u,0}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,0}^{0},\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)\right]\right)\Bigg\}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}\omega_{it}^{\ast}\Bigg\{\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\varrho_{it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\left(O_{u,0}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,0}^{0},\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)\right\}\right]

\displaystyle-\left(F_{it}(0)-F_{it}\left[\varrho_{it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\left(O_{u,0}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,0}^{0},\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)\right]\right)\Bigg\}+o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}O_{p}(\eta_{N})\\
o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)\end{bmatrix},

where the third equality holds by (
A.3.2
) and similar arguments as used
in (
A.3.1
) and (
A.3.1
), and the last equality holds by Lemma
B.30
.

Similarly, combining Lemma
B.30
and Lemma
B.31
, it yields

\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\hat{\mathbb{M}}_{t}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\hat{v}_{t,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{v}_{t,1}^{(3,1)}\right)\right\|_{2}=\left\|\begin{bmatrix}O_{p}(\eta_{N})\\
o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{2}.

Let
\hat{V}_{v_{1},t}^{3}=\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}f_{it}(0)e_{1,it}^{2}u_{i,1}^{0}u_{i,1}^{0\prime},
and

O_{v_{1},t}^{(1)}=\left\{I_{K_{1}}+\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)\prime}\right)^{-1}\left[\hat{V}_{v_{1},t}^{3}\right]^{-1}\left[\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}f_{it}(0)e_{1,it}^{2}u_{i,1}^{0}\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}-\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)}\right)^{\prime}\right]\right\}\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)\prime}\right)^{-1}.

Combining arguments above from (
A.44
), we have

\hat{v}_{t,1}^{(3,1)}-O_{v_{1},t}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}=\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)\prime}\right)^{-1}\left(\hat{V}_{v_{1},t}^{3}\right)^{-1}\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)+\mathcal{R}_{t,v}^{1},

such that
\max_{t\in[T]}\left|\mathcal{R}_{t,v}^{1}\right|=o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)
. This, in conjunction
with the result in Lemma
B.32
, i.e.,
\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|O_{v_{1},t}^{(1)}-\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)\prime}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{F}=o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)
, implies that

\displaystyle\hat{v}_{t,1}^{(3,1)}-\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,1}^{0}

\displaystyle=\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)\prime}\right)^{-1}\left(\hat{V}_{v_{1},t}^{3}\right)^{-1}\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)+\mathcal{R}_{t,v}^{1},

\displaystyle=O_{1}^{(1)}\left(\hat{V}_{v_{1},t}^{3}\right)^{-1}\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)+\mathcal{R}_{t,v}^{1}.

(A.46)

where the second line holds by the fact that
\left\|O_{u,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}\right\|_{F}=O_{p}(\eta_{N})
,
\hat{V}_{v_{1},t}^{I}
is uniformly bounded and that

\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)\right\|_{2}=O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N}}\right)

by similar arguments as in (
A.40
). Further define

\displaystyle\Sigma_{v_{1}}=O_{1}^{(1)}V_{v_{1}}^{-1}\Omega_{u_{1}}V_{u_{1}}^{-1}O_{1}^{(1)\prime},\hskip 9.24994ptV_{v_{1}}=\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}\mathbb{E}\left(f_{it}(0)e_{1,it}^{2}u_{i,1}^{0}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}\right),\text{ and}

\displaystyle\Omega_{v_{1}}=\tau\left(1-\tau\right)\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}\mathbb{E}\left(e_{1,it}^{2}u_{i,1}^{0}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}\right).

Then we have

\displaystyle\sqrt{N_{3}}\left(\hat{v}_{t,1}^{(3,1)}-\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)\rightsquigarrow\mathcal{N}(0,\Sigma_{v_{1}}),

\displaystyle\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\hat{v}_{t,1}^{(3,1)}-O_{u,1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N}}\right),\hskip 9.24994pt\text{and }\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\hat{v}_{t,0}^{(3,1)}-O_{u,0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}(\eta_{N}),

(A.47)

where the second line holds by Bernstein’s inequality with independent data
and is similar to (
A.43
).
\blacksquare

A.4
Proof of Proposition
3.4

A.4.1
Proof of Statement (i)

Focusing on the slope estimators for
i\in I_{3}
, we notice that
\hat{\Theta}_{j,it}=\frac{1}{2}\left\{\hat{u}_{t,j}^{(3,1)\prime}\hat{v}_{t,j}^{(3,1)}+\hat{u}_{t,j}^{(3,2)\prime}\hat{v}_{t,j}^{(3,2)}\right\}.

It follows that

\displaystyle\hat{\Theta}_{j,it}-\Theta_{j,it}^{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\left\{\left(\hat{u}_{i,j}^{(3,1)}-O_{u,j}^{(1)}u_{i,j}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\hat{v}_{t,j}^{(3,1)}-O_{u,j}^{(1)}v_{t,j}^{0}\right)+\left(O_{u,j}^{(1)}u_{i,j}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\hat{v}_{t,j}^{(3,1)}-O_{u,j}^{(1)}v_{t,j}^{0}\right)+\left(\hat{u}_{i,j}^{(3,1)}-O_{u,j}^{(1)}u_{i,j}^{0}\right)^{\prime}O_{u,j}^{(1)}v_{t,j}^{0}\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\left\{\left(\hat{u}_{i,j}^{(3,2)}-O_{u,j}^{(2)}u_{i,j}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\hat{v}_{t,j}^{(3,2)}-O_{u,j}^{(2)}v_{t,j}^{0}\right)+\left(O_{u,j}^{(2)}u_{i,j}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\hat{v}_{t,j}^{(3,2)}-O_{u,j}^{(2)}v_{t,j}^{0}\right)+\left(\hat{u}_{i,j}^{(3,2)}-O_{u,j}^{(2)}u_{i,j}^{0}\right)^{\prime}O_{u,j}^{(1)}v_{t,j}^{0}\right\}

\displaystyle=u_{i,j}^{0\prime}\left(\hat{V}_{v_{j},t}^{(3)}\right)^{-1}\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{j,it}u_{i,j}^{0}\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)+v_{t,j}^{0\prime}\hat{V}_{u_{j}}^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}e_{j,it}v_{t,j}^{0}\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)+\mathcal{R}_{it}^{j}

\displaystyle=u_{i,j}^{0\prime}\left(\hat{V}_{v_{j},t}^{(3)}\right)^{-1}\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}\xi_{j,it}^{0}+v_{t,j}^{0\prime}\hat{V}_{u_{j}}^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}b_{j,it}^{0}+\mathcal{R}_{it}^{j},

such that
\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left|\mathcal{R}_{it}^{j}\right|=o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-1/2}\right)
by
Theorem
3.3
and the second equality above combines Theorem
3.3

and the fact that
\left\|O_{u,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}\right\|_{F}=O_{p}(\eta_{N})
. With similar
results hold for slope estimators for subsamples
I_{1}
and
I_{2}
, and
then we obtain the statement (i).

A.4.2
Proof of Statement (ii)

Combining (
A.43
), (
A.3.2
) and Lemma
B.13
(i), it’s clear that

\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left|\hat{\Theta}_{j,it}-\Theta_{j,it}^{0}\right|=O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N\wedge T}}\right)\text{
}\forall j\in[p]\hskip 9.24994pt\text{and}\hskip 9.24994pt\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left|\hat{\Theta}_{0,it}-\Theta_{0,it}^{0}\right|=O_{p}(\eta_{N}).\text{ }

(A.48)

A.4.3
Proof of Statement (iii)

For
i\in I_{a}
and
a\in[3]
, with the distribution theory defined in
Theorem
3.3
, we notice that

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1}{T}v_{t,j}^{0\prime}\Xi_{u_{j},i}v_{t,j}^{0}+\frac{1}{N_{a}}u_{i,j}^{0\prime}\Xi_{v_{j}}^{a}u_{i,j}^{0}\right)^{-1/2}\left(\hat{\Theta}_{j,it}-\Theta_{j,it}^{0}\right)\rightsquigarrow\mathcal{N}(0,1),

which leads to the proof.
\blacksquare

A.5
Proof of Theorem
4.1

A.5.1
Proof of Statement (i)

The proof is analogous to that in
Castagnetti et al., 2015
and
Lu and Su, 2021
. Recall that
S_{u_{j}}=\max(S_{u_{j}}^{(1,2)},

S_{u_{j}}^{(2,3)},

S_{u_{j}}^{(3,1)})
. For
a\in[3]
and
b\in[3]\setminus\{a\}
, Theorem
3.3
shows that

\hat{u}_{i,j}^{(a,b)}-O_{u,j}^{(b)}u_{i,j}^{0}=O_{j}^{(b)}\hat{V}_{u_{j}}^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}b_{j,it}^{0}+\mathcal{R}_{i,u}^{j}\text{ }\forall i\in I_{a},

where
\max_{i\in I_{a}}\left|\mathcal{R}_{i,u}^{j}\right|=o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-1/2}\right)
. Recall that
\hat{\bar{u}}_{j}^{(a)}=\frac{1}{N_{a}}\sum_{i\in I_{a}}\hat{u}_{i,j}^{(a,b)}
. Under
H_{0}^{I}:u_{i,j}^{0}=u_{j},\forall i\in[N]
, we have

\hat{\bar{u}}_{j}^{(a,b)}-O_{u,j}^{(b)}u_{j}=O_{j}^{(b)}\hat{V}_{u_{j}}^{-1}\frac{1}{N_{a}T}\sum_{i\in I_{a}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}b_{j,it}^{0}+\frac{1}{N_{a}}\sum_{i\in I_{a}}\mathcal{R}_{i,u}^{j}=o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-1/2}\right),

(A.49)

where the last equality holds by a simple application of Bernstein’s
inequality. Note that

\displaystyle T\left(\hat{u}_{i,j}^{(a,b)}-\hat{\bar{u}}_{j}^{(a,b)}\right)^{\prime}\left(\hat{\Sigma}_{u_{j}}\right)^{-1}\left(\hat{u}_{i,j}^{(a,b)}-\hat{\bar{u}}_{j}^{(a,b)}\right)

\displaystyle=T\left(\hat{u}_{i,j}^{(a,b)}-O_{u,j}^{(b)}u_{j}\right)^{\prime}\left(\hat{\Sigma}_{u_{j}}\right)^{-1}\left(\hat{u}_{i,j}^{(a,b)}-O_{u,j}^{(b)}u_{j}\right)+T\left(\hat{\bar{u}}_{j}^{(a,b)}-O_{u,j}^{(b)}u_{j}\right)^{\prime}\left(\hat{\Sigma}_{u_{j}}\right)^{-1}\left(\hat{\bar{u}}_{j}^{(a,b)}-O_{u,j}^{(b)}u_{j}\right)

\displaystyle-2T\left(\hat{u}_{i,j}^{(a,b)}-O_{u,j}^{(b)}u_{j}\right)^{\prime}\left(\hat{\Sigma}_{u_{j}}\right)^{-1}\left(\hat{\bar{u}}_{j}^{(a,b)}-O_{u,j}^{(b)}u_{j}\right):=I_{1ij}+I_{2ij}-2I_{3ij}.

(A.50)

For
I_{2ij},
we have

\max_{i\in I_{a}}\left|I_{2ij}\right|\leq\max_{i\in I_{a}}T\left\|\hat{\bar{u}}_{j}^{(a,b)}-O_{u,j}^{(b)}u_{j}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\{\lambda_{\min}(\Sigma_{u_{j}})+o_{p}(1)\right\}^{-1}=o_{p}(1)

(A.51)

by Lemma
B.33
, Assumption
11
and (
A.49
). For
I_{3ij},
we have

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{a}}\left|I_{3ij}\right|

\displaystyle\leq T\left(\max_{i\in I_{a}}\left\|\hat{u}_{i,j}^{(a,b)}-O_{u,j}^{(b)}u_{j}\right\|_{2}\right)\left(\left\|\hat{\bar{u}}_{j}^{(a,b)}-O_{u,j}^{(b)}u_{j}\right\|_{2}\right)\left[\lambda_{\min}(\Sigma_{u_{j}})+o_{p}(1)\right]^{-1}

\displaystyle=TO_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{T}}\right)o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-1/2}\right)=o_{p}(1)

(A.52)

by (
A.43
) and (
A.49
). It suffices to study
I_{1ij}
below.

Now, let
\mathbb{Z}_{\mathfrak{B}}^{(1)}=\left(\mathbb{Z}_{\mathfrak{B},1}^{(1)\prime},\cdots,\mathbb{Z}_{\mathfrak{B},N}^{(1)\prime}\right)^{\prime}
, where
\mathbb{Z}_{\mathfrak{B},i}^{(1)}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(0,O_{j}^{(1)\prime}\Sigma_{u_{j}}O_{j}^{(1)}\right)
for
i\in I_{3}
,
\mathbb{Z}_{\mathfrak{B},i}^{(1)}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(0,O_{j}^{(3)\prime}\Sigma_{u_{j}}O_{j}^{(3)}\right)
for
i\in I_{2}
and
\mathbb{Z}_{\mathfrak{B},i}^{(1)}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(0,O_{j}^{(2)\prime}\Sigma_{u_{j}}O_{j}^{(2)}\right)
for
i\in I_{1}
. Note that

\displaystyle I_{1ij}

\displaystyle=\left(O_{j}^{(b)}\mathbb{Z}_{\mathfrak{B},i}^{(1)}+o_{p(1)}+\mathcal{R}_{i,u}^{j}\right)^{\prime}\Sigma_{u_{j}}^{-1}\left(O_{j}^{(b)}\mathbb{Z}_{\mathfrak{B},i}^{(1)}+o_{p}(1)+\mathcal{R}_{i,u}^{j}\right)

\displaystyle+T\left(\hat{u}_{i,j}^{(a,b)}-O_{u,j}^{(b)}u_{j}\right)^{\prime}\left[\hat{\Sigma}_{u_{j}}^{-1}-\Sigma_{u_{j}}^{-1}\right]\left(\hat{u}_{i,j}^{(a,b)}-O_{u,j}^{(b)}u_{j}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{Z}_{\mathfrak{B},i}^{(1)\prime}\left(O_{j}^{(b)\prime}\Sigma_{u_{j}}O_{j}^{(b)}\right)^{-1}\mathbb{Z}_{\mathfrak{B},i}^{(1)}+o_{p}(1)\hskip 9.24994pt\text{uniformly over $i\in[N]$.}

(A.53)

It follows that

S_{u_{j}}=\max_{i\in[N]}\left\{\begin{aligned} \mathbb{Z}_{\mathfrak{B},i}^{(1)\prime}\left(O_{j}^{(2)\prime}\Sigma_{u_{j}}O_{j}^{(2)}\right)^{-1}\mathbb{Z}_{\mathfrak{B},i}^{(1)}+o_{p}(1),\hskip 9.24994pt\forall i\in I_{1},\\
\mathbb{Z}_{\mathfrak{B},i}^{(1)\prime}\left(O_{j}^{(3)\prime}\Sigma_{u_{j}}O_{j}^{(3)}\right)^{-1}\mathbb{Z}_{\mathfrak{B},i}^{(1)}+o_{p}(1),\hskip 9.24994pt\forall i\in I_{2},\\
\mathbb{Z}_{\mathfrak{B},i}^{(1)\prime}\left(O_{j}^{(1)\prime}\Sigma_{u_{j}}O_{j}^{(1)}\right)^{-1}\mathbb{Z}_{\mathfrak{B},i}^{(1)}+o_{p}(1),\hskip 9.24994pt\forall i\in I_{3},\end{aligned}\right.

(A.54)

with
\mathbb{Z}_{\mathfrak{B},i}^{(1)\prime}\left(O_{j}^{(2)\prime}\Sigma_{u_{j}}O_{j}^{(2)}\right)^{-1}\mathbb{Z}_{\mathfrak{B},i}^{(1)}\rightarrow\chi^{2}(1)
for each
i\in I_{1}
,
\mathbb{Z}_{\mathfrak{B},i}^{(1)\prime}\left(O_{j}^{(3)\prime}\Sigma_{u_{j}}O_{j}^{(3)}\right)^{-1}\mathbb{Z}_{\mathfrak{B},i}^{(1)}\rightarrow\chi^{2}(1)
for each
i\in I_{2}
, and
\mathbb{Z}_{\mathfrak{B},i}^{(1)\prime}\left(O_{j}^{(1)\prime}\Sigma_{u_{j}}O_{j}^{(1)}\right)^{-1}\mathbb{Z}_{\mathfrak{B},i}^{(1)}\rightarrow\chi^{2}(1)
for each
i\in I_{3}
. As in
Castagnetti et al., 2015
, we can conclude that

\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{1}{2}S_{u_{j}}\leq x+\mathsf{b}(N)\right)\rightarrow e^{-e^{-x}}\ \text{as }\left(N,T\right)\rightarrow\infty.

(A.55)

For the test statistic for
H_{0}^{II}
, the proof is similar. Let
\mathbb{Z}_{\mathfrak{B}}^{(2)}=\left(\mathbb{Z}_{\mathfrak{B},1}^{(2)\prime},\cdots,\mathbb{Z}_{\mathfrak{B},T}^{(2)\prime}\right)^{\prime}
, where

\mathbb{Z}_{\mathfrak{B},t}^{(2)}\sim N\left(0,O_{j}^{(1)\prime}\Sigma_{v_{j}}O_{j}^{(1)}\right)
. As in (
A.54
), we can show that

S_{v_{j}}^{(3,1)}=\max_{t\in[T]}\mathbb{Z}_{\mathfrak{B},t}^{(2)\prime}\left(O_{j}^{(1)\prime}\Sigma_{v_{j}}O_{j}^{(1)}\right)^{-1}\mathbb{Z}_{\mathfrak{B},t}^{(2)}+o_{p}(1)\text{.}

By the strong mixing condition in Assumption
1
(iii), we have
\max_{j\in[p],i\in[N]}\left\|Cov\left(\mathfrak{b}_{j,it}^{(2)},\mathfrak{b}_{j,is}^{(2)}\right)\right\|_{\infty}\log\left(t-s\right)=o(1)
as
t-s\rightarrow\infty
by Davydov’s
inequality. Then by Theorem 3.5.1 in
Leadbetter and Rootzen, 1988
, we have
that

\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{1}{2}\left(\max_{t\in[T]}\mathbb{Z}_{\mathfrak{B},t}^{(2)\prime}\left(O_{j}^{(1)\prime}\Sigma_{v_{j}}O_{j}^{(1)}\right)^{-1}\mathbb{Z}_{\mathfrak{B},t}^{(2)}\right)\leq x+\mathsf{b}(T)\right)\rightarrow e^{-e^{-x}},

which implies that

\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{1}{2}S_{v_{j}}^{(3,1)}\leq x+\mathsf{b}(T)\right)\rightarrow e^{-e^{-x}}\text{ as }\left(N,T\right)\rightarrow\infty.\hskip 9.24994pt

Recall that
\tilde{S}_{v_{j}}^{(a,b)}=\frac{1}{2}S_{v_{j}}^{(a,b)}-\mathsf{b}(T)
and
S_{v_{j}}=\max(\tilde{S}_{v_{j}}^{(1,2)},\tilde{S}_{v_{j}}^{(2,3)},\tilde{S}_{v_{j}}^{(3,1)}).
Noting that
S_{v_{j}}
is
asymptotically distributed as the maximum of three independent Gumbel random
variables under
H_{0}^{II}
, we have
\mathbb{P(}S_{v_{j}}\leq x)\rightarrow e^{-3e^{-x}}
as
\left(N,T\right)\rightarrow\infty.

A.5.2
Proof of Statement (ii)

Under
H_{1}^{I}
, we have that
\forall i\in I_{a}
,

\displaystyle\hat{u}_{i,j}^{(a,b)}-\hat{\bar{u}}_{j}^{(a,b)}

\displaystyle=\left(\hat{u}_{i,j}^{(a,b)}-O_{u,j}^{(b)}u_{i,j}^{0}\right)+O_{u,j}^{(b)}\left(u_{i,j}^{0}-u_{j}\right)-\left(\hat{\bar{u}}_{j}^{(a,b)}-O_{u,j}^{(b)}u_{j}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(\hat{u}_{i,j}^{(a,b)}-O_{u,j}^{(b)}u_{i,j}^{0}\right)+O_{u,j}^{(b)}c_{i,j}^{u}-\left(\hat{\bar{u}}_{j}^{(a,b)}-O_{u,j}^{(b)}u_{j}\right).

Then

\displaystyle T\left(\hat{u}_{i,j}^{(a,b)}-\hat{\bar{u}}_{j}^{(a,b)}\right)^{\prime}\left(\hat{\Sigma}_{u_{j}}\right)^{-1}\left(\hat{u}_{i,j}^{(a,b)}-\hat{\bar{u}}_{j}^{(a,b)}\right)

\displaystyle=T\left(\hat{u}_{i,j}^{(a,b)}-O_{u,j}^{(b)}u_{j}\right)^{\prime}\hat{\Sigma}_{u_{j}}^{-1}\left(\hat{u}_{i,j}^{(1)}-O_{u,j}^{(b)}u_{j}\right)+T\left(\hat{\bar{u}}_{j}^{(1)}-O_{u,j}^{(b)}u_{j}\right)^{\prime}\hat{\Sigma}_{u_{j}}^{-1}\left(\hat{\bar{u}}_{j}^{(a,b)}-O_{u,j}^{(b)}u_{j}\right)

\displaystyle-2T\left(\hat{u}_{i,j}^{(a,b)}-O_{u,j}^{(b)}u_{j}\right)^{\prime}\hat{\Sigma}_{u_{j}}^{-1}\left(\hat{\bar{u}}_{j}^{(1)}-O_{u,j}^{(b)}u_{j}\right)+2T\left(O_{u,j}^{(b)}c_{i,j}^{u}\right)^{\prime}\hat{\Sigma}_{u_{j}}^{-1}\left(\hat{u}_{i,j}^{(a,b)}-O_{u,j}^{(b)}u_{j}\right)

\displaystyle+T\left(O_{u,j}^{(b)}c_{i,j}^{u}\right)^{\prime}\hat{\Sigma}_{u_{j}}^{-1}O_{u,j}^{(b)}c_{i,j}^{u}-2T\left(O_{u,j}^{(b)}c_{i,j}^{u}\right)^{\prime}\hat{\Sigma}_{u_{j}}^{-1}\left(\hat{\bar{u}}_{j}^{(a,b)}-O_{u,j}^{(b)}u_{j}\right)

\displaystyle:

\displaystyle=S_{u_{j},i,1}^{(b)}+S_{u_{j},2}^{(b)}+S_{u_{j},i,3}^{(b)}+S_{u_{j},i,4}^{(b)}+S_{u_{j},i,5}^{(b)}+S_{u_{j},i,6}^{(b)},

where
\max_{i\in I_{a}}\left|S_{u_{j},i,1}^{(b)}\right|=O_{p}\left(\log N\right)
by (
A.53
) and (
A.55
),
\left|S_{u_{j},2}^{(b)}\right|=o_{p}\left(1\right)
by (
A.51
),
\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left|S_{u_{j},i,3}^{(b)}\right|=o_{p}\left(1\right)
by (
A.52
). Next,

\max_{i\in I_{a}}\left|S_{u_{j},i,5}^{(b)}\right|=\max_{i\in I_{a}}T\left(O_{u,j}^{(b)}c_{i,j}^{u}\right)^{\prime}\Sigma_{u_{j}}^{-1}O_{u,j}^{(b)}c_{i,j}^{u}\left\{1+o_{p}(1)\right\}\gtrsim\left[\max_{i\in I_{a}}\lambda_{\max}\left(\Sigma_{u,j}\right)\right]^{-1}T\max_{i\in I_{a}}\left\|c_{i,j}^{u}\right\|_{2}^{2}\asymp T\max_{i\in I_{a}}\left\|c_{i,j}^{u}\right\|_{2}^{2},

which diverges to infinity at the rate faster than
\log N
by condition in
statement (ii). By Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we obtain that

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{a}}\left|S_{u_{j},i,4}^{(b)}\right|\lesssim\max_{i\in I_{a}}\left(S_{u_{j},i,1}^{(b)}\right)^{1/2}\left(S_{u_{j},i,5}^{(b)}\right)^{1/2}=o_{p}\left(\max_{i\in I_{a}}\left|S_{u_{j},i,5}^{(b)}\right|\right),\text{ and}

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{a}}\left|S_{u_{j},i,6}^{(b)}\right|\lesssim\max_{i\in I_{a}}\left(S_{u_{j},i,2}^{a}\right)^{1/2}\left(S_{u_{j},i,5}^{(b)}\right)^{1/2}=o_{p}\left(\max_{i\in I_{a}}\left|S_{u_{j},i,5}^{(b)}\right|\right).

It follows that
\mathbb{P}\left\{S_{u_{j}}>c_{\alpha,N}\right\}\rightarrow 1
as
c_{\alpha,N}\asymp\log N
, and the final result follows.

The power of the test statistic
S_{v_{j}}
can be analyzed analogously.
\blacksquare

A.6
Proof of Theorem
4.2

We first derive the linear expansion of
\hat{\Theta}_{j,it}^{\ast}-\Theta_{j,it}^{\ast}
for
i\in I_{3}
, and similar results hold for
i\in I_{1}\cup I_{2}
. Let
\bar{v}_{j}^{0}:=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}v_{t,j}^{0}\
and
\bar{u}_{j}^{0,I_{a}}:=\frac{1}{N_{a}}\sum_{i\in I_{a}}u_{i,j}^{0}
. By Proposition
3.4
, we obtain that uniformly in
i\in I_{a},

\displaystyle\hat{\bar{\Theta}}_{j,i\cdot}-\bar{\Theta}_{j,i\cdot}=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left(\hat{\Theta}_{j,it}-\Theta_{j,it}^{0}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}u_{i,j}^{0\prime}\left(\hat{V}_{v_{j},t}^{(a)}\right)^{-1}\frac{1}{N_{a}}\sum_{i^{*}\in I_{a}}\xi_{j,i^{*}t}^{0}+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}v_{t,j}^{0\prime}\hat{V}_{u_{j}}^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}b_{j,it}^{0}+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\mathcal{R}_{it}^{j}

\displaystyle=\bar{v}_{j}^{0\prime}\hat{V}_{u_{j}}^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}b_{j,it}^{0}+o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-1/2}\right),

(A.56)

where the third equality holds by the fact that

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{a}}\left|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}u_{i,j}^{0\prime}\left(\hat{V}_{v_{j},t}^{(a)}\right)^{-1}\frac{1}{N_{a}}\sum_{i^{*}\in I_{a}}\xi_{j,i^{*}t}^{0}\right|=\max_{i\in I_{a}}\left|\frac{1}{N_{a}T}\sum_{i^{*}\in I_{a}}\sum_{t\in[T]}u_{i,j}^{0\prime}\left(\hat{V}_{v_{j},t}^{(a)}\right)^{-1}\xi_{j,i^{*}t}^{0}\right|

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log N}{NT}}\xi_{N}\right)=o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-1/2}\right),

by conditional Bernstein’s inequality given
\mathscr{D}_{e}
in Lemma
B.12
(i), Assumption
1
(i)-(ii) and Assumption
1
(ix). Similarly, uniformly in
t\in\left[T\right]
, we have

\displaystyle\hat{\bar{\Theta}}_{j,\cdot t}^{I_{a}}-\bar{\Theta}_{j,\cdot t}^{I_{a}}=\frac{1}{N_{a}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}\left(\hat{\Theta}_{j,it}-\Theta_{j,it}^{0}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N_{a}}\sum_{i\in I_{a}}u_{i,j}^{0\prime}\left(\hat{V}_{v_{j},t}^{(a)}\right)^{-1}\frac{1}{N_{a}}\sum_{i\in I_{a}}\xi_{j,it}^{0}+\frac{1}{N_{a}}\sum_{i\in I_{a}}v_{t,j}^{0\prime}\hat{V}_{u_{j}}^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t^{*}=1}^{T}b_{j,it^{*}}^{0}+\frac{1}{N_{a}}\sum_{i\in I_{a}}\mathcal{R}_{it}^{j}

\displaystyle=\bar{u}_{j}^{0,I_{a}\prime}\left(\hat{V}_{v_{j},t}^{(a)}\right)^{-1}\frac{1}{N_{a}}\sum_{i\in I_{a}}\xi_{j,it}^{0}+o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-1/2}\right),

(A.57)

and

\displaystyle\hat{\bar{\Theta}}_{j}^{I_{a}}-\bar{\Theta}_{j}^{I_{a}}=\frac{1}{N_{a}T}\sum_{i\in I_{a}}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left(\hat{\Theta}_{j,it}-\Theta_{j,it}^{0}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N_{a}T}\sum_{i\in I_{a}}\sum_{t\in[T]}u_{i,j}^{0\prime}\left(\hat{V}_{v_{j},t}^{(a)}\right)^{-1}\frac{1}{N_{a}}\sum_{i\in I_{a}}\xi_{j,it}^{0}+\frac{1}{N_{a}T}\sum_{i\in I_{a}}\sum_{t\in[T]}v_{t,j}^{0\prime}\hat{V}_{u_{j}}^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}b_{j,it}^{0}+\frac{1}{N_{a}T}\sum_{i\in I_{a}}\sum_{t\in[T]}\mathcal{R}_{it}^{j}

\displaystyle=o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-1/2}\right).

(A.58)

Combining (
A.56
)-(
A.58
), we obtain that
\forall i\in I_{a}
and
t\in\left[T\right]
,

\displaystyle\hat{\Theta}_{j,it}^{\ast}-\Theta_{j,it}^{\ast}=\hat{\Theta}_{j,it}-\Theta_{j,it}^{0}-\left(\hat{\bar{\Theta}}_{j,i\cdot}-\bar{\Theta}_{j,i\cdot}\right)-\left(\hat{\bar{\Theta}}_{j,\cdot t}^{I_{a}}-\bar{\Theta}_{j,\cdot t}^{I_{a}}\right)+\left(\hat{\bar{\Theta}}_{j}^{I_{a}}-\bar{\Theta}_{j}^{I_{a}}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(u_{i,j}^{0}-\bar{u}_{j}^{0,I_{a}}\right)^{\prime}\left(\hat{V}_{v_{j},t}^{(a)}\right)^{-1}\frac{1}{N_{a}}\sum_{i\in I_{a}}\xi_{j,it}^{0}+\left(v_{t,j}^{0}-\bar{v}_{j}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\hat{V}_{u_{j}}^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}b_{j,it}^{0}+\bar{\mathcal{R}}_{it}^{j},

such that
\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left|\bar{\mathcal{R}}_{it}^{j}\right|=o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-1/2}\right)
. It
follows that
\hat{\Theta}_{j,it}^{\ast}-\Theta_{j,it}^{\ast}\rightsquigarrow\mathcal{N}(0,\Sigma_{j,it}^{*})

with

\Sigma_{j,it}^{*}=\sum_{a\in[3]}\frac{1}{N_{a}}\left(O_{j}^{(b)}u_{i,j}^{0}-O_{j}^{(b)}\bar{u}_{j}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\Sigma_{v_{j}}\left(O_{j}^{(b)}u_{i,j}^{0}-O_{j}^{(b)}\bar{u}_{j}^{0}\right)\mathbf{1}_{ia}+\frac{1}{T}\left(O_{j}^{(b)}v_{t,j}^{0}-O_{j}^{(b)}\bar{v}_{j}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\Sigma_{u_{j}}\left(O_{j}^{(b)}v_{t,j}^{0}-O_{j}^{(b)}\bar{v}_{j}^{0}\right),

and
\Sigma_{u_{j}}
and
\Sigma_{v_{j}}
are as defined in Theorem
3.3
. The reason why
\Sigma_{j,it}^{*}
is not indexed with
b
is owing
to the fact that
O_{j}^{(b)}
shown in the right side of the equality can
be absorbed by
O_{j}^{(b)}
not shown in
\Sigma_{u_{j}}
and
\Sigma_{v_{j}}
.

Define
\hat{\Sigma}_{j,it}^{*}=\frac{1}{2}\operatornamewithlimits{\sum}_{b}\hat{\Sigma}_{j,it}^{(b)*}
with

\hat{\Sigma}_{j,it}^{(b)*}=\sum_{a\in[3]}\left[\frac{1}{N_{a}}\left(\hat{u}_{i,j}^{(a,b)}-\hat{\bar{u}}_{j}^{(a,b)}\right)^{\prime}\hat{\Sigma}_{v_{j}}\left(\hat{u}_{i,j}^{(a,b)}-\bar{u}_{j}^{(a,b)}\right)\mathbf{1}_{ia}+\frac{1}{T}\left(\hat{v}_{t,j}^{(a,b)}-\hat{\bar{v}}_{j}^{(a,b)}\right)^{\prime}\hat{\Sigma}_{u_{j}}\left(\hat{v}_{t,j}-\hat{\bar{v}}_{j}^{(a,b)}\right)\right],

where
\hat{\bar{u}}_{j}^{(a,b)}=\frac{1}{N_{a}}\sum_{i\in I_{a}}\hat{u}_{i,j}^{(a,b)}
and
\hat{\bar{v}}_{j}^{(a,b)}=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\hat{v}_{t,j}^{(a,b)}
. By Theorem
3.3
and Lemma
B.33
, we have

\max_{j\in[p],i\in[N],t\in[T]}\left|\hat{\Sigma}_{j,it}^{*}-\Sigma_{j,it}^{*}\right|=o_{p}(1).

By arguments as used in the proof of Theorem
3.3
, we have that as
\left(N,T\right)\rightarrow\infty,

\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{1}{2}S_{NT}\leq x+\mathsf{b}(NT)\right)\rightarrow e^{-e^{-x}}\
under
H_{0}^{III},
and
\mathbb{P}\left(S_{NT}>c_{\alpha,3\cdot NT}\right)\rightarrow 1
under
H_{1}^{III}
provided
\frac{N\wedge T}{\log NT}\max_{i\in[N],t\in[T]}\left|\Theta_{j,it}^{\ast}\right|^{2}\rightarrow\infty
.
\blacksquare

Appendix B
Some Technical Lemmas

In this section we state and prove some technical lemmas that are used in
the proofs of the main results in the paper.

B.1
Lemmas for the Proof of Theorem
3.1

Lemma B.1

Consider a matrix sequence
\left\{A_{i},i=1,\cdots,N\right\}
whose values are symmetric matrices with
dimension
d
,

(i)

suppose
\left\{A_{i},i=1,\cdots,N\right\}
is independent
with
\mathbb{E}\left(A_{i}\right)=0
and
\left\|A_{i}\right\|_{op}\leq M
a.s. Let
\sigma^{2}=\left\|\sum_{i\in[N]}\mathbb{E}\left(A_{i}^{2}\right)\right\|_{op}
. Then for all
t>0
, we
have

\mathbb{P}\left(\left\|\sum_{i\in[N]}A_{i}\right\|_{op}>t\right)\leq d\cdot\exp\left\{-\frac{t^{2}/2}{\sigma^{2}+Mt/3}\right\}.

(ii)

suppose
\left\{A_{i},i=1,\cdots,N\right\}
is sequence of
martingale difference matrices with
\mathbb{E}_{i-1}\left(A_{i}\right)=0

and
\left\|A_{i}\right\|_{op}\leq M
a.s., where
\mathbb{E}_{i-1}

denotes
\mathbb{E}\left(\cdot|\mathscr{F}_{i-1}\right)
, where
\left\{\mathscr{F}_{i}:i\leq N\right\}
denotes the filtration that is clear from
the context. Let
\left\|\sum_{i\in[N]}\mathbb{E}_{i-1}\left(A_{i}^{2}\right)\right\|_{op}\leq\sigma^{2}
. Then for all
t>0
, we have

\mathbb{P}\left(\left\|\sum_{i\in[N]}A_{i}\right\|_{op}>t\right)\leq d\cdot\exp\left\{-\frac{t^{2}/2}{\sigma^{2}+Mt/3}\right\}.

Proof.
Lemma
B.1
(i) and (ii) are Matrix Bernstein inequality and
Matrix Freedman inequality, which are respectively stated in Theorem 1.3
and Corollary 4.2 in
Tropp, 2011
.

Lemma B.2

Consider a specific matrix
A\in\mathbb{R}^{N\times T}
whose rows (denoted as
A_{i}^{\prime}
where
A_{i}\in\mathbb{R}^{T}
) are independent random vectors in
\mathbb{R}^{T}
with
\mathbb{E}A_{i}=0
and
\Sigma_{i}=\mathbb{E}\left(A_{i}A_{i}^{\prime}\right)
. Suppose
\max_{i\in[N]}\left\|A_{i}\right\|_{2}\leq\sqrt{m}
a.s. and
\max_{i\in[N]}\left\|\Sigma_{i}\right\|_{op}\leq M

for some positive constant
M
. Then for every
t>0
, with probability
1-2T\exp\left(-c_{1}t^{2}\right)
, we have

\left\|A\right\|_{op}\leq\sqrt{NM}+t\sqrt{m+M},

where
c_{1}
is an absolute constant.

Proof.
The proof follows similar arguments as used in the proof of Theorem 5.41 in

Vershynin, 2010
. Define
Z_{i}:=\frac{1}{N}\left(A_{i}A_{i}^{\prime}-\Sigma_{i}\right)\in\mathbb{R}^{T\times T}
. We
notice that
\left\{Z_{i}\right\}
is an independent sequence with
\mathbb{E}\left(Z_{i}\right)=0
. To use the matrix Bernstein inequality, we
analyze
\left\|Z_{i}\right\|_{op}
and
\left\|\sum_{i\in[N]}\mathbb{E}\left(X_{i}^{2}\right)\right\|_{op}
as follows. First, note
that uniformly over
i,

\left\|Z_{i}\right\|_{op}\leq\frac{1}{N}\left(\left\|A_{i}A_{i}^{\prime}\right\|_{op}+\left\|\Sigma_{i}\right\|_{op}\right)\leq\frac{1}{N}\left(\left\|A_{i}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\left\|\Sigma_{i}\right\|_{op}\right)\leq\frac{m+M}{N},\hskip 9.24994pt\text{a.s.}

(B.1)

Next, noting that
\mathbb{E}\left[\left(A_{i}A_{i}^{\prime}\right)^{2}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|A_{i}\right\|_{2}A_{i}A_{i}^{\prime}\right]\leq m\Sigma_{i}
and
Z_{i}^{2}=\frac{1}{N^{2}}[\left(A_{i}A_{i}^{\prime}\right)^{2}-A_{i}A_{i}^{\prime}\Sigma_{i}

-\Sigma_{i}A_{i}A_{i}^{\prime}+\Sigma_{i}^{2}],
we have

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}\left(Z_{i}^{2}\right)\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N^{2}}\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left(A_{i}A_{i}^{\prime}\right)^{2}-\Sigma_{i}^{2}\right]\right\|_{op}\leq\frac{1}{N^{2}}\left\{\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left(A_{i}A_{i}^{\prime}\right)^{2}\right]\right\|_{op}+\left\|\Sigma_{i}\right\|_{op}^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{N^{2}}\left(m\left\|\Sigma_{i}\right\|_{op}+\left\|\Sigma_{i}\right\|_{op}^{2}\right)\leq\frac{mM+M^{2}}{N^{2}}\text{ uniformly in }i.

It follows that

\left\|\sum_{i\in[N]}\mathbb{E}\left(Z_{i}^{2}\right)\right\|_{op}\leq N\max_{i\in[N]}\left\|\mathbb{E}\left(Z_{i}^{2}\right)\right\|_{op}\leq\frac{mM+M^{2}}{N}.

(B.2)

Let
\varepsilon=\max\left(\sqrt{M}\delta,\delta^{2}\right)
with
\delta=t\sqrt{\frac{m+M}{N}}
. By (
B.1
)-(
B.2
) and the
matrix Bernstein inequality in Lemma
B.1
(i), we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left\{\left\|\frac{1}{N}\left(A^{\prime}A-\sum_{i\in[N]}\Sigma_{i}\right)\right\|_{op}\geq\varepsilon\right\}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\left(\left\|\sum_{i\in[N]}Z_{i}\right\|_{op}\geq\varepsilon\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2T\exp\left\{-c_{1}\min\left(\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{\frac{mM+M^{2}}{N}},\frac{\varepsilon}{\frac{m+M}{N}}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\leq 2T\exp\left\{-c_{1}\min\left(\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{M},\varepsilon\right)\frac{N}{m+M}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq 2T\exp\left\{-\frac{c_{1}\delta^{2}N}{m+M}\right\}=2T\exp\left\{-c_{1}t^{2}\right\},

for some positive constant
c_{1}
, where the third inequality is due to the
fact that

\displaystyle\min\left(\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{M},\varepsilon\right)

\displaystyle=\min\left(\max\left(\delta^{2},\delta^{4}/M\right),\max\left(\sqrt{M}\delta,\delta\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\left\{\begin{aligned} &\min\left(\delta^{2},\sqrt{M}\delta\right)=\delta^{2}\hskip 9.24994pt\text{if}\hskip 9.24994pt\delta^{2}\geq\frac{\delta^{4}}{M},\\
&\min\left(\delta^{4}/M,\delta^{2}\right)=\delta^{2},\hskip 9.24994pt\text{if}\hskip 9.24994pt\delta^{2}<\frac{\delta^{4}}{M}.\end{aligned}\right..

That is,

\left\|\frac{1}{N}A^{\prime}A-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i\in[N]}\Sigma_{i}\right\|_{op}\leq\max\left(\sqrt{M}\delta,\delta^{2}\right)

(B.3)

with probability
1-\exp\left(-c_{1}t^{2}\right)
. Combining the fact that

\left\|\Sigma_{i}\right\|\leq M
uniformly over
i
and (
B.3
), we show that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\left\|A\right\|_{op}^{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\frac{1}{N}A^{\prime}A\right\|_{op}\leq\left\|\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i\in[N]}\Sigma_{i}\right\|_{op}+\left\|\frac{1}{N}A^{\prime}A-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i\in[N]}\Sigma_{i}\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i\in[N]}\left\|\Sigma_{i}\right\|_{op}+\sqrt{M}\delta+\delta^{2}

\displaystyle\leq M+\sqrt{M}t\sqrt{\frac{m+M}{N}}+t^{2}\frac{m+M}{N}\leq\left(\sqrt{M}+t\sqrt{\frac{m+M}{N}}\right)^{2}.

It follows that
\left\|A\right\|_{op}\leq\sqrt{NM}+t\sqrt{m+M}.

Lemma B.3

Recall
a_{it}=\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}
and
a=\{a_{it}\}\in\mathbb{R}^{N\times T}.
Under Assumption
1
, we have
\left\|X_{j}\odot a\right\|_{op}=O_{p}\left(\sqrt{N}+\sqrt{T\log T}\right)

\forall j\in[p]
and
\left\|a\right\|_{op}=O_{p}\left(\sqrt{N}+\sqrt{T\log T}\right)
.

Proof.
We focus on
\left\|X_{j}\odot a\right\|_{op}
as the result for
||a||_{op}
can be derived in the same manner. We first note that,
conditional
\mathscr{D}
, the
i
-th row of
X_{j}\odot a
only depends on
\{e_{it},\epsilon_{it}\}_{t\in[T]}
, which are independent across
i
. Therefore
X_{j}\odot a
has independent rows, denoted as
A_{i}=X_{j,i}\odot a_{i}
, given
\mathscr{D}
, where
X_{j,i}
and
a_{i}

being the
i
-th row of matrix
X_{j}
and
a
, respectively. In addition,
for the
t
-th element of
A_{i}
, we have

\mathbb{E}\left[X_{j,it}a_{it}\bigg|\mathscr{D}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left\{X_{j,it}\mathbb{E}\left[a_{it}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e}\right]\bigg|\mathscr{D}\right\}=0,

where the second equality holds by Assumption
1
(ii) and the fact
that given
\mathscr{D}_{e}
,
X_{j,it}
is known. Therefore, In order to
apply Lemma
B.1
, conditionally on
\mathscr{D}
, we only
need to upper bound
\left\|A_{i}\right\|_{2}
and
\mathbb{E}\left[A_{i}A_{i}^{\prime}\bigg|\mathscr{D}\right]
.

First, under Assumption
1
(iv), we have
\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left(X_{j,it}a_{it}\right)^{2}\leq\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}X_{j,it}^{2}\leq c_{2}\hskip 9.24994pt
a.s. for some positive constant
c_{2}
, which
implies

||A_{i}||_{2}=\left\|X_{j,i}\odot a_{i}\right\|_{2}\leq c_{2}\sqrt{T}\hskip 9.24994pt\text{a.s.}

(B.4)

Second, let
\Sigma_{i}=\mathbb{E}\left\{\left[\left(X_{j,i}\odot a_{i}\right)\left(X_{j,i}\odot a_{i}\right)^{\prime}\right]\bigg|\mathscr{D}\right\}
with
\left(t,s\right)^{th}
element being
\mathbb{E}\left(X_{j,it}X_{j,is}a_{it}a_{is}\bigg|\mathscr{D}\right).
Recall that
\left\|\cdot\right\|_{1}
and
\left\|\cdot\right\|_{\infty}
are matrix norms induced by 1- and
\infty-
norms, i.e.,

\left\|\Sigma_{i}\right\|_{1}=\max_{s\in[T]}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left|\mathbb{E}\left(X_{j,it}X_{j,is}a_{it}a_{is}\bigg|\mathscr{D}\right)\right|,\hskip 9.24994pt\left\|\Sigma_{i}\right\|_{\infty}=\max_{t\in[T]}\sum_{s\in[T]}\left|\mathbb{E}\left(X_{j,it}X_{j,is}a_{it}a_{is}\bigg|\mathscr{D}\right)\right|.

By Davydov’s inequality for conditional strong mixing sequence, we can show
that

\displaystyle\max_{s\in[T]}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left|\mathbb{E}\left(X_{j,it}X_{j,is}a_{it}a_{is}\bigg|\mathscr{D}\right)\right|

\displaystyle=\max_{s\in[T]}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left|Cov\left(X_{j,it}a_{it},X_{j,is}a_{is}\bigg|\mathscr{D}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\max_{s\in[T]}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\{\mathbb{E}\left[\left|X_{j,it}a_{it}\right|^{q}\bigg|\mathscr{D}\right]\right\}^{1/q}\left\{\mathbb{E}\left[\left|X_{j,is}a_{is}\}\right|^{q}\bigg|\mathscr{D}\right]\right\}^{1/q}\times\alpha\left(t-s\right)^{\left(q-2\right)/q}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i\in[N],t\in[T]}\left\{\mathbb{E}\left[\left|X_{j,it}^{q}\right|\bigg|\mathscr{D}\right]\right\}^{2/q}\max_{s\in[T]}\sum_{t\in[T]}\alpha\left(t-s\right)^{\left(q-2\right)/q}

\displaystyle\leq c_{3}~a.s.,

where

c_{3}
is a positive constant which does not depend on
i
and the last
line is by Assumption
1
(iii) and
1
(iv). Similarly, we
have
\max_{t\in[T]}\sum_{s\in[T]}\left|\mathbb{E}\left(X_{j,it}X_{j,is}a_{it}a_{is}\bigg|\mathscr{D}\right)\right|\leq c_{3}\hskip 9.24994pt
a.s. Then by Corollary 2.3.2 in
Golub and Van Loan, 1996
, we have

\max_{i\in[N]}\left\|\Sigma_{i}\right\|_{op}\leq\sqrt{\left\|\Sigma_{i}\right\|_{1}\left\|\Sigma_{i}\right\|_{\infty}}\leq c_{3}\hskip 9.24994pt\text{a.s.}

(B.5)

Combining (
B.1
), (
B.2
), and Lemma
B.1

with
t=\sqrt{\log T}
, we obtain the desired result.

Recall that
\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}=\Theta_{j}-\Theta_{j}^{0}
for any
\Theta_{j}
and define

\mathcal{R}(C_{1}):=\left\{\{\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\}_{j=0}^{p}:\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\mathcal{P}_{j}^{\bot}(\Delta_{\Theta_{j}})\right\|_{\ast}\leq C_{1}\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\mathcal{P}_{j}(\Delta_{\Theta_{j}})\right\|_{\ast}\right\}.

Lemma B.4

Suppose Assumptions
1
-
3
hold.
Then
\{\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j}}\}_{j=0}^{p}\in\mathcal{R}(3)
w.p.a.1.

Proof.
Define
\mathbb{Q}_{\tau}\left(\left\{\Theta_{j}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\right)=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\rho_{\tau}\left(Y_{it}-\Theta_{0,it}-\sum_{j=1}^{p}X_{j,it}\Theta_{j,it}\right)
for generic
\left\{\Theta_{j}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}
. By the definition of the nuclear norm
estimator in (
2.9
), we have

\mathbb{Q}_{\tau}\left(\left\{\Theta_{j}^{0}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\right)-\mathbb{Q}_{\tau}\left(\left\{\tilde{\Theta}_{j}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\right)+\sum_{j=0}^{p}\nu_{j}\left[\left\|\Theta_{j}^{0}(\tau)\right\|_{\ast}-\left\|\tilde{\Theta}_{j}(\tau)\right\|_{\ast}\right]\geq 0.

(B.6)

In addition, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{Q}_{\tau}\left(\left\{\Theta_{j}^{0}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\right)-\mathbb{Q}_{\tau}\left(\left\{\tilde{\Theta}_{j}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\right)

\displaystyle=-\left\{\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}a_{it}\left(\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{0,it}}+\sum_{j=1}^{p}X_{j,it}\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j,it}}\right)+\int_{0}^{\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{0,it}}+\sum_{j=1}^{p}X_{j,it}\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j,it}}}\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq s\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}ds\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\left|\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}a_{it}\left(\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{0,it}}+\sum_{j=1}^{p}X_{j,it}\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j,it}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{p}\left|\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}a_{it}X_{j,it}\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j,it}}\right|+\left|\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}a_{it}\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{0,it}}\right|

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{p}\frac{1}{NT}\left|tr\left[\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j}}^{\prime}\left(X_{j}\odot a\right)\right]\right|+\frac{1}{NT}\left|tr\left(\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{0}}^{\prime}a\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{p}\frac{1}{NT}\left\|\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{\ast}\left\|X_{j}\odot a\right\|_{op}+\frac{1}{NT}\left\|\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{0}}\right\|_{\ast}\left\|a\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle\leq c_{4}\sum_{j=0}^{p}\frac{(\sqrt{N}\vee\sqrt{T\log T})}{NT}\left\|\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{\ast},\hskip 9.24994pt\text{w.p.a.1},

(B.7)

where the first equality holds by Knight’s identity in
Knight, 1998
which states that

\rho_{\tau}(u-v)-\rho_{\tau}(u)=v(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{u\leq 0\right\})+\int_{0}^{v}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{u\leq s\right\}-\mathbf{1}\{u\leq 0\}\right)ds,

(B.8)

the first inequality is due to the fact that the second term in the bracket
of the second line is non-negative, the second inequality holds by triangle
inequality, the third inequality is by the fact that
tr(AB)\leq||A||_{op}||B||_{\ast}
, and the last inequality holds by Lemma
B.3
.

Combining (
B.6
) and (
B.7
), w.p.a.1, we have

0\leq c_{4}\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\{\frac{(\sqrt{N}\vee\sqrt{T\log T})}{NT}||\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j}}||_{\ast}+\nu_{j}\left(\left\|\Theta_{j}^{0}\right\|_{\ast}-\left\|\tilde{\Theta}_{j}\right\|_{\ast}\right)\right\}.

(B.9)

Besides, we can show that

\displaystyle\left\|\tilde{\Theta}_{j}\right\|_{\ast}

\displaystyle=\left\|\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j}}+\Theta_{j}^{0}\right\|_{\ast}=\left\|\Theta_{j}^{0}+\mathcal{P}_{j}^{\bot}(\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j}})+\mathcal{P}_{j}(\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j}})\right\|_{\ast}

\displaystyle\geq\left\|\Theta_{j}^{0}+\mathcal{P}_{j}^{\bot}(\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j}})\right\|_{\ast}-\left\|\mathcal{P}_{j}(\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j}})\right\|_{\ast}=\left\|\Theta_{j}^{0}\right\|_{\ast}+\left\|\mathcal{P}_{j}^{\bot}(\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j}})\right\|_{\ast}-\left\|\mathcal{P}_{j}(\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j}})\right\|_{\ast}

(B.10)

where the second equality holds by Lemma D.2(i) in
Chernozhukov et al., 2019
, the first inequality holds by triangle
inequality, and the last equality is by the construction of the linear space

\mathcal{P}_{j}^{\bot}
and
\mathcal{P}_{j}
. Then combining (
B.9
) and (
B.10
), we obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{p}\nu_{j}\left\{\left\|\mathcal{P}_{j}^{\bot}\left(\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j}}\right)\right\|_{\ast}-\left\|\mathcal{P}_{j}\left(\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j}}\right)\right\|_{\ast}\right\}\leq c_{4}\sum_{j=0}^{p}\frac{(\sqrt{N}\vee\sqrt{T\log T})}{NT}\left\|\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{\ast}

\displaystyle=c_{4}\sum_{j=0}^{p}\frac{(\sqrt{N}\vee\sqrt{T\log T})}{NT}\left\{\left\|\mathcal{P}_{j}\left(\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j}}\right)\right\|_{\ast}+\left\|\mathcal{P}_{j}^{\bot}\left(\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j}}\right)\right\|_{\ast}\right\},~\text{w.p.a.1}

By setting
\nu_{j}=\frac{2c_{4}(\sqrt{N}\vee\sqrt{T\log T})}{NT}
, we
obtain
\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\mathcal{P}_{j}^{\bot}\left(\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j}}\right)\right\|_{\ast}\leq 3\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\mathcal{P}_{j}\left(\tilde{\Delta}_{\Theta_{j}}\right)\right\|_{\ast}~
w.p.a.1
.

Recall
\mathscr{G}_{i,t-1}
is the
\sigma
-field generated by
\{V_{j}^{0}\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}
,
\{W_{j}^{0}\}_{j\in[p]}
,
\{e_{ls}\}_{l\leq i-1,s\in[T]}
,
\{e_{is}\}_{s\leq t}
,
\{\epsilon_{ls}\}_{l\leq i-1,s\in[T]}
, and
\{\epsilon_{is}\}_{s\leq t-1}

and
\mathsf{F}_{it}(\cdot)
and
\mathsf{f}_{it}(\cdot)
are the
conditional CDF and PDF of
\epsilon_{it}
given
\mathscr{G}_{i,t-1}
,
respectively. Specifically, we note that
(\{X_{j,it}\}_{j\in[p]},\{\Theta_{j,it}^{0}\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}})
are measurable w.r.t.
\mathscr{G}_{i,t-1}
.

Lemma B.5

For all
u_{1},u_{2}\in\mathbb{R}
and all
c_{5}\in(0,1]
, we have

(i)

\int_{0}^{u_{2}}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{u_{1}\leq z\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{u_{1}\leq 0\right\}\right)dz\geq\int_{0}^{c_{5}u_{2}}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{u_{1}\leq z\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{u_{1}\leq 0\right\}\right)dz\geq 0
,

(ii)

\int_{0}^{u_{2}}\left\{\mathsf{F}_{it}\left(u_{1}+z\right)-\mathsf{F}_{it}\left(u_{1}\right)\right\}dz\geq\int_{0}^{c_{5}u_{2}}\left\{\mathsf{F}_{it}\left(u_{1}+z\right)-\mathsf{F}_{it}\left(u_{1}\right)\right\}dz\geq 0
.

Proof.
Statement (i) is just
Feng, 2019
. To prove
statement (ii), notice that if
u_{2}\geq 0
, then
z\geq 0
and
\mathsf{F}_{it}\left(u_{1}+z\right)-\mathsf{F}_{it}\left(u_{1}\right)\geq 0
for all

z\in[0,u_{2}]
, which leads to the existence of the second inequality
naturally:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{u_{2}}\left\{\mathsf{F}_{it}\left(u_{1}+z\right)-\mathsf{F}_{it}\left(u_{1}\right)\right\}dz-\int_{0}^{c_{5}u_{2}}\left\{\mathsf{F}_{it}\left(u_{1}+z\right)-\mathsf{F}_{it}\left(u_{1}\right)\right\}dz

\displaystyle=\int_{c_{5}u_{2}}^{u_{2}}\left\{\mathsf{F}_{it}\left(u_{1}+z\right)-\mathsf{F}_{it}\left(u_{1}\right)\right\}dz\geq 0.

On the other hand, if
u_{2}<0
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{u_{2}}\left\{\mathsf{F}_{it}\left(u_{1}+z\right)-\mathsf{F}_{it}\left(u_{1}\right)\right\}dz-\int_{0}^{c_{5}u_{2}}\left\{\mathsf{F}_{it}\left(u_{1}+z\right)-\mathsf{F}_{it}\left(u_{1}\right)\right\}dz

\displaystyle=\int_{u_{2}}^{0}\left\{\mathsf{F}_{it}\left(u_{1}\right)-\mathsf{F}_{it}\left(u_{1}+z\right)\right\}dz-\int_{c_{5}u_{2}}^{0}\left\{\mathsf{F}_{it}\left(u_{1}\right)-\mathsf{F}_{it}\left(u_{1}+z\right)\right\}dz

\displaystyle=\int_{u_{2}}^{c_{5}u_{2}}\left\{\mathsf{F}_{it}\left(u_{1}\right)5\mathsf{F}_{it}\left(u_{1}+z\right)\right\}dz\geq 0,

where the last inequality holds as the same reason for
u_{2}\geq 0
case.

Lemma B.6

Under Assumptions
1
-
4
,
for any
\left\{\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\in\mathcal{R}(3,C_{2})
such that
||\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}||_{\max}\leq M
for some
constant
M>0
, we have

Q_{\tau}\left(\left\{\Theta_{j}^{0}+\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\right)-Q_{\tau}\left(\left\{\Theta_{j}^{0}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\right)\geq\frac{c_{7}C_{3}}{NT\xi_{N}^{2}}\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{F}^{2}-\frac{c_{7}C_{4}}{NT\xi_{N}^{2}}\left(N+T\right)~w.p.a.1,

where
Q_{\tau}(\left\{\Theta_{j}\right\}_{j=0}^{p})=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\rho_{\tau}\left(Y_{it}-\Theta_{0,it}-\sum_{j=1}^{p}X_{j,it}\Theta_{j,it}\right)\bigg|\mathscr{G}_{i,t-1}\right]
,
c_{7}=\frac{\underline{\mathsf{f}}c_{6}^{2}}{4}
with
c_{6}
being a positive constant between 0 and 1.

Proof.
We can choose a sufficiently large constant
M
such that
c_{6}:=\frac{3\underline{\mathsf{f}}}{2\bar{\mathsf{f}}^{\prime}M(1+p)}\in\left(0,1\right]
. Then we have

\displaystyle Q_{\tau}\left(\left\{\Theta_{j}^{0}+\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\right)-Q_{\tau}\left(\left\{\Theta_{j}^{0}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left\{\rho_{\tau}\left(Y_{it}-\Theta_{0,it}-\sum_{j=1}^{p}X_{j,it}\Theta_{j,it}\right)-\rho_{\tau}\left(Y_{it}-\Theta_{0,it}^{0}-\sum_{j=1}^{p}X_{j,it}\Theta_{j,it}^{0}\right)\bigg|\mathscr{G}_{i,t-1}\right\}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left\{\left(\Delta_{\Theta_{0,it}}+\sum_{j=1}^{p}X_{j,it}\Delta_{\Theta_{j,it}}\right)\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)\bigg|\mathscr{G}_{i,t-1}\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left\{\int_{0}^{\Delta_{\Theta_{0,it}}+\sum_{j=1}^{p}X_{j,it}\Delta_{\Theta_{j,it}}}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq s\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)ds\bigg|\mathscr{G}_{i,t-1}\right\}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left\{\int_{0}^{\Delta_{\Theta_{0,it}}+\sum_{j=1}^{p}X_{j,it}\Delta_{\Theta_{j,it}}}\left(\mathbf{1}_{\epsilon_{it}\leq s}-\mathbf{1}_{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0}\right)ds\bigg|\mathscr{G}_{i,t-1}\right\}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left\{\int_{0}^{c_{6}\xi_{N}^{-1}\left(\Delta_{\Theta_{0,it}}+\sum_{j=1}^{p}X_{j,it}\Delta_{\Theta_{j,it}}\right)}\left(\mathbf{1}_{\epsilon_{it}\leq s}-\mathbf{1}_{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0}\right)ds\bigg|\mathscr{G}_{i,t-1}\right\}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\int_{0}^{c_{6}\xi_{N}^{-1}\left(\Delta_{\Theta_{0,it}}+\sum_{j=1}^{p}X_{j,it}\Delta_{\Theta_{j,it}}\right)}\left[\mathsf{F}_{it}\left(s\right)-\mathsf{F}_{it}\left(0\right)\right]ds

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\int_{0}^{c_{6}\xi_{N}^{-1}\left(\Delta_{\Theta_{0,it}}+\sum_{j=1}^{0}X_{j,it}\Delta_{\Theta_{j,it}}\right)}\left[s\mathsf{f}_{it}\left(0\right)+\frac{s^{2}}{2}\mathsf{f}_{it}^{\prime}\left(\tilde{s}\right)\right]ds

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\underline{\mathsf{f}}c_{6}^{2}}{2NT\xi_{N}^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\Delta_{\Theta_{0,it}}+\sum_{j=1}^{p}X_{j,it}\Delta_{\Theta_{j,it}}\right)^{2}-\frac{\bar{\mathsf{f}}^{\prime}c_{6}^{3}}{6NT\xi_{N}^{3}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left|\Delta_{\Theta_{0,it}}+\sum_{j=1}^{p}X_{j,it}\Delta_{\Theta_{j,it}}\right|^{3}

\displaystyle=\frac{\underline{\mathsf{f}}c_{6}^{2}}{4NT\xi_{N}^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\Delta_{\Theta_{0,it}}+\sum_{j=1}^{p}X_{j,it}\Delta_{\Theta_{j,it}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{NT\xi_{N}^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{\frac{\underline{\mathsf{f}}c_{6}^{2}}{4}\left(\Delta_{\Theta_{0,it}}+\sum_{j=1}^{p}X_{j,it}\Delta_{\Theta_{j,it}}\right)^{2}\left(1-\frac{2c_{6}\bar{\mathsf{f}}^{\prime}}{3\underline{\mathsf{f}}\xi_{N}}\left|\Delta_{\Theta_{0,it}}+\sum_{j=1}^{p}X_{j,it}\Delta_{\Theta_{j,it}}\right|\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\underline{\mathsf{f}}c_{6}^{2}}{4NT\xi_{N}^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{\Delta_{\Theta_{0,it}}+\sum_{j=1}^{p}X_{j,it}\Delta_{\Theta_{j,it}}\right\}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{\underline{\mathsf{f}}c_{6}^{2}}{4NT\xi_{N}^{2}}\left\|\Delta_{\Theta_{0}}+\sum_{j=1}^{p}X_{j}\odot\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\underline{\mathsf{f}}c_{6}^{2}}{4NT\xi_{N}^{2}}\left\{C_{3}\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{F}^{2}-C_{4}\left(N+T\right)\right\}\hskip 9.24994pt\text{w.p.a.1}

\displaystyle=\frac{c_{7}C_{3}}{NT\xi_{N}^{2}}\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{F}^{2}-\frac{c_{7}C_{4}}{NT\xi_{N}^{2}}\left(N+T\right)

(B.11)

where the second equality is by (
B.8
), the first inequality is by
Lemma
B.5
and the fact that
c_{6}/\xi_{N}\leq 1
, the fifth
equality is by the mean-value theorem, the third inequality is by the fact
that

1-\frac{2c_{6}\bar{\mathsf{f}}^{\prime}}{3\underline{\mathsf{f}}\xi_{N}}\left|\Delta_{\Theta_{0,it}}+\sum_{j=1}^{p}X_{j,it}\Delta_{\Theta_{j,it}}\right|\geq 1-\frac{2c_{6}\bar{\mathsf{f}}^{\prime}}{3\underline{\mathsf{f}}\xi_{N}}M(1+p)\xi_{N}\geq 0,

and the fourth inequality holds under Assumption
4
. This concludes
the proof.

Lemma B.7

Under Assumptions
1
–
4
, for any
\left\{\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\in\mathcal{R}(3,C_{2})
, we have
\left\|\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{\ast}\leq c_{8}\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{F}\,\,\,\forall j\in\left\{0,\cdots,p\right\}
where
c_{8}=4\sqrt{2\bar{K}}
.

Proof.
For
\forall j\in\left\{0,\cdots,p\right\}
, we obtain that

\displaystyle\left\|\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{\ast}

\displaystyle=\left\|\mathcal{P}_{j}(\Delta_{\Theta_{j}})\right\|_{\ast}+\left\|\mathcal{P}_{j}^{\bot}(\Delta_{\Theta_{j}})\right\|_{\ast}\leq\left\|\mathcal{P}_{j}(\Delta_{\Theta_{j}})\right\|_{\ast}+3\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\mathcal{P}_{j}(\Delta_{\Theta_{j}})\right\|_{\ast}

\displaystyle\leq 4\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\mathcal{P}_{j}(\Delta_{\Theta_{j}})\right\|_{\ast}\leq 4\sum_{j=0}^{p}\sqrt{2K_{j}}\left\|\mathcal{P}_{j}\left(\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right)\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq 4\sqrt{2\bar{K}}\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{F}:=c_{8}\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{F},

where the first equality is by

Chernozhukov et al., 2019
, the first inequality is by the
definition of
\mathcal{R}(C_{1},C_{2})
, and the last two inequalities
follow the facts that
\left\|A\right\|_{\ast}\leq\sqrt{\text{rank}(A)}\left\|A\right\|_{F}
for any matrix
A\in\mathbb{R}^{N\times T}

and
\text{rank}\left(\mathcal{P}_{j}\left(\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right)\right)\leq 2K_{j}
, which hold by

Chernozhukov et al., 2019
.

Let
\mathcal{Z}
be a separable metric space,
\left\{Z_{1},\cdots,Z_{n}\right\}
be a sequence of random variables in
\mathcal{Z}
adapted to the filtration
\{\mathscr{F}_{t}\}_{t\in[n]}
,
\mathcal{F}=\left\{f:\mathcal{Z}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}\right\}
be a set of bounded
real valued functions on
\mathbb{R}
, and
u_{1},\cdots,u_{n}
be i.i.d.
Rademacher random variables. Here we allow the dependence of sequence
\left\{Z_{1},\cdots,Z_{n}\right\}
. Similarly as in
Rakhlin et al., 2015
, we define a
\mathcal{Z}
-valued tree
\mathbf{z}

of depth
n
with the sequence
\left(\mathbf{z}_{1},\cdots,\mathbf{z}_{n}\right)
such that
\mathbf{z}_{t}:\left\{u_{1},\cdots,u_{t-1}\right\}\rightarrow\mathcal{Z}
. For simplicity, we denote as
\mathbf{z}_{t}\left(u\right)=\mathbf{z}_{t}\left(u_{1},\cdots,u_{t-1}\right)
and
\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\cdot\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\cdot\big|\mathscr{F}_{t}\right]
for short. Also, denote
u_{1:t}:=\left(u_{1},\cdots,u_{t}\right)
and similarly for
Z_{1:t}
.

Lemma B.8

Let
\mathcal{F}
be a class of
functions. For any
\alpha>0
, it holds that

\beta_{n}\mathbb{P}\left\{\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=1}^{n}\left(f\left(Z_{t}\right)-\mathbb{E}_{t-1}\left[f\left(Z_{t}\right)\right]\right)\right|>\alpha\right\}\leq 2\sup_{\mathbf{z}}\mathbb{P}\left\{\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=1}^{n}u_{t}f\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}\left(u\right)\right)\right|>\frac{\alpha}{4}\right\}.

where
\beta_{n}\geq 1-\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\frac{4\sum_{t=1}^{n}Var\left(f\left(Z_{t}\right)\big|\mathscr{F}_{t-1}\right)}{n^{2}\alpha^{2}}

and the outer supremum is taken over all
\mathcal{Z}
-valued tree of depth
n
.
1
1

1

This means the supremum is taken over all
\mathbf{z}=\{\mathbf{z}_{t}(\cdot)\}_{t\in[n]}
.

Proof.
Let
Z_{1:n}^{\prime}
be a decoupled sequence tangent to
Z_{1:n}
. For
the sequence of random variables
\left\{Z_{t}:t\in[n]\right\}
adapted
to the filtration
\left\{\mathscr{F}_{t}:t\in[n]\right\}
, the sequence
Z_{1:n}^{\prime}=\left\{Z_{t}^{*},t\in[n]\right\}
is said to be a
decoupled sequence tangent to
\left\{Z_{t}:t\in[n]\right\}
if for each
t\in[n]
,
Z_{t}^{*}
is generated from the conditional distribution of
Z_{t}
given
\mathscr{F}_{t-1}
and independent of everything else. This
means the sequence
Z_{1:n}^{\prime}
is conditionally independent given
\mathscr{F}_{n}
and for any measurable function
f
of
Z_{t}^{*}
,

\mathbb{E}(f(Z_{t}^{*})|\mathscr{F}_{n})=\mathbb{E}(f(Z_{t}^{*})|\mathscr{F}_{t-1})~a.s.

(B.12)

For
\forall f\in\mathcal{F}
, with Chebyshev’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{1}{n}\left|\sum_{t=1}^{n}f\left(Z_{t}^{\ast}\right)-\mathbb{E}_{t-1}\left[f\left(Z_{t}^{\ast}\right)\right]\right|>\alpha/2\bigg|\mathscr{F}_{n}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\mathbb{E}\left\{\left(\sum_{t=1}^{n}f\left(Z_{t}^{\ast}\right)-\mathbb{E}_{t-1}\left[f\left(Z_{t}^{\ast}\right)\right]\right)^{2}\bigg|\mathscr{F}_{n}\right\}}{n^{2}\alpha^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{4\sum_{t=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left\{\left(f\left(Z_{t}^{\ast}\right)-\mathbb{E}_{t-1}\left[f\left(Z_{t}^{\ast}\right)\right]\right)^{2}\bigg|\mathscr{F}_{n}\right\}}{n^{2}\alpha^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{4\sum_{t=1}^{n}Var\left(f\left(Z_{t}\right)\big|\mathscr{F}_{t-1}\right)}{n^{2}\alpha^{2}},

where the first equality holds by the fact that, given
\mathscr{F}_{n}
,
\{Z_{t}^{\ast}\}_{t\in[n]}
are independent and the last equality
holds by (
B.12
). This implies

\displaystyle\beta_{n}

\displaystyle:=\inf_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{1}{n}\left|\sum_{t=1}^{n}f\left(Z_{t}^{\ast}\right)-\mathbb{E}_{t-1}\left[f\left(Z_{t}^{\ast}\right)\right]\right|\leq\alpha/2\bigg|\mathscr{F}_{n}\right)

\displaystyle=1-\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{1}{n}\left|\sum_{t=1}^{n}f\left(Z_{t}^{\ast}\right)-\mathbb{E}_{t-1}\left[f\left(Z_{t}^{\ast}\right)\right]\right|>\alpha/2\bigg|\mathscr{F}_{n}\right)

\displaystyle\geq 1-\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\frac{4\sum_{t=1}^{n}Var\left(f\left(Z_{t}\right)\big|\mathscr{F}_{t-1}\right)}{n^{2}\alpha^{2}}.

Let function
f^{\ast}
be the function that maximizes
