\frac{1}{n}\left|\sum_{t=1}^{n}\left[f\left(Z_{t}^{\ast}\right)-\mathbb{E}_{t-1}\left(f\left(Z_{t}^{\ast}\right)\right)\right]\right|

condition on
\mathscr{F}_{n}
, and define the event
A_{1}=\left\{\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\frac{1}{n}\left|\sum_{t=1}^{n}\left[f\left(Z_{t}\right)-\mathbb{E}_{t-1}\left(f\left(Z_{t}\right)\right)\right]\right|>\alpha\right\}
. Then we obtain that

\beta_{n}\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{1}{n}\left|\sum_{t=1}^{n}f^{\ast}\left(Z_{t}^{\ast}\right)-\mathbb{E}_{t-1}\left[f^{\ast}\left(Z_{t}\right)\right]\right|\leq\alpha/2\bigg|\mathscr{F}_{n}\right),

where the inequality follows by the definition of
\beta_{n}
and the fact
that
\mathbb{E}_{t-1}\left[f^{\ast}\left(Z_{t}^{\ast}\right)\right]=\mathbb{E}_{t-1}\left[f^{\ast}\left(Z_{t}\right)\right]
. As
A_{1}\in\mathscr{F}_{n}
, we have

\beta_{n}\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{1}{n}\left|\sum_{t=1}^{n}f^{\ast}\left(Z_{t}^{\ast}\right)-\mathbb{E}_{t-1}\left[f^{\ast}\left(Z_{t}^{\ast}\right)\right]\right|\leq\alpha/2\bigg|A_{1}\right).

It follows that

\displaystyle\beta_{n}\mathbb{P}\left\{\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=1}^{n}\left(f\left(Z_{t}\right)-\mathbb{E}_{t-1}\left[f\left(Z_{t}\right)\right]\right)\right|>\alpha\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{1}{n}\left|\sum_{t=1}^{n}f^{\ast}\left(Z_{t}^{\ast}\right)-\mathbb{E}_{t-1}\left[f^{\ast}\left(Z_{t}^{\ast}\right)\right]\right|\leq\alpha/2\bigg|A_{1}\right)\mathbb{P}\left(A_{1}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\left(\left\{\frac{1}{n}\left|\sum_{t=1}^{n}f^{\ast}\left(Z_{t}^{\ast}\right)-\mathbb{E}_{t-1}\left[f^{\ast}\left(Z_{t}^{\ast}\right)\right]\right|\leq\alpha/2\right\}\bigcup A_{1}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{1}{n}\left|\sum_{t=1}^{n}f^{\ast}\left(Z_{t}\right)-f^{\ast}\left(Z_{t}^{\ast}\right)\right|\geq\alpha/2\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\left|\sum_{t=1}^{n}f\left(Z_{t}\right)-f\left(Z_{t}^{\ast}\right)\right|\geq\alpha/2\right),

(B.13)

where the second inequality holds by the implication rule. Let
\phi\left(\cdot\right)=\mathbf{1}\left\{\cdot>n\alpha/2\right\}.
By Lemma 18 in

Rakhlin et al., 2015
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\left|\sum_{t=1}^{n}f\left(Z_{t}\right)-f\left(Z_{t}^{\ast}\right)\right|\geq\alpha/2\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\mathbf{1}\left\{\frac{1}{n}\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\left|\sum_{t=1}^{n}f\left(Z_{t}\right)-f\left(Z_{t}^{\ast}\right)\right|\geq\alpha/2\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{z_{1},z_{1}^{\prime}}\mathbb{E}_{u_{1}}\cdots\sup_{z_{n},z_{n}^{\prime}}\mathbb{E}_{u_{n}}\mathbf{1}\left\{\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\left|\sum_{t=1}^{n}u_{t}\left[f\left(z_{t}\right)-f\left(Z_{t}^{\ast}\right)\right]\right|\geq n\alpha/2\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{z_{1},z_{1}^{\prime}}\mathbb{E}_{u_{1}}\cdots\sup_{z_{n},z_{n}^{\prime}}\mathbb{E}_{u_{n}}\mathbf{1}\left\{\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\left|\sum_{t=1}^{n}u_{t}f\left(z_{t}\right)\right|\geq n\alpha/4\right\}+\sup_{z_{1},z_{1}^{\prime}}\mathbb{E}_{u_{1}}\cdots\sup_{z_{n},z_{n}^{\prime}}\mathbb{E}_{u_{n}}\mathbf{1}\left\{\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\left|\sum_{t=1}^{n}u_{t}f\left(Z_{t}^{\ast}\right)\right|\geq n\alpha/4\right\}

\displaystyle=2\sup_{z_{1},z_{1}^{\prime}}\mathbb{E}_{u_{1}}\cdots\sup_{z_{n},z_{n}^{\prime}}\mathbb{E}_{u_{n}}\mathbf{1}\left\{\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\left|\sum_{t=1}^{n}u_{t}f\left(z_{t}\right)\right|\geq n\alpha/4\right\}

\displaystyle=2\sup_{\mathbf{z}}\mathbb{P}\left\{\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=1}^{n}u_{t}f\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}\left(u\right)\right)\right|>\frac{\alpha}{4}\right\},

(B.14)

where the standard text
z_{t}
and
Z_{t}^{\ast}
are
\mathcal{Z}
-valued, the bold text
\mathbf{z}_{t}=\mathbf{z}_{t}(u)
is the
t
-th root
of a tree
\mathbf{z}(\cdot)
(i.e., a function of
u_{1:t-1}
), and the
outer supremum in the last line is taken over all
\mathcal{Z}
-valued tree
of depth
n
. Combining (
B.1
) and (
B.14
), we can conclude
that

\beta_{n}\mathbb{P}\left\{\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=1}^{n}\left(f\left(Z_{t}\right)-\mathbb{E}_{t-1}\left[f\left(Z_{t}\right)\right]\right)\right|>\alpha\right\}\leq 2\sup_{\mathbf{z}}\mathbb{P}\left\{\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=1}^{n}u_{t}f\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}\left(u\right)\right)\right|>\frac{\alpha}{4}\right\}.

The next lemma is an extension of the contraction principle, i.e.,
Ledoux and Talagrand, 1991
, to the case with sequential
symmetrization.

Lemma B.9

Let function
F:\mathbb{R}_{+}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}_{+}
be convex and increasing and
\phi_{t}:\mathbb{R}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
be contractions such that
\phi_{t}\left(0\right)=0
,
\mathbf{z}_{t}
is the
t
-th root of a tree (
\mathbf{z}
) which
depends on
\left\{u_{1},\cdots,u_{t-1}\right\}
. Then we have

\mathbb{E}F\left\{\frac{1}{2}\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\left|\sum_{t=1}^{n}u_{t}\phi_{t}\left(f\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}(u)\right)\right)\right|\right\}\leq\mathbb{E}F\left\{\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\left|\sum_{t=1}^{n}u_{t}f\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}(u)\right)\right|\right\}.

Proof.
We first consider the statement without the absolute value. Let function
G:\mathbb{R}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
be convex and increasing. We observe that

\mathbb{E}G\left\{\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{t=1}^{n}u_{t}\phi_{t}\left(f\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}(u)\right)\right)\right\}=\mathbb{E}\left\{\mathbb{E}\left[G\left\{\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{t=1}^{n}u_{t}\phi_{t}\left(f\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}(u)\right)\right)\right\}\bigg|u_{1:n-1}\right]\right\}

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\{G\left(\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{t=1}^{n}u_{t}\phi_{t}\left(f\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}(u)\right)\right)\bigg|u_{1:n-1}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left\{G\left(\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{t=1}^{n-1}u_{t}\phi_{t}\left(f\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}(u)\right)\right)+u_{n}\phi_{n}\left(f\left(\mathbf{z}_{n}(u)\right)\right)\bigg|u_{1:n-1}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left\{G\left(\sup_{k_{1},k_{2}\in\mathcal{K}}(k_{1}+u_{n}\phi_{n}\left(k_{2}\right))\right)\bigg|u_{1:n-1}\right\},

where
k_{1}=\sum_{t=1}^{n-1}u_{t}\phi_{t}\left(f\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}(u)\right)\right)
,
k_{2}=f\left(\mathbf{z}_{n}(u)\right)
, and
\mathcal{K}=\{(k_{1},k_{2}):f\in\mathcal{F}\}\subset\mathbb{R}^{2}
. We
also note that
k_{1}
and
k_{2}
only depend on
u_{1:n-1}
and is
independent of
u_{n}
. The proof in

Ledoux and Talagrand, 1989
shows

\mathbb{E}G\left\{\sup_{k_{1},k_{2}\in\mathcal{K}}k_{1}+u_{2}\phi_{2}\left(k_{2}\right)\right\}\leq\mathbb{E}G\left\{\sup_{k_{1},k_{2}\in\mathcal{K}}k_{1}+u_{2}k_{2}\right\},

which implies

\mathbb{E}\left\{G\left(\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{t=1}^{n}u_{t}\phi_{t}\left(f\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}(u)\right)\right)\bigg|u_{1:n-1}\right)\right\}\leq\mathbb{E}\left\{G\left(\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{t=1}^{n-1}u_{t}\phi_{t}\left(f\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}(u)\right)\right)+u_{n}f\left(\mathbf{z}_{n}(u)\right)\bigg|u_{1:n-1}\right)\right\}.

Taking expectation on both sides, we have

\mathbb{E}\left\{G\left(\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{t=1}^{n}u_{t}\phi_{t}\left(f\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}(u)\right)\right)\right)\right\}\leq\mathbb{E}\left\{G\left(\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{t=1}^{n-1}u_{t}\phi_{t}\left(f\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}(u)\right)\right)+u_{n}f\left(\mathbf{z}_{n}(u)\right)\right)\right\}.

Next, let
k_{1}=\sum_{t=1}^{n-2}u_{t}\phi_{t}\left(f\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}(u)\right)\right)
,
k_{2}=f(\mathbf{z}_{n-1}(u))
and
k_{3}(u_{n-1})=u_{n}f(\mathbf{z}_{n}(u))
. We emphasize that
k_{1}
and
k_{2}
only depend on
u_{1:n-2}
and
u_{n}
while
k_{3}
also depends on
u_{n-1}
. The dependence of
(k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}(\cdot))
on
f
is made
implicit for notation simplicity. Furthermore, given the fact that
u_{n-1}

only takes values {-1,1}, we have

k_{3}(u_{n-1})=\frac{u_{n-1}+1}{2}k_{3}(1)+\frac{1-u_{n-1}}{2}k_{3}(-1)=\frac{k_{3}(1)+k_{3}(-1)}{2}+u_{n-1}\frac{k_{3}(1)-k_{3}(-1)}{2}.

Given these notation and conditioning on
(u_{1:n-2},u_{n})
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{u_{n-1}}\left\{G\left(\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{t=1}^{n-1}u_{t}\phi_{t}\left(f\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}(u)\right)\right)+u_{n}f\left(\mathbf{z}_{n}(u)\right)\right)\right\}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{u_{n-1}}\left[G\left(\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}k_{1}+u_{n-1}\phi_{n-1}(k_{2})+k_{3}(u_{n-1})\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{u_{n-1}}\left[G\left(\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\left(k_{1}+\frac{k_{3}(1)-k_{3}(-1)}{2}\right)+u_{n-1}\left(\phi_{n-1}(k_{2})+\frac{k_{3}(1)-k_{3}(-1)}{2}\right)\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{u_{n-1}}\left[G\left(\sup_{(h_{1},h_{2},h_{3})\in\mathcal{H}}h_{1}+u_{n-1}\left(\phi_{n-1}(h_{2})+h_{3}\right)\right)\right],

where
\mathbb{E}_{u_{n-1}}
means the expectation is taken conditionally on

(u_{1:n-2},u_{n})
,
h_{1}=\sum_{t=1}^{n-2}u_{t}\phi_{t}\left(f\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}(u)\right)\right)+\frac{u_{n}(f(\mathbf{z}_{n}(u_{1:n-2},1))+f(\mathbf{z}_{n}(u_{1:n-2},-1)))}{2}
,
h_{2}=k_{2}
,
h_{3}=\frac{u_{n}(f(\mathbf{z}_{n}(u_{1:n-2},1))-f(\mathbf{z}_{n}(u_{1:n-2},-1)))}{2}
, and
\mathcal{H}=((h_{1},h_{2},h_{3}):f\in\mathcal{F})\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
. Suppose
(h_{1}^{\ast},h_{2}^{\ast},h_{3}^{\ast})\in\mathcal{H}
and
(h_{1}^{\dagger},h_{2}^{\dagger},h_{3}^{\dagger})\in\mathcal{H}
achieve the supremum of

h_{1}+\left(\phi_{n-1}(h_{2})+h_{3}\right)\hskip 9.24994pt\text{and}\hskip 9.24994pth_{1}-\left(\phi_{n-1}(h_{2})+h_{3}\right),\hskip 9.24994pt\text{respectively}.

Then, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{u_{n-1}}\left[G\left(\sup_{(h_{1},h_{2},h_{3})\in\mathcal{H}}h_{1}+u_{n-1}\left(\phi_{n-1}(h_{2})+h_{3}\right)\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}G((h_{1}^{\ast}+h_{3}^{\ast})+\phi_{n-1}(h_{2}^{\ast}))+\frac{1}{2}G((h_{1}^{\dagger}-h_{3}^{\dagger})-\phi_{n-1}(h_{2}^{\dagger}))

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}G((h_{1}^{\ast}+h_{3}^{\ast})+h_{2}^{\ast})+\frac{1}{2}G((h_{1}^{\dagger}-h_{3}^{\dagger})-h_{2}^{\dagger})

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{u_{n-1}}\left[G\left(\sup_{(h_{1},h_{2},h_{3})\in\mathcal{H}}h_{1}+u_{n-1}\left(h_{2}+h_{3}\right)\right)\right],

where the first inequality is by the fact proved in the proof of
Ledoux and Talagrand, 1991
that for any
t_{1},s_{1},t_{2},s_{2}
,

\frac{1}{2}G(t_{1}+\phi_{n-1}(t_{2}))+\frac{1}{2}G(s_{1}-\phi_{n-1}(s_{2}))\leq\frac{1}{2}G(t_{1}+t_{2})+\frac{1}{2}G(s_{1}-s_{2}).

Plugging back the definition of
h_{1},h_{2},h_{3}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{u_{n-1}}\left\{G\left(\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{t=1}^{n-1}u_{t}\phi_{t}\left(f\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}(u)\right)\right)+u_{n}f\left(\mathbf{z}_{n}(u)\right)\right)\right\}\leq\mathbb{E}_{u_{n-1}}\left\{G\left(\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{t=1}^{n-2}u_{t}\phi_{t}\left(f\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}(u)\right)\right)+\sum_{t=n-1}^{n}u_{t}f\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}(u)\right)\right)\right\}.

Taking expectation on both sides, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\{G\left(\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{t=1}^{n-1}u_{t}\phi_{t}\left(f\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}(u)\right)\right)+u_{n}f\left(\mathbf{z}_{n}(u)\right)\right)\right\}\leq\mathbb{E}\left\{G\left(\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{t=1}^{n-2}u_{t}\phi_{t}\left(f\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}(u)\right)\right)+\sum_{t=n-1}^{n}u_{t}f\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}(u)\right)\right)\right\}.

We can repeat a similar argument by taking conditional expectations given
(u_{1:t-1},u_{t+1:n})
and removing
\phi_{t}
for all
t=n-2,\cdots,2
. This
leads to the result that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\{G\left(\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{t=1}^{n}u_{t}\phi_{t}\left(f\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}(u)\right)\right)\right)\right\}\leq\mathbb{E}\left\{G\left(\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{t=1}^{n}u_{t}f\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}(u)\right)\right)\right\}.

(B.15)

Next, we come back to the case with the absolute value. Note that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}F\left[\frac{1}{2}\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\left|\sum_{t=1}^{n}u_{t}\phi_{t}\left(f\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}(u)\right)\right)\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\left\{\mathbb{E}F\left[\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{n}u_{t}\phi_{t}\left(f\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}(u)\right)\right)\right)^{+}\right]+\mathbb{E}F\left[\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{n}u_{t}\phi_{t}\left(f\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}(u)\right)\right)\right)^{-}\right]\right\}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\left\{\mathbb{E}F\left[\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{n}u_{t}\phi_{t}\left(f\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}(u)\right)\right)\right)^{+}\right]+\mathbb{E}F\left[\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{n}u_{t}^{\ast}\phi_{t}\left(f\left(\mathbf{z}^{\ast}_{t}(u^{\ast})\right)\right)\right)^{+}\right]\right\}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\left\{\mathbb{E}F\left[\left(\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{t=1}^{n}u_{t}\phi_{t}\left(f\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}(u)\right)\right)\right)^{+}\right]+\mathbb{E}F\left[\left(\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{t=1}^{n}u_{t}^{\ast}\phi_{t}\left(f\left(\mathbf{z}^{\ast}_{t}(u^{\ast})\right)\right)\right)^{+}\right]\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\left\{\mathbb{E}F\left[\left(\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{t=1}^{n}u_{t}\left(f\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}(u)\right)\right)\right)^{+}\right]+\mathbb{E}F\left[\left(\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{t=1}^{n}u_{t}^{\ast}f\left(\mathbf{z}^{\ast}_{t}(u^{\ast})\right)\right)^{+}\right]\right\}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\left\{\mathbb{E}F\left[\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{n}u_{t}\left(f\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}(u)\right)\right)\right)^{+}\right]+\mathbb{E}F\left[\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{n}u_{t}^{\ast}f\left(\mathbf{z}^{\ast}_{t}(u^{\ast})\right)\right)^{+}\right]\right\}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\left\{\mathbb{E}F\left[\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{n}u_{t}\left(f\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}(u)\right)\right)\right)^{+}\right]+\mathbb{E}F\left[\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{n}u_{t}f\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}(u)\right)\right)^{-}\right]\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}F\left[\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\left|\sum_{t=1}^{n}u_{t}f\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}(u)\right)\right|\right],

(B.16)

where
u_{t}^{\ast}=-u_{t}
,
\mathbf{z}_{t}^{\ast}(u)=\mathbf{z}_{t}(-u)
,
the first inequality is by the convexity of
F
, the first and second
equalities are by the fact that
(v)^{-}=(-v)^{+}
for any
v
, and the
second inequality is by (
B.15
) and the fact that
F((\cdot)^{+})

is convex and increasing. This leads to the desired result.

Define Rademacher sequence
u=\left(u_{11},\cdots,u_{1T},\cdots,u_{N1},\cdots,u_{NT}\right)=\left(u_{\left(1\right)},\cdots,u_{\left(NT\right)}\right)\in\mathbb{R}^{NT\times 1}
. In the
matrix notation, let
U=\left\{u_{it}\right\}\in\mathbb{R}^{N\times T}
.
By vectorization, for a sequence of independent variables
X_{j,it}
, we
define

\displaystyle\left(X_{j,11},\cdots,X_{j,1T},\cdots,X_{j,N1},\cdots,X_{j,NT}\right)=\left(X_{j,\left(1\right)},\cdots,X_{j,\left(NT\right)}\right),

\displaystyle\left(\epsilon_{11},\cdots,\epsilon_{1T},\cdots,\epsilon_{N1},\cdots,\epsilon_{NT}\right)=\left(\epsilon_{\left(1\right)},\cdots,\epsilon_{\left(NT\right)}\right),

\forall j\in[p]
. Using the binary tree representation, let
x_{j,\left(l\right)}^{\ast}
be
(l)^{th}
root of the tree which takes
values in the support of
X_{j,\left(l\right)}
, i.e.,
x_{j,\left(l\right)}^{\ast}:u^{l-1}\mapsto[-\xi_{N},\xi_{N}]
for
u^{l-1}:=\left(u_{\left(1\right)},\cdots,u_{\left(l-1\right)}\right)
such that
\max_{i\in[N]}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left(x_{j,it}^{\ast}\right)^{2}\leq MT
and
\max_{t\in[T]}\sum_{i\in[N]}\left(x_{j,it}^{\ast}\right)^{2\ell}\leq MN
for some
fixed constant
M<\infty
and
\ell=1,2
. Similar notation follows for
\epsilon_{\left(l\right)}^{\ast}=\epsilon_{\left(l\right)}^{\ast}\left(u^{l-1}\right)
. In the matrix notation, let
x_{j}^{\ast}=\left\{x_{j,it}^{\ast}\right\}\in\mathbb{R}^{N\times T}
such that
x_{j,it}^{\ast}=x_{j,\left(l\right)}
with
i=\lceil\frac{l}{T}\rceil

and
t=l-\left(i-1\right)T
.

Lemma B.10

Under Assumption
1
, for
j\in[p]
,
there exists an absolute constant
C
that is independent of the trees
(x^{\ast},\epsilon^{\ast})
such that when
\log(N\vee T)\geq 2
,

\mathbb{E}\exp\left(\frac{\left\|U\right\|_{op}}{\sqrt{(N\vee T)\log(N\vee T)}}\right)\leq C\hskip 9.24994pt\text{and}\hskip 9.24994pt\mathbb{E}\exp\left(\frac{\left\|U\odot x_{j}^{\ast}\right\|_{op}}{\sqrt{(N\vee T)\log(N\vee T)}}\right)\leq C.

Proof.
The proof here follows similarly as Lemma
B.2
except that we
have martingale difference matrices rather than independent matrices. For a
specific
j
, let
A=U\odot x_{j}^{\ast}=\left(A_{1},\cdots,A_{N}\right)^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}^{N\times T}
,
\mathscr{F}_{i}
be the
\sigma
-field generated by
\{u_{i^{*}t}\}_{i^{*}\leq i,t\in[T]}
,
\mathbb{E}_{i}(\cdot)=\mathbb{E}(\cdot|\mathscr{F}_{i})
,
\Sigma_{i}=\mathbb{E}_{i-1}\left(A_{i}A_{i}^{\prime}\right)
and
Z_{i}=\frac{1}{N}\left(A_{i}A_{i}^{\prime}-\Sigma_{i}\right)
, with
\frac{1}{N}\left(A^{\prime}A-\sum_{i\in[N]}\Sigma_{i}\right)=\sum_{i\in[N]}Z_{i}
.
Note that
\mathbb{E}_{i-1}\left(Z_{i}\right)=0,

\max_{i\in[N]}\left\|A_{i}\right\|_{2}=\max_{i\in[N]}\sqrt{\sum_{t\in[T]}\left(x_{j,it}^{\ast}u_{it}\right)^{2}}=\max_{i\in[N]}\sqrt{\sum_{t\in[T]}\left(x_{j,it}^{\ast}\right)^{2}}\leq\sqrt{MT}~a.s.,

(B.17)

\max_{i\in[N]}\left\|\Sigma_{i}\right\|_{op}=\max_{i\in[N]}\left\|\text{diag}\left(\left(x_{j,i1}^{\ast}\right)^{2},\cdots,\left(x_{j,iT}^{\ast}\right)^{2}\right)\right\|_{op}\leq\xi_{N}^{2}~a.s.,

(B.18)

and for
\ell=1,2,

\left\|\sum_{i\in[N]}\Sigma_{i}^{\ell}\right\|_{op}=\left\|\text{diag}\left(\sum_{i\in[N]}\left(x_{j,i1}^{\ast}\right)^{2\ell},\cdots,\sum_{i\in[N]}\left(x_{j,iT}^{\ast}\right)^{2\ell}\right)\right\|_{op}\leq MN~a.s..

(B.19)

Combining (
B.17
) and (
B.18
) yields

\displaystyle\max_{i\in[N]}\left\|Z_{i}\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{i\in[N]}\frac{1}{N}\left(\left\|A_{i}A_{i}^{\prime}\right\|_{op}+\left\|\Sigma_{i}\right\|_{op}\right)

(B.20)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\left(\max_{i\in[N]}\left\|A_{i}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\max_{i\in[N]}\left\|\Sigma_{i}\right\|_{op}\right)\leq\frac{MT+\xi_{N}^{2}}{N}\,\,a.s.

In addition,

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{i\in[N]}\mathbb{E}_{i-1}\left(Z_{i}^{2}\right)\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle=\left\|\sum_{i\in[N]}\mathbb{E}_{i-1}\left\{\frac{1}{N^{2}}\left[\left(A_{i}A_{i}^{\prime}\right)^{2}-\Sigma_{i}^{2}\right]\right\}\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{N^{2}}\left[\sum_{i\in[N]}\left\|\mathbb{E}_{i-1}\left(\left\|A_{i}\right\|_{2}^{2}A_{i}A_{i}^{\prime}\right)\right\|_{op}+\left\|\sum_{i\in[N]}\Sigma_{i}^{2}\right\|_{op}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{N^{2}}\left[MT\left\|\sum_{i\in[N]}\Sigma_{i}\right\|_{op}+\left\|\sum_{i\in[N]}\Sigma_{i}^{2}\right\|_{op}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\left(MT+1\right)M}{N},

(B.21)

where the last inequality holds by (
B.17
) and (
B.19
).

Combining (
B.20
) and (
B.21
), by matrix Bernstein’s
inequality in Lemma
B.1
(ii), for some sufficiently large
constant
\bar{c}
that depends on
M
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left\|\sum_{i\in[N]}Z_{i}\right\|_{op}>\bar{c}\frac{(N\vee T)}{N}\log(N\vee T)\right)

\displaystyle\leq T\exp\left\{-\frac{\frac{1}{2}\bar{c}^{2}\left(\frac{(N\vee T)}{N}\right)^{2}\left(\log(N\vee T)\right)^{2}}{\frac{\left(MT+1\right)M}{N}+\frac{\frac{MT+\xi_{N}^{2}}{N}\bar{c}\frac{(N\vee T)}{N}\log(N\vee T)}{3}}\right\}=\exp\left(-\left(\frac{\bar{c}}{2}-1\right)\log((N\vee T))\right),

which implies that with probability greater than
1-\exp(-(\bar{c}/2-1)\log(N\vee T))
,

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{N}\left(A^{\prime}A-\sum_{i\in[N]}\Sigma_{i}\right)\right\|_{op}\leq\bar{c}\left(\frac{(N\vee T)}{N}\log(N\vee T)\right),

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{N}A^{\prime}A\right\|_{op}\leq\left\|\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i\in[N]}\Sigma_{i}\right\|_{op}+\left\|\frac{1}{N}\left(A^{\prime}A-\sum_{i\in[N]}\Sigma_{i}\right)\right\|_{op}\leq M+\bar{c}\left(\frac{(N\vee T)}{N}\log(N\vee T)\right),\hskip 9.24994pt\text{and}

\displaystyle\left\|A\right\|_{op}=\left\|U\odot x_{j}^{\ast}\right\|_{op}\leq\sqrt{(1+\bar{c})(N\vee T)\log(N\vee T)}.

Consequently, when
\log(N\vee T)\geq 2
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\exp\left(\frac{\left\|U\odot x_{j}^{\ast}\right\|_{op}}{\sqrt{(N\vee T)\log(N\vee T)}}\right)

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\infty}\exp(u)\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{\left\|U\odot x_{j}^{\ast}\right\|_{op}}{\sqrt{(N\vee T)\log(N\vee T)}}\geq u\right)du

\displaystyle=\left(\int_{0}^{2}+\int_{2}^{\infty}\right)\exp(u)\mathbb{P}\left(\left\|U\odot x_{j}^{\ast}\right\|_{op}\geq u\sqrt{(N\vee T)\log(N\vee T)}\right)du

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{2}\exp(u)du+\int_{2}^{\infty}\exp(u)\exp\left(-\frac{(u^{2}-3)}{2}\log(N\vee T)\right)du

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{2}\exp(u)du+\int_{2}^{\infty}\exp\left(-\left(u-\frac{1}{2}\right)^{2}+\frac{13}{4}\right)du

\displaystyle\leq\exp(2)-1+\sqrt{2\pi}\exp\left(\frac{13}{4}\right):=C,

where the first inequality is by the fact that

\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{\left\|U\odot x_{j}^{\ast}\right\|_{op}}{\sqrt{(N\vee T)\log(N\vee T)}}\geq\sqrt{1+\bar{c}}\right)\leq\exp\left(-\left(\frac{\bar{c}}{2}-1\right)\log((N\vee T))\right)

and by letting
u=\sqrt{1+\bar{c}}\geq 2
for large
\bar{c}
. Similarly, we
can show that when
\log(N\vee T)\geq 2
,
\mathbb{E}\exp\left(\frac{\left\|U\right\|_{op}}{\sqrt{(N\vee T)\log(N\vee T)}}\right)\leq C

for some absolute constant
C
.

Recall that
\tilde{\rho}_{it}\left(\left\{\Delta_{\Theta_{j},it},X_{j,it}\right\}_{j=0}^{p},\epsilon_{it}\right)
is defined in (
A.4
)
and
\mathscr{G}_{i,t}
is defined in Assumption
1
.

Lemma B.11

If Assumptions
1
-
4
hold, then we have

\sup_{\left\{\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\in\mathcal{R}\left(3,C_{2}\right)}\frac{\left|\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\tilde{\rho}_{it}\left(\left\{\Delta_{\Theta_{j},it},X_{j,it}\right\}_{j=0}^{p},\epsilon_{it}\right)\right|}{\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{F}}=O_{p}\left(a_{NT}\right),

where
a_{NT}=\frac{\sqrt{\left(N\vee T\right)\log\left(N\vee T\right)}}{NT}
.

Proof.
Let
n=NT
and for
l\in[n]
,
Z_{l}=(\{X_{j,it}\}_{j\in[p]},\epsilon_{it})
with
i=\lceil\frac{l}{T}\rceil
and
t=l-\left(i-1\right)T
, and
\mathscr{F}_{l}=\mathscr{G}_{it}
. Then,
Z_{l}
is adapted to the filtration
\{\mathscr{F}_{l}\}_{l\in[n]}
. Lemma
B.8
implies

\displaystyle\beta_{NT}\mathbb{P}\left\{\sup_{\left\{\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\in\mathcal{R}\left(3,C_{2}\right)}\left|\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{\tilde{\rho}_{it}\left(\left\{\Delta_{\Theta_{j},it},X_{j,it}\right\}_{j=0}^{p},\epsilon_{it}\right)}{\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{F}}\right|>C_{5}a_{NT}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\sup_{x^{\ast},\epsilon^{\ast}}\mathbb{P}\left\{\sup_{\left\{\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\in\mathcal{R}\left(3,C_{2}\right)}\left|\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{u_{it}\rho_{it}\left(\left\{\Delta_{\Theta_{j},it},x_{j,it}^{\ast}\right\}_{j=0}^{p},\epsilon_{it}^{\ast}\right)}{\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{F}}\right|>\frac{C_{5}a_{NT}}{4}\right\},

(B.22)

for some positive constant
C_{5}
, where the outer supremum on the RHS of
the above display is taken over
[-\xi_{N},\xi_{N}]^{p}\times\mathbb{R}
-valued trees with depth
n
and

\beta_{NT}=1-\sup_{\left\{\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\in\mathcal{R}\left(3,C_{2}\right)}\frac{4}{C_{5}^{2}\left(NT\right)^{2}a_{NT}^{2}}\sum_{(i,t)\in[N]\times[T]}Var\left(\frac{\tilde{\rho}_{it}\left(\left\{\Delta_{\Theta_{j},it},X_{j,it}\right\}_{j=0}^{p},\epsilon_{it}\right)}{\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{F}}\bigg|\mathscr{G}_{i,t-1}\right).

We first note

\displaystyle\sum_{(i,t)\in[N]\times[T]}Var\left(\frac{\tilde{\rho}_{it}\left(\left\{\Delta_{\Theta_{j},it},X_{j,it}\right\}_{j=0}^{p},\epsilon_{it}\right)}{\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{F}}\bigg|\mathscr{G}_{i,t-1}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{(i,t)\in[N]\times[T]}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{\rho_{it}\left(\left\{\Delta_{\Theta_{j},it},X_{j,it}\right\}_{j=0}^{p},\epsilon_{it}\right)}{\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{F}}\right)^{2}\bigg|\mathscr{G}_{i,t-1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{(i,t)\in[N]\times[T]}\left(\frac{\Delta_{\Theta_{0},it}+\sum_{j=1}^{p}X_{j,it}\Theta_{j,it}}{\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{F}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{\sum_{j=1}^{p}\left(X_{j,it}\Delta_{\Theta_{j},it}\right)^{2}+\Delta_{\Theta_{0},it}^{2}}{\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{F}^{2}}\leq c_{10}\xi_{N}^{2}

with some positive constant
c_{10}
, where the first inequality holds by
Jensen inequality, the second inequality is by
\left|\rho_{\tau}(u)-\rho_{\tau}(v)\right|\leq 2\left|u-v\right|
,
and the last line holds by Assumption
1
(iv) and the fact that
\left(\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{F}\right)^{2}=O\left(\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{F}^{2}\right)
. Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\beta_{NT}

\displaystyle\geq 1-\sup_{\left\{\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\in\mathcal{R}\left(3,C_{2}\right)}\frac{4}{C_{5}^{2}\left(NT\right)^{2}a_{NT}^{2}}\sum_{(i,t)\in[N]\times[T]}Var\left(\frac{\tilde{\rho}_{it}\left(\left\{\Delta_{\Theta_{j},it},X_{j,it}\right\}_{j=0}^{p},\epsilon_{it}\right)}{\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{F}}\right)

\displaystyle\geq 1-\sup_{\left\{\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\in\mathcal{R}\left(3,C_{2}\right)}\frac{4c_{10}\xi_{N}^{2}}{C_{5}^{2}\left(N\vee T\right)\log\left(N\vee T\right)}

\displaystyle\geq 1-O\left(\frac{\xi_{N}^{2}}{\left(N\vee T\right)\log(N\vee T)}\right)\rightarrow 1,

(B.23)

where the last line is by Assumption
1
(ix).

Define

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}_{0}=\sup_{\left\{\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\in\mathcal{R}\left(3,C_{2}\right)}\left|\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{u_{it}\Delta_{\Theta_{0},it}}{\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{F}}\right|,

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}_{j}=\sup_{\left\{\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\in\mathcal{R}\left(3,C_{2}\right)}\left|\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{u_{it}x_{j,it}^{\ast}\Delta_{\Theta_{j},it}}{\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{F}}\right|,\hskip 9.24994pt\forall j\in[p],

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}_{p+1}=\sup_{\left\{\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\in\mathcal{R}\left(3,C_{2}\right)}\left|\frac{\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}u_{it}\phi_{it}\left(\Delta_{\Theta_{0},it}+\sum_{j=1}^{p}x_{j,it}^{\ast}\Delta_{\Theta_{j},it}\right)}{\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{F}}\right|,

where
\phi_{it}\left(u\right)=\left(\epsilon_{it}^{\ast}-u\right)^{-}-\left(\epsilon_{it}^{\ast}\right)^{-}
. Notice that

\rho_{it}\left(\left\{\Delta_{\Theta_{j},it},x_{j,it}^{\ast}\right\}_{j=0}^{p},\epsilon_{it}^{\ast}\right)=\tau\left(\Delta_{\Theta_{0},it}+\sum_{j=1}^{p}x_{j,it}^{\ast}\Delta_{\Theta_{j},it}\right)+\phi_{it}\left(\Delta_{\Theta_{0},it}+\sum_{j=1}^{p}x_{j,it}^{\ast}\Delta_{\Theta_{j},it}\right),

we obtain that

\displaystyle\sup_{x^{\ast},\epsilon^{\ast}}\mathbb{P}\left\{\sup_{\left\{\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\in\mathcal{R}\left(3,C_{2}\right)}\left|\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{u_{it}\rho_{it}\left(\left\{\Delta_{\Theta_{j},it},x_{j,it}^{\ast}\right\}_{j=0}^{p},\epsilon_{it}^{\ast}\right)}{\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{F}}\right|>\frac{C_{5}a_{NT}}{4}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{p}\sup_{x^{\ast}}\mathbb{P}\left\{\tau\mathcal{A}_{j}>\frac{C_{5}a_{NT}}{4\left(p+2\right)}\right\}+\sup_{x^{\ast},\epsilon^{\ast}}\mathbb{P}\left\{\mathcal{A}_{p+1}>\frac{C_{5}a_{NT}}{4\left(p+2\right)}\right\}.

(B.24)

We first bound
\mathcal{A}_{j}
for
j\in[p]
. We have

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}_{j}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sup_{\left\{\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\in\mathcal{R}\left(3,C_{2}\right)}\left|\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{u_{it}x_{j,it}^{\ast}\Delta_{\Theta_{j},it}}{\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{F}}\right|\leq\frac{1}{NT}\sup_{\left\{\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\in\mathcal{R}\left(3,C_{2}\right)}\frac{\left|tr\left[\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}^{\prime}\left(U\odot x_{j}^{\ast}\right)\right]\right|}{\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{NT}\left\|U\odot x_{j}^{\ast}\right\|_{op}\sup_{\left\{\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\in\mathcal{R}\left(3,C_{2}\right)}\frac{\left\|\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{\ast}}{\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{F}}\leq\frac{c_{8}}{NT}\left\|U\odot x_{j}^{\ast}\right\|_{op},

(B.25)

where the first inequality holds by
tr(AB)\leq\left\|A\right\|_{op}\left\|B\right\|_{\ast}
and the second inequality
holds by Lemma
B.7
. Then

\displaystyle\sup_{x^{\ast},\epsilon^{\ast}}\mathbb{P}\left\{\tau\mathcal{A}_{j}>\frac{C_{5}a_{NT}}{4\left(p+2\right)}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{x^{\ast}}\mathbb{P}\left\{\frac{\left\|U\odot x_{j}^{\ast}\right\|}{\sqrt{\left(N\vee T\right)\log\left(N\vee T\right)}}>\frac{C_{5}}{4c_{8}\tau\left(p+2\right)}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{x^{\ast}}\left\{\exp\left(-\frac{C_{5}}{4c_{8}\tau\left(p+2\right)}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(\frac{\left\|U\odot x_{j}^{\ast}\right\|}{\sqrt{\left(N\vee T\right)\log\left(N\vee T\right)}}\right)\right]\right\}

\displaystyle\leq C\exp\left(-\frac{C_{5}}{4c_{8}\tau\left(p+2\right)}\right)

(B.26)

for some absolute constant
C
that independent of
(x^{\ast},\epsilon^{\ast})
, where the last inequality holds by Lemma
B.10
. Similarly, we can establish

\sup_{x^{\ast},\epsilon^{\ast}}\mathbb{P}\left\{\tau\mathcal{A}_{0}>\frac{C_{5}a_{NT}}{4\left(p+2\right)}\right\}\leq C\exp\left(-\frac{C_{5}}{4c_{8}\tau\left(p+2\right)}\right).

(B.27)

Next, we turn to
\mathcal{A}_{p+1}
. We have

\sup_{x^{\ast},\epsilon^{\ast}}\mathbb{P}\left\{\mathcal{A}_{p+1}>\frac{C_{5}a_{NT}}{4\left(p+2\right)}\right\}\leq\exp\left(-\frac{C_{5}}{4c_{8}(p+1)\left(p+2\right)}\right)\sup_{x^{\ast},\epsilon^{\ast}}\mathbb{E}\left\{\exp\left(\frac{NT\mathcal{A}_{p+1}}{c_{8}(p+1)\sqrt{\left(N\vee T\right)\log\left(N\vee T\right)}}\right)\right\}.

(B.28)

Because
\phi_{it}(\cdot)
is a contraction, Lemma
B.9

implies

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\{\exp\left(\frac{NT\mathcal{A}_{p+1}}{c_{8}(p+1)\sqrt{\left(N\vee T\right)\left(\log(N\vee T)\right)}}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left\{\exp\left[\frac{1}{c_{8}(p+1)\sqrt{\left(N\vee T\right)\left(\log(N\vee T)\right)}}\sup_{\left\{\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\in\mathcal{R}\left(3,C_{2}\right)}\left|\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}u_{it}\phi_{it}\left(\Delta_{\Theta_{0},it}+\sum_{j=1}^{p}x_{j,it}^{\ast}\Delta_{\Theta_{j},it}\right)}{\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{F}}\right|\right]\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left\{\exp\left[\frac{1}{c_{8}(p+1)\sqrt{\left(N\vee T\right)\left(\log(N\vee T)\right)}}\sup_{\left\{\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\in\mathcal{R}\left(3,C_{2}\right)}\left|\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{u_{it}(\Delta_{\Theta_{0},it}+\sum_{j=1}^{p}x_{j,it}^{\ast}\Delta_{\Theta_{j},it})}{\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{F}}\right|\right]\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left\{\exp\left[\frac{\left(\left\|U\right\|_{op}+\sum_{j\in[p]}\left\|U\odot x_{j}^{\ast}\right\|_{op}\right)}{(p+1)\sqrt{\left(N\vee T\right)\left(\log(N\vee T)\right)}}\right]\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left\{\exp\left[\frac{\left(\left\|U\right\|_{op}\right)}{\sqrt{\left(N\vee T\right)\left(\log(N\vee T)\right)}}\right]\right\}^{1/(1+p)}\Pi_{j\in[p]}\left[\mathbb{E}\left\{\exp\left[\frac{\left(\left\|U\odot x_{j}^{\ast}\right\|_{op}\right)}{\sqrt{\left(N\vee T\right)\left(\log(N\vee T)\right)}}\right]\right\}^{1/(1+p)}\right]\leq C

(B.29)

for some absolute constant
C
, where the first inequality is by Lemma
B.9
, the second inequality is by (
B.25
), the third
inequality is due to the fact that, for random variables
\{A_{i}\}_{i\in[p+1]}
,

\mathbb{E}(\Pi_{i\in[p+1]}|A_{i}|)\leq\Pi_{i\in[p+1]}[\mathbb{E}|A_{i}^{1+p}|]^{1/(1+p)},

and the final inequality is by Lemma
B.10
with an absolute
constant
C
that is independent of
(x^{\ast},\epsilon^{\ast})
.

Combining (
B.28
) and (
B.1
), we have

\sup_{x^{\ast},\epsilon^{\ast}}\mathbb{P}\left\{\mathcal{A}_{p+1}>\frac{C_{5}a_{NT}}{4\left(p+2\right)}\right\}\leq C\exp\left(-\frac{C_{5}}{4c_{8}\left(p+1\right)(P+2)}\right),

which, combined with (
B.1
), (
B.1
), (
B.26
), and (
B.27
), further implies that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left\{\sup_{\left\{\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\in\mathcal{R}\left(3,C_{2}\right)}\left|\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{\tilde{\rho}_{it}\left(\left\{\Delta_{\Theta_{j},it},X_{j,it}\right\}_{j=0}^{p},\epsilon_{it}\right)}{\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{F}}\right|>C_{5}a_{NT}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq 2\beta_{NT}^{-1}\sup_{x^{\ast},\epsilon^{\ast}}\mathbb{P}\left\{\sup_{\left\{\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\in\mathcal{R}\left(3,C_{2}\right)}\left|\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{u_{it}\rho_{it}\left(\left\{\Delta_{\Theta_{j},it},x_{j,it}^{\ast}\right\}_{j=0}^{p},\epsilon_{it}^{\ast}\right)}{\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{F}}\right|>\frac{C_{5}a_{NT}}{4}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq C(1+o(1))\left[(p+1)\exp\left(-\frac{C_{5}}{4c_{8}\tau\left(p+2\right)}\right)+\exp\left(-\frac{C_{5}}{4c_{8}(p+1)\left(p+2\right)}\right)\right].

The RHS of the last inequality converges to zero as
C_{5}\rightarrow\infty
, which implies that

\sup_{\left\{\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\in\mathcal{R}\left(3,C_{2}\right)}\frac{\left|\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\tilde{\rho}_{it}\left(\left\{\Delta_{\Theta_{j},it},X_{j,it}\right\}_{j=0}^{p},\epsilon_{it}\right)\right|}{\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|\Delta_{\Theta_{j}}\right\|_{F}}=O_{p}\left(a_{NT}\right).

B.2
Lemmas for the Proof of Theorem
3.2

Lemma B.12

Let
\left\{\Upsilon_{t},t=1,\cdots,T\right\}

be a zero-mean strong mixing process, not necessarily stationary, with the
mixing coefficients satisfying
\alpha(z)\leq c_{\alpha}\rho^{z}
for some
c_{\alpha}>0
and
\rho\in(0,1)
. If
\sup_{1\leq t\leq T}\left|\Upsilon_{t}\right|\leq M_{T}
, then there exist a constant
c_{9}

depending on
c_{\alpha}
and
\rho
such that for any
T\geq 2
and
d>0
,

(i)

\mathbb{P}\left\{\left|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Upsilon_{t}\right|>d\right\}\leq\exp\left\{-\frac{c_{9}d^{2}}{M_{T}^{2}T+dM_{T}\left(\log T\right)\left(\log\log T\right)}\right\}
.

(ii)

\mathbb{P}\left\{\left|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Upsilon_{t}\right|>d\right\}\leq\exp\left\{-\frac{c_{9}d^{2}}{\upsilon_{0}^{2}T+M_{T}^{2}+dM_{T}\left(\log T\right)^{2}}\right\}
,

with
\upsilon_{0}^{2}=\sup_{t\in[T]}\left[Var(\Upsilon_{t})+2\sum_{s>t}\left|Cov(\Upsilon_{t},\Upsilon_{s})\right|\right]
.

Proof.
The proof is the same as that of Theorems 1 and 2 in
Merlevède et al., 2009
) with the condition assumed
\alpha(a)\leq\exp\left\{-2ca\right\}
for some
c>0
changed with
c_{\alpha}=1
and
\rho=\exp\left\{-2c\right\}
in our lemma instead.

Lemma B.13

Suppose Assumptions
1
-
4
hold. Then, for
j\in\left\{0,\cdots,p\right\}
, we have

(i)

\max_{i\in[N]}\left\|u_{i,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}\leq M
and

\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|v_{t,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}\leq\frac{M}{\sigma_{K_{j},j}}\leq\frac{M}{c_{\sigma}}
.

(ii)

\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|O_{j}^{\prime}\tilde{v}_{t,j}\right\|_{2}\leq\frac{2M}{\sigma_{K_{j},j}}\leq\frac{2M}{c_{\sigma}}
and
\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|O_{j}^{(1)\prime}\tilde{v}_{t,j}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}\leq\frac{2M}{\sigma_{K_{j},j}}\leq\frac{2M}{c_{\sigma}}
w.p.a.1.

(iii)

\max_{i\in I_{2}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|\tilde{\phi}_{it}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}^{2}\leq\frac{4M^{2}}{c_{\sigma}}+\frac{4M^{2}pC}{c_{\sigma}}
w.p.a.1.

Proof.
(i) Recall that
\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\Theta_{j}^{0}=\mathcal{U}_{j}^{0}\Sigma_{j}^{0}\mathcal{V}_{j}^{0\prime},

U_{j}^{0}=\sqrt{N}\mathcal{U}_{j}^{0}\Sigma_{j}^{0}
and
V_{j}=\sqrt{T}\mathcal{V}_{j}
. Then
we have

\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\Theta_{j}^{0}\mathcal{V}_{j}^{0}=\sqrt{N}\mathcal{U}_{j}^{0}\Sigma_{j}^{0}=U_{j}^{0}\hskip 9.24994pt\text{and}\hskip 9.24994pt\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\mathcal{U}_{j}^{0\prime}\Theta_{j}^{0}=\sqrt{T}\Sigma_{j}^{0}\mathcal{V}_{j}^{0\prime}=\Sigma_{j}^{0}V_{j}^{0\prime}.

(B.30)

Hence, it’s natural to see that

\left\|u_{i,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\left\|\left[\Theta_{j}^{0}\mathcal{V}_{j}^{0}\right]_{i.}\right\|_{2}\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\left\|\left[\Theta_{j}\right]_{i.}\right\|_{2}\leq M,

where the first inequality is due to the fact that
\mathcal{V}_{j}
is the
unitary matrix and the last inequality holds by Assumption
2
.
Since the upper bound
M
is not dependent on
i
, this result holds
uniformly in
i
. Analogously,

\left\|v_{t,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}c_{\sigma}^{-1}\left\|\left[\mathcal{U}_{j}^{0\prime}\Theta_{j}^{0}\right]_{.t}\right\|_{2}\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}c_{\sigma}^{-1}\left\|\left[\Theta_{j}^{0}\right]_{.t}\right\|_{2}\leq\frac{M}{c_{\sigma}}.

(ii) As in (
B.30
), we have

\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}_{j}^{\prime}\tilde{\Theta}_{j}=\sqrt{T}\tilde{\Sigma}_{j}\tilde{\mathcal{V}}_{j}^{\prime}=\tilde{\Sigma}_{j}\tilde{V}_{j}^{\prime}.

It follows that

\left\|O_{j}^{\prime}\tilde{v}_{t,j}\right\|_{2}\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\tilde{\sigma}_{K_{j},j}^{-1}\left\|\left[\tilde{\mathcal{U}}_{j}^{\prime}\tilde{\Theta}_{j}\right]_{.t}\right\|_{2}\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\tilde{\sigma}_{K_{j},j}^{-1}\left\|\left[\tilde{\Theta}_{j}\right]_{.t}\right\|_{2}\leq\frac{2M}{c_{\sigma}},

where the last inequality holds due to the fact that
\max_{k\in[K_{j}]}\left|\tilde{\sigma}_{k,j}^{-1}-\Sigma_{k,j}^{-1}\right|\leq\Sigma_{K_{j},j}^{-1}
w.p.a.1. and the bounded parameter space where
\tilde{\Theta}_{j}
lies in by Assumption
2
and ADMM algorithm
proposed in the last section. The upper bound of
\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|O_{j}^{(1)\prime}\tilde{v}_{t,j}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}
follows the same
argument as above.

(iii) We observe that

\max_{i\in I_{2}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}^{2}\leq\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|O_{0}^{\prime}\tilde{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\max_{i\in I_{2}}\sum_{j\in[p]}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|O_{j}^{\prime}\tilde{v}_{t,j}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left|X_{j,it}\right|^{2}\leq\frac{4M^{2}}{c_{\sigma}^{2}}+\frac{4M^{2}pC}{c_{\sigma}^{2}}\hskip 9.24994pt\text{w.p.a.1},

where the last inequality holds by Lemma
B.13
(ii) and
Assumption
1
(iv).

Lemma B.14

Under Assumptions
1
–
5
, we
have

(i)

\operatornamewithlimits{\min}\limits_{i\in I_{2}}\lambda_{\min}\left(\tilde{\Phi}_{i}^{(1)}\right)\geq\frac{c_{\phi}}{2}
,
\operatornamewithlimits{\max}\limits_{i\in I_{2}}\lambda_{\max}\left(\tilde{\Phi}_{i}^{(1)}\right)\leq 2C_{\phi}
w.p.a.1,

(ii)

For
\forall j\in[p]
,
\operatornamewithlimits{\max}\limits_{i\in I_{2}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left[X_{j,it}^{2}-\mathbb{E}\left(X_{j,it}^{2}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{\left\{e_{is}\right\}_{s<t}}^{I_{1}}\right)\right]\left\|\tilde{O}_{j}^{(1)\prime}\tilde{v}_{t,j}^{(1)}-v_{t,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}^{2}=O_{p}(\eta_{N}^{2})
,

(iii)

\operatornamewithlimits{\max}\limits_{i\in I_{2}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}-\Phi_{it}^{0}\right\|_{2}^{2}=O_{p}(\eta_{N}^{2})
.

Proof.
(i) Recall that

\displaystyle\Phi_{i}=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Phi_{it}^{0}\Phi_{it}^{0\prime}\hskip 9.24994pt\text{with}\hskip 9.24994pt\Phi_{it}^{0}=(v_{t,0}^{0\prime},v_{t,1}^{0\prime}X_{1,it},...,v_{t,p}^{0\prime}X_{p,it})^{\prime},\hskip 9.24994pt\text{and}

\displaystyle\tilde{\Phi}_{i}^{(1)}=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\hskip 9.24994pt\text{with}\hskip 9.24994pt\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}=\left[\left(O_{0}^{(1)\prime}\tilde{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime},\left(O_{1}^{(1)\prime}\tilde{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}X_{1,it}\right)^{\prime},\cdots,\left(O_{p}^{(1)\prime}\tilde{v}_{t,p}^{(1)}X_{p,it}\right)^{\prime}\right]^{\prime}.

Uniformly over
i\in I_{2}
, it is clear that

\displaystyle\left\|\tilde{\Phi}_{i}^{(1)}-\Phi_{i}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{4M}{c_{\sigma}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|O_{0}^{(1)\prime}\tilde{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}-v_{t,0}^{0}\right\|_{2}+\frac{4M}{c_{\sigma}T}\sum_{j=1}^{p}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|O_{j}^{(1)\prime}\tilde{v}_{t,j}^{(1)}-v_{t,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}\left|X_{j,it}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4M}{c_{\sigma}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\left\|O_{0}^{(1)\prime}\tilde{V}_{0}^{(1)}-V_{0}^{0}\right\|_{F}+\frac{4M^{2}}{c_{\sigma}}\sum_{j=1}^{p}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\left\|O_{j}^{(1)\prime}\tilde{V}_{j}^{(1)}-V_{j}^{0}\right\|_{F}\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left|X_{j,it}\right|^{2}\right)^{1/2}=O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}\right),

where the third line holds by Lemma
B.13
(i) and
Assumption
1
(iv). It follows that

\min_{i\in I_{2}}\lambda_{\min}\left[\tilde{\Phi}_{i}^{(1)}\right]\geq\min_{i\in I_{2}}\lambda_{\min}\left[\Phi_{i}\right]-O\left(\eta_{N}\right)\geq\frac{c_{\phi}}{2},\hskip 9.24994pt\text{w.p.a.1}

and

\max_{i\in I_{2}}\lambda_{\max}\left[\tilde{\Phi}_{i}^{(1)}\right]\leq\max_{i\in I_{2}}\lambda_{\max}\left[\Phi_{i}\right]+O\left(\eta_{N}\right)\leq 2C_{\phi},\hskip 9.24994pt\text{w.p.a.1}.

(ii) Let
I_{j,i}:=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}I_{j,it}
such that
I_{j,it}=\left[X_{j,it}^{2}-\mathbb{E}\left(X_{j,it}^{2}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{\left\{e_{is}\right\}_{s<t}}^{I_{1}}\right)\right]\left\|\tilde{O}_{j}^{(1)\prime}\tilde{v}_{t,j}^{(1)}-v_{t,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}^{2}
. Then
for a constant
c
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{i\in I_{2}}\left|\sum_{t\in[T]}I_{j,it}\right|>cT\eta_{N}^{2}\right)\leq\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\sum_{t\in[T]}I_{j,it}\right|>cT\eta_{N}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\mathbb{E}\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\sum_{t\in[T]}I_{j,it}\right|>cT\eta_{N}^{2}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{\left\{e_{is}\right\}_{s<T}}^{I_{1}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\exp\left\{-\frac{2(cT\eta_{N}^{2})^{2}}{\sum_{t\in[T]}\left[2\xi_{N}^{2}\left\|\tilde{O}_{j}^{(1)\prime}\tilde{v}_{t,j}^{(1)}-v_{t,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]^{2}}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq 2\exp\left\{-\frac{2(cT\eta_{N}^{2})^{2}}{4\xi_{N}^{4}\left[\frac{M^{2}}{c_{\sigma}^{2}}+\frac{4M^{2}}{c_{\sigma}^{2}}\right]\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|\tilde{O}_{j}^{(1)\prime}\tilde{v}_{t,j}^{(1)}-v_{t,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}^{2}}+\log N\right\}=o(1),

where the first inequality combines the fact that
I_{j,it}
is the
martingale difference sequence, Assumption
1
(v) and the
Azuma-Hoeffding inequality in
Wainwright, 2019
.
The last inequality is by Lemma
B.13
(i) and
B.13
(ii), and the finial result is by the definition of
\eta_{N}
.

(iii) Note that

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{2}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}-\Phi_{it}^{0}\right\|_{2}^{2}\leq\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|\tilde{O}_{0}^{(1)\prime}\tilde{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}-v_{t,0}^{0}\right\|_{2}^{2}+p\max_{i\in I_{2},j\in\left[p\right]}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left|X_{j,it}\right|^{2}\left\|\tilde{O}_{j}^{(1)\prime}\tilde{v}_{t,j}^{(1)}-v_{t,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|\tilde{O}_{0}^{(1)\prime}\tilde{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}-v_{t,0}^{0}\right\|_{2}^{2}+p\max_{i\in I_{2},j\in\left[p\right]}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left[X_{j,it}^{2}-\mathbb{E}\left(X_{j,it}^{2}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{\left\{e_{is}\right\}_{s<t}}^{I_{1}}\right)\right]\left\|\tilde{O}_{j}^{(1)\prime}\tilde{v}_{t,j}^{(1)}-v_{t,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle+\max_{i\in I_{2},j\in\left[p\right]}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\mathbb{E}\left(X_{j,it}^{2}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{\left\{e_{is}\right\}_{s<t}}^{I_{1}}\right)\left\|\tilde{O}_{j}^{(1)\prime}\tilde{v}_{t,j}^{(1)}-v_{t,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|\tilde{O}_{0}^{(1)\prime}\tilde{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}-v_{t,0}^{0}\right\|_{2}^{2}+O_{p}(\eta_{N}^{2})+pM\max_{j\in\left[p\right]}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|\tilde{O}_{j}^{(1)\prime}\tilde{v}_{t,j}^{(1)}-v_{t,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}^{2}\hskip 9.24994pt

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\left\|O_{0}^{(1)}\tilde{V}_{0}^{(1)}-V_{0}^{0}\right\|_{F}^{2}+p\max_{j\in\left[p\right]}\frac{1}{T}\left\|O_{j}^{(1)}\tilde{V}_{j}^{(1)}-V_{j}^{0}\right\|_{F}^{2}+O_{p}(\eta_{N}^{2})

\displaystyle=O_{p}(\eta_{N}^{2}),

where the the second inequality holds by Assumption
1
(iv) and
Lemma
B.14
(ii), and the last equality holds by the Theorem
3.1
(ii).

Lemma B.15

Recall
\{A_{1,i},\cdots,A_{7,i}\}_{i\in I_{2}}
and
q_{i}^{I}
defined in (
A.2.1
) and (
A.6
), respectively.
Suppose Assumptions
1
–
5
hold. Then for any constant
c_{11}<\min(\frac{3\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}{\bar{\mathfrak{f}}^{\prime}},1)
, we have

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{2}}(|A_{m,i}|/\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}\right\|_{2})=O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}\right),\forall m\in\{1,2,3,5,6,7\}\hskip 9.24994pt\text{and}

\displaystyle|A_{4,i}|\geq\min\left(\frac{\left(3c_{11}^{2}\underline{\mathfrak{f}}-c_{11}^{3}\bar{\mathfrak{f}}^{\prime}\right)c_{\phi}\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}\right\|_{2}^{2}}{12},\frac{\left(3c_{11}^{2}\underline{\mathfrak{f}}-c_{11}^{3}\bar{\mathfrak{f}}^{\prime}\right)\sqrt{c_{\phi}}q_{i}^{I}\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}\right\|_{2}}{6\sqrt{2}}\right),\hskip 9.24994pt\forall i\in I_{2},\hskip 9.24994ptw.p.a.1.

Proof.
Recall that
w_{1,it}=\epsilon_{it}-u_{i}^{0\prime}\left(\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}-\Phi_{it}^{0}\right).
Let
w_{2,it}=\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}
. For some positive constant
c_{11}\in(0,1]
, we observe that

\displaystyle A_{4,i}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\int_{0}^{w_{2,it}}\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq s\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}}\right)ds

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\int_{0}^{w_{2,it}}\left[\mathfrak{F}_{it}(s)-\mathfrak{F}_{it}(0)\right]ds\geq\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\int_{0}^{c_{11}w_{2,it}}\left[\mathfrak{F}_{it}(s)-\mathfrak{F}_{it}(0)\right]ds

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\int_{0}^{c_{11}w_{2,it}}\left[s\mathfrak{f}_{it}(0)+\frac{s^{2}}{2}\mathfrak{f}_{it}^{\prime}(\tilde{s})\right]ds

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left[\frac{c_{11}^{2}\underline{\mathfrak{f}}\left(\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}\right)^{2}}{2}-\frac{c_{11}^{3}\bar{\mathfrak{f}}^{\prime}\left|\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}\right|^{3}}{6}\right]

(B.31)

where
\tilde{s}\in(0,s)
. Here, due to Assumption
1
(i), the
conditional CDF of
\epsilon_{it}
given
\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}}
is
the same as that given the
\sigma
-field generated by
\left\{\left\{e_{it}\right\}_{t\in[T]}\bigcup\left\{V_{j}^{0}\right\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\bigcup\left\{W_{j}^{0}\right\}_{j\in[p]}\right\}
, which
leads to the second equality of the above display by Assumption
1
(vii); the first inequality holds by Lemma
B.5
(ii) for any
c_{11}\in\left(0,1\right]
. Here, we choose
c_{11}
such that
c_{11}<\frac{3\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}{\bar{\mathfrak{f}}^{\prime}}
; and the
last inequality holds by Assumption
1
(vii).

Let
q_{i}^{II}=\left[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left(\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}\right)^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}
and
recall that
q_{i}^{I}=\operatornamewithlimits{\inf}\limits_{\Delta}\frac{\left[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left(\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\Delta\right)^{2}\right]^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left|\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\Delta\right|^{3}}
. If
q_{i}^{I}\geq q_{i}^{II}
, we notice that
\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left|\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\Delta\right|^{3}<\left(q_{i}^{II}\right)^{2}
and
A_{4,i}\geq\frac{c_{11}^{2}\underline{\mathfrak{f}}\left(q_{i}^{II}\right)^{2}}{2}-\frac{c_{11}^{3}\bar{\mathfrak{f}}^{\prime}\left(q_{i}^{II}\right)^{2}}{6}=\frac{3c_{11}^{2}\underline{\mathfrak{f}}-c_{11}^{3}\bar{\mathfrak{f}}^{\prime}}{6}\left(q_{i}^{II}\right)^{2}
. If

q_{i}^{I}<q_{i}^{II}
, we have
\left[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left(\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\Delta_{i,u}^{\ast}\right)^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}=q_{i}^{I}
with
\Delta_{i,u}^{\ast}=\frac{q_{i}^{II}\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}}{q_{i}^{I}}
. Define the function

F\left(\Delta\right)=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\int_{0}^{\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\Delta}\left[\mathfrak{F}_{it}(s)-\mathfrak{F}_{it}(0)\right]ds.

Note that the second-order derivative of function
F\left(\Delta\right)
is
no less than zero, which implies
F\left(\Delta\right)
is convex.
Therefore, we have

\displaystyle F\left(\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}\right)

\displaystyle=F\left(\frac{q_{i}^{I}\Delta_{i,u}^{\ast}}{q_{i}^{II}}\right)\geq\frac{q_{i}^{I}}{q_{i}^{II}}F\left(\Delta_{i,u}^{\ast}\right)\geq\frac{q_{i}^{II}}{q_{i}^{I}}\frac{3c_{11}^{2}\underline{\mathfrak{f}}-c_{11}^{3}\bar{\mathfrak{f}}^{\prime}}{6}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left(\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\Delta_{i,u}^{\ast}\right)^{2}=\frac{\left(3c_{11}^{2}\underline{\mathfrak{f}}-c_{11}^{3}\bar{\mathfrak{f}}^{\prime}\right)q_{i}^{I}q_{i}^{II}}{6}.

Combining these two cases, we have

\displaystyle A_{4,i}

\displaystyle\geq\min\left(\frac{3c_{11}^{2}\underline{\mathfrak{f}}-c_{11}^{3}\bar{\mathfrak{f}}^{\prime}}{6}\left(q_{i}^{II}\right)^{2},\frac{\left(3c_{11}^{2}\underline{\mathfrak{f}}-c_{11}^{3}\bar{\mathfrak{f}}^{\prime}\right)q_{i}^{I}q_{i}^{II}}{6}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\min\left(\frac{\left(3c_{11}^{2}\underline{\mathfrak{f}}-c_{11}^{3}\bar{\mathfrak{f}}^{\prime}\right)c_{\phi}\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}\right\|_{2}^{2}}{12},\frac{\left(3c_{11}^{2}\underline{\mathfrak{f}}-c_{11}^{3}\bar{\mathfrak{f}}^{\prime}\right)\sqrt{c_{\phi}}q_{i}^{I}\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}\right\|_{2}}{6\sqrt{2}}\right),

(B.32)

where the second inequality holds by Lemma
B.14
(i).

As for
\left|A_{1,i}\right|
, we notice that

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{2}}\left(\left|A_{1,i}\right|/\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}\right\|_{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\max_{i\in I_{2}}\frac{\left|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left\{\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq u_{i}^{0\prime}\left(\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}-\Phi_{it}^{0}\right)\right\}\right)\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}}\right\}\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}\right|}{\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}\right\|_{2}}

\displaystyle=\max_{i\in I_{2}}\frac{\left|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}\left(\tau-\mathfrak{F}_{it}\left[u_{i}^{0\prime}\left(\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}-\Phi_{it}^{0}\right)\right]\right)\right|}{\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}\right\|_{2}}

\displaystyle=\max_{i\in I_{2}}\frac{\left|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}\left[\mathfrak{f}_{it}(s_{it})u_{i}^{0\prime}\left(\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}-\Phi_{it}^{0}\right)\right]\right|}{\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}\right\|_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i\in I_{2}}\frac{\bar{\mathfrak{f}}}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}\left\|\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}-\Phi_{it}^{0}\right\|_{2}\left\|u_{i}^{0}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i\in I_{2}}\sqrt{\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}\right\|^{2}}\sqrt{\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}-\Phi_{it}^{0}\right\|^{2}}\left\|u_{i}^{0}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq O_{p}(\eta_{N}),

(B.33)

where the second and third equalities hold by Assumption
1
(vii)
and mean-value theorem with some
\left|s_{it}\right|\in\left(0,\left|u_{i}^{0\prime}(\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}-\Phi_{it}^{0})\right|\right),
the second inequality holds by Cauchy-Schwarz
inequality, and the third inequality holds by Lemmas
B.13
(i),
B.13
(iii) and
B.14
(ii).

For
A_{3,i}
, note that

\displaystyle A_{3,i}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\int_{0}^{\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq s\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)-\left\{\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq s\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}}\right)\right\}ds

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{1}\biggl\{\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}s^{\ast}\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)

\displaystyle-\left[\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}s^{\ast}\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}}\right)\right]\biggr\}^{\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}ds^{\ast}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{s\in\mathbb{R}}\left\|A_{3,i}^{I}(s)\right\|_{2}\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}\right\|_{2}

(B.34)

by change of variables with
s^{\ast}=\frac{s}{\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}}
,
A_{3,i}^{I}(s)=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}A_{3,it}^{I}(s)
and

A_{3,it}^{I}(s)=\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}\left[\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq s\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)-\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq s\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}}\right)\right].

Below, we aim to show
\operatornamewithlimits{\sup}_{s\in(-\infty,+\infty)}\max_{i\in I_{2}}\left\|A_{3,i}^{I}(s)\right\|_{2}=O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}\right).

When
\left|s\right|>T^{1/4}
, we notice that

\displaystyle\sup_{\left|s\right|>T^{1/4}}\max_{i\in I_{2}}\left\|A_{3,i}^{I}(s)\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\left|s\right|>T^{1/4}}\max_{i\in I_{2}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq s\right\}-\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq s\right\}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}}\right)\right]\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle+\max_{i\in I_{2}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}}\right)\right]\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i\in I_{2}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}>T^{1/4}\right\}+\max_{i\in I_{2}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}>T^{1/4}\right\}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}}\right)

\displaystyle+\max_{i\in I_{2}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}}\right)\right]\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\max_{i\in I_{2}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}>T^{1/4}\right\}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}}\right)

\displaystyle+\max_{i\in I_{2}}\left|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}>T^{1/4}\right\}-\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}>T^{1/4}\right\}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}}\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle+\max_{i\in I_{2}}\left|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}}\right)\right]\right|,

where the first and third inequalities hold by triangle inequality. Besides,
we observe that

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{2}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}>T^{1/4}\right\}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}}\right)

\displaystyle=\max_{i\in I_{2}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}\mathbb{P}\left(\epsilon_{it}>T^{1/4}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}}\right)\leq\max_{i\in I_{2}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}\frac{\mathbb{E}\left(\epsilon_{it}^{2}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}}\right)}{\sqrt{T}}

\displaystyle\leq T^{-1/2}\max_{i\in I_{2}}\sqrt{\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}^{2}}\max_{i\in I_{2}}\sqrt{\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left[\mathbb{E}\left(\epsilon_{it}^{2}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}\right)\right]^{2}}=O_{p}\left(T^{-1/2}\right),

(B.35)

where the last equality holds by Lemma
B.13
(iii) and
Assumption
1
(iv). For a positive constant
c_{12}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{i\in I_{2}}\left|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}>T^{1/4}\right\}-\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}>T^{1/4}\right\}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}}\right)\right]\right|>c_{12}\frac{\xi_{N}\sqrt{\log(N\vee T)}}{\sqrt{T}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\mathbb{E}\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}>T^{1/4}\right\}-\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}>T^{1/4}\right\}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}}\right)\right]\right|>c_{12}\frac{\xi_{N}\sqrt{\log(N\vee T)}}{\sqrt{T}}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\exp\left\{-\frac{c_{9}c_{12}^{2}T\xi_{N}^{2}\log(N\vee T)}{\frac{16TM^{2}}{c_{\sigma}^{2}}\left(1+p\xi_{N}^{2}\right)+\frac{4Mc_{12}}{c_{\sigma}}\sqrt{1+p\xi_{N}}\sqrt{T}\xi_{N}\sqrt{\log(N\vee T)}\log T\left(\log\log T\right)}\right\}=o(1),

where the first inequality holds by the union bound and Assumption
1
(i), and the second inequality holds by Assumption
1
(iii)
and the conditional Bernstein’s inequality in Lemma
B.12
(i) with
the fact that
\max_{i\in I_{2},t\in[T]}

\left\|\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}\leq\frac{2M}{c_{\sigma}}\sqrt{1+p\xi_{N}^{2}}

w.p.a.1. Here, we can apply the conditional Bernstein’s inequality because
\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}
and
\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}>T^{1/4}\right\}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}}\right)

are deterministic given
\left\{\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}}\right\}_{i\in I_{2},t\in[T]}
so that the only randomness comes from
\{\epsilon_{it}\}_{t\in[T]}
. Furthermore, the joint distribution of
\{\epsilon_{it}\}_{t\in[T]}
given
\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}}
is the same as that given the
\sigma
-field generated by
\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}
due to the independence
structure assumed in Assumption
1
(i). Last, given the
\sigma
-field generated by
\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}
,
\{\epsilon_{it}\}_{t\in[T]}
is
strong mixing with mixing coefficient
\alpha_{i}(\cdot)
as assumed by
Assumption
1
(iii). Similarly, we obtain that

\max_{i\in I_{2}}\left|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}}\right)\right]\right|=O_{p}\left(\frac{\sqrt{\log(N\vee T)}\xi_{N}}{\sqrt{T}}\right),

which implies

\sup_{\left|s\right|>T^{1/4}}\max_{i\in I_{2}}\left|A_{3,i}^{I}(s)\right|=O_{p}(\eta_{N}).

(B.36)

For
|s|\leq T^{1/4}
, let
\mathbb{S}=[-T^{1/4},T^{1/4}]
and divide
\mathbb{S}
into
\mathbb{S}_{m}
for
m=1,\cdots,n_{\mathbb{S}}
such that
\left|s-\bar{s}\right|<\frac{\varepsilon}{T}
for
s
and
\bar{s}\in\mathbb{S}_{m}
and
n_{\mathbb{S}}\asymp T^{5/4}
. Let
s_{m}\in\mathbb{S}_{m}
. For any
s\in\mathbb{S}_{m}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}A_{3,it}^{I}(s)\right\|_{2}\leq\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}A_{3,it}^{I}\left(s_{m}\right)\right\|_{2}+\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left[A_{3,it}^{I}\left(s\right)-A_{3,it}^{I}\left(s_{m}\right)\right]\right\|_{2},

(B.37)

such that

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{2},m\in[n_{\mathbb{S}}]}\sup_{s\in\mathbb{S}_{m}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left[A_{3,it}^{I}\left(s\right)-A_{3,it}^{I}\left(s_{m}\right)\right]\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\max_{i\in I_{2},m\in[n_{\mathbb{S}}]}\sup_{s\in\mathbb{S}_{m}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq s\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq s_{m}\right\}\right)-\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}\left[\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq s\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq s_{m}\right\}\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}}_{e_{i}}\right)\right]\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i\in I_{2},m\in[n_{\mathbb{S}}]}\frac{2}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\in\left[s_{m}-\frac{\varepsilon}{T},s_{m}+\frac{\varepsilon}{T}\right]\right\}\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}}_{e_{i}}\right)

\displaystyle+\max_{i\in I_{2},m\in[n_{\mathbb{S}}]}\left|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}\left[\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\in\left[s_{m}-\frac{\varepsilon}{T},s_{m}+\frac{\varepsilon}{T}\right]\right\}\right)-\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\in\left[s_{m}-\frac{\varepsilon}{T},s_{m}+\frac{\varepsilon}{T}\right]\right\}\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}}_{e_{i}}\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle:=\max_{i\in I_{2},m\in[n_{\mathbb{S}}]}\frac{2}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\in\left[s_{m}-\frac{\varepsilon}{T},s_{m}+\frac{\varepsilon}{T}\right]\right\}\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}}_{e_{i}}\right)+\max_{i\in I_{2},m\in[n_{\mathbb{S}}]}\left|A_{3,i}^{II}(m)\right|.

(B.38)

For the first term in (
B.2
), we notice that

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{2},m\in[n_{\mathbb{S}}]}\frac{2}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\in\left[s_{m}-\frac{\varepsilon}{T},s_{m}+\frac{\varepsilon}{T}\right]\right\}\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}}_{e_{i}}\right)

\displaystyle=\max_{i\in I_{2},m\in[n_{\mathbb{S}}]}\frac{2}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}\left(\mathfrak{F}_{it}(s_{m}+\frac{\varepsilon}{T})-\mathfrak{F}_{it}(s_{m}-\frac{\varepsilon}{T})\right)

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i\in I_{2}}\frac{2}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}\frac{2\varepsilon}{T}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{6M\varepsilon}{c_{\sigma}T}\left(1+\sqrt{p}C\right)\hskip 9.24994pt\text{w.p.a.1},

(B.39)

where the first inequality is by mean-value theorem, and the second
inequality is due to the fact that

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{2}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i\in I_{2}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\sqrt{\frac{4M^{2}}{c_{\sigma}}\left(1+p\max_{j\in[p]}\left|X_{j,it}\right|^{2}\right)}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i\in I_{2}}\frac{2M}{c_{\sigma}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left(1+\sqrt{p}\max_{j\in[p]}\left|X_{j,it}\right|\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2M\left(1+\sqrt{p}C\right)}{c_{\sigma}}\hskip 9.24994pt\text{w.p.a.1}.

For
A_{3,i}^{II}(m)
, let
A_{3,i}^{II}(m)=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}A_{3,it}^{II}(m)
with

\displaystyle A_{3,it}^{II}(m)=\left\|\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}\left[\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\in\left[s_{m}-\frac{\varepsilon}{T},s_{m}+\frac{\varepsilon}{T}\right]\right\}\right)-\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\in\left[s_{m}-\frac{\varepsilon}{T},s_{m}+\frac{\varepsilon}{T}\right]\right\}\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}}_{e_{i}}\right)\right].

We first observe that

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{2},m\in[n_{\mathbb{S}}],t\in[T]}Var\left(A_{3,it}^{II}(m)\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}}_{e_{i}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i\in I_{2},t\in[T]}\left\|\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\in\left[s_{m}-\frac{\varepsilon}{T},s_{m}+\frac{\varepsilon}{T}\right]\right\}\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}}_{e_{i}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i\in I_{2},t\in[T]}\left\|\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}^{2}\frac{2\varepsilon}{T}\lesssim\frac{\xi_{N}^{2}\varepsilon}{T},\hskip 9.24994ptw.p.a.1,

where the first inequality is by
Var(x)\leq\mathbb{E}(x^{2})
for any
random variable
x
and the second inequality is by the mean-value theorem
and Assumption
1
(v). Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{2},m\in[n_{\mathbb{S}}],t\in[T]}\sum_{s=t+1}^{T}\left|Cov\left(A_{3,it}^{II}(m),A_{3,is}^{II}(m)\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}}_{e_{i}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i\in I_{2},m\in[n_{\mathbb{S}}],t\in[T]}\sum_{s=t+1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left(\left|A_{3,it}^{II}(m)\right|^{3}\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}}_{e_{i}}\right)^{1/3}\mathbb{E}\left(\left|A_{3,is}^{II}(m)\right|^{3}\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}}_{e_{i}}\right)^{1/3}\left[\alpha(t-s)\right]^{1/3}

\displaystyle\lesssim\max_{i\in I_{2},m\in[n_{\mathbb{S}}],t\in[T]}\mathbb{E}\left(\left|A_{3,it}^{II}(m)\right|^{3}\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}}_{e_{i}}\right)^{2/3}\lesssim\xi_{N}^{2}\left(\frac{\varepsilon}{T}\right)^{2/3},\hskip 9.24994ptw.p.a.1.

It follows that

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{2},m\in[n_{\mathbb{S}}],t\in[T]}\left\{Var\left(A_{3,it}^{II}(m)\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}}_{e_{i}}\right)+2\sum_{s=t+1}^{T}\left|Cov\left(A_{3,it}^{II}(m),A_{3,is}^{II}(m)\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}}_{e_{i}}\right)\right|\right\}\leq c_{13}\xi_{N}^{2}\left(\frac{\varepsilon}{T}\right)^{2/3},\hskip 9.24994ptw.p.a.1.

and
\max_{i\in I_{2},m\in[n_{\mathbb{S}}],t\in[T]}\left|A_{3,it}^{II}(m)\right|\leq C_{13}\xi_{N}
w.p.a.1 for positive
constants
c_{13}
and
c_{14}
. Denote the events

\displaystyle\mathscr{A}_{3,N}=\begin{pmatrix}&\max_{i\in I_{2},m\in[n_{\mathbb{S}}],t\in[T]}\left\{Var\left(A_{3,it}^{II}(m)\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}}_{e_{i}}\right)+2\sum_{s=t+1}^{T}\left|Cov\left(A_{3,it}^{II}(m),A_{3,is}^{II}(m)\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}}_{e_{i}}\right)\right|\right\}\leq c_{13}\xi_{N}^{2}\left(\frac{\varepsilon}{T}\right)^{2/3}\\
&\text{and}\hskip 9.24994pt\max_{i\in I_{2},t\in[T]}\left\|\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}\leq c_{14}\xi_{N}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle\mathscr{A}_{3,N,i}=\begin{pmatrix}&\max_{m\in[n_{\mathbb{S}}],t\in[T]}\left\{Var\left(A_{3,it}^{II}(m)\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}}_{e_{i}}\right)+2\sum_{s=t+1}^{T}\left|Cov\left(A_{3,it}^{II}(m),A_{3,is}^{II}(m)\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}}_{e_{i}}\right)\right|\right\}\leq c_{13}\xi_{N}^{2}\left(\frac{\varepsilon}{T}\right)^{2/3}\\
&\text{and}\hskip 9.24994pt\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}\leq c_{14}\xi_{N}\end{pmatrix}.

Then, we have
\mathbb{P}(\mathscr{A}_{3,N}^{c})\rightarrow 0
and

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{i\in I_{2},m\in[n_{\mathbb{S}}]}\left|A_{3,i}^{II}(m)\right|>c_{12}\eta_{N}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{i\in I_{2},m\in[n_{\mathbb{S}}]}\left|A_{3,i}^{II}(m)\right|>c_{12}\eta_{N},\mathscr{A}_{3,N}\right)+\mathbb{P}(\mathscr{A}_{3,N}^{c})

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\in[I_{2}]}\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{m\in[n_{\mathbb{S}}]}\left|A_{3,i}^{II}(m)\right|>c_{12}\eta_{N},\mathscr{A}_{3,N}\right)+\mathbb{P}(\mathscr{A}_{3,N}^{c})

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\in[I_{2}]}\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{m\in[n_{\mathbb{S}}]}\left|A_{3,i}^{II}(m)\right|>c_{12}\eta_{N},\mathscr{A}_{3,N,i}\right)+\mathbb{P}(\mathscr{A}_{3,N}^{c})

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\in[I_{2}]}\mathbb{E}\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{m\in[n_{\mathbb{S}}]}\left|A_{3,i}^{II}(m)\right|>c_{12}\eta_{N}\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}}_{e_{i}}\right)\mathbf{1}\{\mathscr{A}_{3,N,i}\}+\mathbb{P}(\mathscr{A}_{3,N}^{c})

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\in I_{2},m\in[n_{\mathbb{S}}]}\exp\left(-\frac{c_{9}c_{12}^{2}T^{2}\eta_{N}^{2}}{c_{13}T\xi_{N}^{2}\left(\frac{\varepsilon}{T}\right)^{2/3}+c_{14}^{2}\xi_{N}^{2}+c_{12}c_{14}T\eta_{N}\xi_{N,1}\left(\log T\right)^{2}}\right)+o(1)=o(1),

where second inequality is by the union bound, the first inequality is by
\mathscr{A}_{3,N}\subset\mathscr{A}_{3,N,i}
, the equality is by the fact
that
\mathscr{A}_{3,N,i}
is
\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}}_{e_{i}}
measurable, and
the last inequality is by Lemma
B.12
(ii), the definition of
\mathscr{A}_{3,N,i}
, and the fact that
\{\epsilon_{it}\}_{t\in[T]}
is
strong mixing given
\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}}_{e_{i}}
. This implies

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{2},m\in[n_{\mathbb{S}}]}\sup_{s\in\mathbb{S}_{m}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left[A_{3,it}^{I}\left(s\right)-A_{3,it}^{I}\left(s_{m}\right)\right]\right\|_{2}=O_{p}(\eta_{N}).

Last, we turn to the first term of (
B.37
). Denote
A_{3,i}^{I,k}(s_{m})

as the
k^{th}
element of
A_{3,i}^{I,k}(s_{m})
and the event set
\mathscr{A}_{4,N,i}=\{\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}\leq c_{14}\xi_{N}\}
. Similarly, we have
\mathbb{P}(\cap_{i\in I_{2}}\mathscr{A}_{4,N,i}^{c})=\mathbb{P}(\max_{i\in I_{2},t\in[T]}\left\|\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}>c_{14}\xi_{N})=o(1)
.
Following the same argument as above, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{i\in I_{2},m\in[n_{\mathbb{S}}]}\left|A_{3,i}^{I,k}(s_{m})\right|>c_{12}\eta_{N}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{m\in[n_{\mathbb{S}}],i\in I_{2}}\mathbb{E}\mathbb{P}\left(\left|A_{3,i}^{I,k}(s_{m})\right|>\frac{c_{12}}{2}\eta_{N}\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}}_{e_{i}}\right)1\{\mathscr{A}_{4,N,i}\}+o(1)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{m\in[n_{\mathbb{S}}],i\in I_{2}}\exp\left(-\frac{c_{9}c_{12}^{2}T^{2}\eta_{N}^{2}/4}{c_{14}^{2}T\xi_{N}^{2}+c_{12}c_{14}T\eta_{N}\xi_{N}\log T\left(\log\log T\right)/2}\right)+o(1)=o(1),

(B.40)

where the second inequality combines Lemma
B.12
(i). Combining (
B.2
), (
B.36
) and (
B.2
), we have

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{2}}\frac{\left|A_{3,i}\right|}{\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}\right\|_{2}}=O_{p}(\eta_{N}).

We now turn to
A_{2,i}
. Let
A_{2,it}^{I}=\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{w_{1,it}\leq 0\right\}\right)\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}

-\mathbb{E}\left(\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{w_{1,it}\leq 0\right\}\right)\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}}\right).
Then
A_{2,i}=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(A_{2,it}^{I}\right)^{\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}.
By conditional Bernstein’s inequality and similarly as (
B.2
), we can show that

\mathbb{P}\left\{\max_{i\in I_{2}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}A_{2,it}^{I}\right\|_{2}\geq c_{12}\eta_{N}\right\}=o(1),

which implies
\max_{i\in I_{2}}\frac{\left|A_{2,i}\right|}{\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}\right\|_{2}}=O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}\right).

By similar arguments for
A_{3,i}
, we can also show that
\max_{i\in I_{2}}\frac{\left|A_{5,i}\right|}{\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}\right\|_{2}}=O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}\right).
For
A_{6,i}
, we note
that

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{2}}\frac{\left|A_{6,i}\right|}{\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}\right\|_{2}}

\displaystyle=\max_{i\in I_{2}}\frac{\left|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\int_{0}^{\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}}\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{w_{1,it}\leq s\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq s\right\}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}}\right)ds\right|}{\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}\right\|_{2}}

\displaystyle=\max_{i\in I_{2}}\frac{\left|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\int_{0}^{\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}}\left[\mathfrak{F}_{it}\left(u_{i}^{0\prime}\left(\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}-\Phi_{it}^{0}\right)+s\right)-\mathfrak{F}_{it}(s)\right]ds\right|}{\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}\right\|_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i\in I_{2}}\frac{\left|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\int_{0}^{\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}}u_{i}^{0\prime}\left(\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}-\Phi_{it}^{0}\right)\mathfrak{f}_{it}(s)ds\right|}{\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}\right\|_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i\in I_{2}}\frac{\frac{\bar{\mathfrak{f}}}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left|\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}\right|\left|u_{i}^{0\prime}\left(\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}-\Phi_{it}^{0}\right)\right|}{\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}\right\|_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\bar{\mathfrak{f}}\max_{i\in I_{2}}\sqrt{\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}\right\|^{2}}\sqrt{\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)}-\Phi_{it}^{0}\right\|^{2}}\left\|u_{i}^{0}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}\right),

where the first inequality is by mean-value theorem and the other
inequalities holds by similar reasons as those in (
B.2
).

Last, for
A_{7,i}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left[\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{w_{1,it}\leq 0\right\}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{w_{1,it}\leq 0\right\}\right)\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}}_{e_{i}}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\{\left[\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{w_{1,it}\leq 0\right\}\right)\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{\Phi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{w_{1,it}\leq 0\right\}\right)\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}}_{e_{i}}\right]\right\}

\displaystyle:=A_{7,i}^{I}+A_{7,i}^{II},

Then, following the same analyses of
A_{1,i}
and
A_{3,i}
, we have

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{2}}\frac{\left|A_{7,i}^{I}\right|}{\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}\right\|_{2}}=O_{p}(\eta_{N})\hskip 9.24994pt\text{and}\hskip 9.24994pt\max_{i\in I_{2}}\frac{\left|A_{7,i}^{II}\right|}{\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}\right\|_{2}}=O_{p}(\eta_{N}),

which implies
\max_{i\in I_{2}}\frac{\left|A_{7,i}\right|}{\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}\right\|_{2}}=O_{p}(\eta_{N})
.

Recall that event
\mathscr{A}_{4}=\left\{\max_{i\in I_{2}}\left\|O_{j}^{(1)\prime}\dot{u}_{i,j}^{(1)}-u_{i,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}=O\left(\eta_{N}\right),\forall j\in[p]\cup\{0\}\right\}
where we define
q_{i}^{III}=\inf_{\Delta}\frac{\left[\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\left(\tilde{\Psi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\Delta\right)^{2}\right]^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\left|\tilde{\Psi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\Delta\right|^{3}}
.

Lemma B.16

. Suppose Assumptions
1
–
5
hold. Then for
\{B_{1,t},\cdots,B_{6,t}\}_{t\in[T]}
defined in (
A.2.2
), for
any constant
0<c_{11}<\min(\frac{3\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}{\bar{\mathfrak{f}}^{\prime}},1)
, we have

\displaystyle\max_{t\in[T]}\frac{|B_{m,t}|}{\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right\|_{2}}=O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}\right)\hskip 9.24994pt\forall m\in\left\{1,2,3,5,6\right\},

\displaystyle|B_{4,t}|\geq\min\left(\frac{\left(3c_{11}^{2}\underline{\mathfrak{f}}-c_{11}^{3}\bar{\mathfrak{f}}^{\prime}\right)c_{\psi}\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right\|_{2}^{2}}{12},\frac{\left(3c_{11}^{2}\underline{\mathfrak{f}}-c_{11}^{3}\bar{\mathfrak{f}}^{\prime}\right)\sqrt{c_{\psi}}q_{i}^{III}\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right\|_{2}}{6\sqrt{2}}\right),\hskip 9.24994pt\forall t\in[T].

Proof.
We first deal with
B_{4,t}
. Let
w_{4,it}=\dot{\Psi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}
. Following the same arguments as used to derive the lower
bound for
A_{4,i}
in the proof of Lemma
B.15
by replacing
w_{2,it}

with
w_{4,it}
, we can show that, for
t\in[T]
and any constants
c_{11}<\min(\frac{3\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}{\bar{\mathfrak{f}}^{\prime}},1)
,

\left|B_{4,t}\right|\geq\min\left(\frac{\left(3c_{11}^{2}\underline{\mathfrak{f}}-c_{11}^{3}\bar{\mathfrak{f}}^{\prime}\right)c_{\psi}\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right\|_{2}^{2}}{12},\frac{\left(3c_{11}^{2}\underline{\mathfrak{f}}-c_{11}^{3}\bar{\mathfrak{f}}^{\prime}\right)\sqrt{c_{\psi}}q_{i}^{III}\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right\|_{2}}{6\sqrt{2}}\right).

For
B_{1,t}
, we have

B_{1,t}=\left(\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\Psi_{it}^{0}\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)\right)^{\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}:=\left(\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}B_{1,it}^{I}\right)^{\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}.

Conditional on the fixed effects
\left\{V_{j}^{0}\right\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}
and
\left\{W_{j}^{0}\right\}_{j\in[p]}
, the randomness in
B_{1,t}
is from
\left\{\epsilon_{it}\right\}_{i\in I_{2},t\in[T]}
and
\left\{e_{j,it}\right\}_{j\in[p],i\in I_{2},t\in[T]}
, which are
independent across
i
. Owing to this, by conditional Hoeffding’s
inequality, we can show that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left\{\max_{t\in[T]}\frac{1}{N_{2}}\left\|\sum_{i\in I_{2}}B_{1,it}^{I,k}\right\|_{2}>c_{15}\eta_{N}\bigg|\mathscr{D}\right\}\leq\sum_{t\in[T]}\mathbb{P}\left\{\left\|\sum_{i\in I_{2}}B_{1,it}^{I,k}\right\|_{2}>c_{15}N_{2}\eta_{N}\bigg|\mathscr{D}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{t\in[T]}\exp\left(-\frac{c_{15}^{2}N_{2}^{2}\eta_{N}^{2}}{4M^{2}\xi_{N}^{2}N_{2}}\right)=o(1),

(B.41)

with
B_{1,it}^{I,k}
being the
k^{th}
element in
B_{1,it}^{I}
,
c_{15}

is a positive constant, where the second inequality is by Hoeffding’s
inequality with the fact that
\max_{i\in I_{2},t\in[T]}\left|B_{1,it}^{I,k}\right|\leq M\xi_{N}
a.s. by Assumption
1
(v)
and Lemma
B.13
(i). It follows that
\max_{t\in[T]}\frac{\left|B_{1,t}\right|}{\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right\|_{2}}=O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}\right).
If we use the
conditional Bernstein’s inequality for the independent sequence rather than
the Hoeffding’s inequality above, we can show that
\max_{t\in[T]}\frac{1}{N_{2}}\left\|\sum_{i\in I_{2}}B_{1,it}^{I,k}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log N\vee T}{N}}\right)
, but here we only need to show the
uniform convergence rate to be
\eta_{N}
.

Let
X_{0,it}=1.
As for
B_{2,t}
, note that

\displaystyle\max_{t\in[T]}\frac{\left|B_{2,t}\right|}{\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right\|_{2}}

\displaystyle=\max_{t\in[T]}\frac{\left|\left[\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\left(\dot{\Psi}_{it}^{(1)}-\Psi_{it}^{0}\right)\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)\right]^{\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right|}{\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right\|_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\left(\dot{\psi}_{it}^{(1)}-\Psi_{it}^{0}\right)\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{t\in[T]}\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\left\|\dot{\Psi}_{it}^{(1)}-\Psi_{it}^{0}\right\|_{2}=\max_{t\in[T]}\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\sqrt{\sum_{j=0}^{p}\left\|O_{0}^{(1)\prime}\dot{u}_{i,j}^{(1)}-u_{i,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}^{2}X_{j,it}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i\in I_{2}}\max_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\left(||O_{0}^{(1)\prime}\dot{u}_{i,j}^{(1)}-u_{i,j}^{0}||_{2}\right)\left[\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\sum_{j=0}^{p}X_{j,it}^{2}\right]^{1/2}=O_{p}(\eta_{N}),

(B.42)

where the first inequality is by Cauchy’s inequality, the second inequality
is by Jensen’s inequality, and the last equality is by Theorem
3.2
(i)
and Assumption
1
(iv).

Next, we deal with
B_{3,t}
.
Following similar arguments as used in (
B.42
), we obtain that

\max_{t\in[T]}\frac{\left|\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\left(\dot{\Psi}_{it}^{(1)}-\Psi_{it}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{w_{3,it}\leq 0\right\}\right]\right|}{\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right\|_{2}}=O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}\right),

which implies

\displaystyle\max_{t\in[T]}\frac{\left|B_{3,t}\right|}{2\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right\|_{2}}

\displaystyle=\max_{t\in[T]}\frac{\left|\left\{\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\Psi_{it}^{0}\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{w_{3,it}\leq 0\right\}\right]\right\}^{\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right|}{\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right\|_{2}}+O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\Psi_{it}^{0}\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{w_{3,it}\leq 0\right\}\right]\right\|_{2}+O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\max_{t\in[T]}\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\left\|\Psi_{it}^{0}\right\|_{2}\mathbf{1}\left\{|\epsilon_{it}|\leq\sum_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\left\|v_{t,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}\left\|\dot{u}_{i,j}^{(1)}-O_{j}^{(1)}u_{i,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}\right\}+O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}\right)

\displaystyle\equiv\Xi_{NT}+O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}\right).

Now define the event set
\mathscr{B}_{N,1}(M)=\{\max_{i\in I_{2},j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}||\dot{u}_{i,j}^{(1)}-O_{j}^{(1)}u_{i,j}^{0}||\leq M\eta_{N}\}
.
Then, Theorem
3.2
(i) implies, for any
e>0
, there is a sufficiently
large
M
such that
\mathbb{P}(\mathscr{B}_{N,1}^{c}(M))\leq e
. Recall
that
\mathscr{D}_{e_{it}}
is the
\sigma
-field generated by
e_{it}\cup\left\{V_{j}^{0}\right\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\cup\left\{W_{j}^{0}\right\}_{j\in[p]}
. Then, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\Xi_{NT}\geq C\eta_{N}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\Xi_{NT}\geq C\eta_{N},\mathscr{B}_{N,1}\right)+e

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\left\|\Psi_{it}^{0}\right\|_{2}\mathbf{1}\left\{|\epsilon_{it}|\leq M\eta_{N}\sum_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\left\|v_{t,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}\right\}\geq C\eta_{N}\right)+e

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{t\in[T]}B_{3,t}^{I}\geq C\eta_{N}+\max_{t\in T}B_{3,t}^{II}\right)+e,

(B.43)

where

\displaystyle B_{3,t}^{I}
