\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\left[\left\|\Psi_{it}^{0}\right\|_{2}\mathbf{1}\left\{|\epsilon_{it}|\leq M\eta_{N}\sum_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\left\|v_{t,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}\right\}-B_{3,t}^{II}\right]\hskip 9.24994pt\text{and}

\displaystyle B_{3,t}^{II}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|\Psi_{it}^{0}\right\|_{2}\mathbf{1}\left\{|\epsilon_{it}|\leq M\eta_{N}\sum_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\left\|v_{t,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}\right\}\bigg|\{\mathscr{D}_{e_{it}}\}_{i\in I_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|\Psi_{it}^{0}\right\|_{2}\mathbf{1}\left\{|\epsilon_{it}|\leq M\eta_{N}\sum_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\left\|v_{t,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}\right\}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{it}}\right),

and the second equality for
B_{3,t}^{II}
holds by Assumption
1
(i). Following this, we show that

\displaystyle\max_{t\in[T]}B_{3,t}^{II}

\displaystyle=\max_{t\in[T]}\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\left\|\Psi_{it}^{0}\right\|_{2}\left[\mathfrak{F}_{it}\left(M\eta_{N}\sum_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\left\|v_{t,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}\right)-\mathfrak{F}_{it}\left(-M\eta_{N}\sum_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\left\|v_{t,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\max_{t\in[T]}\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\sqrt{1+p\max_{j\in[p]}\left|X_{j,it}\right|^{2}}\frac{2(1+p)M^{2}}{c_{\sigma}}\eta_{N}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2(1+p)^{2}M^{2}C}{c_{\sigma}}\eta_{N}\hskip 9.24994pt\text{a.s.},

(B.44)

where
\mathfrak{F}_{it}(\cdot)
is the conditional CDF of
\epsilon_{it}

given
\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}
who also has bounded PDF by Assumption
1
(vii), the first inequality is by mean-value theorem and facts that
\left\|\Psi_{it}^{0}\right\|_{2}^{2}\leq M^{2}\left(1+p\max_{j\in[p]}\left|X_{j,it}\right|^{2}\right)
together with Lemma
B.13
(i), and the second inequality is due to Assumption
1
(iv). In addition, given
\{\mathscr{D}_{e_{it}}\}_{i\in I_{2}}
,

\{\epsilon_{it}\}_{i\in I_{2}}
are still independent across
i
.
Therefore, by Hoeffding’s inequality and similar arguments for term
B_{1,t}

in (
B.2
), we can show that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|B_{3,t}^{I}\right\|_{2}>c_{12}\eta_{N}\right)\leq\sum_{t\in[T]}\mathbb{P}\left(\left\|B_{3,t}^{I}\right\|_{2}>c_{12}\eta_{N}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{t\in[T]}\mathbb{E}\mathbb{P}\left(\left\|B_{3,t}^{I}\right\|_{2}>c_{12}\eta_{N}\bigg|\{\mathscr{D}_{e_{it}}\}_{i\in I_{2}}\right)=o(1).

(B.45)

Combining (
B.43
)-(
B.2
), we obtain that
\max_{t\in[T]}\frac{\left|B_{3,t}\right|}{\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right\|_{2}}=O_{p}(\eta_{N}).

For
B_{5,t}
, we observe that

\displaystyle B_{5,t}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\left\{\int_{0}^{\Psi_{it}^{0\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}}\left[\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq s\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)-\mathbb{E}\left(\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq s\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{it}}\right)\right]ds\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\left\{\int_{\Psi_{it}^{0\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}}^{\dot{\Psi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}}\left[\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq s\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)-\mathbb{E}\left(\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq s\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{it}}\right)\right]ds\right\}

\displaystyle:=B_{5,t}^{I}+B_{5,t}^{II}.

Following similar arguments for
A_{3,i}
with Bernstein’s inequality
replacing by Hoeffding’s inequality, we can show that
\max_{t\in[T]}\frac{\left|B_{5,t}^{I}\right|}{\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right\|_{2}}=O_{p}(\eta_{N})
. Besides, we obtain that

\max_{t\in[T]}\frac{\left|B_{5,t}^{II}\right|}{\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right\|_{2}}\leq\max_{t\in[T]}\frac{4}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\left\|\dot{\Psi}_{it}^{(1)}-\Psi_{it}^{0}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}(\eta_{N}),

which implies
\max_{t\in[T]}\frac{\left|B_{5,t}\right|}{\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right\|_{2}}=O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}\right).

For
B_{6,t}
, we first note that

\max_{t\in[T]}\frac{\left|\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\int_{\Psi_{it}^{0\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}}^{\dot{\Psi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}}\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq s\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{w_{3,it}\leq s\right\}\right]\right|}{\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right\|_{2}}\leq\max_{t\in[T]}\frac{2}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\left\|\dot{\Psi}_{it}^{(1)}-\Psi_{it}^{0}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}(\eta_{N}),

and this implies

\displaystyle\max_{t\in[T]}\frac{\left|B_{6,t}\right|}{\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right\|_{2}}

\displaystyle=\max_{t\in[T]}\frac{\left|\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\int_{0}^{\dot{\Psi}_{it}^{(1)\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{w_{3,it}\leq s\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq s\right\}\right)ds\right|}{\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right\|_{2}}

\displaystyle=\max_{t\in[T]}\frac{\left|\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\int_{0}^{\Psi_{it}^{0\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{w_{3,it}\leq s\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq s\right\}\right)ds\right|}{\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right\|_{2}}+O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}\right).

In addition, for the first term on the RHS of the last equality, we have

\displaystyle\max_{t\in[T]}\frac{\left|\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\int_{0}^{\Psi_{it}^{0\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{w_{3,it}\leq s\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq s\right\}\right)ds\right|}{\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right\|_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{t\in[T]}\frac{\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\int_{0}^{\left\|\Psi_{it}^{0}\right\|_{2}\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right\|_{2}}\left(\mathbf{1}\left\{|\epsilon_{it}-s|\leq\sum_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\left\|v_{t,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}\left\|\dot{u}_{i,j}^{(1)}-O_{j}^{(1)}u_{i,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}\right\}\right)ds}{\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right\|_{2}}

\displaystyle=\max_{t\in[T]}\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\int_{0}^{1}\left\|\Psi_{it}^{0}\right\|_{2}\mathbf{1}\left\{\left|\epsilon_{it}-\left\|\Psi_{it}^{0}\right\|_{2}\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right\|_{2}s\right|\leq\sum_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\left\|v_{t,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}\left\|\dot{u}_{i,j}^{(1)}-O_{j}^{(1)}u_{i,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}\right\}ds

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{s\geq 0,t\in[T]}\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\left\|\Psi_{it}^{0}\right\|_{2}\mathbf{1}\left\{\left|\epsilon_{it}-s\right|\leq\sum_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\left\|v_{t,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}\left\|\dot{u}_{i,j}^{(1)}-O_{j}^{(1)}u_{i,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}\right\}.

Following the same argument for
B_{3,t}
, we only need to upper bound the
RHS of the last display by
\sup_{s\geq 0,t\in[T]}B_{6,t}^{I}(s)
on
\mathscr{B}_{N,1}(M)
for some sufficiently large but fixed constant
M
,
where

B_{6,t}^{I}(s)=\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\left\|\Psi_{it}^{0}\right\|_{2}\mathbf{1}\left\{\left|\epsilon_{it}-s\right|\leq M\eta_{N}\sum_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\left\|v_{t,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}\right\}.

Let

B_{6,t}^{II}(s)=\mathbb{E}(B_{6,t}^{I}(s)|\left\{\mathscr{D}_{e_{it}}\right\}_{i\in I_{2}})=\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|\Psi_{it}^{0}\right\|_{2}\mathbf{1}\left\{\left|\epsilon_{it}-s\right|\leq M\eta_{N}\sum_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\left\|v_{t,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}\right\}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{it}}\right).

We note that
\sup_{s\geq 0,t\in[T]}B_{6,t}^{II}(s)=O_{p}(\eta_{N}).

Similar to the arguments in (
B.2
), to show
\max_{t\in[T]}\frac{\left|B_{6,t}\right|}{\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right\|_{2}}=O_{p}(\eta_{N})
, it suffices to show

\mathbb{P}\left(\sup_{s\geq 0,t\in[T]}\left|B_{6,t}^{I}(s)-B_{6,t}^{II}(s)\right|>c_{12}\eta_{N}\right)=o(1).

(B.46)

Further denote

\displaystyle B_{6,t}^{III}(s)=\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\left\|\Psi_{it}^{0}\right\|_{2}\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}>s-M\eta_{N}\sum_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\left\|v_{t,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}\right\},

\displaystyle B_{6,t}^{IV}(s)=\frac{1}{N_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\left\|\Psi_{it}^{0}\right\|_{2}\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\geq s+M\eta_{N}\sum_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\left\|v_{t,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}\right\}.

Then, we have
B_{6,t}^{I}(s)=B_{6,t}^{III}(s)-B_{6,t}^{IV}(s)
and thus,

\displaystyle\sup_{s>T^{1/4},t\in[T]}\left|B_{6,t}^{I}(s)-B_{6,t}^{II}(s)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{s>T^{1/4},t\in[T]}\left|B_{6,t}^{III}(s)-\mathbb{E}\left(B_{6,t}^{III}(s)\big|\{\mathscr{D}_{e_{it}}\}_{i\in I_{2}}\right)\right|+\sup_{s>T^{1/4},t\in[T]}\left|B_{6,t}^{IV}(s)-\mathbb{E}\left(B_{6,t}^{IV}(s)\big|\{\mathscr{D}_{e_{it}}\}_{i\in I_{2}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\max_{t\in[T]}B_{6,t}^{III}(T^{1/4})+\max_{t\in[T]}\mathbb{E}\left(B_{6,t}^{III}(T^{1/4})\big|\{\mathscr{D}_{e_{it}}\}_{i\in I_{2}}\right)+\max_{t\in[T]}B_{6,t}^{IV}(T^{1/4})+\max_{t\in[T]}\mathbb{E}\left(B_{6,t}^{IV}(T^{1/4})\big|\{\mathscr{D}_{e_{it}}\}_{i\in I_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\max_{t\in[T]}\left|B_{6,t}^{III}(T^{1/4})-\mathbb{E}\left(B_{6,t}^{III}(T^{1/4})\big|\{\mathscr{D}_{e_{it}}\}_{i\in I_{2}}\right)\right|+2\max_{t\in[T]}\mathbb{E}\left(B_{6,t}^{III}(T^{1/4})\big|\{\mathscr{D}_{e_{it}}\}_{i\in I_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle+\max_{t\in[T]}\left|B_{6,t}^{IV}(T^{1/4})-\mathbb{E}\left(B_{6,t}^{IV}(T^{1/4})\big|\{\mathscr{D}_{e_{it}}\}_{i\in I_{2}}\right)\right|+2\max_{t\in[T]}\mathbb{E}\left(B_{6,t}^{IV}(T^{1/4})\big|\{\mathscr{D}_{e_{it}}\}_{i\in I_{2}}\right),

where the second inequality holds because both
B_{6,t}^{III}(s)
and
B_{6,t}^{IV}(s)
are non-decreasing in
s
. Further note that

\left\|\Psi_{it}^{0}\right\|_{2}\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}>s-M\eta_{N}\sum_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\left\|v_{t,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}\right\}\hskip 9.24994pt\text{and}\hskip 9.24994pt\left\|\Psi_{it}^{0}\right\|_{2}\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}>s+M\eta_{N}\sum_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\left\|v_{t,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}\right\}

are independent across
i\in I_{2}
given
\{\mathscr{D}_{e_{it}}\}_{i\in I_{2}}
. Therefore, following the same argument in the analysis of
A_{6,t}

with the Bernstein’s inequality replaced by the Hoeffding’s inequality, we
have

\displaystyle\max_{t\in[T]}\left|B_{6,t}^{III}(T^{1/4})-\mathbb{E}\left(B_{6,t}^{III}(T^{1/4})\big|\{\mathscr{D}_{e_{it}}\}_{i\in I_{2}}\right)\right|=O_{p}(\eta_{N}),\hskip 9.24994pt\max_{t\in[T]}\mathbb{E}\left(B_{6,t}^{III}(T^{1/4})\big|\{\mathscr{D}_{e_{it}}\}_{i\in I_{2}}\right)=O_{p}(\eta_{N})

\displaystyle\max_{t\in[T]}\left|B_{6,t}^{IV}(T^{1/4})-\mathbb{E}\left(B_{6,t}^{IV}(T^{1/4})\big|\{\mathscr{D}_{e_{it}}\}_{i\in I_{2}}\right)\right|=O_{p}(\eta_{N}),\hskip 9.24994pt\max_{t\in[T]}\mathbb{E}\left(B_{6,t}^{IV}(T^{1/4})\big|\{\mathscr{D}_{e_{it}}\}_{i\in I_{2}}\right)=O_{p}(\eta_{N}),

which implies

\sup_{s>T^{1/4},t\in[T]}\left|B_{6,t}^{I}(s)-B_{6,t}^{II}(s)\right|=O_{p}(\eta_{N}).

In addition, following the same analysis in (
B.37
) and (
B.2
), we have

\sup_{s\in[0,T^{1/4}],t\in[T]}\left|B_{6,t}^{I}(s)-B_{6,t}^{II}(s)\right|=O_{p}(\eta_{N}),

which leads to the desired result that
\max_{t\in[T]}\frac{\left|B_{6,t}\right|}{\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right\|_{2}}=O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}\right).

Recall for
i\in I_{3}
,
\dot{\mathcal{H}}_{i}\left(\left\{u_{i,j}\right\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}}\right)=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{\left[\tau-\mathfrak{F}_{it}\left(g_{it}(\{u_{i,j}\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}})\right)\right]\dot{\varpi}_{it}\right\},
where

g_{it}(\{u_{i,j}\}_{j\in[p]\cup\{0\}})=u_{i,0}^{\prime}\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}+\sum_{j\in[p]}u_{i,j}^{\prime}\dot{v}_{t,j}^{(1)}X_{j,it}-u_{i,0}^{0\prime}v_{t,0}^{0}-\sum_{j\in[p]}u_{i,j}^{0\prime}v_{t,j}^{0}X_{j,it},

and
\dot{\varpi}_{it}=\left(\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)\prime},X_{1,it}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)\prime},\cdots,X_{p,it}\dot{v}_{t,p}^{(1)\prime}\right)^{\prime}
.

Lemma B.17

Under Assumptions
1
–
5
, the
second-order derivative of
\dot{\mathcal{H}}_{i}\left(\left\{u_{i,j}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\right)
is bounded in probability.

Proof.
Noted that

\displaystyle\frac{\dot{\mathcal{H}}_{i}\left(\left\{u_{i,j}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\right)}{\partial u_{i}^{\prime}}=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}-\mathfrak{f}_{it}\left(u_{i,0}^{\prime}\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}-u_{i,0}^{0\prime}v_{t,0}^{0}+X_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{\prime}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-X_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)\dot{\varpi}_{it}\dot{\varpi}_{it}^{\prime}.

For notation simplicity, we focus on the case with
p=1
and denote
u_{i}=\left(u_{i,0}^{\prime},u_{i,1}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}
. Further
denote
\dot{\mathcal{H}}_{i,k}\left(\left\{u_{i,j}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\right)

as the
k^{th}
element in
\dot{\mathcal{H}}_{i}\left(\left\{u_{i,j}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\right)
and
\dot{v}_{t,0,k}^{(1)}
as the
k^{th}
element in
\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}
. For
k\in\left[K_{0}\right]
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\dot{\mathcal{H}}_{i,k}\left(\left\{u_{i,j}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\right)}{\partial u_{i}}=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}-\mathfrak{f}_{it}\left(u_{i,0}^{\prime}\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}-u_{i,0}^{0\prime}v_{t,0}^{0}+X_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{\prime}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-X_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)\dot{v}_{t,0,k}^{(1)}\dot{\varpi}_{it}\hskip 9.24994pt\text{and}

\displaystyle\frac{\partial^{2}\dot{\mathcal{H}}_{i,k}\left(\left\{u_{i,j}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\right)}{\partial u_{i}\partial u_{i}^{\prime}}=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}-\mathfrak{f}_{it}^{\prime}\left(u_{i,0}^{\prime}\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}-u_{i,0}^{0\prime}v_{t,0}^{0}+X_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{\prime}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-X_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)\dot{v}_{t,0,k}^{(1)}\dot{\varpi}_{it}\dot{\varpi}_{it}^{\prime}.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial^{2}\dot{\mathcal{H}}_{i,k}\left(\left\{u_{i,j}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\right)}{\partial u_{i}\partial u_{i}^{\prime}}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\bar{\mathfrak{f}}^{\prime}}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\dot{v}_{t,0,k}^{(1)}\dot{\varpi}_{it}\dot{\varpi}_{it}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq c\bar{\mathfrak{f}}^{\prime}\left[\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}\right]\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\dot{\varpi}_{it}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq c\bar{\mathfrak{f}}^{\prime}\left[\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}\right]\left[\max_{t\in[T]}(\left\|\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\left\|\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}^{2})\right]\left(1+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}X_{1,it}^{2}\right)=O_{p}(1),

where we use the fact that
\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}(1)
by Theorem
3.2
(ii) and Lemma
B.13
(i).

For
k\in\left[K_{0}+1,\cdots,K_{0}+K_{1}\right]
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\dot{\mathcal{H}}_{i,k}\left(\left\{u_{i,j}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\right)}{\partial u_{i}}=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}-\mathfrak{f}_{it}\left(u_{i,0}^{\prime}\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}-u_{i,0}^{0\prime}v_{t,0}^{0}+X_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{\prime}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-X_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)\dot{v}_{t,1,k-K_{0}}^{(1)}X_{1,it}\dot{\varpi}_{it}\hskip 9.24994pt\text{and}

\displaystyle\frac{\partial^{2}\dot{\mathcal{H}}_{i,k}\left(\left\{u_{i,j}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\right)}{\partial u_{i}\partial u_{i}^{\prime}}=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}-\mathfrak{f}_{it}^{\prime}\left(u_{i,0}^{\prime}\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}-u_{i,0}^{0\prime}v_{t,0}^{0}+X_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{\prime}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-X_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)\dot{v}_{t,1,k-K_{0}}^{(1)}X_{1,it}\dot{\varpi}_{it}\dot{\varpi}_{it}^{\prime}.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial^{2}\dot{\mathcal{H}}_{i,k}\left(\left\{u_{i,j}\right\}_{j=0}^{p}\right)}{\partial u_{i}\partial u_{i}^{\prime}}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq c\bar{\mathfrak{f}}^{\prime}\left[\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}\right]\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}|X_{1,it}|\left\|\dot{\varpi}_{it}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq c\bar{\mathfrak{f}}^{\prime}\left[\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}\right]\left[\max_{t\in[T]}(\left\|\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\left\|\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}^{2})\right]\left(1+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in T}X_{1,it}^{3}\right)=O_{p}(1).

Recall that

\displaystyle\dot{\varpi}_{it}=\left(\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)\prime},\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)\prime}X_{1,it},\cdots,\dot{v}_{t,p}^{(1)\prime}X_{p,it}\right)^{\prime},

\displaystyle\varpi_{it}^{0}=\left(\left(O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}\right)^{\prime},\left(O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}X_{1,it},\cdots,\left(O_{p}^{(1)}v_{t,p}^{0}\right)^{\prime}X_{p,it}\right)^{\prime},

\displaystyle\dot{D}_{i}^{I}:=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathfrak{f}_{it}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}^{\prime}\Psi_{it}^{0}\right)\dot{\varpi}_{it}\dot{\varpi}_{it}^{\prime},\hskip 9.24994pt\dot{D}_{i}^{II}:=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left[\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}^{\prime}\Psi_{it}^{0}\right\}\right]\dot{\varpi}_{it},

\displaystyle D_{i}^{I}=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathfrak{f}_{it}(0)\varpi_{it}^{0}\varpi_{it}^{0\prime},\hskip 9.24994ptD_{i}^{II}=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left[\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right]\varpi_{it}^{0}.

Lemma B.18

Under Assumptions
1
–
5
, we have

(i)

\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|D_{i}^{II}\right\|_{F}=O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log\left(N\vee T\right)}{T}}\xi_{N}\right)
,

(ii)

\max_{i\in\in I_{3}}\left\|\dot{D}_{i}^{I}-D_{i}^{I}\right\|_{F}=O_{p}(\eta_{N})
,

(iii)

\max_{i\in\in I_{3}}\left\|\dot{D}_{i}^{II}-D_{i}^{II}-\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}^{\prime}\Psi_{it}^{0}\right\}\right]\varpi_{it}^{0}\right\|_{2}=o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)
.

Proof.
Throughout the proof, we assume there is only one regressor
p=1
for
notation simplicity.

(i) We notice that
\mathbb{E}\left(D_{i}^{II}\bigg|\mathscr{D}\right)=0
.
By conditional Bernstein’s inequality, for a positive constant
c_{16}
, we
have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\sum_{t\in[T]}\left[\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right]O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}X_{1,it}\right\|_{2}>c_{16}\sqrt{T\log(N\vee T)}\xi_{N}\bigg|\mathscr{D}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\in I_{3}}\exp\left(-\frac{c_{9}c_{16}^{2}T\xi_{N}^{2}\log(N\vee T)}{\frac{4M^{2}}{c_{\sigma}^{2}}T\xi_{N}^{2}+\frac{2Mc_{16}}{c_{\sigma}}\xi_{N}^{2}\sqrt{T\log(N\vee T)}\log T\log\log T}\right)=o(1),

where the inequality follows from Lemma
B.12
(i), Assumption
1
(ii), Assumption
1
(v), and the fact that
\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left\|\left[\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right]O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}X_{1,it}\right\|_{2}\leq\frac{2M}{c_{\sigma}}\xi_{N}
a.s. Similar arguments hold for the upper block of
D_{i}^{II}
. This concludes the proof of (i).

(ii) Notice that

\dot{D}_{i}^{I}-D_{i}^{I}=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\begin{bmatrix}\mathfrak{f}_{it}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}^{\prime}\Psi_{it}^{0}\right)\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}-\mathfrak{f}_{it}(0)v_{t,0}^{0}v_{t,0}^{0\prime}&\mathfrak{f}_{it}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}^{\prime}\Psi_{it}^{0}\right)\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}X_{1,it}-\mathfrak{f}_{it}(0)v_{t,0}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}X_{1,it}\\
\mathfrak{f}_{it}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}^{\prime}\Psi_{it}^{0}\right)\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}X_{1,it}-\mathfrak{f}_{it}(0)v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,0}^{0\prime}X_{1,it}&\mathfrak{f}_{it}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}^{\prime}\Psi_{it}^{0}\right)\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}X_{1,it}^{2}-\mathfrak{f}_{it}(0)v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}X_{1,it}^{2}\end{bmatrix}.

To show the upper bound of
\dot{D}_{i}^{I}-D_{i}^{I}
, we take the lower
block for instance and all other three blocks follow the same pattern. Noted
that

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left[\mathfrak{f}_{it}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}^{\prime}\Psi_{it}^{0}\right)\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)\prime}X_{1,it}^{2}-\mathfrak{f}_{it}(0)v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}X_{1,it}^{2}\right]\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left[\mathfrak{f}_{it}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}^{\prime}\Psi_{it}^{0}\right)-\mathfrak{f}_{it}(0)\right]\left[\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)\prime}-v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}\right]X_{1,it}^{2}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle+\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left[\mathfrak{f}_{it}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}^{\prime}\Psi_{it}^{0}\right)-\mathfrak{f}_{it}(0)\right]v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}X_{1,it}^{2}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle+\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\mathfrak{f}_{it}(0)\left[\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)\prime}-v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}\right]X_{1,it}^{2}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(\eta_{N}),

(B.47)

where the equality is by the fact that

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left|\mathfrak{f}_{it}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}^{\prime}\Psi_{it}^{0}\right)-\mathfrak{f}_{it}(0)\right|\lesssim\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left\|\Psi_{it}^{0}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right\|_{2},\hskip 9.24994pt\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}+\left\|v_{t,1}^{0}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}(1),

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{3}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}X_{1,it}^{2}\leq C,\hskip 9.24994pt\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)\prime}-v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}\right\|_{F}\leq\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left(\left\|\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}+\left\|v_{t,1}^{0}\right\|_{2}\right)\left\|\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-v_{t,1}^{0}\right\|_{2},

and

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left[\mathfrak{f}_{it}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}^{\prime}\Psi_{it}^{0}\right)-\mathfrak{f}_{it}(0)\right]\left[\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)\prime}-v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}\right]X_{1,it}^{2}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\bar{\mathfrak{f}}^{\prime}\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right\|_{2}\left\|\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)\prime}-v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}\right\|_{F}\max_{i\in I_{3}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|\Psi_{it}^{0}\right\|_{2}\left|X_{1,it}\right|^{2}=O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right).

(iii) Note that

\displaystyle\dot{D}_{i}^{II}-D_{i}^{II}-\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}^{\prime}\Psi_{it}^{0}\right\}\right]\varpi_{it}^{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}^{\prime}\Psi_{it}^{0}\right\}\right]\left(\dot{\varpi}_{it}-\varpi_{it}^{0}\right)+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left[\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right]\left(\dot{\varpi}_{it}-\varpi_{it}^{0}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left\{\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}^{\prime}\Psi_{it}^{0}\right\}\right]\left(\dot{\varpi}_{it}-\varpi_{it}^{0}\right)\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}^{\prime}\Psi_{it}^{0}\right\}\right]\left(\dot{\varpi}_{it}-\varpi_{it}^{0}\right)-\mathbb{E}\left[\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}^{\prime}\Psi_{it}^{0}\right\}\right]\left(\dot{\varpi}_{it}-\varpi_{it}^{0}\right)\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right]\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left[\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right]\left(\dot{\varpi}_{it}-\varpi_{it}^{0}\right)

\displaystyle:=S_{1,i}+S_{2,i}+S_{3,i},

where

\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|S_{1,i}\right\|_{2}\leq\max_{i\in I_{3}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|\dot{\varpi}_{it}-\varpi_{it}^{0}\right\|_{2}\left|\mathfrak{F}_{it}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}^{\prime}\Psi_{it}^{0}\right)-\mathfrak{F}_{it}(0)\right|\lesssim\max_{i\in I_{3}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T],j\in[p]}\left|X_{j,it}^{2}\right|\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right\|_{2}^{2}=O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right).

As for
S_{2,i}
and
S_{3,i}
, we first recall, for any
e>0
, there exists
a sufficiently large constant
M
such that for

\mathscr{A}_{7}(M)=\left\{\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|O_{j}^{(1)\prime}\dot{u}_{i,j}^{(1)}-u_{i,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}\leq M\eta_{N},\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|O_{j}^{(1)\prime}\dot{v}_{t,j}^{(1)}-v_{t,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}\leq M\eta_{N},\forall j\in[p]\cup\{0\}\right\}

we have
\mathbb{P}(\mathscr{A}_{7}^{c}(M))\leq e
. In addition, let

\mathscr{A}_{7,i}(M)=\left\{\left\|O_{j}^{(1)\prime}\dot{u}_{i,j}^{(1)}-u_{i,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}\leq M\eta_{N},\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|O_{j}^{(1)\prime}\dot{v}_{t,j}^{(1)}-v_{t,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}\leq M\eta_{N},\forall j\in[p]\cup\{0\}\right\}.

Then, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|S_{2,i}\right\|_{2}>c_{17}\eta_{N}^{2}\right)\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|S_{2,i}\right\|_{2}>c_{17}\eta_{N}^{2},\mathscr{A}_{7}(M)\right)+e

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\in I_{3}}\mathbb{P}\left(\left\|S_{2,i}\right\|_{2}>c_{17}\eta_{N}^{2},\mathscr{A}_{7}(M)\right)+e

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\in I_{3}}\mathbb{P}\left(\left\|S_{2,i}\right\|_{2}>c_{17}\eta_{N}^{2},\mathscr{A}_{7,i}(M)\right)+e

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\in I_{3}}\mathbb{E}\mathbb{P}\left(\left\|S_{2,i}\right\|_{2}>c_{17}\eta_{N}^{2}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)1\{\mathscr{A}_{7,i}(m)\}+e

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\in I_{2}}\exp\left(-\frac{c_{9}c_{17}^{2}T^{2}\eta_{N}^{4}}{c_{18}T\eta_{N}^{2}\xi_{N}^{2}+c_{17}c_{18}^{1/2}T\eta_{N}^{3}\xi_{N}\log T\log\log T}\right)+e=o(1)+e

with a positive constant
c_{17}
and the inequality above is by Lemma
B.12
(i) with the fact that, under
\mathscr{A}_{6,i}(M)
,

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left\|\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}^{\prime}\Psi_{it}^{0}\right\}\right]\left(\dot{\varpi}_{it}-\varpi_{it}^{0}\right)-\mathbb{E}\left[\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}^{\prime}\Psi_{it}^{0}\right\}\right]\left(\dot{\varpi}_{it}-\varpi_{it}^{0}\right)\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e_{i}}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right]\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq c_{18}\eta_{N}\xi_{N}.

As
e
is arbitrary, we have
\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|S_{2,i}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}(\eta_{N}^{2})
. Following a similar argument,
we have
\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|S_{3,i}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}(\eta_{N}^{2})
. By Assumption
1
(ix),
we note that
O_{p}(\eta_{N}^{2})=o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-1/2}\right)
.

B.3
Lemmas for the Proof Theorem
3.3

Lemma B.19

Define
H_{x,j}^{l}=\left(\frac{\hat{L}_{j}^{\prime}L_{j}^{0}}{N}\right)^{-1}
and
H_{x,j}^{w}=W_{j}^{0\prime}\hat{W}_{j}\left(\hat{W}_{j}^{\prime}\hat{W}_{j}\right)^{-1}
. Under Assumptions
6
-
8
, we have

(i)

H_{x,j}^{l}=H_{x,j}^{w}+O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{N\wedge T}\right)
,

(ii)

\frac{1}{N}\left\|\hat{L}_{j}-L_{j}^{0}H_{x,j}^{w}\right\|_{F}^{2}=O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{N\wedge T}\right)
,

(iii)

\frac{1}{T}\left\|\hat{W}_{j}-W_{j}\left(H_{x,j}^{l\prime}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{F}^{2}=O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{N\wedge T}\right)
.

Proof.
The proof can be found in
Bai and Ng, 2020
.

Lemma B.20

Under Assumptions
6
-
8
, we have

(i)

\frac{1}{N}L_{j}^{0\prime}\left(\hat{L}_{j}-L_{j}^{0}H_{x,j}^{w}\right)=O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{N\wedge T}\right)
,

(ii)

\frac{1}{T}W_{j}^{0\prime}\left(\hat{W}_{j}-W_{j}^{0}\left(H_{x,j}^{l\prime}\right)^{-1}\right)=O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{N\wedge T}\right)
,

(iii)

\max_{t\in[T]}\frac{1}{N}\left(\hat{L}_{j}-L_{j}^{0}H_{x,j}^{w}\right)^{\prime}e_{j,t}=O_{p}\left(\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N\wedge T}\right)
,

(iv)

\max_{i\in[N]}\frac{1}{T}\left(\hat{W}_{j}-W_{j}^{0}\left(H_{x,j}^{l\prime}\right)^{-1}\right)^{\prime}e_{j,i}=O_{p}\left(\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N\wedge T}\right)
.

Proof.
Statements (i) and (ii) are the same as Lemma 4(i) and (ii) in
Bai and Ng, 2020
. Statements (iii) and (iv) are the uniform version of Lemma
4(iii) and (iv) in
Bai and Ng, 2020
. Below we focus on part (iv) as the
proof of part (iii) follows analogously.

Noting that
\hat{W}_{j}^{\prime}-\left(H_{x,j}^{l}\right)^{-1}W_{j}^{0\prime}=\frac{1}{N}\hat{L}_{j}^{\prime}X_{j}-\frac{\hat{L}_{j}^{\prime}L_{j}^{0}}{N}W_{j}^{0\prime}=\frac{1}{N}\hat{L}_{j}^{\prime}E_{j},
we have

\frac{1}{T}\left(\hat{W}_{j}-W_{j}^{0}\left(H_{x,j}^{l\prime}\right)^{-1}\right)^{\prime}e_{j,i}=\frac{1}{NT}e_{j,i}^{\prime}E_{j}^{\prime}\hat{L}_{j}=\frac{1}{NT}e_{j,i}^{\prime}E_{j}^{\prime}L_{j}^{0}H_{x,j}^{w}+\frac{1}{NT}e_{j,i}^{\prime}E_{j}^{\prime}\left(\hat{L}_{j}-L_{j}^{0}H_{x,j}^{w}\right).

For the first term on the right side,

\max_{i\in[N]}\frac{1}{NT}e_{j,i}^{\prime}E_{j}^{\prime}L_{j}^{0}H_{x,j}^{w}\lesssim\max_{i\in[N]}\frac{1}{NT}\left\|e_{j,i}^{\prime}E_{j}^{\prime}L_{j}^{0}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}\left(\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N\wedge T}\right),

by Assumption
8
(i). For the second term on the right side, we have

\displaystyle\max_{i\in[N]}\frac{1}{NT}\left\|e_{j,i}^{\prime}E_{j}^{\prime}\left(\hat{L}_{j}-L_{j}^{0}H_{x,j}^{w}\right)\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i\in[N]}\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}T}\left\|e_{j,i}^{\prime}E_{j}^{\prime}\right\|_{2}\frac{\left\|\hat{L}_{j}-L_{j}^{0}H_{x,j}^{w}\right\|_{F}}{\sqrt{N}}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{\sqrt{N\wedge T}}\right)O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{N\wedge T}}\right),

by Assumption
6
(iv) and Lemma
B.19
(ii). Combining the above
results completes the proof of part (iv).

Lemma B.21

Under Assumption
1
and Assumptions
6
-
8
, we have
\forall j\in[p]
,

(i)

\hat{w}_{j,t}-\left(H_{x,j}^{l}\right)^{-1}w_{j,t}^{0}=H_{x,j}^{l\prime}\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i\in[N]}l_{j,i}^{0}e_{j,it}+\mathcal{R}_{w,t}
,

(ii)

\hat{l}_{j,i}-H_{x,j}^{w\prime}l_{j,i}^{0}=\left(\frac{W_{j}^{0\prime}W_{j}^{0}}{T}\right)^{-1}\left(H_{x,j}^{l}\right)^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}w_{j,t}^{0}e_{j,it}+\mathcal{R}_{l,i}
,

(iii)

\hat{\mu}_{j,it}-\mu_{j,it}=e_{j,it}-\hat{e}_{j,it}=w_{j,t}^{0\prime}\left(\frac{W_{j}^{0\prime}W_{j}^{0}}{T}\right)^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}w_{j,t}^{0}e_{j,it}+l_{j,i}^{0\prime}\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i\in[N]}l_{j,i}^{0}e_{j,it}+\mathcal{R}_{j,it}
,

such that

\displaystyle\max_{t\in[T]}\left|\mathcal{R}_{w,t}\right|=O_{p}\left(\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N\wedge T}\right),\hskip 9.24994pt\max_{i\in[N]}\left|\mathcal{R}_{l,i}\right|=O_{p}\left(\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N\wedge T}\right),\hskip 9.24994pt\max_{i\in[N],t\in[T],j\in[p]}\left|\mathcal{R}_{j,it}\right|=O_{p}\left(\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N\wedge T}\right),

\displaystyle\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\hat{w}_{j,t}-\left(H_{x,j}^{l}\right)^{-1}w_{j,t}^{0}\right\|_{F}=O_{p}(\eta_{N}),\hskip 9.24994pt\max_{i\in[N]}\left\|\hat{l}_{j,i}-H_{x,j}^{w\prime}l_{j,i}^{0}\right\|_{F}=O_{p}(\eta_{N}),

\displaystyle\max_{j\in[p],i\in[N],t\in[T]}\left|e_{j,it}-\hat{e}_{j,it}\right|=\max_{j\in[p],i\in[N],t\in[T]}\left|\hat{\mu}_{j,it}-\mu_{j,it}\right|=O_{p}(\eta_{N}),

with
\eta_{N}=\frac{\sqrt{\log\left(N\vee T\right)}}{\sqrt{N\wedge T}}\xi_{N}^{2}
.

Proof.
Recall that
X_{j,it}=\mu_{j,it}+e_{j,it}=l_{j,i}^{0\prime}w_{j,t}^{0}+e_{j,it}
and
X_{j}=L_{j}^{0}W_{j}^{0\prime}+E_{j}
in
matrix form, where
L_{j}^{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{N\times r_{j}}
is the factor
loading and
W_{j}^{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{T\times r_{j}}
is the factor matrix.
Following
Bai and Ng, 2002
,
Bai, 2003
and
Bai and Ng, 2020
, if we impose the normalization restrictions that

\frac{L_{j}^{0\prime}L_{j}^{0}}{N}=I_{r_{j}}\hskip 9.24994pt\text{and}\hskip 9.24994pt\frac{W_{j}^{0\prime}W_{j}^{0}}{T}\hskip 9.24994pt\text{is a diagonal matrix with descending
diagonal elements,}

we have the principal components estimators:

\hat{L}_{j}=X_{j}\hat{W}_{j}\left(\hat{W}_{j}^{\prime}\hat{W}_{j}\right)^{-1}\text{ and }\hat{W}_{j}^{\prime}=\left(\hat{L}_{j}^{\prime}\hat{L}_{j}\right)^{-1}\hat{L}_{j}^{\prime}X_{j}=\frac{1}{N}\hat{L}_{j}^{\prime}X_{j}.

(B.48)

Let
H_{x,j}^{l}=\left(\frac{1}{N}\hat{L}_{j}^{\prime}L_{j}^{0}\right)^{-1}.
Premultiplying
\frac{1}{N}\hat{L}_{j}^{\prime}
on both sides of
X_{j}=L_{j}^{0}W_{j}^{0\prime}+E_{j}
yields

\frac{1}{N}\hat{L}_{j}^{\prime}X_{j}=\frac{1}{N}\hat{L}_{j}^{\prime}L_{j}^{0}W_{j}^{0\prime}+\frac{1}{N}\hat{L}_{j}^{\prime}E_{j}.

It follows that

\displaystyle\hat{W}_{j}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\left(H_{x,j}^{l}\right)^{-1}W_{j}^{0\prime}+\frac{1}{N}\hat{L}_{j}^{0\prime}E_{j}

\displaystyle=\left(H_{x,j}^{l}\right)^{-1}W_{j}^{0\prime}+\frac{1}{N}\left(H_{x,j}^{l}\right)^{\prime}L_{j}^{0\prime}E_{j}+\frac{1}{N}\left[\hat{L}-L_{j}^{0}H_{x,j}^{l}\right]^{\prime}E_{j}.

We then show the expansion for each factor, i.e.,

\hat{w}_{j,t}-\left(H_{x,j}^{l}\right)^{-1}w_{j,t}^{0}=H_{x,j}^{l\prime}\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i\in[N]}l_{j,i}^{0}e_{j,it}+\frac{1}{N}\left[\hat{L}_{j}-L_{j}^{0}H_{x,j}^{l}\right]^{\prime}e_{j,t}.

(B.49)

For equation (
B.49
), we have the uniform bound for the second term,
i.e.,

\max_{t\in[T]}\frac{1}{N}\left[\hat{L}_{j}-L_{j}^{0}H_{x,j}^{l}\right]^{\prime}e_{j,t}=O_{p}\left(\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N\wedge T}\right)

by Lemma
B.20
(iii). With
c_{19}
being a positive constant and
\max_{i\in[N],t\in[T]}\left|l_{j,i}^{0}e_{j,it}\right\|_{2}\leq c_{19}\xi_{N}
a.s., we show that

\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i\in[N]}l_{j,i}^{0}e_{j,it}\right\|_{2}>c_{20}\sqrt{\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N}}\xi_{N}\right)\leq\max_{t\in[T]}2\exp\left(-\frac{c_{20}^{2}N\xi_{N}^{2}\log(N\vee T)}{N4c_{19}^{2}\xi_{N}^{2}}\right)=o(1)

(B.50)

for a positive constant
c_{20}
by Hoeffding’s inequality. It follows that
\hat{w}_{j,t}-\left(H_{x,j}^{l}\right)^{-1}w_{j,t}^{0}=H_{x,j}^{l\prime}\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i\in[N]}l_{j,i}^{0}e_{j,it}+\mathcal{R}_{w,t},
such that

\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\hat{w}_{j,t}-\left(H_{x,j}^{l}\right)^{-1}w_{j,t}^{0}\right\|_{F}=O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{T}}\xi_{N}\right),

(B.51)

and
\max_{t\in[T]}\left|\mathcal{R}_{w,t}\right|=O_{p}\left(\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N\wedge T}\right)
.

Similarly, if we premultiply
\hat{W}_{j}\left(\hat{W}_{j}^{\prime}\hat{W}_{j}\right)^{-1}
to both sides of
X_{j}=L_{j}^{0}W_{j}^{0\prime}+E_{j}
,
it yields

X_{j}\hat{W}_{j}\left(\hat{W}_{j}^{\prime}\hat{W}_{j}\right)^{-1}=L_{j}^{0}W_{j}^{0\prime}\hat{W}_{j}\left(\hat{W}_{j}^{\prime}\hat{W}_{j}\right)^{-1}+E_{j}\hat{W}_{j}\left(\hat{W}_{j}^{\prime}\hat{W}_{j}\right)^{-1}.

It follows that

\displaystyle\hat{L}_{j}

\displaystyle=L_{j}^{0}H_{x,j}^{w}+E_{j}\hat{W}_{j}\left(\hat{W}_{j}^{\prime}\hat{W}_{j}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=L_{j}^{0}H_{x,j}^{w}+E_{j}W_{j}^{0}\left(H_{x,j}^{l\prime}\right)^{-1}\left(\hat{W}_{j}^{\prime}\hat{W}_{j}\right)^{-1}+E_{j}\left(\hat{W}_{j}-W_{j}^{0}\left(H_{x,j}^{l\prime}\right)^{-1}\right)\left(\hat{W}_{j}^{\prime}\hat{W}_{j}\right)^{-1},

where the fist line is due to (
B.48
) and the definition that
H_{x,l}=W_{j}^{0\prime}\hat{W}_{j}\left(\hat{W}_{j}^{\prime}\hat{W}_{j}\right)^{-1}
. Then we obtain the expansion for the factor loading

\hat{l}_{j,i}-H_{x,j}^{w\prime}l_{j,i}^{0}=\left(\frac{\hat{W}_{j}^{\prime}\hat{W}_{j}}{T}\right)^{-1}\left(H_{x,j}^{l}\right)^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}w_{j,t}^{0}e_{j,it}+\left(\frac{\hat{W}_{j}^{\prime}\hat{W}_{j}}{T}\right)^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\left[\left(\hat{W}-W\left(H_{x,j}^{l\prime}\right)^{-1}\right)^{\prime}e_{j,i}\right].

Note that

\displaystyle\frac{\hat{W}_{j}^{\prime}\hat{W}_{j}}{T}

\displaystyle=\frac{\left(\hat{W}_{j}-W_{j}^{0}\left(H_{x,j}^{l\prime}\right)^{-1}\right)^{\prime}\hat{W}_{j}}{T}+\frac{\left(H_{x,j}^{l}\right)^{-1}W_{j}^{0\prime}\hat{W}_{j}}{T}

\displaystyle=\frac{\left(\hat{W}_{j}-W_{j}^{0}\left(H_{x,j}^{l\prime}\right)^{-1}\right)^{\prime}\left(\hat{W}_{j}-W_{j}^{0}\left(H_{x,j}^{l\prime}\right)^{-1}\right)}{T}+\frac{\left(\hat{W}_{j}-W_{j}^{0}\left(H_{x,j}^{l\prime}\right)^{-1}\right)^{\prime}W_{j}^{0}\left(H_{x,j}^{l\prime}\right)^{-1}}{T}

\displaystyle+\frac{\left(H_{x,j}^{l}\right)^{-1}W_{j}^{0\prime}\left(\hat{W}_{j}-W_{j}\left(H_{x,j}^{l\prime}\right)^{-1}\right)}{T}+\frac{\left(H_{x,j}^{l}\right)^{\prime}W_{j}^{0\prime}W_{j}^{0}\left(H_{x,j}^{l\prime}\right)^{-1}}{T}

\displaystyle=\frac{\left(H_{x,j}^{l}\right)^{\prime}W_{j}^{0\prime}W_{j}\left(H_{x,j}^{l\prime}\right)^{-1}}{T}+O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{N\wedge T}\right),

(B.52)

where the last equality holds by Lemma
B.19
(iii). Note that
\max_{i\in[N]}\frac{1}{T}\left(\hat{W}-W\left(H_{x,j}^{l\prime}\right)^{-1}\right)^{\prime}e_{i}=O_{p}\left(\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N\wedge T}\right)
by Lemma
B.20
(iv), and we can show that

\max_{i\in[N]}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}w_{j,t}^{0}e_{j,it}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{T}}\xi_{N}\right)

(B.53)

as in (
B.50
) by conditional Bernstein’s inequality in Lemma
B.12
(i) given
\left\{W_{j}^{0}\right\}_{j\in[p]}
. It follows that
\hat{l}_{j,i}-H_{x,j}^{w\prime}l_{j,i}^{0}=\left(\frac{W_{j}^{0\prime}W_{j}^{0}}{T}\right)^{-1}\left(H_{x,j}^{l}\right)^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}w_{j,t}^{0}e_{j,it}+\mathcal{R}_{l,i}\
such that

\max_{i\in[N]}\left\|\hat{l}_{j,i}-H_{x,j}^{w\prime}l_{j,i}^{0}\right\|_{F}=O_{p}(\eta_{N})\ \text{and }\max_{i\in[N]}\left|\mathcal{R}_{l,i}\right|=O_{p}\left(\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N\wedge T}\right).

Then, it’s natural to obtain that

\displaystyle\hat{\mu}_{j,it}-\mu_{j,it}

\displaystyle=\hat{l}_{j,i}^{\prime}\hat{w}_{t}-l_{j,i}^{0\prime}w_{j,t}^{0}

\displaystyle=\left(\hat{l}_{j,i}-\left(H_{x,j}^{l}\right)^{\prime}l_{j,i}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\hat{w}_{j,t}-\left(H_{x,j}^{l}\right)^{-1}w_{j,t}^{0}\right)+\left(\hat{l}_{j,i}-\left(H_{x,j}^{l}\right)^{\prime}l_{j,i}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(H_{x,j}^{l}\right)^{-1}w_{j,t}^{0}

\displaystyle+l_{j,i}^{0\prime}H_{x,j}^{l}\left(\hat{w}_{j,t}-\left(H_{x,j}^{l}\right)^{-1}w_{j,t}^{0}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(\hat{l}_{j,i}-\left(H_{x,j}^{l}\right)^{\prime}l_{j,i}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(H_{x,j}^{l}\right)^{-1}w_{j,t}^{0}+l_{j,i}^{0\prime}H_{x,j}^{l}\left(\hat{w}_{j,t}-\left(H_{x,j}^{l}\right)^{-1}w_{j,t}^{0}\right)+O_{p}\left(\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N\wedge T}\right)

\displaystyle=w_{j,t}^{0}\left(\frac{W_{j}^{0\prime}W_{j}^{0}}{T}\right)^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}w_{j,t}^{0}e_{j,it}+l_{j,i}^{0\prime}\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i\in[N]}l_{j,i}^{0}e_{j,it}+\mathcal{R}_{j,it}

where the second equality holds by statements (i) and (ii), and the last
equality holds with
\max_{i\in[N],t\in[T]}\left|\mathcal{R}_{j,it}\right|=O_{p}\left(\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N\wedge T}\right)
. By
Assumption
1
(iv),
\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|w_{j,t}^{0}\right\|_{2}\leq M
a.s. and
\max_{i\in[N]}\left\|l_{j,i}^{0}\right\|_{2}\leq M
a.s., which leads to
\max_{j\in[p],i\in[N],t\in[T]}\left|\hat{\mu}_{j,it}-\mu_{j,it}\right|=O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log\left(N\vee T\right)}{N\wedge T}}\xi_{N}\right)=O_{p}(\eta_{N})
.

Lemma B.22

Under Assumptions
1
-
9
, for
matrices
\dot{D}_{i}^{F}
and
D_{i}^{F}
defined in the proof of Theorem
3.3
, we have
\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\dot{D}_{i}^{F}-D_{i}^{F}\right\|_{F}=O_{p}(\eta_{N})
with
\eta_{N}=\frac{\sqrt{\log\left(N\vee T\right)}\xi_{N}^{2}}{\sqrt{N\wedge T}}
.

Proof.
Recall that

\displaystyle\dot{D}_{i}^{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}\left[\iota_{it}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right]\begin{bmatrix}\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)\prime}&\hat{e}_{1,it}\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)\prime}\\
\hat{e}_{1,it}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)\prime}&\hat{e}_{1,it}^{2}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)\prime}\end{bmatrix}~\text{and}

\displaystyle D_{i}^{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)\begin{bmatrix}O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}v_{t,0}^{0\prime}O_{0}^{(1)\prime}&0\\
0&e_{1,it}^{2}O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}O_{1}^{(1)\prime}\end{bmatrix},

with
\max_{i\in I_{2}}\left\|D_{i}^{F}\right\|_{F}=O(1)
a.s..

Let
\dot{\iota}_{it}
denote
\iota_{it}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)
for short. We have

\displaystyle\dot{D}_{i}^{F}-D_{i}^{F}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)\begin{bmatrix}\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)\prime}-O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}v_{t,0}^{0\prime}O_{0}^{(1)\prime}&\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)\prime}\hat{e}_{1,it}-O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}O_{1}^{(1)\prime}e_{1,it}\\
\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)\prime}\hat{e}_{1,it}-O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,0}^{0\prime}O_{0}^{(1)\prime}e_{1,it}&\hat{e}_{1,it}^{2}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)\prime}-e_{1,it}^{2}O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}O_{1}^{(1)\prime}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)\begin{bmatrix}0&O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}O_{1}^{(1)\prime}e_{1,it}\\
O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,0}^{0\prime}O_{0}^{(1)\prime}e_{1,it}&0\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(f_{it}\left(\dot{\iota}_{it}\right)-f_{it}(0)\right)\begin{bmatrix}\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)\prime}&\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)\prime}\hat{e}_{1,it}\\
\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)\prime}\hat{e}_{1,it}&\hat{e}_{1,it}^{2}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)\prime}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle:

\displaystyle=F_{1,i}+F_{2,i}+F_{3,i}.

We define
F_{1,i}^{m}
for
m=\left\{1,2,3,4\right\}
as fourth clockwise
blocks in
F_{1,i}:=\begin{bmatrix}F_{1,i}^{1}&F_{1,i}^{2}\\
F_{1,i}^{4}&F_{1,i}^{3}\end{bmatrix}.
Define
F_{2,i}^{m}
and
F_{3,i}^{m}
similarly. We aim to show the
uniform bound for each block.

First, we observe that

\displaystyle F_{1,i}^{1}=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)\left(\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)\prime}-O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}v_{t,0}^{0\prime}O_{0}^{(1)\prime}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)\left[\left(\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}-O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}\right)\left(\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}-O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}\right)^{\prime}+O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}\left(\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}-O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}\right)^{\prime}+\left(\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}-O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}\right)\left(O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\right]

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right)+O_{p}(\eta_{N})=O_{p}(\eta_{N})

(B.54)

uniformly over
i\in I_{3}
. For
F_{1,i}^{2}
, we have by Theorem
3.2

and Lemma
B.21
,

\displaystyle F_{1,i}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)\left(\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)\prime}\hat{e}_{1,it}-O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}O_{1}^{(1)\prime}e_{1,it}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)\Bigg\{\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)\prime}\left(\hat{e}_{1,it}-e_{1,it}\right)+\left(\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)\prime}-O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}O_{1}^{(1)\prime}\right)e_{1,it}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(\eta_{N})\text{ uniformly in }i\in I_{3}.

(B.55)

The same order holds for
\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|F_{1,i}^{3}\right\|_{F}
as
F_{1,i}^{3}=F_{1,i}^{2\prime}
. Next, we
study
F_{1,i}^{4}
. Noting that

|\hat{e}_{1,it}^{2}-e_{1,it}^{2}|=\left|\left(\hat{e}_{1,it}+e_{1,it}\right)\left(\hat{e}_{1,it}-e_{1,it}\right)\right|\leq\left(2|e_{1,it}|+\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}|\hat{e}_{1,it}-e_{1,it}|\right)\left(\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}|\hat{e}_{1,it}-e_{1,it}|\right),

we have

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{3}}||F_{1,i}^{4}||_{F}

\displaystyle=\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)\left(\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)\prime}\hat{e}_{1,it}^{2}-O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}O_{1}^{(1)\prime}e_{1,it}^{2}\right)\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i\in I_{3}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)\left\|\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left(|\hat{e}_{1,it}^{2}-e_{1,it}^{2}|\right)+\max_{i\in I_{3}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)\left\|\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)\prime}-O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}O_{1}^{(1)\prime}\right\|_{F}e_{1,it}^{2}

\displaystyle=\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left(\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}|\hat{e}_{1,it}-e_{1,it}|\right)\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left(\frac{2}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)|e_{1,it}|\right)

\displaystyle+\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left(\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}|\hat{e}_{1,it}-e_{1,it}|^{2}\right)\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left(\frac{2}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)\right)

\displaystyle+\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)\prime}-O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}O_{1}^{(1)\prime}\right\|_{F}\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)e_{1,it}^{2}\right)=O_{p}(\eta_{N}).

(B.56)

Combining (
B.3
)-(
B.3
), we conclude
\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|F_{1,i}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}(\eta_{N}).

For
F_{2,i}^{2}
, we note that
\mathbb{E}\left(f_{it}(0)v_{t,0}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}e_{1,it}\bigg|\mathscr{D}\right)=0
by Assumption
9
and
\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left\|f_{it}(0)v_{t,0}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}e_{1,it}\right\|_{F}\leq c_{22}\xi_{N}~a.s.
by Assumption
1
(iv) and Lemma
B.13
(i). Then, by conditional Bernstein’s inequality in Lemma
B.12
(i) and Assumption
9
(iii), we can show that, with
positive constants
c_{21}
and
c_{22}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}f_{it}(0)v_{t,0}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}e_{1,it}\right\|_{F}>c_{21}\sqrt{\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{T}}\xi_{N}\bigg|\mathscr{D}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\in I_{3}}\exp\left(-\frac{c_{12}c_{21}^{2}T\xi_{N}^{2}\log(N\vee T)}{c_{22}^{2}T\xi_{N}^{2}+c_{21}c_{22}\sqrt{T\log(N\vee T)}\xi_{N}^{2}\log T\log\log T}\right)=o(1),

(B.57)

which yields
\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|F_{2,i}\right\|_{F}=O_{p}(\eta_{N})
.

As for
F_{3,i}
, we show the bound for the first block and all other three
blocks follow the same argument. Recall that

\displaystyle\dot{\iota}_{it}

\displaystyle=u_{i,0}^{0\prime}O_{0}^{(1)}\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}-u_{i,0}^{0\prime}v_{t,0}^{0}+\hat{\mu}_{1,it}\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)\prime}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-\mu_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}+\hat{e}_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}O_{1}^{(1)}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}

\displaystyle=\left[\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}-O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}\right)\right]+\left(\hat{\mu}_{1,it}\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)\prime}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-\mu_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)+\left(\hat{e}_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}O_{1}^{(1)}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)

\displaystyle:=\dot{\iota}_{it}^{I}+\dot{\iota}_{it}^{II}+\dot{\iota}_{it}^{III},

(B.58)

with the fact that
\left|\dot{\iota}_{it}^{I}\right|\leq\left\|O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0}\right\|_{2}\left\|\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}-O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}\right\|_{2}:=R_{1,it}^{I}
such that
\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}R_{1,it}^{I}=O_{p}(\eta_{N})
. In addition, we have

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left|\dot{\iota}_{it}^{II}\right|

\displaystyle=\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left|\left(\hat{\mu}_{1,it}-\mu_{1,it}\right)\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)\prime}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}+\mu_{1,it}\left(\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)\prime}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq R_{1,it}^{II}+R_{2,it}^{II}|\mu_{1,it}|,

(B.59)

where
\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}|R_{1,it}^{II}|=O_{p}(\eta_{N})
and
\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}|R_{2,it}^{II}|=O_{p}(\eta_{N})
. Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\left|\dot{\iota}_{it}^{III}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\hat{e}_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}O_{1}^{(1)}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|(\hat{e}_{1,it}-e_{1,it})\left(O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)+(\hat{e}_{1,it}-e_{1,it})\left(O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}\right|

\displaystyle+\left|e_{1,it}\left(O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)\right|

\displaystyle=R_{1,it}^{III}+R_{2,it}^{III}|e_{1,it}|,

(B.60)

where
\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}|R_{1,it}^{III}|=O_{p}(\eta_{N})
and
\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}|R_{2,it}^{III}|=O_{p}(\eta_{N})
.
Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{3}}||F_{3,i}^{1}||_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left|f_{it}\left(\dot{\iota}_{it}\right)-f_{it}(0)\right|\left\|\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}(R_{1,it}^{I}+R_{1,it}^{II}+R_{2,it}^{II}+R_{1,it}^{III}+R_{2,it}^{III})\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}(1+|e_{1,it}|+|\mu_{1,it}|)

\displaystyle=O_{p}(\eta_{N}),

(B.61)

Combining all results above, we obtain that
\max_{i\in I_{3}}||\hat{D}_{i}^{F}-D_{i}^{F}||_{F}=O_{p}(\eta_{N})
.

Lemma B.23

Under Assumptions
1
-
9
, for
matrices
\dot{D}_{i}^{J}
and
D_{i}^{J}
defined in the proof of Theorem
3.3
, we have

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\dot{D}_{i}^{J}-D_{i}^{J}\right\|_{F}=\left\|\begin{bmatrix}O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right)&O_{p}(\eta_{N})\\
O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right)&O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right)\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F}.

Proof.
Recall that

D_{i}^{J}=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)\begin{bmatrix}\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}-\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime}&0\\
0&e_{1,it}^{2}O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}\left(O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}-\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime}\end{bmatrix}.

We have

\displaystyle\hat{D}_{i}^{J}-D_{i}^{J}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)\begin{bmatrix}0&\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}O_{1}^{(1)\prime}\left(e_{1,it}-\hat{e}_{1,it}\right)+\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\left(O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}-\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime}\left(\hat{e}_{1,it}-e_{1,it}\right)\\
\left(\hat{e}_{1,it}-e_{1,it}\right)\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}-\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime}&\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}O_{1}^{(1)\prime}e_{1,it}\left(\hat{e}_{1,it}-e_{1,it}\right)-\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)\prime}\left(\hat{e}_{1,it}^{2}-e_{1,it}^{2}\right)\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)\begin{bmatrix}0&\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\left(O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}-\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime}e_{1,it}\\
\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}-\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime}e_{1,it}&\left(\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)\left(O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}-\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime}e_{1,it}^{2}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left[f_{it}(\tilde{\iota}_{it})-f_{it}(0)\right]\begin{bmatrix}\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}-\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime}&\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\left(e_{1,it}O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}-\hat{e}_{1,it}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime}\\
\hat{e}_{1,it}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}-\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime}&\hat{e}_{1,it}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\left(e_{1,it}O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}-\hat{e}_{1,it}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle:

\displaystyle=J_{1,i}+J_{2,i}+J_{3,i}.

As in the proof of the last lemma, we define
J_{1,i}^{m}
,
J_{2,i}^{m}

and
J_{3,i}^{m}
for
m=1,2,3,4
as four clockwise blocks in
J_{1,i}
,
J_{2,i}
and
J_{3,i}
, respectively.

First, we study
J_{1,i}.
For
J_{1,i}^{2}
, we notice that

\displaystyle J_{1,i}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}O_{1}^{(1)\prime}\left(e_{1,it}-\hat{e}_{1,it}\right)+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\left(O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}-\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime}\left(\hat{e}_{1,it}-e_{1,it}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}O_{1}^{(1)\prime}\left(e_{1,it}-\hat{e}_{1,it}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)\left(\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}-O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}\right)v_{t,1}^{0\prime}O_{1}^{(1)\prime}\left(e_{1,it}-\hat{e}_{1,it}\right)+O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}O_{1}^{(1)\prime}\left(e_{1,it}-\hat{e}_{1,it}\right)+O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right)+O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=O_{p}(\eta_{N})\hskip 9.24994pt\text{uniformly over }i\in I_{3}.

(B.62)

Noted that the leading term in
J_{1,i}^{2}
is
\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}O_{1}^{(1)\prime}\left(e_{1,it}-\hat{e}_{1,it}\right)
, which will remain as the bias
term of
\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)}
.

Furthermore, it is clear that

J_{1,i}^{3}=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)\left(\hat{e}_{1,it}-e_{1,it}\right)\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}-\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime}=O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right)\hskip 9.24994pt\text{uniformly over }i\in I_{3}.

Next, we obtain that

\displaystyle J_{1,i}^{4}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}O_{1}^{(1)\prime}e_{1,it}\left(\hat{e}_{1,it}-e_{1,it}\right)-\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)\prime}\left(\hat{e}_{1,it}^{2}-e_{1,it}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=O_{1}^{(1)}\left[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}e_{1,it}\left(\hat{e}_{1,it}-e_{1,it}\right)\right]O_{1}^{(1)\prime}-\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)\prime}\left(\hat{e}_{1,it}-e_{1,it}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{2}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)\prime}e_{1,it}\left(\hat{e}_{1,it}-e_{1,it}\right)+O_{p}(\eta_{N}^{2})

\displaystyle=-O_{1}^{(1)}\left[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}e_{1,it}\left(\hat{e}_{1,it}-e_{1,it}\right)\right]O_{1}^{(1)\prime}+O_{p}(\eta_{N}^{2}),

(B.63)

where the first and second equalities hold by Theorem
3.2
(ii) and
Lemma
B.21
. We deal with the first term in the second equality of (
B.3
) by inserting the linear expansion of
\hat{e}_{1,it}-e_{1,it}

in Lemma
B.21
(iii), i.e.,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}e_{1,it}\left(\hat{e}_{1,it}-e_{1,it}\right)

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{f_{it}(0)v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}e_{1,it}\left[w_{1,t}^{0\prime}\left(\frac{W_{1}^{0\prime}W_{1}^{0}}{T}\right)^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}w_{1,t}^{0}e_{1,it}\right]\right\}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{f_{it}(0)v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}\left[e_{1,it}l_{1,i}^{0\prime}\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i^{*}\in[N]}l_{1,i^{*}}^{0}e_{1,i^{*}t}\right]\right\}+O_{p}\left(\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N\wedge T}\right)

(B.64)

uniformly over
i\in I_{3}
. For the first term in the right side of (
B.3
), we notice that

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{f_{it}(0)v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}e_{1,it}\left[w_{1,t}^{0\prime}\left(\frac{W_{1}^{0\prime}W_{1}^{0}}{T}\right)^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}w_{1,t}^{0}e_{1,it}\right]\right\}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{f_{it}(0)v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}e_{1,it}\left[\sum_{k\in[r_{1}]}w_{1,t,k}^{0}\left[\left(\frac{W_{1}^{0\prime}W_{1}^{0}}{T}\right)^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}w_{1,t}^{0}e_{1,it}\right]_{k}\right]\right\}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k\in[r_{1}]}\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\left[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}w_{1,t,k}^{0}e_{1,it}\right]\right\|_{F}\max_{k\in[r_{1}],i\in I_{3}}\left|\left[\left(\frac{W_{1}^{0\prime}W_{1}^{0}}{T}\right)^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}w_{1,t}^{0}e_{1,it}\right]_{k}\right|

\displaystyle=\sum_{k\in[r_{1}]}\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\left[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}w_{1,t,k}^{0}e_{1,it}\right]\right\|_{F}O_{p}(\eta_{N})

\displaystyle=O_{p}(\eta_{N}^{2}),

where
w_{1,t}
is the fact of
X_{1,it}
,
r_{1}
is the dimension of
w_{1,t}
, and
w_{1,t,k}
is the
k
th element of
w_{1,it}
, the second
equality holds by the results in (
B.3
) and (
B.53
), and the
last equality is by the fact that

\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\left[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}w_{1,t,k}^{0}e_{1,it}\right]\right\|_{F}>c_{23}\sqrt{\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{T}}\xi_{N}\bigg|\mathscr{D}\right)=o(1)

following analogous analysis as (
B.3
) with a positive constant
c_{23}
.

For the second term on the RHS of equation (
B.3
), we notice that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{f_{it}(0)v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}\left[e_{1,it}l_{1,i}^{0\prime}\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i^{*}\in N}l_{1,i^{*}}^{0}e_{1,i^{*}t}\right]\right\}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i^{*}\in[N]}\sum_{t\in[T]}f_{it}(0)v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}l_{1,i}^{0\prime}l_{1,i^{*}}^{0}e_{1,it}e_{1,i^{*}t}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{t\in[T]}f_{it}(0)v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}l_{1,i}^{0\prime}l_{1,i}^{0}e_{1,it}^{2}+\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i^{*}\neq i}\sum_{t\in[T]}f_{it}(0)v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}l_{1,i}^{0\prime}l_{1,i^{*}}^{0}e_{1,it}e_{1,i^{*}t}

(B.65)

with

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{t\in[T]}f_{it}(0)v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}l_{1,i}^{0\prime}l_{1,i}^{0}e_{1,it}^{2}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{N}\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|l_{1,i}^{0}\right\|_{2}^{2}\max_{t\in[T]}\left(\left\|v_{t,1}^{0}\right\|_{2}\left\|v_{t,1}^{0}\right\|_{2}\right)\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}e_{1,it}^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{\xi_{N}^{2}}{N}\right),

where the equality holds by Assumption
1
(iv), Lemma
B.13
(i) and Theorem
3.2
(ii). For the second term
in (
B.3
), by Assumption
1
(iii),
e_{j,it}
is strong
mixing across
t
and independent given fixed effect. We define
E_{i}^{vec}=(\tilde{e}_{1,1}^{\prime},\cdots,\tilde{e}_{1,i-1}^{\prime},\tilde{e}_{1,i+1}^{\prime},\cdots,\tilde{e}_{1,N}^{\prime})^{\prime}
with
\tilde{e}_{1,i^{*}}=\left(e_{1,i^{*}1}e_{1,i1},\cdots,e_{1,i^{*}T}e_{1,iT}\right)
for
i^{*}\neq i
. We can see
E_{i}^{vec}
will also be a strong
mixing sequence, conditional on
\mathscr{D}
, which implies

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i^{*}\neq i}\sum_{t\in[T]}f_{it}(0)\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}O_{1}^{(1)\prime}l_{1,i}^{0\prime}l_{1,i^{*}}^{0}e_{1,it}e_{1,i^{*}t}\right\|_{F}>c_{23}\eta_{N}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\in I_{3}}\mathbb{E}\mathbb{P}\left(\left\|\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i^{*}\neq i}\sum_{t\in[T]}f_{it}(0)\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}O_{1}^{(1)\prime}l_{1,i}^{0\prime}l_{1,i^{*}}^{0}e_{1,it}e_{1,i^{*}t}\right\|_{F}>c_{23}\eta_{N}^{2}\bigg|\mathscr{D}\right)=o(1),

where the equality holds by Lemma
B.12
(i). This implies

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|O_{1}^{(1)}\left[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}e_{1,it}\left(\hat{e}_{1,it}-e_{1,it}\right)\right]O_{1}^{(1)\prime}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}e_{1,it}\left(\hat{e}_{1,it}-e_{1,it}\right)\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(\eta_{N}^{2}).

Therefore, we conclude that
\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|J_{1,i}^{4}\right\|_{F}=O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right)
. Then

\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|J_{1,i}\right\|_{F}=\left\|\begin{bmatrix}0&O_{p}(\eta_{N})\\
O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right)&O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right)\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F}.

Next, for
J_{2,i}^{2}
and
J_{2,i}^{3}
, conditioning on
\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}
and following Bernstein’s inequality in Lemma
B.12
(i), we have

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|J_{2,i}^{2}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\left(O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}-\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime}e_{1,it}\right\|_{F}=O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right),

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|J_{2,i}^{3}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}-\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime}e_{1,it}\right\|_{F}=O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right),

which can be obtained by the similar arguments as Lemma
B.24
(i).
These results, in conjunction with the fact that

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|J_{2,i}^{4}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)\left(\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)\left(O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}-\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime}e_{1,it}^{2}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)e_{1,it}^{2}\right)\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}\right\|_{2}^{2}=O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right),

imply that
\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|J_{2,i}\right\|_{F}=O_{p}(\eta_{N}^{2}).

For
J_{3,i}
, we have

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|J_{3,i}^{1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left[f_{it}\left(\tilde{\iota}_{it}\right)-f_{it}(0)\right]\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\left(O_{0}^{\prime}v_{t,0}^{0}-\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\right\|_{2}\left\|\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\left(O_{0}^{\prime}v_{t,0}^{0}-\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime}\right\|_{2}\max_{i\in I_{3}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left|f_{it}\left(\tilde{\iota}_{it}\right)-f_{it}(0)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim O_{p}(\eta_{N})\max_{i\in I_{3}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left|\tilde{\iota}_{it}\right|

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right),

(B.66)

where the third line is by Lipschitz continuity of the density function,
Theorem
3.2
(ii), Assumption
2
, and the fact that
\left|\tilde{\iota}_{it}\right|
lies between
0
and
\left|\dot{\iota}_{it}\right|
, and the last line is by the fact that
\max_{i\in I_{3}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left|\tilde{\iota}_{it}\right|=O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}\right)
by (
B.3
) and (
B.3
). The bounds for other three blocks in
J_{3,i}
can be
established in the same manner. Hence, we have
\max_{i\in I_{3}}||J_{3,i}||_{F}=O_{p}(\eta_{N}^{2}).

Combining all results above yields the desired result.

Lemma B.24

Under Assumptions
1
-
9
, we have

(i)

\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}-O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}\right)\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)\right\|_{F}=O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right)
,

(ii)

\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)\left(\hat{e}_{1,it}-e_{1,it}\right)\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)\right\|_{F}=O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right)
,

(iii)

\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}e_{1,it}\left(\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)\right\|_{F}=O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right)
,

(iv)

\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}\left(\hat{e}_{1,it}-e_{1,it}\right)\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)\right\|_{F}=O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right)
.

Proof.
(i) We notice that
\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}-O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}\right)\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right]=0
by Assumption
1
(ii). Define event
\mathscr{A}_{10}(M)=\left\{\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}-O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}\right\|_{2}\leq M\xi_{N}\right\}

with
\mathbb{P}\left(\mathscr{A}_{10}(M)^{c}\right)\leq e
for any
e>0

by Theorem
3.2
(ii). With some positive constant
c_{24}
, it follows
that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}-O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}\right)\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)\right\|_{F}>c_{24}\eta_{N}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}-O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}\right)\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)\right\|_{F}>c_{24}\eta_{N}^{2},\mathscr{A}_{10}(M)\right)+e

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\in I_{3}}\mathbb{P}\left(\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}-O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}\right)\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)\right\|_{F}>c_{24}\eta_{N}^{2},\mathscr{A}_{10}(M)\right)+e

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\in I_{3}}\mathbb{E}\mathbb{P}\left(\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}-O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}\right)\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)\right\|_{F}>c_{24}\eta_{N}^{2}\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)\mathbf{1}\left\{\mathscr{A}_{10}(M)\right\}+e

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\in I_{3}}\exp\left\{-\frac{c_{12}c_{24}^{2}T^{2}\eta_{N}^{4}}{M^{2}T\eta_{N}^{2}+c_{24}MT\eta_{N}^{2}\log T\log\log T}\right\}+e

\displaystyle=o(1)+e.

(B.67)

Since
e
can be made arbitrarily small, this completes the proof of
statement (i).

(ii) It’s clear that

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)\left(\hat{e}_{1,it}-e_{1,it}\right)\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left|\hat{e}_{1,it}-e_{1,it}\right|\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}(\eta_{N}^{2}),

where the equality holds by Lemma
B.21
(iii) and Theorem
3.2
(ii).

(iii) Noting that

\mathbb{E}\left[e_{1,it}\left(\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right]=0

by law of iterated expectation and Assumption
1
(ii), we can obtain
the desired result as in (
B.3
).

(iv) Note that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}v_{t,1}^{0}\left(\hat{e}_{1,it}-e_{1,it}\right)\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{s\in[T]}\sum_{t\in[T]}v_{t,1}^{0}\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)w_{j,t}^{0\prime}\left(\frac{W_{j}^{0\prime}W_{j}^{0}}{T}\right)^{-1}w_{j,s}^{0}e_{j,is}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{m\in[N]}\sum_{t\in[T]}v_{t,1}^{0}\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)l_{j,i}^{0\prime}l_{j,m}^{0}e_{j,ms}+\mathcal{R}_{j,it}.

(B.68)

For the first term on the RHS of (
B.68
), we have

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{s\in[T]}\sum_{t\in[T]}v_{t,1}^{0}\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)w_{j,t}^{0\prime}\left(\frac{W_{j}^{0\prime}W_{j}^{0}}{T}\right)^{-1}w_{j,s}^{0}e_{j,is}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}v_{t,1}^{0}\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)w_{j,t}^{0\prime}\right\|_{F}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{s\in[T]}\left(\frac{W_{j}^{0\prime}W_{j}^{0}}{T}\right)^{-1}w_{j,s}^{0}e_{j,is}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right).

For the second term on RHS of (
B.68
), by Assumption
9
(ii), Bernstein’s inequality in Lemma
B.12
(i) conditional on
factors, we have

\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{m\in[N]}\sum_{t\in[T]}v_{t,1}^{0}\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)l_{j,i}^{0\prime}l_{j,m}^{0}e_{j,ms}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right).

Then statement (iv) follows.

Lemma B.25

Under Assumptions
1
-
9
, we have

(i)

\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}e_{1,it}O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}f_{it}(0)\left(\mu_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}-\hat{\mu}_{1,it}\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)\prime}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\right)\right\|_{2}=O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right)
,

(ii)

\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}f_{it}(0)\left(\mu_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}-\hat{\mu}_{1,it}\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)\prime}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\right)\right\|_{2}=O_{p}(\eta_{N})
.

Proof.
(i) Recall from (
B.3
), we have

\displaystyle\hat{\mu}_{1,it}\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)\prime}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-\mu_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}

\displaystyle=\left(\hat{\mu}_{1,it}-\mu_{1,it}\right)u_{i,j}^{0\prime}v_{t,j}^{0}+\mu_{1,it}\left(\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}

\displaystyle+\mu_{1,it}\left(O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)+O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right)\hskip 9.24994pt\text{uniformly over $i\in I_{3},t\in[T]$}.

Then

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}e_{1,it}O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}f_{it}(0)\left(\mu_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}-\hat{\mu}_{1,it}\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)\prime}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}e_{1,it}O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}f_{it}(0)u_{i,j}^{0\prime}v_{t,j}^{0}\left(\hat{\mu}_{1,it}-\mu_{1,it}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}e_{1,it}O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}f_{it}(0)\mu_{1,it}\left(\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}e_{1,it}O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}f_{it}(0)\mu_{1,it}\left(O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)+O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right).

(B.69)

First, note that
\mathbb{E}\left(e_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0}f_{it}(0)\mu_{1,it}\left(O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)=0
by Assumption
9
(i). Following similar arguments
used in the proof of Lemma
B.21
(i), we can show that the third term
on the RHS of (
B.3
) is
O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right)
. By
analogous arguments in (
B.3
) with the fact that
\hat{\mu}_{1,it}-\mu_{1,it}=e_{1,it}-\hat{e}_{1,it}
, we obtain that the first term
on the RHS of (
B.3
) is
O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right)
. In
addition

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}e_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0}f_{it}(0)\mu_{1,it}\left(\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i\in I_{3}}||\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}||_{2}\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)e_{1,it}\mu_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}\right\|_{F}=O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right),

(B.70)

where the equality holds by the fact that

\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)e_{1,it}\mu_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}\right\|_{F}>c_{25}\sqrt{\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{T}}\xi_{N}\bigg|\mathscr{D}\right)=o(1)

as in (
B.3
). Noted that Assumption
1
(iv) implies factor
and factor loading of
X_{j,it}
is uniformly bounded for
\forall j\in[p]
,
which indicates that
\mu_{j,it}
is also uniformly bounded a.s.. This
completes the proof statement (i).

(ii). Note that uniformly over
i\in I_{3}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}f_{it}(0)\left(\mu_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}-\hat{\mu}_{1,it}\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)\prime}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}f_{it}(0)\left(\hat{\mu}_{1,it}-\mu_{1,it}\right)u_{i,j}^{0\prime}v_{t,j}^{0}+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}f_{it}\mu_{1,it}\left(\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}f_{it}\mu_{1,it}\left(O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)+O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=O_{p}(\eta_{N}).

(B.71)

This term will remain as the bias term in the linear expansion of
\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)}
.

Lemma B.26

Under Assumptions
1
-
9
, we have

(i)

\max_{i\in I_{3}}\Bigg\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}e_{1,it}O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}\Bigg\{\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\iota_{it}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right\}\right]\newline
-\left(F_{it}(0)-F_{it}\left[\iota_{it}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right]\right)\Bigg\}\Bigg\|_{2}=o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)
,

(ii)

\max_{i\in I_{3}}\Bigg\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}\Bigg\{\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\iota_{it}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right\}\right]\newline
-\left(F_{it}(0)-F_{it}\left[\iota_{it}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right]\right)\Bigg\}\Bigg\|_{2}=o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)
.

Proof.
(i) We still assume
K_{1}=1
for notation simplicity. Let
O^{(1)}=diag\left(O_{0}^{(1)},O_{1}^{(1)}\right)
. Recall from Theorem
3.2
(iii) that with

\displaystyle D_{i}^{I}=O^{(1)}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)\begin{bmatrix}v_{t,0}^{0}v_{t,0}^{\prime}&&v_{t,0}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}X_{1,it}\\
v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,0}^{0\prime}X_{1,it}&&v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}X_{1,it}^{2}\end{bmatrix}O^{(1)\prime},\hskip 9.24994ptD_{i}^{II}=O^{(1)}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left[\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right]\begin{bmatrix}v_{t,0}^{0}\\
v_{t,1}^{0}X_{1,it}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle\mathbb{J}_{i}\left(\left\{\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right\}_{t\in[T]}\right):=O^{(1)}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}^{\prime}\Psi_{it}^{0}\right\}\right]\begin{bmatrix}v_{t,0}^{0}\\
v_{t,1}^{0}X_{1,it}\end{bmatrix},

uniformly over
i\in I_{3}
, we have

\displaystyle\dot{\Delta}_{i,u}

\displaystyle=\left[D_{i}^{I}\right]^{-1}\left[D_{i}^{II}+\mathbb{J}_{i}\left(\left\{\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right\}_{t\in[T]}\right)\right]+o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(O^{(1)\prime}\right)^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)\begin{bmatrix}v_{t,0}^{0}v_{t,0}^{\prime}&&v_{t,0}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}X_{1,it}\\
v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,0}^{0\prime}X_{1,it}&&v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}X_{1,it}^{2}\end{bmatrix}\right)^{-1}\Bigg[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left[\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right]\begin{bmatrix}v_{t,0}^{0}\\
v_{t,1}^{0}X_{1,it}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}^{\prime}\Psi_{it}^{0}\right\}\right]\begin{bmatrix}v_{t,0}^{0}\\
v_{t,1}^{0}X_{1,it}\end{bmatrix}\Bigg]+o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle:=h_{i}+o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right).

Let
\mathfrak{l}=\left(\mathbf{0}_{K_{0}}^{\prime},\mathbf{1}_{K_{1}}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}
with
\mathbf{0}_{K_{0}}
being a
K_{0}\times 1
vector of zeros and
\mathbf{1}_{K_{1}}
a
K_{1}\times 1

vector of ones. Let

h_{i}^{I}=\mathfrak{l}^{\prime}h_{i}.

(B.72)

Then we have

O_{1}^{(1)\prime}\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}-u_{i,1}^{0}=h_{i}^{I}+o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)\hskip 9.24994pt\text{uniformly over $i\in I_{3}$,}

and
\max_{i\in I_{3}}||h_{i}^{I}||_{2}=O_{p}(\eta_{N}).

Combining (
B.3
)-(
B.3
), uniformly in
i\in I_{3}
and
t\in\left[T\right]
we have

\displaystyle\iota_{it}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0},O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)

\displaystyle=\left[\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}-O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}\right)\right]+\left(\hat{\mu}_{1,it}\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)\prime}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-\mu_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)+\left(\hat{e}_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}O_{1}^{(1)}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}-O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}\right)+\left(\hat{\mu}_{1,it}^{(1)}-\mu_{1,it}\right)\left(O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}

\displaystyle+\mu_{1,it}\left(\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}+\mu_{1,it}\left(O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)+(\hat{e}_{1,it}-e_{1,it})\left(O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}

\displaystyle+e_{1,it}\left(O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)+o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=\mu_{1,it}\left(\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}+\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}-O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}\right)+\left(O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)X_{1,it}

\displaystyle+o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=\mu_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}h_{i}^{I}+\left\{\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}-O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}\right)+\left(O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)X_{1,it}\right\}+\mathcal{R}_{\iota,it}

\displaystyle:=\mu_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}h_{i}^{I}+h_{it}^{II}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right)+\mathcal{R}_{\iota,it}

(B.73)

\displaystyle:=R_{\iota,it}^{1}\left(\left|\mu_{1,it}\right|+\left|e_{1,it}\right|\right)+R_{\iota,it}^{2}

such that
\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left|\mathcal{R}_{\iota,it}\right|=o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)
,
\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left|R_{\iota,it}^{1}\right|=O_{p}(\eta_{N})
and
\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left|R_{\iota,it}^{2}\right|=O_{p}(\eta_{N})
, where we use the fact that
\hat{\mu}_{1,it}-\mu_{1,it}+\hat{e}_{1,it}-e_{1,it}=0
, and
h_{it}^{II}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right)=\Psi_{it}^{0\prime}\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}
with
\Psi_{it}=\left(O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0})^{\prime},(O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}X_{1,it})^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}
. Let

\displaystyle\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{3,it}^{I}

\displaystyle=e_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0}\Bigg\{\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\mu_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}h_{i}^{I}+h_{it}^{II}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right)\right\}\right]-\left(F_{it}(0)-F_{it}\left[\mu_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}h_{i}^{I}+h_{it}^{II}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right)\right]\right)\Bigg\},

\displaystyle\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{3,it}^{II}

\displaystyle=e_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0}\Bigg\{\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\mu_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}h_{i}^{I}+h_{it}^{II}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right)+\mathcal{R}_{\iota,it}\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\mu_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}h_{i}^{I}+h_{it}^{II}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right)\right\}\right]

\displaystyle-\left(F_{it}\left[\mu_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}h_{i}^{I}+h_{it}^{II}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right)+\mathcal{R}_{\iota,it}\right]-F_{it}\left[\mu_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}h_{i}^{I}+h_{it}^{II}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right)\right]\right)\Bigg\}.

We first show that
\max_{i\in I_{3}}\Bigg\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{3,it}^{I}\Bigg\|_{2}=O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right)
.

For some sufficiently large constant
M
, define event
\mathscr{A}_{11}(M)=\left\{\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|h_{i}^{I}\right\|_{2}\leq M\eta_{N}\right\}
. We have
\mathbb{P}\left(\mathscr{A}_{11}^{c}(M)\right)\leq e
for any
e>0
. For some positive large enough constant
c_{26}
, we
have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{i\in I_{3}}\Bigg\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{3,it}^{I}\Bigg\|_{2}>c_{26}\xi_{N}^{\frac{5+\vartheta}{2+\vartheta}}\left(\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N\wedge T}\right)^{\frac{1}{4+2\vartheta}}\sqrt{\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{T}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{i\in I_{3}}\Bigg\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{3,it}^{I}\Bigg\|_{2}>c_{26}\xi_{N}^{\frac{5+\vartheta}{2+\vartheta}}\left(\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N\wedge T}\right)^{\frac{1}{4+2\vartheta}}\sqrt{\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{T}},\mathscr{A}_{11}(M)\right)+e

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{i\in I_{3}}\sup_{\xi\in\Xi}\Bigg\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{3,it}^{I}(\xi)\Bigg\|_{2}>c_{26}\xi_{N}^{\frac{5+\vartheta}{2+\vartheta}}\left(\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N\wedge T}\right)^{\frac{1}{4+2\vartheta}}\sqrt{\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{T}}\right)+e.

(B.74)

with
\Xi^{1}:=\left\{\xi\in\mathbb{R}^{\left(K_{0}+K_{1}\right)\times 1}:\left\|\xi\right\|_{2}\leq M\eta_{N}\right\}
and

\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{3,it}^{I}(\xi)=e_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0}\Bigg\{\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\mu_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}\xi+h_{it}^{II}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right)\right\}\right]-\left[F_{it}(0)-F_{it}\left(\mu_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}\xi+h_{it}^{II}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right)\right)\right]\Bigg\}.

Divide
\Xi^{1}
into sub classes
\Xi_{s}^{1}
for
s=1,...,n_{\Xi^{1}}

such that
\left\|\xi-\tilde{\xi}\right\|_{2}<\frac{\varepsilon}{T}

for
\forall\xi,\,\,\bar{\xi}\in\Xi_{s}^{1}
and
n_{\Xi^{1}}\asymp T^{K_{0}+K_{1}}
. With analogous arguments from (
A.14
)-(
A.2.3
), for
\forall\xi_{s}\in\Xi_{s}^{1}
