, we have

\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{3,it}^{I}(\xi)\right\|_{2}\leq\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{3,it}^{I}(\xi_{s})\right\|_{2}+\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left[\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{3,it}^{I}(\xi)-\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{3,it}^{I}(\xi_{s})\right]\right\|_{2}

with

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{3},s\in[n_{\Xi^{1}}]}\sup_{\xi\in\Xi_{s}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left[\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{3,it}^{I}(\xi)-\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{3,it}^{I}(\xi_{s})\right]\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i\in I_{3}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|e_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0}\right\|_{2}\mathbf{1}\left\{\left|\epsilon_{it}-h_{it}^{II}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right)\right|\leq\left|\mu_{1,it}\right|\left\|v_{t,1}^{0\prime}\right\|_{2}\frac{\varepsilon}{T}\right\}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right]

\displaystyle+\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{3,i}^{III}\right\|_{2}+\max_{i\in I_{3}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left\|e_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0}\right\|_{2}\left|\mu_{1,it}\right|\left\|v_{t,1}^{0\prime}\right\|_{2}\frac{\varepsilon}{T}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{3,i}^{III}\right\|_{2}+O\left(\frac{\varepsilon}{T}\right)~a.s.

(B.75)

where
\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{3,i}^{III}=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{3,it}^{III}
and

\displaystyle\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{3,it}^{III}

\displaystyle=\left\|e_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0}\right\|_{2}\Bigg\{\mathbf{1}\left\{\left|\epsilon_{it}-h_{it}^{II}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right)\right|\leq\left|\mu_{1,it}\right|\left\|v_{t,1}^{0\prime}\right\|_{2}\frac{\varepsilon}{T}\right\}

\displaystyle-\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\left|\epsilon_{it}-h_{it}^{II}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right)\right|\leq\left|\mu_{1,it}\right|\left\|v_{t,1}^{0\prime}\right\|_{2}\frac{\varepsilon}{T}\right\}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right]\Bigg\}.

Similarly to (
A.2.3
), we can show that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{3,it}^{III}\right\|_{2}>c_{26}\xi_{N}^{\frac{5+\vartheta}{2+\vartheta}}\left(\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N\wedge T}\right)^{\frac{1}{4+2\vartheta}}\sqrt{\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{T}}\right)=o(1),\hskip 9.24994pt\text{and}

(B.76)

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{i\in I_{3}}\max_{s\in[n_{\Xi^{1}}]}\left\|\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{3,it}^{I}\left(\xi_{s}\right)\right\|_{2}>c_{26}\xi_{N}^{\frac{5+\vartheta}{2+\vartheta}}\left(\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N\wedge T}\right)^{\frac{1}{4+2\vartheta}}\sqrt{\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{T}}\right)=o(1).

(B.77)

Combining (
B.3
)-(
B.76
), we have

\max_{i\in I_{3}}\Bigg\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{3,it}^{I}\Bigg\|_{2}=o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right).

Next, we notice that

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{3}}\Bigg\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{3,it}^{II}\Bigg\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i\in I_{3}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|e_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0}\right\|_{2}\mathbf{1}\left\{\left|\mu_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}h_{i}^{I}+h_{it}^{II}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right)\right|\leq\left|\epsilon_{it}\right|\leq\left|\mu_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}h_{i}^{I}+h_{it}^{II}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right)\right|+\left|\mathcal{R}_{\iota,it}\right|\right\}

\displaystyle+\max_{i\in I_{3}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|e_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0}\right\|_{2}\left|F_{it}\left[\mu_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}h_{i}^{I}+h_{it}^{II}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right)+\mathcal{R}_{\iota,it}\right]-F_{it}\left[\mu_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}h_{i}^{I}+h_{it}^{II}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i\in I_{3}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|e_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0}\right\|_{2}\mathbf{1}\left\{\left|\mu_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}h_{i}^{I}+h_{it}^{II}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right)\right|\leq\left|\epsilon_{it}\right|\leq\left|\mu_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}h_{i}^{I}+h_{it}^{II}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right)\right|+\left|\mathcal{R}_{\iota,it}\right|\right\}

\displaystyle+\max_{i\in I_{3}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|e_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0}\right\|_{2}\left|\mathcal{R}_{\iota,it}\right|

\displaystyle=\max_{i\in I_{3}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{3,it}^{IV}+o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right),

(B.78)

where the first inequality is by triangle inequality, the second inequality
is by mean value theorem and the last line is by the uniform convergence
rate for
\mathcal{R}_{\iota,it}
with

\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{3,it}^{IV}=\left\|e_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0}\right\|_{2}\mathbf{1}\left\{\left|\mu_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}h_{i}^{I}+h_{it}^{II}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right)\right|\leq\left|\epsilon_{it}\right|\leq\left|\mu_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}h_{i}^{I}+h_{it}^{II}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right)\right|+\left|\mathcal{R}_{\iota,it}\right|\right\}.

Define the event
\mathscr{A}_{12}(M):=\left\{\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left|\mathcal{R}_{\iota,it}\right|\leq M\eta_{N}^{2}\right\}

with
\mathbb{P}\left\{\mathscr{A}_{12}^{c}(M)\right\}\leq e
for any
e>0
. Then for a large enough constant
c_{26}
, similarly as (
A.3.1
),
we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{3,it}^{IV}\right\|_{2}>c_{26}\eta_{N}^{2}\right)\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{3,it}^{IV}\right\|_{2}>c_{26}\eta_{N}^{2},\mathscr{A}_{12}(M)\right)+e

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{i\in I_{3}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\tilde{\mathbf{1}}_{it}(h_{i}^{I})>c_{26}\eta_{N}^{2}\right)+e

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\sup_{i\in I_{3},\xi\in\Xi^{1}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\tilde{\mathbf{1}}_{it}(\xi)>c_{26}\eta_{N}^{2}\right)+2e

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{i\in I_{3}}\sup_{\xi\in\Xi^{1}}\left|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\tilde{\mathbf{1}}_{it}(\xi)-\bar{\tilde{\mathbf{1}}}_{it}(\xi)\right|>\frac{c_{26}\eta_{N}^{2}}{2}\right)+\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{i\in I_{3}}\sup_{\xi\in\Xi^{1}}\left|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bar{\tilde{\mathbf{1}}}_{it}(\xi)\right|>\frac{c_{26}\eta_{N}^{2}}{2}\right)+2e

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{i\in I_{3}}\sup_{\xi\in\Xi^{1}}\left|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\tilde{\mathbf{1}}_{it}(\xi)-\bar{\tilde{\mathbf{1}}}_{it}(\xi)\right|>\frac{c_{26}\eta_{N}^{2}}{2}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)\right]+\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{i\in I_{3}}\sup_{\xi\in\Xi^{1}}\left|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bar{\tilde{\mathbf{1}}}_{it}(\xi)\right|>\frac{c_{26}\eta_{N}^{2}}{2}\right)+2e

(B.79)

where
\tilde{\mathbf{1}}_{it}(h_{i}^{I}):=\left\|e_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0}\right\|_{2}\mathbf{1}\left\{\left|\mu_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}h_{i}^{I}+h_{it}^{II}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right)\right|\leq\left|\epsilon_{it}\right|\leq\left|\mu_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}h_{i}^{I}+h_{it}^{II}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right)\right|+M\eta_{N}^{2}\right\}
and
\bar{\tilde{\mathbf{1}}}_{it}(h_{i}^{I})=\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{\mathbf{1}}_{it}(h_{i}^{I})\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right]
. By analogous arguments for the
first term on the RHS of inequality (
B.3
), we can show that

\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{i\in I_{3}}\sup_{\xi\in\Xi^{1}}\left|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\tilde{\mathbf{1}}_{it}(\xi)-\bar{\tilde{\mathbf{1}}}_{it}(\xi)\right|>\frac{c_{26}\eta_{N}^{2}}{2}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)\right]=o(1).

Besides, we observe that

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{3}}\sup_{\xi\in\Xi^{1}}\left|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bar{\tilde{\mathbf{1}}}_{it}(\xi)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i\in I_{3}}\sup_{\xi\in\Xi^{1}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|e_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0}\right\|_{2}\left|F_{it}\left[\left|\mu_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}\xi+h_{it}^{II}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right)\right|+M\eta_{N}^{2}\right]-F_{it}\left[\left|\mu_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}\xi+h_{it}^{II}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right)\right|\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i\in I_{3}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|e_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0}\right\|_{2}M\eta_{N}^{2},

which yields

\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{i\in I_{3}}\sup_{\xi\in\Xi^{1}}\left|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bar{\tilde{\mathbf{1}}}_{it}(\xi)\right|>\frac{c_{26}\eta_{N}^{2}}{2}\right)=o(1).

(B.80)

Combining (
B.3
)-(
B.80
) yields
\max_{i\in I_{3}}\Bigg\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\hat{\mathbb{I}}_{3,it}^{II}\Bigg\|_{2}=O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right)=o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)

by Assumption
1
(ix). This concludes the proof of statement (i).

(ii) The proof is analogous to that of part (i) and thus omitted.

Lemma B.27

Under Assumptions
1
-
9
,
uniformly in
i\in I_{3}
, we can show that

(i)

\max_{i\in I_{3}}\Bigg\|\frac{1}{T}\operatornamewithlimits{\sum}\limits_{t=1}^{T}e_{1,it}O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}\Bigg\{\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\iota_{it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right\}-\left(F_{it}(0)-F_{it}\left[\iota_{it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right]\right)\Bigg\}\Bigg\|_{2}\newline
=o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)
,

(ii)

\max_{i\in I_{3}}\Bigg\|\frac{1}{T}\operatornamewithlimits{\sum}\limits_{t=1}^{T}v_{t,0}^{0}\Bigg\{\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\iota_{it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right\}-\left(F_{it}(0)-F_{it}\left[\iota_{it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)\right]\right)\Bigg\}\Bigg\|_{2}\newline
=o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)
.

Proof.
As in (
B.3
), we can show that

\displaystyle\iota_{it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}\right)

\displaystyle=\mu_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}h_{i}^{I}+\left[\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)}-O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0}\right)^{\prime}O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}+e_{1,it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}\right]

\displaystyle+\left[\left(O_{0}^{(0)}u_{i,0}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\dot{v}_{t,0}^{(1)}-O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}\right)+X_{1,it}\left(O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)\right]+\mathcal{R}_{\hat{\iota},it}

\displaystyle:

\displaystyle=\mu_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}h_{i}^{I}+\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)}-O_{0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0}\right)^{\prime}O_{0}^{(1)}v_{t,0}^{0}+e_{1,it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}+h_{it}^{II}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right)+\mathcal{R}_{\hat{\iota},it},

(B.81)

where
\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left|\mathcal{R}_{\hat{\iota},it}\right|=o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)
.
As in the proof of Theorem
3.2
, we can show that
\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\hat{u}_{i,j}^{(3,1)}-O_{j}^{(1)}u_{i,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}

=O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}\right)
for
\forall j\in[p]\cup\{0\}
. Then by
changing the event set
\mathscr{A}_{11}(M)
to

\left\{\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|h_{i}^{I}\right\|_{2}\leq M\eta_{N},\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\hat{u}_{i,j}-O_{j}^{(1)}u_{i,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}\leq M\eta_{N}\right\},

we can repeat the analysis in Lemma
B.26
and obtain the desired
results for statement (i). With some obvious adjustment, statement (ii) can
be proved.

Lemma B.28

Under Assumptions

-

, for

\hat{D}_{t}^{F}
,

,

and

\max_{t\in[T]}||\hat{D}_{t}^{F}-D_{t}^{F}||_{F}=O_{p}(\eta_{N}),\text{ and }\max_{t\in[T]}||\hat{D}_{t}^{J}-D_{t}^{J}||_{F}=\left\|\begin{bmatrix}O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right)&O_{p}(\eta_{N})\\
O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right)&O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right)\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F}.

Proof.
By analogous arguments as used in the proofs of Lemmas
B.22
and
