, we can prove the lemma.

Lemma B.29

Under Assumptions
1
-
9
, we have

(i)

\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}O_{u,0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0}f_{it}(0)\left(\mu_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}-\hat{\mu}_{1,it}\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)\prime}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\right)\right\|_{2}=O_{p}(\eta_{N})
,

(ii)

\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}O_{u,1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}f_{it}(0)\left(\mu_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}-\hat{\mu}_{1,it}\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)\prime}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}\right)\right\|_{2}=o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)
.

Proof.
(i) Note that

\displaystyle\hat{\mu}_{1,it}\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)\prime}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-\mu_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}

\displaystyle=\left(\hat{\mu}_{1,it}-\mu_{1,it}\right)\left(\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)}-O_{u,1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)+\left(\hat{\mu}_{1,it}-\mu_{1,it}\right)\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)

\displaystyle+\left(\hat{\mu}_{1,it}-\mu_{1,it}\right)\left(\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)}-O_{u,1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}+\left(\hat{\mu}_{1,it}-\mu_{1,it}\right)\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}

\displaystyle+\mu_{1,it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)}-O_{u,1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)+\mu_{1,it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)}-O_{u,1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}

\displaystyle+\mu_{1,it}\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)+\mu_{1,it}\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}-\mu_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}

\displaystyle=\left(\hat{\mu}_{1,it}-\mu_{1,it}\right)\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}+\mu_{1,it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)}-O_{u,1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}+\mu_{1,it}\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)

\displaystyle+\mu_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}O_{1}^{(1)\prime}\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}\right)u_{i,1}^{0}+O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(\hat{\mu}_{1,it}-\mu_{1,it}\right)u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}+\mu_{1,it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)}-O_{u,1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}+\mu_{1,it}\left(O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)

\displaystyle+\mu_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}O_{1}^{(1)\prime}\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}\right)u_{i,1}^{0}+O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right)=O_{p}(\eta_{N}),

(B.82)

uniformly over
i\in I_{3}
and
t\in[T]
, where the last equality holds by
the fact that
\left\|O_{u,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}\right\|_{F}=O_{p}(\eta_{N})
. It follows that

\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}O_{u,0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0}f_{it}(0)\left(\hat{\mu}_{1,it}\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)\prime}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-\mu_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)\right\|_{2}=O_{p}(\eta_{N}).

(ii) Observe that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}O_{u,1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}f_{it}(0)\left(\hat{\mu}_{1,it}^{(1)}\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)\prime}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-\mu_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)

\displaystyle=O_{u,1}^{(1)}\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}f_{it}(0)\left(\hat{\mu}_{1,it}^{(1)}-\mu_{1,it}\right)u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}

\displaystyle+O_{u,1}^{(1)}\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}f_{it}(0)\mu_{1,it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)}-O_{u,1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}

\displaystyle+O_{u,1}^{(1)}\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}f_{it}(0)\mu_{1,it}\left(O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)

\displaystyle+O_{u,1}^{(1)}\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}f_{it}(0)\mu_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{(1)\prime}O_{1}^{(1)\prime}\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}\right)u_{i,1}^{0}+O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{2}\right).

(B.83)

By similar arguments as used in (
B.3
), we can show that the first
term on the RHS of (
B.3
) is
o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)
uniformly over
t
. For the second term, by inserting
the linear expansion for
\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)}-O_{u,1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}
in
(
A.41
), we notice that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}f_{it}(0)\mu_{1,it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)}-O_{u,1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N_{3}T}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}\sum_{t\in[T]}e_{1,it}f_{it}\left(0\right)\mu_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}v_{t^{*},1}^{0\prime}\hat{V}_{u_{1}}^{-1}e_{1,it^{*}}v_{t^{*},1}^{0}\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it^{*}}\leq 0\right\}\right)+o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right).

By arguments as used in (
B.3
), we can show the leading term in
the last equality is
O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{NT}}\xi_{N}^{2}\right)
. Then the second term on the RHS of (
B.3
) is
o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)
. For the third and
the fourth terms on the RHS of (
B.3
), by conditional on
\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}
, we notice that
\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}f_{it}(0)\mu_{1,it}\left(O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)

and
\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}f_{it}(0)\mu_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}O_{1}^{(1)\prime}\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}\right)u_{i,1}^{0}
are both mean
zero and the randomness depends only on
\{e_{1,it}f_{it}(0)\}
. By
conditional Hoeffding’s inequality, we can show that both these two terms
are
o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)
.

Define

\displaystyle\mathbb{J}_{it}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right)=\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}^{\prime}\Psi_{it}^{0}\right\}\right]\begin{bmatrix}v_{t,0}^{0}\\
v_{t,1}^{0}X_{1,it}\end{bmatrix},

\displaystyle h_{i}^{I,1}=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[f_{it}(0)\begin{bmatrix}v_{t,0}^{0}v_{t,0}^{\prime}&&v_{t,0}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}X_{1,it}\\
v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,0}^{0\prime}X_{1,it}&&v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}X_{1,it}^{2}\end{bmatrix}\bigg|\mathscr{D}\right],

\displaystyle h_{i}^{I,2}=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbb{J}_{i}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right)\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right),

\displaystyle h_{it}^{III}=v_{t,0}^{0\prime}\hat{V}_{u_{0},i}^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t^{*}=1}^{T}v_{t^{*},0}^{0}\left(\tau-1\left\{\epsilon_{it^{*}}\leq 0\right\}\right)+X_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}\hat{V}_{u_{1}}^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t^{*}=1}^{T}e_{1,it^{*}}v_{t^{*},1}^{0}\left(\tau-1\left\{\epsilon_{it^{*}}\leq 0\right\}\right)

\displaystyle-\left(v_{t,0}^{0\prime}\hat{V}_{u_{0},i}^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t^{*}=1}^{T}v_{t^{*},0}^{0}f_{it^{*}}(0)\mu_{1,it^{*}}v_{t^{*},1}^{0\prime}\right)\mathfrak{l}^{\prime}\left(O_{1}^{(1)}\right)^{-1}\left(h_{i}^{I,1}\right)^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t^{*}=1}^{T}\left[\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it^{*}}\leq 0\right\}\right]\begin{bmatrix}v_{t^{*},0}^{0}\\
v_{t^{*},1}^{0}X_{1,it^{*}}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle-\left(v_{t,0}^{0\prime}\hat{V}_{u_{0},i}^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t^{*}=1}^{T}v_{t^{*},0}^{0}\mathbb{E}\left[f_{it^{*}}(0)\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right]\mu_{1,it^{*}}v_{t^{*},1}^{0\prime}\right)\mathfrak{l}^{\prime}\left(O_{1}^{(1)}\right)^{-1}\left(h_{i}^{I,1}\right)^{-1}h_{i}^{I,2}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{R}_{h,it}^{1}+X_{1,it}\mathcal{R}_{h,it}^{2},

(B.84)

with
\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left|\mathcal{R}_{h,it}^{a}\right|=O_{p}(\eta_{N})
for
a\in\{1,2\}
. Note that
\mathbb{E}\left[f_{it}(0)\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[f_{it}(0)\bigg|\mathscr{D}\right]
.

Assumption 13

Let
\mathcal{F}_{it}(\cdot)
and
f_{it|h_{it}^{III}}(\cdot)

be the conditional CDF and PDF of
\epsilon_{it}
given
\mathscr{D}_{e}

and
h_{it}^{III}
.

(i)

The derivative of the density
f_{it|h_{it}^{III}}
is uniformly
bounded in absolute value.

(ii)

\max_{i\in[N],t\in[T]}\left|f_{it|h_{it}^{III}}(0)-f_{it|h_{it}^{III}=0}(0)\right|\leq C\left|h_{it}^{III}\right|
for some Lipschitz constant
C>0
.

Lemma B.30

Under Assumptions
1
-
9
and
Assumption
13
, we have

(i)

\max_{t\in[T]}\Bigg\|\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}O_{u,1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\Bigg\{\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\varrho_{it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\left(O_{u,0}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,0}^{0},\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)\right\}\right]\newline
-\left(F_{it}(0)-F_{it}\left[\varrho_{it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\left(O_{u,0}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,0}^{0},\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)\right]\right)\Bigg\}\Bigg\|_{2}=o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)
,

(ii)

\max_{t\in[T]}\Bigg\|\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}O_{u,0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0}\Bigg\{\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\varrho_{it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\left(O_{u,0}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,0}^{0},\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)\right\}\right]\newline
-\left(F_{it}(0)-F_{it}\left[\varrho_{it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\left(O_{u,0}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,0}^{0},\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)\right]\right)\Bigg\}\Bigg\|_{2}=O_{p}(\eta_{N})
.

Proof.
(i) Recall from (
B.72
) that

\displaystyle h_{i}^{I}

\displaystyle=\mathfrak{l}^{\prime}\left(O_{1}^{(1)}\right)^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it}(0)\begin{bmatrix}v_{t,0}^{0}v_{t,0}^{\prime}&&v_{t,0}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}X_{1,it}\\
v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,0}^{0\prime}X_{1,it}&&v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}X_{1,it}^{2}\end{bmatrix}\right)^{-1}\Bigg[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left[\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right]\begin{bmatrix}v_{t,0}^{0}\\
v_{t,1}^{0}X_{1,it}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}^{\prime}\Psi_{it}^{0}\right\}\right]\begin{bmatrix}v_{t,0}^{0}\\
v_{t,1}^{0}X_{1,it}\end{bmatrix}\Bigg].

By Bernstein’s inequality in Lemma
B.12
, we can show that

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{f_{it}(0)\begin{bmatrix}v_{t,0}^{0}v_{t,0}^{\prime}&&v_{t,0}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}X_{1,it}\\
v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,0}^{0\prime}X_{1,it}&&v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}X_{1,it}^{2}\end{bmatrix}-\mathbb{E}\left(f_{it}(0)\begin{bmatrix}v_{t,0}^{0}v_{t,0}^{\prime}&&v_{t,0}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}X_{1,it}\\
v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,0}^{0\prime}X_{1,it}&&v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}X_{1,it}^{2}\end{bmatrix}\bigg|\mathscr{D}\right)\right\}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{T}}\xi_{N}^{2}\right),\text{ and}

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left[\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right]\begin{bmatrix}v_{t,0}^{0}\\
v_{t,1}^{0}X_{1,it}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{T}}\xi_{N}\right).

Besides, we observe that

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left\|Var\left(\mathbb{J}_{it}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right)\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left\|\mathbb{E}\left(\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}^{\prime}\Psi_{it}^{0}\right\}\right]\begin{bmatrix}v_{t,0}^{0}v_{t,0}^{\prime}&&v_{t,0}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}X_{1,it}\\
v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,0}^{0\prime}X_{1,it}&&v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}X_{1,it}^{2}\end{bmatrix}\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left\|\mathbb{E}\left(\left[F_{it}(0)-F_{it}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}^{\prime}\Psi_{it}^{0}\right)\right]\begin{bmatrix}v_{t,0}^{0}v_{t,0}^{\prime}&&v_{t,0}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}X_{1,it}\\
v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,0}^{0\prime}X_{1,it}&&v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}X_{1,it}^{2}\end{bmatrix}\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right\|_{2}\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left\|\mathbb{E}\left(f_{it}(\tilde{s}_{it})\Psi_{it}^{0}\begin{bmatrix}v_{t,0}^{0}v_{t,0}^{\prime}&&v_{t,0}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}X_{1,it}\\
v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,0}^{0\prime}X_{1,it}&&v_{t,1}^{0}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}X_{1,it}^{2}\end{bmatrix}\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(\eta_{N}),

(B.85)

where the first equality holds by law of iterated expectations, the second
equality is by mean-value theorem for
\left|\tilde{s}_{it}\right|

lies between 0 and
\left|\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}^{\prime}\Psi_{it}^{0}\right|
and the last line is by Theorem
3.2
(ii) and Assumption
1
(iv). Similarly, for any
\vartheta>0
, we also have

\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\sum_{s=t+1}^{T}\left\|Cov\left(\mathbb{J}_{it}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right),\mathbb{J}_{is}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right)^{\prime}\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)\right\|_{F}=O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}^{\frac{2}{2+\vartheta}}\right).

By similar arguments as used in (
A.2.3
) and (
A.2.3
), we
have

\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{\mathbb{J}_{it}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right)-\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbb{J}_{it}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right)\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)\right\}\right\|_{2}=o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-1/2}\right).

(B.86)

Together with the fact that
\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\mathbb{J}_{i}\left(\dot{\Delta}_{t,v}\right)\right\|_{2}=O_{p}(\eta_{N})
, uniformly over
t\in[T]
, we have

h_{i}^{I}=\mathfrak{l}^{\prime}\left(O_{1}^{(1)}\right)^{-1}\left(h_{i}^{I,1}\right)^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left[\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right]\begin{bmatrix}v_{t,0}^{0}\\
v_{t,1}^{0}X_{1,it}\end{bmatrix}+\mathfrak{l}^{\prime}\left(O_{1}^{(1)}\right)^{-1}\left(h_{i}^{I,1}\right)^{-1}h_{i}^{I,2}+o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-1/2}\right).

By Assumption
1
(iv) and Lemma
B.13
, we have

\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|h_{i}^{I,1}\right\|_{F}=O(1)~a.s.
. Like (
B.3
), we can show that
\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|h_{i}^{I,2}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}(\eta_{N})
.

Similarly as (
B.3
), uniformly in
i\in I_{3}
and
t\in[T]
we
have

\displaystyle\varrho_{it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\left(O_{u,0}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,0}^{0},\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)

\displaystyle=\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)\prime}\left(O_{u,0}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,0}^{0}-u_{i,0}^{0\prime}v_{t,0}^{0}+\hat{\mu}_{1,it}\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)\prime}\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-\mu_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}

\displaystyle+\hat{e}_{1,it}\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)\prime}\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,1}^{0}-e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t,1}^{0}

\displaystyle=v_{t,0}^{0\prime}O_{u,0}^{-1}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)}-O_{u,0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0}\right)+X_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}O_{u,1}^{(1)-1}\left(\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)}-O_{u,1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)+\mu_{1,it}\left(O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)

\displaystyle+\mu_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}O_{u,1}^{(1)\prime}\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}\right)v_{t,1}^{0}+o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-1/2}\right)

\displaystyle=v_{t,0}^{0\prime}\left(O_{0}^{(1)}\right)^{-1}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)}-O_{u,0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0}\right)+X_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}\left(O_{1}^{(1)}\right)^{-1}\left(\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)}-O_{u,1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)+\mu_{1,it}\left(O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)

\displaystyle+\mu_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}O_{1}^{(1)\prime}\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}\right)v_{t,1}^{0}+o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-1/2}\right),

(B.87)

where the last line is by the fact that
\left\|O_{u,0}^{(1)}-O_{0}^{(1)}\right\|_{F}=O_{p}(\eta_{N})
and
\left\|O_{u,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}\right\|_{F}=O_{p}(\eta_{N})
. Combining (
A.41
), (
A.42
) and Theorem
3.2
(iii), we have

\displaystyle\varrho_{it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\left(O_{u,0}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,0}^{0},\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)

\displaystyle=v_{t,0}^{0\prime}\hat{V}_{u_{0},i}^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t^{*}=1}^{T}v_{t^{*},0}^{0}\left(\tau-1\left\{\epsilon_{it^{*}}\leq 0\right\}\right)+v_{t,0}^{0\prime}\hat{V}_{u_{0},i}^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t^{*}=1}^{T}v_{t^{*},0}^{0}f_{it^{*}}(0)\left(\mu_{1,it^{*}}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t^{*},1}^{0}-\hat{\mu}_{1,it^{*}}^{(1)}\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)\prime}\dot{v}_{t^{*},1}^{(1)}\right)

\displaystyle+v_{t,0}^{0\prime}\hat{V}_{u_{0},i}^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{it^{*}}(0)v_{t^{*},0}^{0}v_{t^{*},1}^{0\prime}u_{i,1}^{0}\left(e_{1,it^{*}}-\hat{e}_{1,it^{*}}\right)+X_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}\hat{V}_{u_{1}}^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t^{*}=1}^{T}e_{1,it^{*}}v_{t^{*},1}^{0}\left(\tau-1\left\{\epsilon_{it^{*}}\leq 0\right\}\right)

\displaystyle+\mu_{1,it}\left(O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)+\mu_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}O_{1}^{(1)\prime}\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}\right)v_{t,1}^{0}+o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-1/2}\right)

\displaystyle=v_{t,0}^{0\prime}\hat{V}_{u_{0},i}^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t^{*}=1}^{T}v_{t^{*},0}^{0}\left(\tau-1\left\{\epsilon_{it^{*}}\leq 0\right\}\right)+X_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}\hat{V}_{u_{1}}^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t^{*}=1}^{T}e_{1,it^{*}}v_{t^{*},1}^{0}\left(\tau-1\left\{\epsilon_{it^{*}}\leq 0\right\}\right)

\displaystyle-v_{t,0}^{0\prime}\hat{V}_{u_{0},i}^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t^{*}=1}^{T}v_{t^{*},0}^{0}f_{it^{*}}(0)\mu_{1,it^{*}}\left(\dot{u}_{i,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t^{*},1}^{0}

\displaystyle-v_{t,0}^{0\prime}\hat{V}_{u_{0},i}^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t^{*}=1}^{T}v_{t^{*},0}^{0}f_{it^{*}}(0)\left(\hat{\mu}_{1,it^{*}}-\mu_{1,it^{*}}\right)u_{i,1}^{0\prime}v_{t^{*},1}^{0}-v_{t,0}^{0\prime}\hat{V}_{u_{0},i}^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t^{*}=1}^{T}v_{t^{*},0}^{0}f_{it^{*}}(0)\mu_{1,it^{*}}\left(O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\dot{v}_{t^{*},1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t^{*},1}^{0}\right)

\displaystyle+v_{t,0}^{0\prime}\hat{V}_{u_{0},i}^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t^{*}=1}^{T}f_{it^{*}}(0)v_{t^{*},0}^{0}v_{t^{*},1}^{0\prime}u_{i,1}^{0}\left(e_{1,it^{*}}-\hat{e}_{1,it^{*}}\right)+\mu_{1,it}\left(O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)

\displaystyle+\mu_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}O_{1}^{(1)\prime}\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}\right)v_{t,1}^{0}+o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-1/2}\right)

\displaystyle=v_{t,0}^{0\prime}\hat{V}_{u_{0},i}^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t^{*}=1}^{T}v_{t^{*},0}^{0}\left(\tau-1\left\{\epsilon_{it^{*}}\leq 0\right\}\right)+X_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}\hat{V}_{u_{1}}^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t^{*}=1}^{T}e_{1,it^{*}}v_{t^{*},1}^{0}\left(\tau-1\left\{\epsilon_{it^{*}}\leq 0\right\}\right)

\displaystyle-v_{t,0}^{0\prime}\hat{V}_{u_{0},i}^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t^{*}=1}^{T}v_{t^{*},0}^{0}\mathbb{E}\left[f_{it^{*}}(0)\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right]\mu_{1,it^{*}}v_{t^{*},1}^{0\prime}h_{i}^{I}+h_{it}^{IV}+o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-1/2}\right)

\displaystyle-v_{t,0}^{0\prime}\hat{V}_{u_{0},i}^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t^{*}=1}^{T}v_{t^{*},0}^{0}\left(f_{it^{*}}(0)-\mathbb{E}\left[f_{it^{*}}(0)\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right]\right)\mu_{1,it}\left(O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\dot{v}_{t^{*},1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t^{*},1}^{0}\right)

\displaystyle-v_{t,0}^{0\prime}\hat{V}_{u_{0},i}^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t^{*}=1}^{T}v_{t^{*},0}^{0}\left(f_{it^{*}}(0)-\mathbb{E}\left[f_{it^{*}}(0)\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right]\right)\mu_{1,it^{*}}v_{t^{*},1}^{0\prime}h_{i}^{I}

\displaystyle:=h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}+\mathcal{R}_{\varrho,it}

(B.88)

such that
\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left|\mathcal{R}_{\varrho,it}\right|=O_{p}(\eta_{N}^{2})
, where the first equality is
by inserting the linear expansion for
\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)}-O_{u,0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0}
in (
A.41
) and
\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)}-O_{u,1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}
in (
A.42
), the second
equality is by inserting the linear expansion for
\dot{u}_{i,0}^{(1)}-O_{u,0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0}
, the third equality is by the fact
that
\hat{\mu}_{1,it}-\mu_{1,it}=e_{1,it}-\hat{e}_{1,it}
which leads to
the cancelling of the fourth and sixth terms in the second equality, the
last equality is by the definition in (
B.84
) and

\displaystyle h_{it}^{IV}

\displaystyle=-v_{t,0}^{0\prime}\hat{V}_{u_{0},i}^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t^{*}=1}^{T}v_{t^{*},0}^{0}\mathbb{E}\left[f_{it^{*}}(0)\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right]\mu_{1,it^{*}}\left(O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\dot{v}_{t^{*},1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t^{*},1}^{0}\right)

\displaystyle+\mu_{1,it}\left(O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)+\mu_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}O_{1}^{(1)\prime}\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}\right)v_{t,1}^{0}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{R}_{h,it}^{3}+\mu_{1,it}\mathcal{R}_{h,it}^{4}

with
\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left|\mathcal{R}_{h,it}^{a}\right|=O_{p}(\eta_{N})
for
a\in\{3,4\}
, and the last equality holds by the
fact that

\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t^{*}=1}^{T}v_{t^{*},0}^{0}\left(f_{it^{*}}(0)-\mathbb{E}\left[f_{it^{*}}(0)\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right]\right)\mu_{1,it^{*}}\left(O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\dot{v}_{t^{*},1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}v_{tv,1}^{0}\right)\right\|_{2}=o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-1/2}\right)

and

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t^{*}=1}^{T}v_{t,0}^{0}\left(f_{it^{*}}(0)-\mathbb{E}\left[f_{it^{*}}(0)\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right]\right)\mu_{1,it^{*}}v_{t^{*},1}^{0\prime}h_{i}^{I}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i\in I_{3}}\left|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t^{*}=1}^{T}v_{t^{*},0}^{0}\left(f_{it^{*}}(0)-\mathbb{E}\left[f_{it^{*}}(0)\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right]\right)\mu_{1,it^{*}}v_{t^{*},1}^{0\prime}\right|\max_{i\in I_{3}}||h_{i}^{I}||_{2}=o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-1/2}\right)

by conditional Bernstein’s inequality given
\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}

and similar arguments as used in (
B.86
).

We notice that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}\Bigg\{\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\varrho_{it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\left(O_{u,0}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,0}^{0},\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)\right\}\right]

\displaystyle-\left(F_{it}(0)-F_{it}\left[\varrho_{it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\left(O_{u,0}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,0}^{0},\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)\right]\right)\Bigg\}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}\left\{\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}\right\}\right]-\left(F_{it}(0)-F_{it}\left[h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}\right]\right)\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}\Bigg\{\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}+\mathcal{R}_{\varrho,it}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle-\left[F_{it}\left(h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}\right)-F_{it}\left(h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}+\mathcal{R}_{\varrho,it}\right)\right]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}\left\{\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}\right\}\right]-\left[\mathcal{F}_{it}(0)-\mathcal{F}_{it}\left(h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}\right)\right]\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}\Bigg\{\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}+\mathcal{R}_{\varrho,it}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle-\left[\mathcal{F}_{it}\left(h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}\right)-\mathcal{F}_{it}\left(h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}+\mathcal{R}_{\varrho,it}\right)\right]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}\left\{\left[\mathcal{F}_{it}(0)-\mathcal{F}_{it}\left(h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}\right)\right]-\left[F_{it}(0)-F_{it}\left(h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}\right)\right]\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}\left[\mathcal{F}_{it}\left(h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}\right)-\mathcal{F}_{it}\left(h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}+\mathcal{R}_{\varrho,it}\right)\right]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}\left[F_{it}\left(h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}\right)-F_{it}\left(h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}+\mathcal{R}_{\varrho,it}\right)\right]

\displaystyle:=\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{I}+\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{II}-\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{III}+\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{IV}+o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)

(B.89)

such that
\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{I}:=\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}\mathbb{I}_{6,it}^{I}
,
\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{II}:=\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}\mathbb{I}_{6,it}^{II}
,
\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{III}:=\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}\mathbb{I}_{6,it}^{III}
,
\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{IV}:=\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}\mathbb{I}_{6,it}^{IV}
, with

\displaystyle\mathbb{I}_{6,it}^{I}

\displaystyle=e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}\left\{\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}\right\}\right]-\left[\mathcal{F}_{it}(0)-\mathcal{F}_{it}\left(h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}\right)\right]\right\},

\displaystyle\mathbb{I}_{6,it}^{II}

\displaystyle=e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}\Bigg\{\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}+\mathcal{R}_{\varrho,it}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle-\left[\mathcal{F}_{it}\left(h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}\right)-\mathcal{F}_{it}\left(h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}+\mathcal{R}_{\varrho,it}\right)\right]\Bigg\},

(B.90)

\displaystyle\mathbb{I}_{6,it}^{III}

\displaystyle=e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}\left[F_{it}(0)-F_{it}\left(h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}\right)\right],

\displaystyle\mathbb{I}_{6,it}^{IV}

\displaystyle=e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}\left[\mathcal{F}_{it}(0)-\mathcal{F}_{it}\left(h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}\right)\right].

The last line in (
B.3
) is due to the fact that the last two terms in
the second equality is
o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)
by mean-value theorem, Assumption (
1
)(iv), and
Assumption (
1
)(viii) and the union bound for
\mathcal{R}_{\varrho,it}
.

For
\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{I}
and
\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{II}
, conditional on
\mathscr{D}_{e}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}
and
h_{it}^{III}
, the randomness is
from
\epsilon_{it}
, and we observe that
\mathbb{I}_{6,it}^{I}
and
\mathbb{I}_{6,it}^{II}
are independent over
i
by conditioning on
\mathscr{D}_{e}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}
and
h_{it}^{III}
. Therefore, we obtain
that
\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{I}\right\|_{2}=o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)
and
\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{II}\right\|_{2}=o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)
by conditional Bernstein’s inequality for
independent sequence given
\mathscr{D}_{e}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}
and
h_{it}^{III}
.

For
\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{III}
, we notice that

\displaystyle\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{III}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}\left[F_{it}(0)-F_{it}\left(h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}f_{it}(\dot{s}_{it})\left(h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}f_{it}(0)\left(h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}\right)+\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}\left[f_{it}(\dot{s}_{it})-f_{it}(0)\right]\left(h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}f_{it}(0)\left(h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}\right)+o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)\hskip 9.24994pt\text{uniformly,}

(B.91)

where the second line is by mean-value theorem with
\left|\dot{s}_{it}\right|
lies between 0 and
\left|h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}\right|
and the last line is by Assumption
1
(viii). By inserting
h_{it}^{III}
and
h_{it}^{IV}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}f_{it}(0)\left(h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}f_{it}(0)v_{t,0}^{0\prime}\hat{V}_{u_{0},i}^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t^{*}=1}^{T}v_{t^{*},0}^{0}\left(\tau-1\left\{\epsilon_{it^{*}}\leq 0\right\}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}f_{it}(0)X_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}\hat{V}_{u_{1}}^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t^{*}=1}^{T}e_{1,it^{*}}v_{t^{*},1}^{0}\left(\tau-1\left\{\epsilon_{it^{*}}\leq 0\right\}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}f_{it}(0)\left(v_{t,0}^{0\prime}\hat{V}_{u_{0},i}^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t^{*}=1}^{T}v_{t^{*},0}^{0}f_{it^{*}}(0)\mu_{1,it^{*}}v_{t^{*},1}^{0\prime}\right)\mathfrak{l}^{\prime}\left(O_{1}^{(1)}\right)^{-1}\left(h_{i}^{I,1}\right)^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t^{*}=1}^{T}\left[\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it^{*}}\leq 0\right\}\right]\begin{bmatrix}v_{t^{*},0}^{0}\\
v_{t^{*},1}^{0}X_{1,it^{*}}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}f_{it}(0)\left(v_{t,0}^{0\prime}\hat{V}_{u_{0},i}^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t^{*}=1}^{T}v_{t^{*},0}^{0}\mathbb{E}\left[f_{it^{*}}(0)\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right]\mu_{1,it^{*}}v_{t^{*},1}^{0\prime}\right)\mathfrak{l}^{\prime}\left(O_{1}^{(1)}\right)^{-1}\left(h_{i}^{I,1}\right)^{-1}h_{i}^{I,2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}f_{it}(0)v_{t,0}^{0\prime}\hat{V}_{u_{0},i}^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t^{*}=1}^{T}v_{t^{*},0}^{0}\mathbb{E}\left[f_{it^{*}}(0)\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right]\mu_{1,it^{*}}\left(O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\dot{v}_{t^{*},1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t^{*},1}^{0}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}f_{it}(0)\mu_{1,it}\left(O_{1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}f_{it}(0)\mu_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}O_{1}^{(1)\prime}\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}\right)v_{t,1}^{0}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{m\in[7]}\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{III,m}.

(B.92)

\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{III,1}
,
\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{III,2}
,
\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{III,3}
can be analyzed in the same manner, and we take
\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{III,1}
for instance. Noticed that

\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{III,1}=\frac{1}{N_{3}T}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}\sum_{t^{*}=1}^{T}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}f_{it}(0)v_{t,0}^{0\prime}\hat{V}_{u_{0},i}^{-1}v_{t^{*},0}^{0}\left(\tau-1\left\{\epsilon_{it^{*}}\leq 0\right\}\right),

it is clear that conditioning on
\mathscr{D}_{e}
,
\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{III,1}
is mean zero by Assumption
1
(ii) and the
randomness is from
\epsilon_{it}
which is strong mixing. With the similar
arguments as the second term in (
B.3
), we obtain that
\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{III,1}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{NT}}\xi_{N}\right)
, and by Assumption
1
(ix), it follows that

\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{III,1}\right\|_{2}=o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-1/2}\right).

We can also analyze
\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{III,4}
and
\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{III,5}

in the same manner. Take
\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{III,4}
for instance. Note that

-\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{III,4}=\frac{1}{N_{3}T}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}\sum_{t^{*}=1}^{T}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}f_{it}(0)\left(v_{t,0}^{0\prime}\hat{V}_{u_{0},i}^{-1}v_{t,0}^{0}\mathbb{E}\left[f_{it^{*}}(0)\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right]\mu_{1,it^{*}}v_{t^{*},1}^{0\prime}\right)\mathfrak{l}^{\prime}\left(O_{1}^{(1)}\right)^{-1}\left(h_{i}^{I,1}\right)^{-1}h_{i}^{I,2}

which is mean zero by conditioning on
\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}
owing
to Assumption
9
(i) and the fact that
h_{i}^{I,1}
and
h_{i}^{I,2}
are fixed given
\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}
. Then by
similar arguments for
\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{III,1}
above, we have

\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{III,4}\right\|=o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-1/2}\right).

(B.93)

For
\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{III,6}
and
\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{III,7}
, we have

\displaystyle\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{III,6}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}f_{it}(0)\mu_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}\right\|_{2}\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\dot{v}_{t,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}v_{t,1}^{0}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}f_{it}(0)\mu_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}\right\|_{2}O_{p}(\eta_{N})

\displaystyle\leq O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N}}\xi_{N}\right)O_{p}(\eta_{N})=o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-1/2}\right),

(B.94)

\displaystyle\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{III,7}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}f_{it}(0)\mu_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}\right\|_{2}\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|v_{t,1}^{0}\right\|_{2}\left\|O_{u,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N}}\xi_{N}\right)O_{p}(\eta_{N})=o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-1/2}\right),

(B.95)

where the second inequality is by Theorem
3.2
(ii) and the third
inequality is by Hoeffding’s inequality conditional on
\mathscr{D}
and the
last line combines Lemma
B.13
(i) and the fact that
\left\|O_{u,1}^{(1)}-O_{1}^{(1)}\right\|_{F}=O_{p}(\eta_{N})
.
Combining (
B.3
)-(
B.3
), we have
\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\mathbb{I}_{6,it}^{III}\right\|_{2}=o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-1/2}\right)
.

Last, we analyze
\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{IV}
. Like (
B.3
), we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{IV}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}\left[\mathcal{F}_{it}(0)-\mathcal{F}_{it}\left(h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}f_{it|h_{it}^{III}}(\dot{s}_{it})\left(h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}f_{it|h_{it}^{III}}(0)\left(h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}\right)+\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}\left[f_{it|h_{it}^{III}}(\dot{s}_{it})-f_{it|h_{it}^{III}}(0)\right]\left(h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}f_{it|h_{it}^{III}}(0)h_{it}^{III}+\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}f_{it|h_{it}^{III}}(0)h_{it}^{IV}+o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}f_{it|h_{it}^{III}}(0)\left[h_{it}^{III}-\mathbb{E}\left(h_{it}^{III}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)\right]+\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}f_{it|h_{it}^{III}}(0)\mathbb{E}\left(h_{it}^{III}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}h_{it}^{IV}\left[f_{it|h_{it}^{III}}(0)-\mathbb{E}\left(f_{it|h_{it}^{III}}(0)|\mathscr{D}_{e}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)\right]+\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}h_{it}^{IV}\mathbb{E}\left(f_{it|h_{it}^{III}}(0)|\mathscr{D}_{e}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle+o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}\left[f_{it|h_{it}^{III}}(0)-f_{it|h_{it}^{III}=0}(0)\right]\left[h_{it}^{III}-\mathbb{E}\left(h_{it}^{III}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}f_{it|h_{it}^{III}=0}(0)\left[h_{it}^{III}-\mathbb{E}\left(h_{it}^{III}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}\mathbb{E}\left(h_{it}^{III}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)\left[f_{it|h_{it}^{III}}(0)-\mathbb{E}\left(f_{it|h_{it}^{III}}(0)|\mathscr{D}_{e}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}\mathbb{E}\left(h_{it}^{III}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)\mathbb{E}\left(f_{it|h_{it}^{III}}(0)|\mathscr{D}_{e}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}h_{it}^{IV}\left[f_{it|h_{it}^{III}}(0)-\mathbb{E}\left(f_{it|h_{it}^{III}}(0)|\mathscr{D}_{e}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)\right]+\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}h_{it}^{IV}\mathbb{E}\left(f_{it|h_{it}^{III}}(0)|\mathscr{D}_{e}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle+o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle:=\sum_{m\in[6]}\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{IV,m}+o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right),

(B.96)

where all the
o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)

terms hold uniformly over
t\in[T]
and
o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)
term in the fourth equality is by mean-value theorem,
Assumption
13
(i) and the fact that
\left|\dot{s}_{it}\right|
lies between 0 and
\left|h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}\right|
.

For
\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{IV,1}
, with Assumption
13
(ii), (
B.84
) and the fact that

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left|\mathbb{E}\left(h_{it}^{III}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle=\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left(v_{t,0}^{0\prime}\hat{V}_{u_{0},i}^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}v_{t,0}^{0}\mathbb{E}\left[f_{it}(0)\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right]\mu_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}\right)\mathfrak{l}^{\prime}\left(O_{1}^{(1)}\right)^{-1}\left(h_{i}^{I,1}\right)^{-1}h_{i}^{I,2}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right]\right|

\displaystyle=\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left|\left(v_{t,0}^{0\prime}\hat{V}_{u_{0},i}^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}v_{t,0}^{0}\mathbb{E}\left[f_{it}(0)\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right]\mu_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}\right)\mathfrak{l}^{\prime}\left(O_{1}^{(1)}\right)^{-1}\left(h_{i}^{I,1}\right)^{-1}h_{i}^{I,2}\right|

\displaystyle=O_{p}(\eta_{N}),

we have

\displaystyle\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{IV,1}\right\|_{2}\lesssim\max_{t\in[T]}\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}\left\|e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}\right\|_{2}\left|h_{it}^{III}\right|\left(\left|h_{it}^{III}\right|+\left|\mathbb{E}\left(h_{it}^{III}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)\right|\right)=O_{p}(\eta_{N}^{2}).

(B.97)

For
\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{IV,2}
,
\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{IV,3}
and
\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{IV,5}
, conditioning on
\mathscr{D}_{e}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}
, the
randomness is only from
h_{it}^{III}
, which is independent across
i
, and

\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{IV,2}
,
\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{IV,3}
and
\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{IV,5}
are zero mean by conditioning on
\mathscr{D}_{e}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}
. Similar to the arguments for
\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{I}
and
\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{II}
in (
B.3
), we have

\displaystyle\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{IV,m}\right\|_{2}=o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right),m\in\{2,3,5\}.

(B.98)

For
\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{IV,4}
, by inserting
\mathbb{E}\left(h_{it}^{III}\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)
and the fact that
\mathbb{E}\left(f_{it|h_{it}^{III}}(0)|\mathscr{D}_{e}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)=f_{it}(0)
, it yields

\displaystyle\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{IV,4}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}f_{it}(0)u_{i,1}^{0}\left(v_{t,0}^{0\prime}\hat{V}_{u_{0},i}^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}v_{t,0}^{0}\mathbb{E}\left[f_{it}(0)\bigg|\mathscr{D}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right]\mu_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0\prime}\right)\mathfrak{l}^{\prime}\left(O_{1}^{(1)}\right)^{-1}\left(h_{i}^{I,1}\right)^{-1}h_{i}^{I,2}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{III,4}=o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-1/2}\right)\hskip 9.24994pt\text{uniformly},

(B.99)

where the last equality is by (
B.93
).

For
\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{IV,6}
, we notice that

\displaystyle\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{IV,6}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}u_{i,1}^{0}h_{it}^{IV}\mathbb{E}\left(f_{it|h_{it}^{III}}(0)|\mathscr{D}_{e}^{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}f_{it}(0)u_{i,1}^{0}h_{it}^{IV}=\sum_{m\in\{5,6,7\}}\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{III,m}=o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-1/2}\right)\hskip 9.24994pt\text{uniformly}.

(B.100)

Combining (
B.3
)-(
B.3
) yields
\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\mathbb{I}_{6,it}^{III}\right\|_{2}=o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-1/2}\right),
which leads to the desired result in statement (i).

(ii) As in (
B.3
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}O_{u,0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0}\Bigg\{\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\varrho_{it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\left(O_{u,0}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,0}^{0},\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)\right\}\right]

\displaystyle-\left(F_{it}(0)-F_{it}\left[\varrho_{it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\left(O_{u,0}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,0}^{0},\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime-1}v_{t,1}^{0}\right)\right]\right)\Bigg\}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}u_{i,1}^{0}\left\{\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}\right\}\right]-\left[\mathcal{F}_{it}(0)-\mathcal{F}_{it}\left(h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}\right)\right]\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}u_{i,1}^{0}\Bigg\{\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}+\mathcal{R}_{\varrho,it}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle-\left[\mathcal{F}_{it}\left(h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}\right)-\mathcal{F}_{it}\left(h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}+\mathcal{R}_{\varrho,it}\right)\right]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}u_{i,1}^{0}\left\{\left[\mathcal{F}_{it}(0)-\mathcal{F}_{it}\left(h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}\right)\right]-\left[F_{it}(0)-F_{it}\left(h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}\right)\right]\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}u_{i,1}^{0}\left[\mathcal{F}_{it}\left(h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}\right)-\mathcal{F}_{it}\left(h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}+\mathcal{R}_{\varrho,it}\right)\right]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}u_{i,1}^{0}\left[F_{it}\left(h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}\right)-F_{it}\left(h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}+\mathcal{R}_{\varrho,it}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}u_{i,1}^{0}\left\{\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}\right\}\right]-\left[\mathcal{F}_{it}(0)-\mathcal{F}_{it}\left(h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}\right)\right]\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}u_{i,1}^{0}\Bigg\{\left[\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}+\mathcal{R}_{\varrho,it}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle-\left[\mathcal{F}_{it}\left(h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}\right)-\mathcal{F}_{it}\left(h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}+\mathcal{R}_{\varrho,it}\right)\right]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}u_{i,1}^{0}\left\{\left[\mathcal{F}_{it}(0)-\mathcal{F}_{it}\left(h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}\right)\right]-\left[F_{it}(0)-F_{it}\left(h_{it}^{III}+h_{it}^{IV}\right)\right]\right\}+o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-1/2}\right),

where the last equality holds by similar arguments as used in the last line
in (
B.3
). We can show the first and second terms are
o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-1/2}\right)
by similar arguments for
\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{I}
and
\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{II}
. The third term is
O_{p}(\eta_{N})
by mean-value theorem and Assumption
1
(viii).
Compared with
\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{III}
and
\mathbb{I}_{6,t}^{IV}
in the
proof of statement (i), the third term here is not mean zero, and converges
to zero at the rate
\eta_{N}
.

Lemma B.31

Under Assumptions
1
-
9
and
Assumption
13
, we have

(i)

\max_{t\in[T]}\Bigg\|\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}e_{1,it}O_{u,1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}\Bigg\{\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\varrho_{it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\hat{v}_{t,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{v}_{t,1}^{(3,1)}\right)\right\}\newline
-\left(F_{it}(0)-F_{it}\left[\varrho_{it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\hat{v}_{t,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{v}_{t,1}^{(3,1)}\right)\right]\right)\Bigg\}\Bigg\|_{2}=o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)
,

(ii)

\max_{t\in[T]}\Bigg\|\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}O_{u,0}^{(1)}u_{i,0}^{0}\Bigg\{\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq\varrho_{it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\hat{v}_{t,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{v}_{t,1}^{(3,1)}\right)\right\}\newline
-\left(F_{it}(0)-F_{it}\left[\varrho_{it}\left(\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)},\hat{v}_{t,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{v}_{t,1}^{(3,1)}\right)\right]\right)\Bigg\}\Bigg\|_{2}=O_{p}(\eta_{N})
.

Proof.
To handle the correlation between
\{\epsilon_{it},e_{it}\}
and
\left\{\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)}\right\}
, we follow similar
arguments as used in the proof of Lemma
B.27
by putting
\left\{\hat{u}_{i,0}^{(3,1)},\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)}\right\}
in a parameter set. Then by
similar arguments as used in the proof of Lemma
B.30
, we can obtain
the desired results.

Lemma B.32

Under Assumptions
1
-
9
and
Assumption
13
, we have

\displaystyle\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|O_{v_{1},t}^{(1)}-\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)\prime}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{F}=o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right).

Proof.
Recall that

O_{v_{1},t}^{(1)}=\left\{I_{K_{1}}+\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)\prime}\right)^{-1}\left[\hat{V}_{v_{1},t}^{I}\right]^{-1}\left[\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}f_{it}(0)e_{1,it}^{2}u_{i,1}^{0}\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}-\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)}\right)^{\prime}\right]\right\}\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)\prime}\right)^{-1}.

Then

O_{v_{1},t}^{(1)}-\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)\prime}\right)^{-1}=\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)\prime}\right)^{-1}\left[\hat{V}_{v_{1},t}^{3}\right]^{-1}\left[\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}f_{it}(0)e_{1,it}^{2}u_{i,1}^{0}\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}-\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)}\right)^{\prime}\right].

Note that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}f_{it}(0)e_{1,it}^{2}u_{i,1}^{0}\left(O_{u,1}^{(1)}u_{i,1}^{0}-\hat{u}_{i,1}^{(3,1)}\right)^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}f_{it}(0)e_{1,it}^{2}\left\{O_{1}^{(1)}\hat{V}_{u_{1}}^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}e_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0}\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)+\mathcal{R}_{i,u}^{1}\right\}u_{i,1}^{0\prime}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N_{3}}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}f_{it}(0)e_{1,it}^{2}O_{1}^{(1)}\hat{V}_{u_{1}}^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}e_{1,it}v_{t,1}^{0}\left(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\}\right)u_{i,1}^{0\prime}+o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N_{3}T}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}\sum_{t=1^{\prime}}^{T}f_{it}(0)e_{1,it}^{2}O_{1}^{(1)}\hat{V}_{u_{1}}^{-1}e_{1,it^{*}}v_{t^{*},1}^{0}\left(\tau-1\left\{\epsilon_{it^{*}}\leq 0\right\}\right)u_{i,1}^{0\prime}+o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=o_{p}\left(\left(N\vee T\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)\hskip 9.24994pt\text{uniformly over }t\in[T]\text{,}

where the second equality is by uniform convergence rate of
\mathcal{R}_{i,u}^{1}
and the last line follows by similar arguments as in (
B.3
) by Bernstein’s inequality conditional on
\mathscr{D}_{e}
. Then
the result follows by noting that
O_{u,1}
is bounded and
\hat{V}_{v_{1},t}^{I}
is bounded uniformly over
t\in[T]
.

B.4
Lemmas for the Consistent Estimation of the Asymptotic Variances

Recall that

\displaystyle\hat{\mathbb{V}}_{u_{j}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i\in[N]}\sum_{t\in[T]}k_{h_{N}}(\hat{\epsilon}_{it})\hat{e}_{j,it}^{2}\hat{\mathrm{v}}_{t,t,j},\hskip 9.24994pt\hat{\mathbb{V}}_{v_{j}}=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i\in[N]}\sum_{t\in[T]}k_{h_{N}}(\hat{\epsilon}_{it})\hat{e}_{j,it}^{2}\hat{\mathrm{u}}_{i,i,j},

\displaystyle\hat{\Omega}_{u_{j}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i\in[N]}\sum_{t\in[T]}\tau(1-\tau)\hat{e}_{j,it}^{2}\hat{\mathrm{v}}_{t,t,j}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i\in[N]}\sum_{t=1}^{T-T_{1}}\sum_{s=t+1}^{t+T_{1}}\hat{e}_{j,it}\hat{e}_{j,is}\hat{\mathrm{v}}_{t,s,j}\left[\tau-K\left(\frac{\hat{\epsilon}_{it}}{h_{N}}\right)\right]\left[\tau-K\left(\frac{\hat{\epsilon}_{is}}{h_{N}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i\in[N]}\sum_{t=1+T_{1}}^{T}\sum_{s=t-T_{1}}^{t-1}\hat{e}_{j,it}\hat{e}_{j,is}\hat{\mathrm{v}}_{t,s,j}\left[\tau-K\left(\frac{\hat{\epsilon}_{it}}{h_{N}}\right)\right]\left[\tau-K\left(\frac{\hat{\epsilon}_{is}}{h_{N}}\right)\right],

\displaystyle\hat{\Omega}_{v_{j}}

\displaystyle=\tau(1-\tau)\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i\in[N]}\sum_{t\in[T]}\hat{e}_{j,it}^{2}\hat{\mathrm{u}}_{i,i,j},

\displaystyle\hat{\Sigma}_{u_{j}}

\displaystyle=\left(\hat{\mathbb{V}}_{u_{j}}\right)^{-1}\hat{\Omega}_{u_{j}}\left(\hat{\mathbb{V}}_{u_{j}}\right)^{-1},\hskip 9.24994pt\hat{\Sigma}_{v_{j}}=\left(\hat{\mathbb{V}}_{v_{j}}\right)^{-1}\hat{\Omega}_{v_{j}}\left(\hat{\mathbb{V}}_{v_{j}}\right)^{-1},

\displaystyle V_{u_{j}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[f_{it}(0)e_{j,it}^{2}v_{t,j}^{0}v_{t,j}^{0\prime}\right],\hskip 9.24994ptV_{v_{j}}^{(a)}=\frac{1}{N_{a}}\sum_{i\in I_{a}}\mathbb{E}\left[f_{it}(0)e_{j,it}^{2}\right]u_{i,j}^{0}u_{i,j}^{0\prime},

\displaystyle\Omega_{u_{j}}

\displaystyle=Var\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}e_{j,it}v_{t,j}^{0}(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\})\right],\hskip 9.24994pt\Omega_{v_{j}}=\tau\left(1-\tau\right)\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i\in I_{3}}\mathbb{E}\left(e_{j,it}^{2}u_{i,j}^{0}u_{i,j}^{0\prime}\right),

\displaystyle\Sigma_{u_{j}}

\displaystyle=O_{j}^{(1)}V_{u_{j}}^{-1}\Omega_{u_{j}}V_{u_{j}}^{-1}O_{j}^{(1)\prime},\hskip 9.24994pt\Sigma_{v_{j}}=O_{j}^{(1)}V_{v_{j}}^{-1}\Omega_{v_{j}}V_{v_{j}}^{-1}O_{j}^{(1)\prime}.

Lemma B.33

Under Assumptions
1
-
10
and
Assumption
13
,
\hat{\Sigma}_{u_{j}}=\Sigma_{u_{j}}+o_{p}(1)
and
\hat{\Sigma}_{v_{j}}=\Sigma_{v_{j}}+o_{p}(1)
.

Proof.
First, we show that
\hat{\mathbb{V}}_{u_{j}}=O_{j}^{(1)}V_{u_{j}}O_{j}^{(1)\prime}+o_{p}(1)
. Note that

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left|\hat{\epsilon}_{it}-\epsilon_{it}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left|\hat{\Theta}_{0,it}-\Theta_{0,it}^{0}\right|+\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\sum_{j\in[p]}\left|X_{j,it}\right|\left|\hat{\Theta}_{j,it}-\Theta_{j,it}^{0}\right|

\displaystyle=R_{\epsilon,it}^{1}+\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T],j\in[p]}\left|X_{j,it}\right|R_{\epsilon,it}^{2},\text{ and}

\displaystyle\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left|k_{h_{N}}(\hat{\epsilon}_{it})-k_{h_{N}}(\epsilon_{it})\right|

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h_{N}}\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left|k(\frac{\hat{\epsilon}_{it}}{h_{N}})-k(\frac{\epsilon_{it}}{h_{N}})\right|\lesssim\frac{1}{h_{N}^{2}}\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left|\hat{\epsilon}_{it}-\epsilon_{it}\right|

\displaystyle=R_{k,it}^{1}+\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T],j\in[p]}\left|X_{j,it}\right|R_{k,it}^{2},

(B.101)

where
\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left|R_{\epsilon,it}^{1}\right|=O_{p}(\eta_{N})
,
\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left|R_{\epsilon,it}^{2}\right|=O_{p}\left(\frac{\log N\vee T}{N\wedge T}\right)
,
\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left|R_{k,it}^{1}\right|=O_{p}(\eta_{N}h_{N}^{-2})
and
\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left|R_{k,it}^{2}\right|=O_{p}(\frac{\log N\vee T}{N\wedge T}h_{N}^{-2})
by (
A.48
) and Assumption
1
(iv). Let

\mathrm{v}_{t,t,j}^{0}=\frac{1}{6}\sum_{a\in[3]}\sum_{b\in[3]\setminus\{a\}}O_{j}^{(b)}v_{t,j}^{0}v_{t,j}^{0\prime}O_{j}^{(b)\prime}

and recall that
\hat{\mathrm{v}}_{t,t,j}=\frac{1}{6}\sum_{a\in[3]}\sum_{b\in[3]\setminus\{a\}}\hat{v}_{t,j}^{(a,b)}\hat{v}_{t,j}^{(a,b)\prime}
. With
Theorem
3.3
, it is clear that

\displaystyle\max_{t\in[T]}\left\|\hat{\mathrm{v}}_{t,t,j}-\mathrm{v}_{t,t,j}^{0}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{6}\sum_{a\in[3]}\sum_{b\in[3]\setminus\{a\}}\left(\hat{v}_{t,j}^{(a,b)}\hat{v}_{t,j}^{(a,b)\prime}-O_{j}^{(b)}v_{t,j}^{0}v_{t,j}^{0\prime}O_{j}^{(b)\prime}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{6}\sum_{a\in[3]}\sum_{b\in[3]\setminus\{a\}}\bigg[\left(\hat{v}_{t,j}^{(a,b)}-O_{j}^{(b)}v_{t,j}^{0}\right)\left(\hat{v}_{t,j}^{(a,b)}-O_{j}^{(b)}v_{t,j}^{0}\right)^{\prime}+O_{j}^{(b)}v_{t,j}^{0}\left(\hat{v}_{t,j}^{(a,b)}-O_{j}^{(b)}v_{t,j}^{0}\right)^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\left(\hat{v}_{t,j}^{(a,b)}-O_{j}^{(b)}v_{t,j}^{0}\right)\left(O_{j}^{(b)}v_{t,j}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\bigg]

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log N\vee T}{N}}\right).

Let
\mathrm{v}_{t,s,j}^{0}=\frac{1}{6}\sum_{a\in[3]}\sum_{b\in[3]\setminus\{a\}}O_{j}^{(b)}v_{t,j}^{0}v_{s,j}^{0\prime}O_{j}^{(b)\prime}
and
recall that
\hat{\mathrm{v}}_{t,s,j}=\frac{1}{6}\sum_{a\in[3]}\sum_{b\in[3]\setminus\{a\}}\hat{v}_{t,j}^{(a,b)}\hat{v}_{s,j}^{(a,b)\prime}
, similarly
as above, we have
\max_{t\in[T],s\in[T]}\left\|\hat{\mathrm{v}}_{t,s,j}-\mathrm{v}_{t,s,j}^{0}\right\|_{F}=O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log N\vee T}{N}}\right)
. It follows that

\displaystyle\hat{\mathbb{V}}_{u_{j}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i\in[N]}\sum_{t\in[T]}k_{h_{N}}(\hat{\epsilon}_{it})\hat{e}_{j,it}^{2}\hat{\mathrm{v}}_{t,t,j}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i\in[N]}\sum_{t\in[T]}k_{h_{N}}(\epsilon_{it})e_{j,it}^{2}\mathrm{v}_{t,t,j}^{0}+\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i\in[N]}\sum_{t\in[T]}k_{h_{N}}(\epsilon_{it})e_{j,it}^{2}\left(\hat{\mathrm{v}}_{t,t,j}-\mathrm{v}_{t,t,j}^{0}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i\in[N]}\sum_{t\in[T]}k_{h_{N}}(\epsilon_{it})\left(\hat{e}_{j,it}^{2}-e_{j,it}^{2}\right)\mathrm{v}_{t,t,j}^{0}+\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i\in[N]}\sum_{t\in[T]}k_{h_{N}}(\epsilon_{it})\left(\hat{e}_{j,it}^{2}-e_{j,it}^{2}\right)\left(\hat{\mathrm{v}}_{t,t,j}-\mathrm{v}_{t,t,j}^{0}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i\in[N]}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left[k_{h_{N}}(\hat{\epsilon}_{it})-k_{h_{N}}(\epsilon_{it})\right]e_{j,it}^{2}\mathrm{v}_{t,t,j}^{0}+\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i\in[N]}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left[k_{h_{N}}(\hat{\epsilon}_{it})-k_{h_{N}}(\epsilon_{it})\right]e_{j,it}^{2}\left(\hat{\mathrm{v}}_{t,t,j}-\mathrm{v}_{t,t,j}^{0}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i\in[N]}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left[k_{h_{N}}(\hat{\epsilon}_{it})-k_{h_{N}}(\epsilon_{it})\right]\left(\hat{e}_{j,it}^{2}-e_{j,it}^{2}\right)\mathrm{v}_{t,t,j}^{0}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i\in[N]}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left[k_{h_{N}}(\hat{\epsilon}_{it})-k_{h_{N}}(\epsilon_{it})\right]\left(\hat{e}_{j,it}^{2}-e_{j,it}^{2}\right)\left(\hat{\mathrm{v}}_{t,t,j}-\mathrm{v}_{t,t,j}^{0}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i\in[N]}\sum_{t\in[T]}k_{h_{N}}(\epsilon_{it})e_{j,it}^{2}\mathrm{v}_{t,t,j}^{0}+O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}h_{N}^{-2}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i\in[N]}\sum_{t\in[T]}k_{h_{N}}(\epsilon_{it})e_{j,it}^{2}\mathrm{v}_{t,t,j}^{0}+o_{p}(1),

(B.102)

where the last two lines combines (
A.3.2
), (
B.4
),
Assumption
10
(ii) and facts that
\hat{e}_{j,it}^{2}-e_{j,it}^{2}=\left(\hat{e}_{j,it}-e_{j,it}\right)^{2}+e_{j,it}\left(\hat{e}_{j,it}-e_{j,it}\right)=O_{p}(\eta_{N}^{2})+e_{j,it}O_{p}(\eta_{N})

uniformly by Lemma
B.21
and
\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left|k_{h_{N}}(\epsilon_{it})\right|=O(h_{N}^{-1})
. By Bernstein’s
inequality, we obtain that

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i\in[N]}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left[k_{h_{N}}(\epsilon_{it})e_{j,it}^{2}-\mathbb{E}\left(k_{h_{N}}(\epsilon_{it})e_{j,it}^{2}\big|\mathscr{D}\right)\right]v_{t,j}^{0}v_{t,j}^{0\prime}\right\|_{F}=O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log N}{NT}}\frac{\xi_{N}^{2}}{h_{N}}\right),

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\mathbb{E}\left[f_{it}(0)e_{j,it}^{2}\big|\mathscr{D}\right]v_{t,j}^{0}v_{t,j}^{0\prime}-\mathbb{E}\left[f_{it}(0)e_{j,it}^{2}v_{t,j}^{0}v_{t,j}^{0\prime}\right]\right\|_{F}=O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log T}{T}}\xi_{N}^{2}\right).

(B.103)

Besides, by Assumption
10
(i), we observe that

\mathbb{E}\left[k_{h_{N}}(\epsilon_{it})\big|\mathscr{D}_{e}\right]=f_{it}(0)+O(h_{N}^{m}),

(B.104)

together with Assumption
10
(v), and it gives

\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i\in[N]}\sum_{t\in[T]}\mathbb{E}\left(k_{h_{N}}(\epsilon_{it})e_{j,it}^{2}\big|\mathscr{D}\right)v_{t,j}^{0}v_{t,j}^{0\prime}=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in[T]}\mathbb{E}\left[f_{it}(0)e_{j,it}^{2}\big|\mathscr{D}\right]v_{t,j}^{0}v_{t,j}^{0\prime}+O(h_{N}^{m}).

(B.105)

Combining (
B.4
)-(
B.105
) and Assumption
10
(ii), we
obtain that
\hat{\mathbb{V}}_{u_{j}}=O_{j}^{(1)}V_{u_{j}}O_{j}^{(1)\prime}+o_{p}(1)
. By analogous analysis and Assumption
10
(v), we
can also show that
\hat{\mathbb{V}}_{v_{j}}^{(1)}=O_{j}^{(1)}V_{v_{j}}O_{j}^{(1)\prime}+o_{p}(1)
.

Next, we show the consistency of
\hat{\Omega}_{u_{j}}
. With the
restriction for
T_{1}
in Assumption
10
(iii), we first note that

\displaystyle\Omega_{u_{j}}

\displaystyle=Var\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}e_{j,it}v_{t,j}^{0}(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\})\right]

\displaystyle=\tau(1-\tau)\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left(e_{j,it}^{2}v_{t,j}^{0}v_{t,j}^{0\prime}\right)+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T-T_{1}}\sum_{s=t+1}^{t+T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[e_{j,it}e_{j,is}v_{t,j}^{0}v_{s,j}^{0\prime}(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\})(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{is}\leq 0\right\})\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1+T_{1}}^{T}\sum_{s=t-T_{1}}^{t-1}\mathbb{E}\left[e_{j,it}e_{j,is}v_{t,j}^{0}v_{s,j}^{0\prime}(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{it}\leq 0\right\})(\tau-\mathbf{1}\left\{\epsilon_{is}\leq 0\right\})\right]+O(\alpha^{T_{1}})

\displaystyle=\tau(1-\tau)\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left(e_{j,it}^{2}v_{t,j}^{0}v_{t,j}^{0\prime}\right)+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T-T_{1}}\sum_{s=t+1}^{t+T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[e_{j,it}e_{j,is}v_{t,j}^{0}v_{s,j}^{0\prime}\left(F_{i,ts}(0,0)-\tau^{2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1+T_{1}}^{T}\sum_{s=t-T_{1}}^{t-1}\mathbb{E}\left[e_{j,it}e_{j,is}v_{t,j}^{0}v_{s,j}^{0\prime}\left(F_{i,ts}(0,0)-\tau^{2}\right)\right]+o(1),

where the second equality is by Assumption
1
(iii), the third
equality is by Assumption
1
(vii) and Assumption
10
(iii).
Compared to
\hat{\Omega}_{u_{j}}
, what remains to show are

\displaystyle\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i\in[N]}\sum_{t\in[T]}\hat{e}_{j,it}^{2}\hat{\mathrm{v}}_{t,t,j}=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left(e_{j,it}^{2}\mathrm{v}_{t,t,j}^{0}\right)+o_{p}(1),

(B.106)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i\in[N]}\sum_{t=1}^{T-T_{1}}\sum_{s=t+1}^{t+T_{1}}\hat{e}_{j,it}\hat{e}_{j,is}\hat{\mathrm{v}}_{t,s,j}\left[\tau-K\left(\frac{\hat{\epsilon}_{it}}{h_{N}}\right)\right]\left[\tau-K\left(\frac{\hat{\epsilon}_{is}}{h_{N}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T-T_{1}}\sum_{s=t+1}^{t+T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[e_{j,it}e_{j,is}\mathrm{v}_{t,s,j}^{0}\left(F_{i,ts}(0,0)-\tau^{2}\right)\right]+o_{p}(1),

(B.107)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i\in[N]}\sum_{t=1+T_{1}}^{T}\sum_{s=t-T_{1}}^{t-1}\hat{e}_{j,it}\hat{e}_{j,is}\hat{\mathrm{v}}_{t,s,j}\left[\tau-K\left(\frac{\hat{\epsilon}_{it}}{h_{N}}\right)\right]\left[\tau-K\left(\frac{\hat{\epsilon}_{is}}{h_{N}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1+T_{1}}^{T}\sum_{s=t-T_{1}}^{t-1}\mathbb{E}\left[e_{j,it}e_{j,is}\mathrm{v}_{t,s,j}^{0}\left(F_{i,ts}(0,0)-\tau^{2}\right)\right]+o_{p}(1).

(B.108)

For (
B.106
), like (
B.4
), we notice that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i\in[N]}\sum_{t\in[T]}\hat{e}_{j,it}^{2}\hat{\mathrm{v}}_{t,t,j}=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i\in[N]}\sum_{t\in[T]}e_{j,it}^{2}\mathrm{v}_{t,t,j}^{0}+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left(e_{j,it}^{2}\mathrm{v}_{t,t,j}^{0}\right)+\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i\in[N]}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left[e_{j,it}^{2}\mathrm{v}_{t,t,j}^{0}-\mathbb{E}\left(e_{j,it}^{2}\mathrm{v}_{t,t,j}^{0}\right)\right]+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left(e_{j,it}^{2}\mathrm{v}_{t,t,j}^{0}\right)+o_{p}(1),

(B.109)

where the first equality is by Lemma
B.21
and (
A.3.2
), and the
second equality is by Bernstein’s inequality and Assumption
10
(v).

For (
B.107
), we observe that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i\in[N]}\sum_{t=1}^{T-T_{1}}\sum_{s=t+1}^{t+T_{1}}\hat{e}_{j,it}\hat{e}_{j,is}\hat{\mathrm{v}}_{t,s,j}\left[\tau-K\left(\frac{\hat{\epsilon}_{it}}{h_{N}}\right)\right]\left[\tau-K\left(\frac{\hat{\epsilon}_{is}}{h_{N}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\tau^{2}\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i\in[N]}\sum_{t=1}^{T-T_{1}}\sum_{s=t+1}^{t+T_{1}}\hat{e}_{j,it}\hat{e}_{j,is}\hat{\mathrm{v}}_{t,s,j}-\tau\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i\in[N]}\sum_{t=1}^{T-T_{1}}\sum_{s=t+1}^{t+T_{1}}\hat{e}_{j,it}\hat{e}_{j,is}\hat{\mathrm{v}}_{t,s,j}K\left(\frac{\hat{\epsilon}_{it}}{h_{N}}\right)

\displaystyle-\tau\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i\in[N]}\sum_{t=1}^{T-T_{1}}\sum_{s=t+1}^{t+T_{1}}\hat{e}_{j,it}\hat{e}_{j,is}\hat{\mathrm{v}}_{t,s,j}K\left(\frac{\hat{\epsilon}_{is}}{h_{N}}\right)+\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i\in[N]}\sum_{t=1}^{T-T_{1}}\sum_{s=t+1}^{t+T_{1}}\hat{e}_{j,it}\hat{e}_{j,is}\hat{\mathrm{v}}_{t,s,j}K\left(\frac{\hat{\epsilon}_{it}}{h_{N}}\right)K\left(\frac{\hat{\epsilon}_{is}}{h_{N}}\right)

(B.110)

such that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i\in[N]}\sum_{t=1}^{T-T_{1}}\sum_{s=t+1}^{t+T_{1}}\hat{e}_{j,it}\hat{e}_{j,is}\hat{\mathrm{v}}_{t,s,j}=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i\in[N]}\sum_{t=1}^{T-T_{1}}\sum_{s=t+1}^{t+T_{1}}e_{j,it}e_{j,is}\mathrm{v}_{t,s,j}^{0}+O_{p}\left(T_{1}\eta_{N}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i\in[N]}\sum_{t=1}^{T-T_{1}}\sum_{s=t+1}^{t+T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left(e_{j,it}e_{j,is}\mathrm{v}_{t,s,j}^{0}\right)+\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i\in[N]}\sum_{t=1}^{T-T_{1}}\sum_{s=t+1}^{t+T_{1}}\left[e_{j,it}e_{j,is}\mathrm{v}_{t,s,j}^{0}-\mathbb{E}\left(e_{j,it}e_{j,is}\mathrm{v}_{t,s,j}^{0}\right)\right]

\displaystyle+O_{p}\left(T_{1}\eta_{N}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i\in[N]}\sum_{t=1}^{T-T_{1}}\sum_{s=t+1}^{t+T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left(e_{j,it}e_{j,is}\mathrm{v}_{t,s,j}^{0}\right)+o_{p}(1),

(B.111)

where the first equality is by the similar arguments as (
B.4
) and
the last line combines Assumption
10
(iii) and the fact that the
second term in the second equality can be shown to be
o_{p}(1)
by
Bernstein’s inequality. Furthermore, with the fact that
\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left|K\left(\frac{\hat{\epsilon}_{it}}{h_{N}}\right)-K\left(\frac{\epsilon_{it}}{h_{N}}\right)\right|\lesssim\frac{1}{h_{N}}\max_{i\in I_{3},t\in[T]}\left|\hat{\epsilon}_{it}-\epsilon_{it}\right|=O_{p}\left(\eta_{N}h_{N}^{-1}\right)
and by the analogous arguments
as above, we can show that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i\in[N]}\sum_{t=1}^{T-T_{1}}\sum_{s=t+1}^{t+T_{1}}\hat{e}_{j,it}\hat{e}_{j,is}\hat{\mathrm{v}}_{t,s,j}K\left(\frac{\hat{\epsilon}_{it}}{h_{N}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i\in[N]}\sum_{t=1}^{T-T_{1}}\sum_{s=t+1}^{t+T_{1}}e_{j,it}e_{j,is}\mathrm{v}_{t,s,j}^{0}K\left(\frac{\epsilon_{it}}{h_{N}}\right)+O_{p}\left(T_{1}\eta_{N}h_{N}^{-1}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i\in[N]}\sum_{t=1}^{T-T_{1}}\sum_{s=t+1}^{t+T_{1}}e_{j,it}e_{j,is}\mathrm{v}_{t,s,j}^{0}\mathbb{E}\left[K\left(\frac{\epsilon_{it}}{h_{N}}\right)\bigg|\mathscr{D}_{e}\right]+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=\frac{\tau}{NT}\sum_{i\in[N]}\sum_{t=1}^{T-T_{1}}\sum_{s=t+1}^{t+T_{1}}e_{j,it}e_{j,is}\mathrm{v}_{t,s,j}^{0}+O(h_{N}^{m})+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=\frac{\tau}{NT}\sum_{i\in[N]}\sum_{t=1}^{T-T_{1}}\sum_{s=t+1}^{t+T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[e_{j,it}e_{j,is}\mathrm{v}_{t,s,j}^{0}\right]+O(h_{N}^{m})+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=\frac{\tau}{NT}\sum_{i\in[N]}\sum_{t=1}^{T-T_{1}}\sum_{s=t+1}^{t+T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[e_{j,it}e_{j,is}\mathrm{v}_{t,s,j}^{0}\right]+o_{p}(1),

(B.112)

where the first equality is similar as (
B.4
), the second equality
is by addition and subtracting and Assumption
10
(iii), the third
equality is by the fact that
\mathbb{E}\left[K\left(\frac{\epsilon_{it}}{h_{N}}\right)\big|\mathscr{D}_{e}\right]=\tau+O(h_{N}^{m})
by the
calculation of nonparametric kernel estimator which can be found in
Galvao and Kato, 2016
. The last equality is similar as the second equality
and combines Assumption
10
(i) and Assumption
10
(ii).

Moreover, similarly as (
B.111
), with the fact that
\mathbb{E}\left[K\left(\frac{\epsilon_{it}}{h_{N}}\right)K\left(\frac{\epsilon_{is}}{h_{N}}\right)\big|\mathscr{D}_{e}\right]=F_{i,ts}(0,0)+O(h_{N}^{m})
, we
can show that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i\in[N]}\sum_{t=1}^{T-T_{1}}\sum_{s=t+1}^{t+T_{1}}\hat{e}_{j,it}\hat{e}_{j,is}\hat{\mathrm{v}}_{t,s,j}K\left(\frac{\hat{\epsilon}_{it}}{h_{N}}\right)K\left(\frac{\hat{\epsilon}_{is}}{h_{N}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i\in[N]}\sum_{t=1}^{T-T_{1}}\sum_{s=t+1}^{t+T_{1}}e_{j,it}e_{j,is}\mathrm{v}_{t,s,j}^{0}K\left(\frac{\epsilon_{it}}{h_{N}}\right)K\left(\frac{\epsilon_{is}}{h_{N}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log(N\vee T)}{N\wedge T}}\frac{T_{1}\xi_{N}^{2}}{h_{N}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i\in[N]}\sum_{t=1}^{T-T_{1}}\sum_{s=t+1}^{t+T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[e_{j,it}e_{j,is}\mathrm{v}_{t,s,j}^{0}F_{i,ts}(0,0)\right]+o_{p}(1).

(B.113)

Combining (
B.110
)-(
B.113
), we complete the proof for (
B.107
). By the analogous arguments, we can show the proof for (
B.108
), which yields
\hat{\Omega}_{u_{j}}=\Omega_{u_{j}}+o_{p}(1)
.

Appendix C
Algorithm for low-rank Estimation

In this section, we provide the algorithm for the case of low-rank
estimation with two regressors, the case of more than two regressors is
self-evident. To solve the regularized quantile regression, let the
optimization problem with two regressors be as

\operatornamewithlimits{min}\limits_{\Theta_{0},\Theta_{1},\Theta_{2}}\hskip 9.24994pt\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\rho_{\tau}\left(y_{it}-\Theta_{0,it}-x_{1,it}\Theta_{1,it}-x_{2,it}\Theta_{2,it}\right)+\nu_{0}\left\|\Theta_{0}\right\|_{\ast}+\nu_{1}\left\|\Theta_{1}\right\|_{\ast}+\nu_{2}\left\|\Theta_{2}\right\|_{\ast}.

As in
Belloni et al., 2022
, the above minimization problem is equivalent
to the following one:

\displaystyle\operatornamewithlimits{min}\limits_{\Theta_{0},\Theta_{1},\Theta_{2},V,W,Z_{\Theta_{0}},Z_{\Theta_{1}},Z_{\Theta_{2}}}\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\rho_{\tau}\left(V_{it}\right)+\nu_{0}\left\|\Theta_{0}\right\|_{\ast}+\nu_{1}\left\|Z_{\Theta_{1}}\right\|_{\ast}+\nu_{2}\left\|Z_{\Theta_{2}}\right\|_{\ast}

\displaystyle s.t.\,\,V=W,\,\,\,W=Y-X_{1}\odot\Theta_{1}-X_{2}\odot\Theta_{2}-Z_{\Theta_{0}},

\displaystyle Z_{\Theta_{0}}-\Theta_{0}=0,\,\,\,Z_{\Theta_{1}}-\Theta_{1}=0,\,\,\,Z_{\Theta_{2}}-\Theta_{2}=0.

As our theoretical results show,
\nu_{0}
,
\nu_{1}
and
\nu_{2}
converge
to zero at rate
\frac{\sqrt{N}\vee\sqrt{T}}{NT}
. The augmented Lagrangian
is

\displaystyle\mathscr{L}\left(V,W,\Theta_{0},Z_{\Theta_{0}},\Theta_{1},Z_{\Theta_{1}},\Theta_{2},Z_{\Theta_{2}},U_{v},U_{w},U_{\Theta_{0}},U_{\Theta_{1}},U_{\Theta_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\rho_{\tau}(V_{it})+\nu_{0}\left\|\Theta_{0}\right\|_{\ast}+\nu_{1}\left\|Z_{\Theta_{1}}\right\|_{\ast}+\nu_{2}\left\|Z_{\Theta_{2}}\right\|_{\ast}+\frac{\rho}{2NT}\left\|V-W+U_{v}\right\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{\rho}{2NT}\left\|W-Y+X_{1}\odot\Theta_{1}+X_{2}\odot\Theta_{2}+Z_{\Theta_{0}}+U_{W}\right\|_{F}^{2}+\frac{\rho}{2NT}\left\|Z_{\Theta_{0}}-\Theta_{0}+U_{\Theta_{0}}\right\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{\rho}{2NT}\left\|Z_{\Theta_{1}}-\Theta_{1}+U_{\Theta_{1}}\right\|_{F}^{2}+\frac{\rho}{2NT}\left\|Z_{\Theta_{2}}-\Theta_{2}+U_{\Theta_{2}}\right\|_{F}^{2},

where
\rho>0
is the penalty parameter.

By ADMM algorithm, similarly as Belloni et al. (2019), updates are as
follows:

\displaystyle V^{k+1}\leftarrow\argmin\limits_{V}\left\{\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i}\sum_{t}\rho_{\tau}\left(V_{it}\right)+\frac{\rho}{2NT}\left\|V-W^{k}+U_{v}^{k}\right\|_{F}^{2}\right\}

(C.1)

\displaystyle(\Theta_{1}^{k+1},\Theta_{2}^{k+1)}\leftarrow\argmin\limits_{\Theta_{1},\Theta_{2}}\bigg\{\left\|W^{k}-Y+X_{1}\odot\Theta_{1}+X_{2}\odot\Theta_{2}+Z_{\Theta_{0}}^{k}+U_{W}^{k}\right\|_{F}^{2}+\left\|Z_{\Theta_{1}}^{k}-\Theta_{1}+U_{\Theta_{1}}^{k}\right\|_{F}^{2}

(C.2)

\displaystyle+\left\|Z_{\Theta_{2}}^{k}-\Theta_{2}+U_{\Theta_{2}}^{k}\right\|_{F}^{2}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\Theta_{0}^{k+1}\leftarrow\argmin\limits_{\Theta_{0}}\left\{\frac{1}{2}\left\|Z_{\Theta_{0}}^{k}-\Theta_{0}+U_{\Theta_{0}}^{k}\right\|_{F}^{2}+\frac{\nu_{0}NT}{\rho}\left\|\Theta_{0}\right\|_{\ast}\right\}

(C.3)

\displaystyle Z_{\Theta_{1}}^{k+1}\leftarrow\argmin\limits_{Z_{\Theta_{1}}}\left\{\frac{1}{2}\left\|\Theta_{1}^{k+1}-U_{\Theta_{1}}^{k}-Z_{\Theta_{1}}\right\|_{F}^{2}+\frac{\nu_{1}NT}{\rho}\left\|Z_{\Theta_{1}}\right\|_{\ast}\right\}

\displaystyle Z_{\Theta_{2}}^{k+1}\leftarrow\argmin\limits_{Z_{\Theta_{2}}}\left\{\frac{1}{2}\left\|\Theta_{2}^{k+1}-U_{\Theta_{2}}^{k}-Z_{\Theta_{2}}\right\|_{F}^{2}+\frac{\nu_{2}NT}{\rho}\left\|Z_{\Theta_{2}}\right\|_{\ast}\right\}

\displaystyle(Z_{\Theta_{0}}^{k+1},W^{k+1})\leftarrow\argmin\limits_{Z_{\Theta_{0}},W}\bigg\{\left\|V^{k+1}-W+U_{v}^{k}\right\|_{F}^{2}+\left\|W-Y+X_{1}\odot\Theta_{1}^{k+1}+X_{2}\odot\Theta_{2}^{k+1}+Z_{\Theta_{0}}+U_{W}^{k}\right\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle+\left\|Z_{\Theta_{0}}-\Theta_{0}^{k+1}+U_{\Theta_{0}}^{k}\right\|_{F}^{2}\bigg\}

\displaystyle U_{v}^{k+1}\leftarrow V^{k+1}-W^{k+1}+U_{v}^{k}

\displaystyle U_{W}^{k+1}\leftarrow W^{k+1}-Y+X_{1}\odot\Theta_{1}^{k+1}+X_{2}\odot\Theta_{2}^{k+1}+Z_{\Theta_{0}}^{k+1}+U_{W}^{k}

\displaystyle U_{\Theta_{0}}^{k+1}\leftarrow Z_{\Theta_{0}}^{k+1}-\tilde{\Theta_{0}}^{k+1}+U_{\Theta_{0}}^{k}

\displaystyle U_{\Theta_{1}}^{k+1}\leftarrow Z_{\Theta_{1}}^{k+1}-\Theta_{1}^{k+1}+U_{\Theta_{1}}^{k}

\displaystyle U_{\Theta_{2}}^{k+1}\leftarrow Z_{\Theta_{2}}^{k+1}-\Theta_{2}^{k+1}+U_{\Theta_{2}}^{k}

For (
C.1
), by Ali et al. (2016),

\displaystyle V^{k+1}\leftarrow P_{+}\left(W^{k}-U_{v}^{k}-\frac{\tau}{\rho}\iota_{N}\iota_{T}^{\prime}\right)+P_{-}\left(W^{k}-U_{v}^{k}-\frac{\left(1-\tau\right)}{\rho}\iota_{N}\iota_{T}^{\prime}\right).

where
\iota_{N}
is the
N\times 1
all-ones vector, and same for
\iota_{T}
. For (
C.2
), first order condition gives

\displaystyle\Theta_{1,it}^{k+1}=\frac{\left(1+x_{2,it}^{2}\right)\left(Z_{\Theta_{1},it}^{k}+U_{\Theta_{1},it}^{k}-A_{it}x_{1,it}\right)-x_{1,it}x_{2,it}\left(Z_{\Theta_{2},it}^{k}+U_{\Theta_{2},it}^{k}-A_{it}x_{2,it}\right)}{1+x_{1,it}^{2}+x_{2,it}^{2}},

\displaystyle\Theta_{2,it}^{k+1}=\frac{\left(1+x_{1,it}^{2}\right)\left(Z_{\Theta_{2},it}^{k}+U_{\Theta_{2},it}^{k}-A_{it}x_{2,it}\right)-x_{1,it}x_{2,it}\left(Z_{\Theta_{1},it}^{k}+U_{\Theta_{1},it}^{k}-A_{it}x_{1,it}\right)}{1+x_{1,it}^{2}+x_{2,it}^{2}},

where

\displaystyle A:=W^{k}+Z_{\Theta_{0}}^{k}+U_{W}^{k}-Y.

To solve (
C.3
), by singular value thresholding estimations, the update
for
\Theta_{0}^{k+1}
is

\displaystyle\Theta_{0}^{k+1}\leftarrow P_{0}D_{0,\frac{\nu_{1}NT}{\rho}}Q_{0}^{\prime},

where
Z_{\Theta_{0}}^{k}+U_{\Theta_{0}}^{k}=P_{0}D_{0}Q_{0}^{\prime}
, and
D_{0,\frac{\nu_{0}}{\rho},ii}=\max(D_{0,ii}-\frac{\nu_{0}}{\rho},0)
.
Similarly for
Z_{\Theta_{1}}^{k+1}
and
Z_{\Theta_{2}}^{k+1}
,

\displaystyle Z_{\Theta_{1}}^{k+1}\leftarrow P_{1}D_{1,\frac{\nu_{1}NT}{\rho}}Q_{1}^{\prime},

\displaystyle Z_{\Theta_{2}}^{k+1}\leftarrow P_{2}D_{2,\frac{\nu_{2}NT}{\rho}}Q_{2}^{\prime},

where
\Theta_{1}^{k+1}-U_{\Theta_{1}}^{k}=P_{1}D_{1}Q_{1}^{\prime}
,
\Theta_{2}^{k+1}-U_{\Theta_{2}}^{k}=P_{2}D_{2}Q_{2}^{\prime}
,
D_{1,\frac{\nu_{1}}{\rho},ii}=\max\left(D_{1,ii}-\frac{\nu_{1}}{\rho},0\right)
, and
D_{2,\frac{\nu_{2}}{\rho},ii}=\max\left(D_{2,ii}-\frac{\nu_{2}}{\rho},0\right)
.

Finally, let
\tilde{A}:=-Y+X_{1}\odot\Theta_{1}^{k+1}+X_{2}\odot\Theta_{2}^{k+1}+U_{W}^{k+1},\,\,\tilde{B}:=-V^{k+1}-U_{v}^{k},\,\,\tilde{C}:=-\tilde{\Theta_{0}}^{k+1}+U_{\Theta_{0}}^{k}
, then

\displaystyle Z_{\Theta_{0}}^{k+1}\leftarrow\frac{-\tilde{A}-2\tilde{C}+\tilde{B}}{3},

\displaystyle W^{k+1}\leftarrow-\tilde{A}-\tilde{C}-2Z_{\Theta_{0}}^{k+1}.
